    if (f.error) throw f.error;
    var cut = Date.now() - KEEP_DAYS * 86400000;
    var flows = (f.data || []).filter(function (x) {
      if (x.status === 'backed_out') return !x.updated_at || Date.parse(x.updated_at) >= cut;
      return x.status === 'running' || !x.call_at || Date.parse(x.call_at) >= cut;
    });
    if (!flows.length) return [];
    var ids = flows.map(function (x) { return x.id; }), cids = flows.map(function (x) { return x.candidate_id; });
    var res = await Promise.all([db.from('hub_onboard_steps').select(STEP_COLS).in('flow_id', ids), db.from('hub_candidates').select(CAND_COLS).in('id', cids)]);
    if (res[0].error) throw res[0].error;
    if (res[1].error) throw res[1].error;
    return flows.map(function (x) {
      var c = (res[1].data || []).filter(function (y) { return String(y.id) === String(x.candidate_id); })[0] || {};
      var rows = (res[0].data || []).filter(function (y) { return String(y.flow_id) === String(x.id); });
      return Object.assign({}, x, { name: c.name || 'Someone', role: c.role || '', email: c.email || '', member_id: c.member_id, steps: rows });
    });
  }
  /* an offer that has gone out with no answer on the card, for those who answer by replying to the email */
  async function readOffers() {
    var r = await db.from('hub_candidates').select(OFFER_COLS).eq('status', 'offer');
    if (r.error) throw r.error;
    return (r.data || []).filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'offer' && c.offer_sent_at && !c.accepted_on; });
  }
  async function readPeople() {
    if (S.people.length) return;
    var r = await db.from('team_members').select('id, name, active, email');   /* wave275: the email names who proposed */
    if (!r.error) S.people = (r.data || []).filter(function (p) { return p.name; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
  }
  async function readCfg() {
    var r = await db.from('app_config').select('key, value').in('key', ['onboard_grid_days', 'onboard_grid_start', 'onboard_grid_end', 'onboard_grid_cell_min']);
    var map = { onboard_grid_days: 'days', onboard_grid_start: 'start', onboard_grid_end: 'end', onboard_grid_cell_min: 'cell' };
    ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { var n = Number(x.value); if (map[x.key] && isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && (n > 0 || x.key === 'onboard_grid_start')) S.cfg[map[x.key]] = n; });
  }
  function load() { return R.load(); }
  function loadHr() {
    if (S.loading) return S.loading;
    S.loading = (async function () {
      S.refused = false; S.loadErr = '';   /* wave275 reads these: the database's own "Only HR" refusal, and any other failure, apart */
      try {
        var r = await db.rpc('wa271_ready_list');
        if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
        S.denied = false; S.err = '';
        S.ready = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (x) { return R.readyStatuses.indexOf(x.status) >= 0; });   /* wave275 adds offered */
        var begun = await readStarted();
        S.started = begun.filter(function (x) { return x.status !== 'backed_out'; });
        S.backed = begun.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'backed_out'; });
        S.offers = (await readOffers()).filter(function (c) { return !S.ready.some(function (x) { return String(x.candidate_id) === String(c.id); }); });   /* wave275: an offered flow has its own card */
        await readWelcomes(S.started);
        await Promise.all([readPeople(), readCfg(), readPw()]);
      } catch (e) {
        var msg = String((e && e.message) || e);
        if (/Only HR/i.test(msg)) S.refused = true; else S.loadErr = msg;
        if (/Only HR/i.test(msg) || !surelyHr()) S.denied = true;
        else S.err = 'The Hub could not load this list: ' + msg;
      }
      S.loaded = true; S.loading = null;
      /* a picker or panel for someone who has left the list goes with them */
      Object.keys(S.pick).forEach(function (id) { if (!S.ready.some(function (x) { return String(x.flow_id) === id; })) delete S.pick[id]; });
      Object.keys(S.act).forEach(function (id) { if (!flowOf(id)) delete S.act[id]; });
      paint();
    })();
    return S.loading;
  }
  R.load = loadHr;

  /* ---------- Ready to onboard: one card ---------- */
  function warnBox(w) { return '<div class="o271warn' + (/practice applicant/i.test(w) ? ' pr' : '') + '">' + E(w) + '</div>'; }
  function noteHtml(id) {
    var n = S.note[id]; if (!n) return '';
    return (n.ok ? '<div class="o271okn">' + E(n.ok) + '</div>' : '') + (n.warnings || []).map(warnBox).join('') + (n.err ? '<div class="o271err">' + E(n.err) + '</div>' : '');
  }
  function managerLine(x) {
    if (x.manager_member_id) return 'Manager: ' + E(nameOf(x.manager_member_id)) + ' (' + E(RULE.chosen) + ')';
    var m = x.default_manager;
    if (m && m.member_id) return 'Manager: ' + E(m.name) + ' (' + E(RULE[m.how] || m.how) + ')';
    return 'Manager: ' + E(RULE.none);
  }
  function readyCard(x) {
    var other = Number(x.other_open_rows) || 0, id = x.flow_id, P = S.pick[id];
    return '<div class="o271cd" data-o271flow="' + E(id) + '">'
      + '<div class="o271top"><b>' + E(x.name) + '</b><span>' + E(x.role || '') + '</span>' + (x.practice ? '<i class="o271tag pr" style="font-style:normal">Practice</i>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="o271chips">'
      + '<span class="o271tag' + (x.grids && x.grids.hire === 'marked' ? ' ok' : '') + '">New hire\'s grid: ' + (x.grids && x.grids.hire === 'marked' ? 'marked' : 'not marked yet') + '</span>'
      + '<span class="o271tag' + (x.grids && x.grids.manager === 'marked' ? ' ok' : '') + '">Manager\'s grid: ' + (x.grids && x.grids.manager === 'marked' ? 'marked' : 'not marked yet') + '</span>'
      + (x.late ? '<span class="o271tag late">Answered after the reply-by date</span>' : '')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">' + (x.accepted_on ? 'Accepted ' + E(dayText(x.accepted_on)) : '') + (x.offer_start ? ' &middot; The offer suggested ' + E(dayText(x.offer_start)) : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">' + managerLine(x) + '</div>'
      + (other ? warnBox('This person has ' + other + ' other application' + (other === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' still open. Close ' + (other === 1 ? 'it' : 'them') + ' by hand so they do not get a second account.') : '')
      + noteHtml(id)
      + '<div class="o271btns">' + (R.hrTools() ? '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="link" data-f="' + E(id) + '" data-cand="' + E(x.candidate_id) + '"' + (S.busy['l' + id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Send the availability link</button>'
      + '<button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-o271a="open" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (S.busy['o' + id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (P ? 'Close' : 'Choose times and preview') + '</button>' : '')
      + '<button type="button" class="o271b dng" data-o271a="act" data-kind="back" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (S.act[id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>They backed out</button></div>'
      + (S.act[id] ? R.panelHtml(flowOf(id), S.act[id]) : '')
      + (P ? pickHtml(x, P) : '')
      + '</div>';
  }
  R.readyCard = readyCard;
  /* an offer still out: an answer by email has its own button, and asks first */
  function offerCard(c) {
    var id = c.id, asking = S.yes[id], busy = S.busy['y' + id];
    return '<div class="o271cd" data-o271offer="' + E(id) + '">'
      + '<div class="o271top"><b>' + E(c.name) + '</b><span>' + E(c.role || '') + '</span></div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">Offer sent ' + E(dayText(c.offer_sent_at)) + '</div>'
      + noteHtml('c' + id)
      + (asking
        ? '<div class="o271ln"><b>Did ' + E(c.name) + ' say yes?</b> The Hub saves that they accepted today and emails them the availability link.</div>'
          + '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-o271a="yesgo" data-cand="' + E(id) + '"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Yes, send the link</button><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="yesno" data-cand="' + E(id) + '">Cancel</button></div>'
        : '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="yesask" data-cand="' + E(id) + '">They said yes by email: send the availability link</button></div>')
      + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the grid: a key names it, 'p<flow>' on the picker and 'a<flow>' on a change panel ---------- */
  function gridOf(key) { var id = String(key).slice(1); var o = key.charAt(0) === 'p' ? S.pick[id] : S.act[id]; return o && o.g; }
  function newGrid(key, pv, mode) {
    var g = { key: key, pv: pv, mode: mode, vals: { call: '', one: '' }, hideManager: false, days: [], rows: [] };
    var slots = (pv.grids && (pv.grids.hire || []).concat(pv.grids.manager || [])) || [];
    /* walk the instants and read each one on the Toronto clock: a clock change takes care of itself */
    var cfg = S.cfg, step = cfg.cell * 60000, now = Date.now(), t = Math.ceil(now / step) * step, last = now + cfg.days * 86400000;
    slots.forEach(function (x) { var ms = Date.parse(x); if (ms > last) last = ms + step; });
    var byDate = {}, rows = {};
    for (; t < last; t += step) {
      var w = wall(t);
      if (w.mins < cfg.start * 60 || w.mins + cfg.cell > cfg.end * 60) continue;
      if (!byDate[w.date]) { byDate[w.date] = { date: w.date, at: t, cells: {} }; g.days.push(byDate[w.date]); }
      byDate[w.date].cells[w.mins] = t;
      rows[w.mins] = true;
    }
    g.rows = Object.keys(rows).map(Number).sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; });
    g.hire = setOf(pv.grids && pv.grids.hire); g.manager = setOf(pv.grids && pv.grids.manager);
    g.events = (pv.hub_events || []).map(function (e) { return { from: Date.parse(e.start_at), to: Date.parse(e.end_at), who: e.who || [], summary: e.summary || 'A meeting' }; });
    return g;
  }
  function setOf(a) { var o = {}; (a || []).forEach(function (x) { o[Date.parse(x)] = true; }); return o; }
  function clashes(g, ms, mode) {
    var end = ms + S.cfg.cell * 60000;
    return g.events.filter(function (e) { return e.from < end && e.to > ms && (mode === 'one' ? (e.who.indexOf('hire') >= 0 || (e.who.indexOf('manager') >= 0 && !g.hideManager)) : e.who.indexOf('hire') >= 0); });
  }
  function rowLabel(mins) { var h = Math.floor(mins / 60), mi = mins % 60; return (h % 12 || 12) + ':' + pad2(mi) + (h < 12 ? ' am' : ' pm'); }
  function dayHead(d) {
    var at = new Date(d.at);
    return E(at.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: TZ, weekday: 'short' })) + '<br>' + E(at.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: TZ, month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }));
  }
  function tableHtml(g) {
    var call = toInstant(g.vals.call), one = toInstant(g.vals.one);
    var h = '<table class="o271g"><thead><tr><th></th>' + g.days.map(function (d) { return '<th>' + dayHead(d) + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    g.rows.forEach(function (mins) {
      h += '<tr><th>' + E(rowLabel(mins)) + '</th>';
      g.days.forEach(function (d) {
        var t = d.cells[mins];
        if (t == null) { h += '<td></td>'; return; }
        var cl = ['o271c'], bits = [whenText(t)];
        if (g.hire[t]) { cl.push('h'); bits.push('the new hire marked this'); }
        if ((g.mode === 'one' || g.both) && !g.hideManager && g.manager[t]) { cl.push('m'); bits.push(g.words ? g.words.mTip : 'the manager marked this'); }   /* wave275: both, the manager's hours under both picks */
        var busy = clashes(g, t, g.mode);
        if (busy.length) { cl.push('busy'); bits.push('in a meeting: ' + busy.map(function (e) { return e.summary; }).join(', ')); }
        var mk = g.mark && g.mark(t, g.mode); if (mk && !busy.length) { cl.push('busy'); bits.push(mk); }   /* wave275: too soon, or another new hire's time */
        var label = '';
        if (t === call) { cl.push('scall'); label = 'Call'; } else if (t === one) { cl.push('sone'); label = '1-1'; }
        h += '<td><button type="button" class="' + cl.join(' ') + '" data-o271cell="' + E(new Date(t).toISOString()) + '" data-g="' + E(g.key) + '" title="' + E(bits.join('; ')) + '" aria-label="' + E(bits.join('; ')) + '">' + label + '</button></td>';
      });
      h += '</tr>';
    });
    return h + '</tbody></table>';
  }
  function legendHtml(g) {
    var w = g.words || { h: 'New hire marked', m: 'Manager marked', busy: 'In a meeting' };   /* wave275 names its own marks */
    return '<div class="o271lg"><span><i style="background:rgba(16,63,57,.30)"></i>' + E(w.h) + '</span>'
      + ((g.mode === 'one' || g.both) && !g.hideManager ? '<span><i style="background:rgba(111,100,171,.34)"></i>' + E(w.m) + '</span><span><i style="background:' + GREEN + '"></i>Both</span>' : '')
      + '<span><i style="background:repeating-linear-gradient(45deg,#C9C8C1,#C9C8C1 3px,#E6E5DE 3px,#E6E5DE 6px)"></i>' + E(w.busy) + '</span><span>Toronto time</span></div>';
  }
  function gridHtml(g) {
    if (!g.days.length) return '';
    return '<div data-o271lg="' + E(g.key) + '">' + legendHtml(g) + '</div><div class="o271gw" data-o271grid="' + E(g.key) + '">' + tableHtml(g) + '</div>';
  }
  function refreshGrid(g) {
    var box = document.querySelector('[data-o271grid="' + g.key + '"]'); if (box) box.innerHTML = tableHtml(g);   /* the box keeps its scroll on a phone */
    var lg = document.querySelector('[data-o271lg="' + g.key + '"]'); if (lg) lg.innerHTML = legendHtml(g);
  }
  function saidHtml(g, mode) {
    var v = g.vals[mode]; if (!v) return { text: mode === 'call' ? 'Not picked yet' : 'Not picked: it can be booked later', cl: '' };
    var ms = toInstant(v); if (ms == null) return { text: 'That time does not exist on the Toronto clock. Pick another.', cl: 'cl' };
    var busy = clashes(g, ms, mode);
    return { text: whenText(ms) + (busy.length ? '. Clashes with ' + busy.map(function (e) { return e.summary; }).join(', ') : ''), cl: busy.length ? 'cl' : '' };
  }

  /* ---------- the picker and preview ---------- */
  function memberOptions(chosen) {
    return '<option value="">Choose a manager...</option>' + S.people.filter(function (p) { return p.active !== false; }).map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + E(p.id) + '"' + (String(p.id) === String(chosen) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(p.name) + '</option>'; }).join('');
  }
  function startBlock(P) {
    var addr = String(P.addr || '').trim().toLowerCase(), googleOn = !P.skip.google, g = P.g, canva = String(P.canva || '').trim();
    if (canva && !CANVA_LINK.test(canva)) return 'The Canva link must start with https://www.canva.com/. Copy it from Invite people in Canva, or leave it empty.';
    if (!g.vals.call) return 'Pick a time for the onboarding call.';
    if (toInstant(g.vals.call) == null) return 'That call time does not exist on the Toronto clock. Pick another.';
    if (g.vals.one && toInstant(g.vals.one) == null) return 'That 1-on-1 time does not exist on the Toronto clock. Pick another.';
    if (!P.managerId) return 'Choose a manager.';
    if (addr && !ADDR.test(addr)) return 'The new address must be a plain address ending in @welfareavenue.com.';
    if (!addr && googleOn) return 'Type the new address, or turn off the Google account step.';
    if (P.takenAddr && P.takenAddr === addr) {
      if (P.owner) return addr + ' is on the roster profile of ' + P.owner + '. Change the address, or clear it.';
      if (googleOn) return addr + ' is already in use. Change the address, or turn off the Google account step.';
      if (!P.own) return addr + ' is already in use, so the Hub will not store it. Change the address, or clear it.';
    }
    return '';
  }
  /* HR turned the Google step off while the address clashes: it must not stay in the box and reach another person's mailbox. Their own address stays. */
  function clearClash(id) {
    var P = S.pick[id]; if (!P) return;
    var addr = String(P.addr || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!P.takenAddr || P.takenAddr !== addr || P.own) return;
    P.addr = ''; P.takenAddr = ''; P.owner = '';
    var box = document.querySelector('[data-o271flow="' + id + '"] [data-o271i="addr"]'); if (box) box.value = '';
  }
  function canvaHowHtml() { return '<div class="o271ln">' + E(CANVA_HOW) + ' <a href="https://www.canva.com/settings/people" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open Canva people settings</a></div>'; }
  function canvaSaid(v) { return String(v || '').trim() ? '' : 'No Canva link yet. You can start without one, but get a fresh link before the welcome email goes out.'; }
  function saidBlock(g, mode) { var s = saidHtml(g, mode); return '<div class="o271said ' + s.cl + '" data-o271said="' + mode + '">' + E(s.text) + '</div>'; }
  function pickHtml(x, P) {
    var id = x.flow_id, pv = P.pv, g = P.g, block = startBlock(P);
    var welcomeStep = (pv.steps || []).filter(function (s) { return s.key === 'welcome'; })[0];
    var h = '<div class="o271pn">' + (pv.warnings || []).map(warnBox).join('')
      + '<h3 class="o271h">1. The times</h3>'
      + '<div class="o271md" role="group" aria-label="Which meeting you are picking"><button type="button" class="' + (g.mode === 'call' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-o271a="mode" data-mode="call" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Onboarding call</button><button type="button" class="' + (g.mode === 'one' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-o271a="mode" data-mode="one" data-f="' + E(id) + '">1-on-1</button></div>'
      + (g.hideManager ? '<div class="o271ln">The grid does not show the manager you just chose: these marks are the other manager\'s.</div>' : '')
      + gridHtml(g)
      + '<div class="o271two"><div><label class="o271f">Onboarding call (Toronto time)</label><input type="datetime-local" data-o271i="call" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(g.vals.call) + '">' + saidBlock(g, 'call') + '</div>'
      + '<div><label class="o271f">1-on-1 with the manager (Toronto time, optional)</label><input type="datetime-local" data-o271i="one" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(g.vals.one) + '">' + saidBlock(g, 'one') + '</div></div>'
      + '<h3 class="o271h">2. The preview</h3>'
      + '<div class="o271two"><div><label class="o271f">New address</label><input type="text" autocomplete="off" data-o271i="addr" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(P.addr) + '"></div>'
      + '<div><label class="o271f">Manager</label><select data-o271i="mgr" data-f="' + E(id) + '">' + memberOptions(P.managerId) + '</select><div class="o271said" data-o271said="rule">' + E(ruleLine(P)) + '</div></div></div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">Department: ' + E(pv.department || 'not set') + '</div>'
      + '<label class="o271f">Group session (leave blank to leave the line out)</label><input type="text" data-o271i="group" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(P.group) + '">'
      + '<label class="o271f">Welcome email: subject</label><input type="text" style="max-width:none" data-o271i="subj" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(P.subject) + '">'
      + '<label class="o271f">Welcome email: text</label><textarea data-o271i="text" data-f="' + E(id) + '">' + E(P.text) + '</textarea>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">Anything in [square brackets] fills in from the times, manager, address and links, and changes with them after Start. Leave the brackets in place, even if you change the rest.</div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">[first password] is filled in only in the email itself, as it goes: nobody reads the password here or on the card. If the Google account is not made by then, that step says HR will send the login details separately.</div>'
      + (welcomeStep && welcomeStep.mode === 'hr_task' ? '<div class="o271ln"><b>HR sends this one for now.</b> The Hub saves your edits and leaves it as a to-do on the card.</div>'
        : welcomeStep && welcomeStep.mode === 'run' ? '<div class="o271ln" data-o271who="welcome"><b>The Hub sends this one</b> from hub@ to their personal address once the invites are out, with your edits. A copy stays on the card.</div>' : '')
      + canvaHowHtml()
      + '<label class="o271f">Fresh Canva invite link</label><input type="text" style="max-width:none" autocomplete="off" data-o271i="canva" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(P.canva) + '">'
      + '<div class="o271said" data-o271said="canva">' + E(canvaSaid(P.canva)) + '</div>'
      + '<h3 class="o271h">3. The steps that will run</h3>'
      + (pv.steps || []).map(function (s) {
          var locked = s.key === 'roster', word = s.mode === 'hr_task' ? 'HR does this one' : s.mode === 'practice' ? 'Practice: nothing real changes' : 'The Hub does this';
          return '<label class="o271sw"><input type="checkbox" data-o271skip="' + E(s.key) + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (P.skip[s.key] ? '' : ' checked') + (locked ? ' disabled' : '') + '> ' + E(s.label) + ' <small>' + E(word) + (locked ? '. Every other step needs it.' : '') + '</small></label>';
        }).join('')
      + (P.err ? '<div class="o271err">' + E(P.err) + '</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="o271said cl" data-o271said="block">' + E(block) + '</div>'
      + '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-o271a="start" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (block || S.busy['s' + id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy['s' + id] ? 'Working through the steps...' : 'Start onboarding') + '</button></div>'
      + '</div>';
    return h;
  }
  function ruleLine(P) {
    if (!P.managerId) return RULE.none;
    if (String(P.managerId) === String(P.pv.manager && P.pv.manager.member_id) && P.pv.manager.how !== 'chosen') return 'The Hub used: ' + (RULE[P.pv.manager.how] || P.pv.manager.how);
    return RULE.chosen;
  }
  /* HR changed the dropdown: saved now, so the hourly job sends the right manager their link before Start. The other manager's marks went with it. */
  async function saveManager(id, member, before) {
    var x = S.ready.filter(function (r) { return String(r.flow_id) === String(id); })[0];
    try {
      var r = await run('set_manager', { flow_id: Number(id), member_id: Number(member) });
      if (x) x.manager_member_id = Number(member);
      var P = S.pick[id]; if (!P) { paint(); return; }
      P.err = '';
      if (!r.practice) {
        P.pv.manager = { member_id: Number(member), name: nameOf(member), how: 'chosen' };
        if (r.cleared) { P.pv.grids = Object.assign({}, P.pv.grids, { manager: [] }); P.g.manager = {}; }
        P.g.hideManager = false;
      }
      paint();
    } catch (e) {
      var Q = S.pick[id];
      if (Q) { Q.managerId = before; Q.err = e.message; Q.g.hideManager = String(before) !== String(Q.pv.manager && Q.pv.manager.member_id); }
      toast(e.message); paint();
    }
  }
  /* an input changed: what depends on it, without redrawing what HR is typing in */
  function liveUpdate(id) {
    var P = S.pick[id]; if (!P) return;
    var root = document.querySelector('[data-o271flow="' + id + '"]'); if (!root) return;
    ['call', 'one'].forEach(function (m) { var el = root.querySelector('[data-o271said="' + m + '"]'); if (el) { var s = saidHtml(P.g, m); el.textContent = s.text; el.className = 'o271said ' + s.cl; } });
    var rule = root.querySelector('[data-o271said="rule"]'); if (rule) rule.textContent = ruleLine(P);
    var cs = root.querySelector('[data-o271said="canva"]'); if (cs) cs.textContent = canvaSaid(P.canva);
    var block = startBlock(P), bl = root.querySelector('[data-o271said="block"]'); if (bl) bl.textContent = block;
    var b = root.querySelector('[data-o271a="start"]'); if (b && !S.busy['s' + id]) b.disabled = !!block;
  }
  async function openPick(x) {
    var id = x.flow_id;
    if (S.pick[id]) { delete S.pick[id]; paint(); return; }
    S.busy['o' + id] = true; delete S.note[id]; paint();
    try {
      var pv = await run('preview', { flow_id: id });
      var g = newGrid('p' + id, pv, 'call');
      if (x.call_at) g.vals.call = toLocal(x.call_at);
      if (x.one_on_one_at) g.vals.one = toLocal(x.one_on_one_at);
      S.pick[id] = { pv: pv, g: g, addr: pv.address || '', managerId: (pv.manager && pv.manager.member_id) || '', group: '', subject: (pv.welcome && pv.welcome.subject) || '', text: (pv.welcome && pv.welcome.text) || '', skip: {}, takenAddr: pv.address_taken || pv.address_owner ? String(pv.address || '').toLowerCase() : '', owner: pv.address_owner || '', own: !!pv.address_own, canva: pv.canva_link || '', err: '' };
    } catch (e) { S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; }
    delete S.busy['o' + id]; paint();
  }
  /* the address changed: ask again. Only the warnings and the clash change; what HR typed stays */
  async function recheckAddress(id) {
    var P = S.pick[id]; if (!P) return;
    var addr = String(P.addr || '').trim().toLowerCase(); P.addr = addr;
    if (!ADDR.test(addr)) { liveUpdate(id); return; }
    try {
      var pv = await run('preview', { flow_id: Number(id), wa_email: addr });
      if (!S.pick[id] || String(S.pick[id].addr).trim().toLowerCase() !== addr) return;
      P.pv.warnings = pv.warnings; P.pv.address_taken = pv.address_taken;
      P.takenAddr = pv.address_taken || pv.address_owner ? addr : '';
      P.owner = pv.address_owner || ''; P.own = !!pv.address_own;
      if (P.skip.google) clearClash(id);
      paint();
    } catch (e) { P.err = e.message; paint(); }
  }
  async function start(id) {
    var P = S.pick[id], x = S.ready.filter(function (r) { return String(r.flow_id) === String(id); })[0];
    if (!P || !x || S.busy['s' + id] || startBlock(P)) return;
    S.busy['s' + id] = true; P.err = ''; paint();
    var body = {
      flow_id: Number(id), wa_email: String(P.addr || '').trim().toLowerCase(), manager_member_id: Number(P.managerId), call_at: isoOf(P.g.vals.call),
      one_on_one_at: P.g.vals.one ? isoOf(P.g.vals.one) : null, group_session: P.group || null, canva_link: String(P.canva || '').trim(),
      skip: Object.keys(P.skip).filter(function (k) { return P.skip[k]; })
    };
    var w0 = P.pv.welcome || {};
    if (P.subject !== (w0.subject || '') || P.text !== (w0.text || '')) body.welcome = { subject: P.subject, text: P.text };   /* an untouched email stays the Hub's own */
    try {
      await run('start', body);
      delete S.pick[id];
      delete S.busy['s' + id];
      await load();
      toast('Onboarding started');
    } catch (e) { delete S.busy['s' + id]; P.err = e.message; toast(e.message); paint(); }
  }

  /* ---------- a started card ---------- */
  function stepHtml(f, key, label, n) {
    var r = (f.steps || []).filter(function (x) { return x.step === key; })[0];
    if (!r) return '<li><div class="ttl">' + n + '. ' + E(label) + '</div></li>';
    var status = r.status, id = f.id;
    var stuck = (status === 'running' || status === 'pending') && Date.now() - Date.parse(r.at) >= STALE_MS;
    var tagClass = status === 'done' ? 'ok' : status === 'failed' ? 'no' : status === 'hr_task' ? 'hr' : '';
    var h = '<li><div class="ttl">' + n + '. ' + E(label) + ' <span class="o271tag ' + tagClass + '">' + E(WORD[status] || status) + '</span>' + (r.practice ? '<span class="o271tag pr">Practice</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (r.detail) h += '<div class="dt' + (status === 'failed' ? ' fail' : '') + '">' + (status === 'hr_task' ? 'To do: ' : '') + E(r.detail) + '</div>';
    if (key === 'welcome' && WELCOME_COPY.indexOf(status) >= 0) h += welcomeHtml(f) + (status === 'done' ? '' : canvaCardHtml(f));   /* wave275: a copy stays on the card once it is sent */
    if (status === 'hr_task' && key !== 'google') h += '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="taskdone" data-f="' + E(id) + '" data-step="' + E(key) + '"' + (S.busy['t' + id + key] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Done</button></div>';
    if (status === 'failed' || stuck) h += '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="retry" data-f="' + E(id) + '" data-step="' + E(key) + '"' + (S.busy['r' + id + key] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Retry</button></div>';
    if (key === 'google' && (status === 'hr_task' || status === 'failed') && R.hrTools()) {   /* "I created it, continue" is HR's (google_manual) */
      h += '<div class="row"><input type="text" autocomplete="off" aria-label="The address you created" placeholder="first.last@welfareavenue.com" data-o271i="gaddr" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(gaddrOf(f)) + '">'
        + '<button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-o271a="gmanual" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (S.busy['g' + id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>I created it, continue</button></div>';
    }
    return h + '</li>';
  }
  /* the fresh Canva link under the welcome to-do: Canva's links last 30 days, so HR pastes or replaces it before sending. none: the sentence when there
     is no link yet (wave275's card, before booking, says the welcome goes when the times are booked) */
  function canvaCardHtml(f, none) {
    var id = f.id, typed = S.canva[id] != null ? S.canva[id] : (f.canva_link || '');
    return '<div data-o271canva="' + E(id) + '">' + canvaHowHtml()
      + (f.canva_link
        ? '<div class="row"><span class="o271ln"><b>Canva link:</b> ' + E(f.canva_link) + '</span><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="copycanva" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Copy the link</button></div>'
        : '<div class="o271warn">' + E(none || 'No Canva link yet. Get a fresh one before you send the welcome email.') + '</div>')
      + '<div class="row"><input type="text" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Fresh Canva invite link" placeholder="https://www.canva.com/..." data-o271i="ccanva" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(typed) + '">'
      + '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="canvasave" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (S.busy['v' + id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Save the link</button></div></div>';
  }
  async function copyCanva(id) {
    var f = flowOf(id); if (!f || !f.canva_link) return;
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(f.canva_link); toast('Copied'); }
    catch (e) { toast('The browser would not copy it. Select the link and copy it yourself.'); }
  }
  async function saveCanva(id) {
    var f = flowOf(id); if (!f || S.busy['v' + id]) return;
    var link = String(S.canva[id] != null ? S.canva[id] : (f.canva_link || '')).trim();
    S.busy['v' + id] = true; delete S.note[id]; paint();
    try { await run('set_canva_link', { flow_id: Number(id), link: link }); delete S.canva[id]; delete S.busy['v' + id]; S.note[id] = { ok: link ? 'The Canva link is saved.' : 'The Canva link is cleared.' }; await load(); }
    catch (e) { delete S.busy['v' + id]; S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; toast(e.message); paint(); }
  }
  /* what HR edited in the preview, under the welcome to-do, to send by hand */
  /* the email as it reads now, from onboard-run (welcome): HR's wording kept, today's times, manager, address and links filled in. wave275: also once the
     Hub has sent it (a copy on the card) or when sending failed; [first password] stays as it is, since only the sent email has the password */
  var WELCOME_COPY = ['hr_task', 'failed', 'done'];
  function welcomeStatus(f) { var r = (f.steps || []).filter(function (x) { return x.step === 'welcome'; })[0]; return r ? r.status : ''; }
  function welcomeHtml(f) {
    var w = S.welcome[f.id];
    if (!w) return '<div class="dt"><div class="o271ln">Loading the email...</div></div>';
    if (w.err) return '<div class="dt"><div class="o271err">' + E(w.err) + '</div></div>';
    return '<div class="dt">'
      + (w.sent_at ? '<div class="o271ln" data-o271sent><b>Sent ' + E(whenText(w.sent_at)) + '.</b> This copy reads as the email does now.</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="o271ln"><b>Subject:</b> ' + E(w.subject || '') + (w.edited ? ' <small>(your edit)</small>' : '') + '</div><div class="o271pre">' + E(w.text || '') + '</div>'
      + (/\[first password\]|\[premier mot de passe\]/.test(w.text || '') ? '<div class="o271ln" data-o271pwnote>' + (welcomeStatus(f) === 'hr_task'
          ? 'You send this one yourself, and the Hub cannot fill in [first password] for you: ask an admin for the login details and put them in before you send it.'
          : '[first password] stays as it is here: the Hub puts the password in only as the email goes, so nobody reads it on the card.') + '</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="copywelcome" data-f="' + E(f.id) + '">Copy the text</button></div>'
      + '</div>';
  }
  async function readWelcomes(flows) {
    S.welcome = {};
    await Promise.all(flows.filter(function (f) { return (f.steps || []).some(function (r) { return r.step === 'welcome' && WELCOME_COPY.indexOf(r.status) >= 0; }); }).map(async function (f) {
      try { S.welcome[f.id] = await run('welcome', { flow_id: Number(f.id) }); } catch (e) { S.welcome[f.id] = { err: e.message }; }
    }));
  }
  async function copyWelcome(id) {
    var w = S.welcome[id]; if (!w || w.err) return;
    var text = [w.subject, w.text].filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');   /* the group session is already a line of the text */
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); toast('Copied'); }
    catch (e) { toast('The browser would not copy it. Select the text and copy it yourself.'); }
  }
  /* ---------- wave275: the first password of new Google accounts, for admins (first_password_state and set_first_password refuse everyone else) ---------- */
  /* The page never holds it: the box is type password, its value is never written into the page's HTML (a repaint gives it back as a property only), and
     Set sends it once and empties the box. The line says only whether it is set, and when. */
  function isAdmin() { try { return WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  async function readPw() {
    if (!isAdmin()) { S.pw = null; return; }
    try { S.pw = await run('first_password_state', {}); } catch (e) { S.pw = null; }
  }
  function dayLong(at) { try { return new Date(at).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: TZ, year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(at); } }
  function pwHtml() {
    var P = S.pw; if (!P) return '';
    return '<div class="o271cd" data-o271pw>'
      + '<div class="o271ln" data-o271pwstate><b>First password:</b> ' + (P.set && P.set_at ? 'set on ' + E(dayLong(P.set_at)) : 'not set') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">Every Google account the Hub makes starts with it, and Google asks the new person to change it the first time they sign in. Only the welcome email carries it. At least 8 characters, with a letter and a digit.</div>'
      + '<div class="o271btns"><input type="password" autocomplete="new-password" aria-label="' + (P.set ? 'A new first password' : 'The first password') + '" data-o271i="pw" data-f="pw">'
      + '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="pwset" data-f="pw"' + (S.busy.pw ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Set</button></div>'
      + noteHtml('pw') + '</div>';
  }
  async function setPw() {
    var box = document.querySelector('[data-o271i="pw"]'); if (!box || S.busy.pw) return;
    var typed = box.value;
    S.busy.pw = true; delete S.note.pw; paint();
    try {
      var r = await run('set_first_password', { password: typed });
      var now = document.querySelector('[data-o271i="pw"]'); if (now) now.value = '';
      S.pw = { set: true, set_at: r.set_at };
      S.note.pw = { ok: 'The first password is set.', warnings: r.warnings || [] };
    } catch (e) { S.note.pw = { err: e.message }; }
    typed = '';
    delete S.busy.pw; paint();
  }
  function gaddrOf(f) { if (S.gaddr && S.gaddr[f.id] != null) return S.gaddr[f.id]; return f.wa_email || ''; }
  function nameOf(id) { var p = S.people.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; return p ? p.name : 'Not chosen'; }
  function panelHtml(f, A) {
    var id = f.id, g = A.g, h = '<div class="o271pn">', busy = S.busy['a' + id];
    if (A.kind === 'back') {
      h += '<div class="o271ln"><b>Did ' + E(f.name) + ' back out?</b> ' + (f.before
          ? 'This closes their flow and moves the card to Withdrawn. No invites have gone out yet.'
          : 'This cancels their invites, closes the onboarding and moves the card to Withdrawn. It removes nothing from Google, Slack or the Hub: you get a list of what to take out by hand.') + '</div>'
        + '<label class="o271f">Why (optional)</label><input type="text" data-o271i="reason" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(A.reason || '') + '">'
        + '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b dngp" data-o271a="actgo" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Yes, they backed out</button><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="actno" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Cancel</button></div>';
    } else if (A.kind === 'manager') {
      h += '<label class="o271f">New manager</label><select data-o271i="member" data-f="' + E(id) + '">' + memberOptions(A.member) + '</select>'
        + '<div class="o271ln">The Hub moves the 1-on-1 invite to them and tells both managers.</div>'
        + '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-o271a="actgo" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (busy || !A.member ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Change manager</button><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="actno" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Cancel</button></div>';
    } else {
      var isBook = A.kind === 'book', mode = isBook ? 'one' : (A.which === 'one_on_one' ? 'one' : 'call');
      if (!isBook) {
        h += '<label class="o271f">Which meeting</label><select data-o271i="which" data-f="' + E(id) + '"><option value="call"' + (mode === 'call' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Onboarding call</option>' + (f.one_on_one_at ? '<option value="one_on_one"' + (mode === 'one' ? ' selected' : '') + '>1-on-1</option>' : '') + '</select>';
      }
      if (g) h += '<div style="margin-top:8px">' + gridHtml(g) + '</div>';
      h += '<label class="o271f">' + (isBook ? '1-on-1' : 'New time') + ' (Toronto time)</label><input type="datetime-local" data-o271i="at" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(A.at || '') + '">'
        + '<div class="o271ln">' + (isBook ? 'The invite goes out as soon as you book it.' : (mode === 'call' ? 'Google tells the guests, the manager is told, and the plan\'s dates move with it.' : 'Google tells the guests and the manager is told.')) + '</div>'
        + '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-o271a="actgo" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (busy || !A.at ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (isBook ? 'Book the 1-on-1' : 'Change time') + '</button><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="actno" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Cancel</button></div>';
    }
    return h + '</div>';
  }
  function startedCard(f) {
    var id = f.id, A = S.act[id], acting = A && A.kind;
    return '<div class="o271cd" data-o271flow="' + E(id) + '">'
      + '<div class="o271top"><b>' + E(f.name) + '</b><span>' + E(f.role || '') + '</span>' + (f.practice ? '<i class="o271tag pr" style="font-style:normal">Practice</i>' : '') + '<i class="o271tag" style="font-style:normal">' + (f.status === 'started' ? 'Started' : 'Under way') + '</i></div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">Onboarding call: ' + E(f.call_at ? whenText(f.call_at) : 'not set') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">1-on-1: ' + E(f.one_on_one_at ? whenText(f.one_on_one_at) : 'not booked yet') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">Manager: ' + E(nameOf(f.manager_member_id)) + (f.wa_email ? ' &middot; Address: ' + E(f.wa_email) : '') + '</div>'
      + R.startedMore(f)   /* wave275: the setup rundown ticks */
      + noteHtml(id)
      + '<ol class="o271st">' + STEPS.map(function (s, i) { return stepHtml(f, s[0], s[1], i + 1); }).join('') + '</ol>'
      + R.startedAfter(f)   /* wave278: the access steps, the leader line and Re-check, after these eight */
      + '<div class="o271btns">'
      + '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="act" data-kind="time" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (acting === 'time' ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Change time</button>'
      + '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="act" data-kind="manager" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (acting === 'manager' ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Change manager</button>'
      + (f.one_on_one_at ? '' : '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o271a="act" data-kind="book" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (acting === 'book' ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Book the 1-on-1</button>')
      + '<button type="button" class="o271b dng" data-o271a="act" data-kind="back" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (acting === 'back' ? ' disabled' : '') + '>They backed out</button>'
      + '</div>'
      + (A ? R.panelHtml(f, A) : '')
      + '</div>';
  }
  R.startedCard = startedCard; R.panelHtml = panelHtml;
  R.preview = function (id) { return run('preview', { flow_id: Number(id) }); };   /* the picker's marks and meetings; wave275 reads them another way for a manager */
  function flowOf(id) {
    var f = S.started.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (f) return f;
    var x = S.ready.filter(function (r) { return String(r.flow_id) === String(id); })[0];
    return x ? { id: x.flow_id, name: x.name, before: true, canva_link: x.canva_link || null } : null;   /* canva_link: wave275's card saves and copies it before booking */
  }

  /* ---------- the changes ---------- */
  async function openAct(id, kind) {
    var f = flowOf(id); if (!f) return;
    delete S.note[id];
    var A = S.act[id] = { kind: kind, at: '', member: '', which: 'call', reason: '', g: null };
    paint();
    if (kind !== 'book' && kind !== 'time') return;
    /* the marks and meetings for the picker. Typing a time works without them */
    try {
      var pv = await R.preview(Number(id));
      if (S.act[id] !== A) return;
      A.g = newGrid('a' + id, pv, kind === 'book' ? 'one' : 'call');
      paint();
    } catch (e) { if (S.act[id] === A) { S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; paint(); } }
  }
  async function actGo(id) {
    var A = S.act[id], f = flowOf(id); if (!A || !f || S.busy['a' + id]) return;
    var at = null;
    if (A.kind === 'book' || A.kind === 'time') {
      at = isoOf(A.at);
      if (!at) { S.note[id] = { err: 'That time does not exist on the Toronto clock. Pick another.' }; paint(); return; }
    }
    S.busy['a' + id] = true; paint();
    try {
      var note = { ok: 'Done.' };
      if (A.kind === 'book') {
        var bk = await run('book_one_on_one', { flow_id: Number(id), at: at });
        note.ok = bk.practice ? 'Practice: the 1-on-1 time is saved, and no invite was sent.' : bk.mode === 'test' ? 'The 1-on-1 is booked. Email is in test mode, so the invite went only to the test inboxes.' : 'The 1-on-1 is booked and the invite has gone out.';
      }
      else if (A.kind === 'time') { var r = await run('change_time', { flow_id: Number(id), which: A.which, at: at }); note = { ok: 'The time has changed.', warnings: r.warnings || [] }; }
      else if (A.kind === 'manager') { var m = await run('change_manager', { flow_id: Number(id), member_id: Number(A.member) }); note = { ok: 'The manager has changed.', warnings: m.warnings || [] }; }
      else {
        var b = await run('backed_out', { flow_id: Number(id), reason: A.reason || '' });
        delete S.note[id];
        note = (b.warnings || []).length ? { warnings: b.warnings } : null;   /* the list itself is worked out from the flow, on its card */
      }
      delete S.act[id];
      if (note) S.note[id] = note;
      delete S.busy['a' + id];
      await load();
    } catch (e) { delete S.busy['a' + id]; S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; toast(e.message); paint(); }
  }
  async function retry(id, step) {
    var key = 'r' + id + step; if (S.busy[key]) return;
    S.busy[key] = true; delete S.note[id]; paint();
    try { await run('retry', { flow_id: Number(id), step: step }); delete S.busy[key]; await load(); }
    catch (e) { delete S.busy[key]; S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; toast(e.message); paint(); }
  }
  async function googleManual(id) {
    var f = flowOf(id); if (!f || S.busy['g' + id]) return;
    S.busy['g' + id] = true; delete S.note[id]; paint();
    try {
      await run('google_manual', { flow_id: Number(id), wa_email: String(gaddrOf(f)).trim().toLowerCase() });
      delete S.busy['g' + id]; if (S.gaddr) delete S.gaddr[id];
      await load();
    } catch (e) { delete S.busy['g' + id]; S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; toast(e.message); paint(); }
  }
  async function sendLink(id, candidate) {
    if (S.busy['l' + id]) return;
    S.busy['l' + id] = true; delete S.note[id]; paint();
    try { var r = await run('send_grid_link', { candidate_id: Number(candidate) }); var said = linkWord(r); S.note[id] = { ok: said }; toast(said); }
    catch (e) { S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; toast(e.message); }
    delete S.busy['l' + id]; paint();
  }
  function linkWord(r) { return r && r.practice ? 'Practice: no email was sent.' : r && r.mode === 'test' ? 'Email is in test mode, so the availability link went only to the test inboxes.' : 'The availability link has gone out.'; }
  async function saidYes(candidate) {
    if (S.busy['y' + candidate]) return;
    S.busy['y' + candidate] = true; paint();
    try { var r = await run('send_grid_link', { candidate_id: Number(candidate), accepted: true }); toast(linkWord(r)); delete S.note['c' + candidate]; }
    catch (e) { S.note['c' + candidate] = { err: e.message }; toast(e.message); }   /* the mail can fail after the acceptance is saved: the person then shows above, with Send the availability link */
    delete S.busy['y' + candidate]; delete S.yes[candidate];
    await load();
  }
  async function taskDone(id, step) {
    var key = 't' + id + step; if (S.busy[key]) return;
    S.busy[key] = true; delete S.note[id]; paint();
    try { await run('task_done', { flow_id: Number(id), step: step }); delete S.busy[key]; await load(); }
    catch (e) { delete S.busy[key]; S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; toast(e.message); paint(); }
  }

  /* ---------- the section ---------- */
  /* what a flow that backed out leaves for HR, worked out from the flow and its steps, so a reload keeps it. The same list and words as backed_out in onboard-run. */
  function removeList(f) {
    if (f.practice) return [];
    var st = {}; (f.steps || []).forEach(function (r) { st[r.step] = r.status; });
    var out = [];
    if (st.google === 'done' && f.wa_email) out.push('Google account ' + f.wa_email + ': suspend or delete it in Google Admin.');
    if (st.roster === 'done' && f.member_id) out.push('Roster profile' + (f.wa_email ? ' (' + f.wa_email + ')' : '') + (f.roster_created ? ': Created for this person. Deactivate it by hand in Roster.' : ': Already on the roster before. Check whether it should stay.'));
    if (f.slack_channel_id) out.push('Slack onboarding channel ' + f.slack_channel_id + ': archive it.');
    if (st.hub === 'done') out.push('Hub access' + (f.wa_email ? ' for ' + f.wa_email : '') + ': remove it from Roster.');
    return out;
  }
  function backedHtml(f) {
    var remove = removeList(f);
    return '<div class="o271out" data-o271backed="' + E(f.id) + '"><div class="o271ln"><b>' + E(f.name) + ' backed out.</b>' + (f.practice ? ' <i class="o271tag pr" style="font-style:normal">Practice</i>' : '') + (remove.length ? ' Take these out by hand:' : '') + '</div>'
      + (remove.length ? '<ul>' + remove.map(function (r) { return '<li>' + E(r) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<div class="o271ln">Nothing was made for them, so there is nothing to take out.</div>')
      + noteHtml(f.id)
      + '</div>';
  }
  function paint() { return R.paint(); }
  function paintHr() {
    var el = document.getElementById('o271'); if (!el) return;
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (S.denied) {
      el.innerHTML = ''; el.setAttribute('data-w197s', 'none'); el.classList.remove('w197on');
      return;
    }
    var active = document.activeElement, keep = active && el.contains(active) && active.getAttribute && active.getAttribute('data-o271i');
    var keepFlow = keep ? active.getAttribute('data-f') : '', sel = keep && active.selectionStart != null ? [active.selectionStart, active.selectionEnd] : null;
    var html = '<h2>Ready to onboard</h2><p class="o271sub">Everyone who has accepted an offer. Pick the times, check the preview, then press Start onboarding.</p>' + pwHtml();
    if (S.err) html += '<div class="o271err">' + E(S.err) + '</div>';
    if (!S.loaded) html += '<p class="o271sub">Loading...</p>';
    else if (!S.ready.length && !S.started.length && !S.offers.length && !S.backed.length) html += '<p class="o271sub">Nobody is waiting to be onboarded.</p>';
    html += S.ready.map(function (x) { return R.readyCard(x); }).join('');
    if (S.offers.length) html += '<h3 class="o271h">Offers waiting for an answer</h3>' + S.offers.map(offerCard).join('');
    if (S.started.length) html += '<h3 class="o271h">Onboarding started</h3>' + S.started.map(function (f) { return R.startedCard(f); }).join('');
    if (S.backed.length) html += '<h3 class="o271h">Backed out</h3>' + S.backed.map(backedHtml).join('');
    /* each grid keeps its scroll through a repaint */
    var scrolls = {}; Array.prototype.forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('[data-o271grid]'), function (b) { scrolls[b.getAttribute('data-o271grid')] = b.scrollLeft; });
    var pwBox = el.querySelector('[data-o271i="pw"]'), pwTyped = pwBox ? pwBox.value : '';   /* a first password being typed survives the repaint, as a property, never in the HTML */
    el.innerHTML = html;
    if (pwTyped) { var pwAgain = el.querySelector('[data-o271i="pw"]'); if (pwAgain) pwAgain.value = pwTyped; }
    pwTyped = '';
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('[data-o271grid]'), function (b) { var v = scrolls[b.getAttribute('data-o271grid')]; if (v) b.scrollLeft = v; });
    if (keep) {
      var again = el.querySelector('[data-o271i="' + keep + '"][data-f="' + keepFlow + '"]');
      if (again) { try { again.focus(); if (sel) again.setSelectionRange(sel[0], sel[1]); } catch (e) { /* not a text box */ } }
    }
    if (panel && panel.getAttribute('data-w197') === 'onboarding') el.classList.add('w197on');
  }
  R.paint = paintHr;
  function onbSection() { var a = document.querySelector('#w46body > .w46sec [data-w46assign]'); return a ? a.closest('.w46sec') : null; }
  function onPage() { var p = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); return (p && p.getAttribute('data-w197') === 'onboarding') || /^#hrcentre\/onboarding(\?|$)/.test(String(location.hash || '')); }
  function install() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body'); if (!body || !hrPage()) return;
    var sec = onbSection(); if (!sec) return;   /* still loading, or a page redraw is on its way */
    var el = document.getElementById('o271');
    if (el && el.parentNode === body && el.nextElementSibling === sec) return;
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'o271'; el.className = 'o271 w197card w197blk'; el.setAttribute('data-w197s', S.denied ? 'none' : 'onboarding');
    }
    body.insertBefore(el, sec);
    paint();
    if (onPage() && !S.loaded) load();
  }
  function arrive() { install(); if (document.getElementById('o271') && onPage()) load(); }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var cell = t.closest('[data-o271cell]');
    if (cell) {
      var g = gridOf(cell.getAttribute('data-g')); if (!g) return;
      var key = cell.getAttribute('data-g'), id = key.slice(1), local = toLocal(cell.getAttribute('data-o271cell'));
      if (key.charAt(0) === 'a') {
        var A = S.act[id]; if (A) { A.at = local; g.vals[g.mode] = local; refreshGrid(g); var box = document.querySelector('[data-o271flow="' + id + '"] [data-o271i="at"]'); if (box) box.value = local; var go = document.querySelector('[data-o271flow="' + id + '"] [data-o271a="actgo"]'); if (go) go.disabled = false; }
      } else {
        g.vals[g.mode] = local; refreshGrid(g);
        var inp = document.querySelector('[data-o271flow="' + id + '"] [data-o271i="' + g.mode + '"]'); if (inp) inp.value = local;
        liveUpdate(id);
      }
      return;
    }
    var b = t.closest('[data-o271a]'); if (!b || b.disabled) return;
    var a = b.getAttribute('data-o271a'), f = b.getAttribute('data-f');
    if (a === 'link') { sendLink(f, b.getAttribute('data-cand')); return; }
    if (a === 'open') { var x = S.ready.filter(function (r) { return String(r.flow_id) === String(f); })[0]; if (x) openPick(x); return; }
    if (a === 'mode') { var P = S.pick[f]; if (P) { P.g.mode = b.getAttribute('data-mode'); paint(); } return; }
    if (a === 'start') { start(f); return; }
    if (a === 'retry') { retry(f, b.getAttribute('data-step')); return; }
    if (a === 'gmanual') { googleManual(f); return; }
    if (a === 'act') { openAct(f, b.getAttribute('data-kind')); return; }
    if (a === 'actgo') { actGo(f); return; }
    if (a === 'actno') { delete S.act[f]; paint(); return; }
    if (a === 'taskdone') { taskDone(f, b.getAttribute('data-step')); return; }
    if (a === 'copywelcome') { copyWelcome(f); return; }
    if (a === 'copycanva') { copyCanva(f); return; }
    if (a === 'canvasave') { saveCanva(f); return; }
    if (a === 'pwset') { setPw(); return; }
    var cand = b.getAttribute('data-cand');
    if (a === 'yesask') { S.yes[cand] = true; delete S.note['c' + cand]; paint(); return; }
    if (a === 'yesno') { delete S.yes[cand]; paint(); return; }
    if (a === 'yesgo') { saidYes(cand); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
    var k = t.getAttribute('data-o271i'), id = t.getAttribute('data-f'); if (!k || !id) return;
    var P = S.pick[id], A = S.act[id];
    if (k === 'gaddr') { S.gaddr = S.gaddr || {}; S.gaddr[id] = t.value; return; }
    if (k === 'ccanva') { S.canva[id] = t.value; return; }
    if (A && k === 'reason') { A.reason = t.value; return; }
    if (A && k === 'at') {
      A.at = t.value;
      if (A.g) { A.g.vals[A.g.mode] = t.value; refreshGrid(A.g); }
      var go = document.querySelector('[data-o271flow="' + id + '"] [data-o271a="actgo"]'); if (go) go.disabled = !A.at;
      return;
    }
    if (!P) return;
    if (k === 'call' || k === 'one') { P.g.vals[k] = t.value; refreshGrid(P.g); liveUpdate(id); return; }
    if (k === 'addr') { P.addr = t.value; liveUpdate(id); return; }
    if (k === 'group') { P.group = t.value; return; }
    if (k === 'canva') { P.canva = t.value; liveUpdate(id); return; }
    if (k === 'subj') { P.subject = t.value; return; }
    if (k === 'text') { P.text = t.value; return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
    var skip = t.getAttribute('data-o271skip'), k = t.getAttribute('data-o271i'), id = t.getAttribute('data-f');
    if (skip && S.pick[id]) { S.pick[id].skip[skip] = !t.checked; if (skip === 'google' && !t.checked) clearClash(id); liveUpdate(id); return; }
    if (!k || !id) return;
    var P = S.pick[id], A = S.act[id];
    if (k === 'addr' && P) { P.addr = t.value; recheckAddress(id); return; }
    if (k === 'mgr' && P) {
      var was = P.managerId;
      P.managerId = t.value;
      var changed = String(P.managerId) !== String(P.pv.manager && P.pv.manager.member_id);
      if (changed !== P.g.hideManager) { P.g.hideManager = changed; paint(); } else liveUpdate(id);
      if (t.value && t.value !== String(was)) saveManager(id, t.value, was);
      return;
    }
    if (k === 'member' && A) { A.member = t.value; paint(); return; }
    if (k === 'which' && A) {
      A.which = t.value; A.at = '';
      if (A.g) { A.g.mode = t.value === 'one_on_one' ? 'one' : 'call'; A.g.vals = { call: '', one: '' }; }
      paint();
    }
  });

  /* ---------- wiring: the section stays above the Onboarding section whenever wave46 redraws ---------- */
  function arm(tries) {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel) { if ((tries || 0) < 40) setTimeout(function () { arm((tries || 0) + 1); }, 800); return; }
    if (panel.dataset.o271mo) return;
    panel.dataset.o271mo = '1';
    new MutationObserver(function () { try { install(); } catch (e) { /* the next redraw */ } }).observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    /* wave197 can change page without a hashchange (replaceState): its page attribute says when Onboarding opens */
    new MutationObserver(function (list) { if (list.some(function (m) { return m.oldValue !== 'onboarding'; }) && panel.getAttribute('data-w197') === 'onboarding') { try { arrive(); } catch (e) { /* the next redraw */ } } }).observe(panel, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-w197'], attributeOldValue: true });
    arrive();
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa271 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var out = await __sa271.apply(this, arguments); try { arm(0); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } return out; };
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (onPage()) setTimeout(arrive, 120); });

  /* ---------- test hook ---------- */
  window.o271 = { load: load, toInstant: toInstant, toLocal: toLocal, wall: wall, state: function () { return S; },
    /* wave275 Task 6: what wave275's card reuses (the same words, grid, steps and calls as this wave's) */
    E: E, run: run, whenText: whenText, dayText: dayText, dayLong: dayLong, isoOf: isoOf, pad2: pad2, newGrid: newGrid, gridHtml: gridHtml, refreshGrid: refreshGrid,
    noteHtml: noteHtml, warnBox: warnBox, nameOf: nameOf, flowOf: flowOf, stepHtml: stepHtml, canvaCardHtml: canvaCardHtml, memberOptions: memberOptions, STEPS: STEPS, STALE_MS: STALE_MS, KEEP_DAYS: KEEP_DAYS,
    isAdmin: isAdmin, surelyHr: surelyHr, hrPage: hrPage, readPeople: readPeople, readCfg: readCfg, readPw: readPw, readWelcomes: readWelcomes, pwHtml: pwHtml, paint: paint };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v275 One streamlined onboarding: the card for HR and the manager (02/11/26, Task 6)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (02/11/26): from the offer onward there is one flow, driven by HR or by the hire's own manager. This wave gives wave271's Ready to onboard a
   card for every flow on the new path (offered, proposed, answered, accepted): the offer and the proposal as plain lines ("Offer sent 02 Nov, 6 hours a
   week; waiting for the manager's times", "Proposed by Kahkashan: Wed 4 Nov 10 am and Thu 5 Nov 10 am; waiting for James", "James said no to the
   times" or "the times moved; James is marking their availability"), the hours, the manager and their week (wave276's typical week, from Home), the
   switch "Availability: ask the hire / already communicated", the steps that ran at Accept with Retry, the hire's setup rundown ticks, the fresh Canva link
   until booking (whole-branch fix), and the actions:
   Propose times (the picker over the manager's week, hub@ meetings and other hires greyed), Book the times (from the hire's grid), Enter their
   availability, Edit the welcome email, Set <manager>'s exceptions and Change manager (HR's), They backed out. A started card gains the rundown ticks.
   A manager who is not HR sees only their own hires' cards (wa275_my_cards), as "Your new hires" on Home and on their team page; HR and admins see
   every card in HR Center > Onboarding, and their own hires on Home as short lines. Every write goes through onboard-run, which checks who may
   (wa275_manager_of); the menu is not the lock. Wraps wave271's doors (window.o271R: load, paint, readyCard, panelHtml, startedMore, preview) and reuses
   its words, grid and steps (window.o271). Times are America/Toronto wall clock, through Intl only.
   wave275b (03/11/26, Steel: "Can we make the availability check for onboarding optional"): the card row's manager_hours (wa275b's wa275_flow_row, from
   onboard_manager_hours) and the times answer's manager_hours. False: the week line is one quiet line of information ("<first> has not set their week
   on Home; times are not limited to it"), and the picker offers every cell of the window ("Open to pick"), greying only hub@ meetings, other hires and
   too-soon cells. Missing (an older row or function): today's rule. The page never reads app_config for it.
   wave278 (04/11/26, Task 6): the rundown counts twelve steps, and the card gains the access block (c_access.js): the groups, drives, meeting and channel
   steps, the leader decision with Change, Re-check Drives and Groups, and the welcome's copies with Add people. c_access.js closes this wrapper. */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa275) return; window.__wa275 = true;
  var O = window.o271, R = window.o271R;
  if (!O || !R) return;   /* wave271 is not on this page */
  var S = O.state(), E = O.E, run = O.run;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GROUND = '#1F201E', MUTE = '#797773', LINE = '#DEE0D8', CREAM = '#FFFAF2';
  var MON = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'], WDN = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'], WDK = ['sun', 'mon', 'tue', 'wed', 'thu', 'fri', 'sat'];
  var DEFAULT_WEEK = { mon: [['09:00', '17:00']], tue: [['09:00', '17:00']], wed: [['09:00', '17:00']], thu: [['09:00', '17:00']], fri: [['09:00', '17:00']] };
  /* the hire's setup rundown (onboard-link SETUP, 12 steps since wave278; wa278a carried the old ticks over: profile to chrome, drives to drive, loom
     dropped); ticks are setup_ticks.rd_<key> only, never the Hub's own keys kept beside them */
  var RUNDOWN = [['google', 'their Google account'], ['chrome', 'Chrome'], ['bookmarks', 'Gmail and Calendar'], ['drive', 'Drive'], ['groups', 'Groups'], ['hub', 'the Hub'], ['slack', 'Slack'],
    ['canva', 'Canva'], ['academy', 'the Academy'], ['jd', 'the job description'], ['weekly', 'the weekly meeting'], ['phone', 'the phone apps']];
  var MINE_KINDS = { propose: 1, bookg: 1, gridfor: 1, exc: 1, welcome: 1, mgr: 1 };
  R.readyStatuses.push('offered');   /* a card runs from the offer (wa271_ready_list lists offered flows since wa275a) */

  /* ---------- style ---------- */
  var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'o275css';
  st.textContent =
    '.o275state{font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:' + GROUND + ';margin:6px 0 4px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}' +
    '.o275state .o271ln{font-weight:400;margin:0 0 2px;}' +
    '.o275sw{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:13px;margin:8px 0 2px;color:' + GROUND + ';}' +
    '.o275sw .o271md{margin:0;}' +
    '.o275sw .o271md button{min-height:32px;}' +
    '.o275xd{border:1px solid ' + LINE + ';border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;margin:8px 0;background:' + CREAM + ';min-width:0;}' +
    '.o275xr{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:4px 0;min-width:0;}' +
    '.o275xr input[type=date],.o275xr input[type=time]{font:inherit;font-size:14px;min-height:36px;padding:4px 8px;border:1px solid ' + LINE + ';border-radius:8px;background:#FFFAF2;color:' + GROUND + ';min-width:0;max-width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;}' +
    '.o275cnt{font-size:12.5px;color:' + MUTE + ';margin:6px 0 0;}' +
    '.o275line{border-top:1px solid #ECEBE3;padding:10px 0;min-width:0;}' +
    '.o275line:first-of-type{border-top:none;}' +
    '.o275line b{color:' + GROUND + ';overflow-wrap:anywhere;}' +
    '.o275line span{color:' + MUTE + ';font-size:13px;margin-left:6px;}' +
    '.o275line a{font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:' + GREEN + ';}' +
    '#o275mine{margin:0 0 16px;min-width:0;box-sizing:border-box;}' +
    '#o275mine > h2{font-size:22px;}' +
    '#mypage.w272 #mpBody > #o275mine{order:5;grid-column:1 / -1;}' +
    '#w116host > #o275mine{background:var(--wa-card,' + CREAM + ');border:1px solid ' + LINE + ';border-radius:16px;padding:14px 16px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---------- who and times ---------- */
  function hrList() { return S.hrList === true; }   /* the database gave this person HR's list (see R.load) */
  function staffUser() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function firstOf(name) { return String(name || '').replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || 'the new hire'; }
  function clockOf(h, m) { return ((h % 12) || 12) + (m ? ':' + O.pad2(m) : '') + (h < 12 ? ' am' : ' pm'); }
  /* "Wed 4 Nov 10 am", on the Toronto clock */
  function brief(at) { var w = O.wall(at); return WDN[new Date(Date.UTC(w.y, w.mo - 1, w.d)).getUTCDay()] + ' ' + w.d + ' ' + MON[w.mo - 1] + ' ' + clockOf(w.h, w.mi); }
  /* "02 Nov": a date (YYYY-MM-DD) as it is, an instant on the Toronto clock */
  function day(v) {
    if (!v) return '';
    var s = String(v), m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(s);
    if (m) return m[3] + ' ' + MON[Number(m[2]) - 1];
    var w = O.wall(s); return O.pad2(w.d) + ' ' + MON[w.mo - 1];
  }
  function personName(email) {
    var e = String(email || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!e) return 'someone';
    var p = S.people.filter(function (x) { return String(x.email || '').trim().toLowerCase() === e; })[0];
    return p ? p.name : e;
  }
  function hm(s) { var m = /^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(String(s || '')); return m ? Number(m[1]) * 60 + Number(m[2]) : NaN; }
  /* the manager's hours as onboard-run reads them (it decides): an except date replaces the weekday; the whole span inside one block of that date */
  function inside(av, s, e) {
    var a = O.wall(s), b = O.wall(e); if (a.date !== b.date) return false;
    var ex = (av.except || []).filter(function (x) { return x.date === a.date; })[0];
    var list = ex ? ex.free : (av.weekly || {})[WDK[new Date(Date.UTC(a.y, a.mo - 1, a.d)).getUTCDay()]];
    return (list || []).some(function (blk) { return hm(blk[0]) <= a.mins && b.mins <= hm(blk[1]); });
  }
  function rowOf(id) {
    return S.ready.filter(function (x) { return String(x.flow_id) === String(id); })[0] || S.started.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] || null;
  }
  function cardEl(id) { return document.querySelector('[data-o271flow="' + id + '"]'); }
  function ticked(ticks) { var t = ticks && typeof ticks === 'object' ? ticks : {}; return RUNDOWN.filter(function (r) { var v = t['rd_' + r[0]]; return v != null && v !== false; }); }

  /* ---------- the loader and painter: HR keeps wave271's list; anyone else reads their own hires (wa275_my_cards) ----------
     Who gets HR's list is the database's call (wa271_ready_list: wa_is_hr or an admin), never the menu profile: the list is asked for first, and only its
     refusal sends this person to their own hires. S.hrList: true (HR's list), false (refused: their own hires), null (not known yet). */
  var loadHr = R.load;
  R.load = function () {
    if (S.loading) return S.loading;
    if (!staffUser()) return loadHr();
    if (S.hrList === false) return loadMine();
    return loadHr().then(function () {
      if (S.refused) { S.hrList = false; return loadMine(); }   /* the database's own "Only HR": their own hires from now on */
      if (S.loadErr) {   /* anything else decides nothing: the error shows as wave271 shows it, and the next load asks for HR's list again */
        if (S.denied) { S.denied = false; S.err = 'The Hub could not load this list: ' + S.loadErr; R.paint(); }
        return;
      }
      S.hrList = true;
    });
  };
  R.hrTools = function () { return !S.mine; };   /* a manager's view leaves HR's own buttons off wave271's cards too */
  /* Home asks only whether the list is theirs, so it never loads HR's whole list there; a refusal other than "Only HR" decides nothing */
  async function probe() {
    S.probing = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa271_ready_list');
      if (!r || !r.error) S.hrList = true;
      else if (/Only HR/i.test(String(r.error.message || ''))) S.hrList = false;
    } catch (e) { /* decided on the next visit */ }
    S.probing = false; S.probed = true;
    mount();
  }
  function loadMine() {
    S.mine = true;
    S.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.rpc('wa275_my_cards');
        if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
        var rows = (r && r.data) || [], cut = Date.now() - O.KEEP_DAYS * 86400000;
        S.denied = true;   /* HR's list is HR's: wave271's own section stays away for this person */
        S.ready = rows.filter(function (x) { return ['offered', 'waiting', 'ready'].indexOf(x.status) >= 0; });
        S.started = rows.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'running' || (x.status === 'started' && (!x.call_at || Date.parse(x.call_at) >= cut)); })
          .map(function (x) { return Object.assign({}, x, { id: x.flow_id }); });
        S.backed = []; S.offers = []; S.mineErr = '';
        await O.readWelcomes(S.started);
        await Promise.all([O.readPeople(), O.readCfg()]);
      } catch (e) { S.mineErr = 'The Hub could not load your new hires: ' + String((e && e.message) || e); }
      S.loaded = true; S.mineLoaded = true; S.loading = null;
      Object.keys(S.act).forEach(function (id) { if (!O.flowOf(id)) delete S.act[id]; });
      R.paint();
    })();
    return S.loading;
  }
  /* HR's own hires, for Home: short lines that open Onboarding (their cards are in HR Center, so the page never holds two copies of one card) */
  async function loadMineList() {
    S.mineListLoading = true;
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa275_my_cards'); S.mineList = (r && !r.error && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { S.mineList = []; }
    S.mineListLoading = false; S.mineListLoaded = true;
    if (!S.people.length) await O.readPeople();
    paintMine();
  }
  var paintHr = R.paint;
  R.paint = function () { paintHr(); paintMine(); };
  /* a manager's change panel reads the picker's marks through times (preview is HR's) */
  var preview271 = R.preview;
  R.preview = async function (id) { if (!S.mine) return preview271(id); return pvOf(await run('times', { flow_id: Number(id) })); };

  /* ---------- the card on the new path ---------- */
  var readyCard271 = R.readyCard, panel271 = R.panelHtml;
  R.readyCard = function (x) { return x.propose_path || x.status === 'offered' ? card275(x) : readyCard271(x); };
  R.panelHtml = function (f, A) { return MINE_KINDS[A.kind] ? panel275(rowOf(f.id), A) : panel271(f, A); };
  R.startedMore = function (f) { return f.propose_path || f.proposed_at || ticked(f.setup_ticks).length ? ticksHtml(f) : ''; };

  function stateLines(x) {
    var first = firstOf(x.name), hours = Number(x.offer_hours) > 0 ? Number(x.offer_hours) + ' hours a week' : '';
    var ask = x.ask_availability !== false, lines = [];
    var proposal = x.proposed_at && x.proposed_call_at && x.proposed_one_on_one_at ? 'Proposed by ' + personName(x.proposed_by) + ': ' + brief(x.proposed_call_at) + ' and ' + brief(x.proposed_one_on_one_at) : '';
    if (x.call_at) { lines.push('Booked: the onboarding call on ' + brief(x.call_at) + (x.one_on_one_at ? ' and the 1-on-1 on ' + brief(x.one_on_one_at) : '')); return lines; }
    if (!x.accepted_on) {
      lines.push(proposal ? proposal + '; waiting for ' + first + ' to accept the offer' : 'Offer sent ' + day(x.offer_sent_at) + (hours ? ', ' + hours : '') + '; waiting for the manager\'s times');
      return lines;
    }
    lines.push('Accepted ' + day(x.accepted_on) + (hours ? ', ' + hours + (x.hours_confirmed_at ? ' confirmed' : '') : ''));
    var grid = x.grids && x.grids.hire === 'marked';
    if (x.hire_answer === 'no' && x.hire_answered_at) {
      lines.push(first + ' said no to the times' + (!ask ? '; availability already communicated: get in touch with them, then book the times here' : grid ? '; their times are on their grid: book from it' : '; ' + first + ' is marking their availability'));
    } else if (x.hire_answer === 'no') {
      lines.push(ask ? 'The times moved; ' + first + ' is marking their availability' + (grid ? ': book from their grid' : '') : 'The times moved; availability already communicated: get in touch with ' + first + ' about other times');
    } else if (proposal) lines.push(proposal + '; waiting for ' + first);
    else lines.push(ask ? 'Waiting for the manager\'s times' : 'Availability already communicated: book the times here, or propose them');
    return lines;
  }
  function mgrLine(x) {
    if (!x.manager_member_id) return 'Manager: not chosen yet. Choose one with Change manager.';
    var name = x.manager_name || O.nameOf(x.manager_member_id), first = firstOf(name);
    if (x.manager_hours === false) {   /* wave275b: the week is information, not a rule, so no prompt */
      var info = x.manager_no_week ? first + ' has not set their week on Home; times are not limited to it'
        : x.manager_availability_at ? 'their week was set on ' + day(x.manager_availability_at) + '; times are not limited to it' : 'times are not limited to their week';
      return 'Manager: ' + E(name) + ' &middot; ' + E(info);
    }
    var week = x.manager_no_week ? first + ' has not set their week on the Hub\'s Home page yet, so Monday to Friday, 9 to 5 is used'
      : x.manager_week_stale ? first + '\'s team asked for their week again' + (x.manager_availability_at ? ' since they set it on ' + day(x.manager_availability_at) : '') + ': they are asked on Home'
      : x.manager_availability_at ? 'their week was set on ' + day(x.manager_availability_at) : '';
    return 'Manager: ' + E(name) + (week ? ' &middot; ' + E(week) : '');
  }
  function ticksHtml(x) {
    var done = ticked(x.setup_ticks);
    return '<div class="o271ln" data-o275ticks title="' + E(done.length ? 'Done: ' + done.map(function (r) { return r[1]; }).join(', ') : 'Nothing ticked yet') + '">' + E((done.length ? firstOf(x.name) + '\'s' : 'Their') + ' setup rundown: ' + done.length + ' of ' + RUNDOWN.length + ' setup steps done') + '</div>';
  }
  function switchHtml(x) {
    var ask = x.ask_availability !== false, id = E(x.flow_id), busy = S.busy['k' + x.flow_id] ? ' disabled' : '';
    return '<div class="o275sw"><span>Availability:</span><div class="o271md" role="group" aria-label="Availability: ask the hire, or already communicated">'
      + '<button type="button" class="' + (ask ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + ask + '" data-o275a="ask" data-on="1" data-f="' + id + '"' + busy + '>ask the hire</button>'
      + '<button type="button" class="' + (ask ? '' : 'on') + '" aria-pressed="' + !ask + '" data-o275a="ask" data-on="0" data-f="' + id + '"' + busy + '>already communicated</button></div></div>';
  }
  function stepsHtml(x) {
    var rows = x.steps || [];
    if (!rows.length) return '';
    var f = { id: x.flow_id, steps: rows, wa_email: x.wa_email, canva_link: x.canva_link };   /* HR's "I created it, continue" follows R.hrTools in wave271's stepHtml */
    return '<ol class="o271st">' + O.STEPS.map(function (s, i) { return rows.some(function (r) { return r.step === s[0]; }) ? O.stepHtml(f, s[0], s[1], i + 1) : ''; }).join('') + '</ol>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">The other steps run when the times are booked.</div>';
  }
  /* whole-branch fix (design decision 5 and section 6): the fresh Canva link, HR's or the manager's to paste before booking. The welcome email goes when the
     times are booked and reads the link from the flow, so here is its only place before then (wave271's field sits under the welcome step, made at booking).
     Canva's links last 30 days, so the note says to get a new one for each hire. Saved through set_canva_link, as on wave271's card. */
  function canvaHtml(x) {
    return '<div data-o275canva>' + O.canvaCardHtml({ id: x.flow_id, canva_link: x.canva_link }, 'No Canva link yet. Paste a fresh one before the times are booked: the welcome email goes then, and leaves Canva out without one.') + '</div>';
  }
  function btn(kind, label, x, pri) {
    var A = S.act[x.flow_id];
    return '<button type="button" class="o271b' + (pri ? ' pri' : '') + '" data-o275a="open" data-kind="' + kind + '" data-f="' + E(x.flow_id) + '"' + (A && A.kind === kind ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + E(label) + '</button>';
  }
  function buttonsHtml(x) {
    var hr = R.hrTools(), booked = !!x.call_at, live = !!(x.proposed_at && !x.hire_answer), mfirst = firstOf(x.manager_name || O.nameOf(x.manager_member_id)), b = [];
    if (!booked && x.hire_answer !== 'yes') b.push(btn('propose', x.proposed_at ? 'Propose new times' : 'Propose times', x, !x.accepted_on || live));
    if (x.accepted_on && !booked && !live) b.push(btn('bookg', 'Book the times', x, true));
    if (x.accepted_on && !booked) b.push(btn('gridfor', 'Enter their availability', x));
    if (!booked && !x.welcome_sent_at) b.push(btn('welcome', 'Edit the welcome email', x));
    if (hr && x.manager_member_id && !booked) b.push(btn('exc', 'Set ' + mfirst + '\'s exceptions', x));
    if (hr && !booked) b.push(btn('mgr', 'Change manager', x));
    if (hr && x.manager_member_id && !booked && !x.proposed_at) b.push('<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o275a="askmgr" data-f="' + E(x.flow_id) + '"' + (S.busy['m' + x.flow_id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Ask ' + E(mfirst) + ' for times again</button>');
    b.push('<button type="button" class="o271b dng" data-o271a="act" data-kind="back" data-f="' + E(x.flow_id) + '"' + (S.act[x.flow_id] && S.act[x.flow_id].kind === 'back' ? ' disabled' : '') + '>They backed out</button>');
    return '<div class="o271btns">' + b.join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function card275(x) {
    var id = x.flow_id, A = S.act[id], booked = !!x.call_at, tag = booked ? ['Booked', 'ok'] : x.accepted_on ? ['Accepted', ''] : ['Offer sent', ''];
    return '<div class="o271cd o275cd" data-o271flow="' + E(id) + '">'
      + '<div class="o271top"><b>' + E(x.name) + '</b><span>' + E(x.role || '') + '</span>' + (x.practice ? '<i class="o271tag pr" style="font-style:normal">Practice</i>' : '') + '<i class="o271tag ' + tag[1] + '" style="font-style:normal">' + tag[0] + '</i></div>'
      + '<div class="o275state" data-o275state>' + stateLines(x).map(function (l, i) { return i ? '<div>' + E(l) + '</div>' : '<div class="o271ln">' + E(l) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="o271ln" data-o275mgr>' + mgrLine(x) + '</div>'
      + (x.accepted_on && !booked ? '<div class="o271ln" data-o275hire>New hire\'s times: ' + (x.grids && x.grids.hire === 'marked' ? 'marked' : 'not marked yet') + '</div>' : '')
      + (booked ? '' : switchHtml(x))
      + (x.accepted_on ? ticksHtml(x) : '')
      + stepsHtml(x)
      + accessHtml(x, id)   /* wave278: c_access.js */
      + (booked ? '' : canvaHtml(x))
      + O.noteHtml(id)
      + buttonsHtml(x)
      + (A ? (MINE_KINDS[A.kind] ? panel275(x, A) : panel271(O.flowOf(id), A)) : '')
      + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the panels: open, draw, go ---------- */
  /* the picker's data in the shape wave271's grid takes: the hire's marks, the manager's free cells (their hours over the window), their hub@ meetings */
  function hoursRule(tv) { return !(tv && tv.manager_hours === false); }   /* wave275b: onboard_manager_hours, as times says; missing means on */
  function pvOf(tv) {
    var av = (tv && tv.availability) || { weekly: DEFAULT_WEEK, except: [] }, len = S.cfg.cell * 60000, rule = hoursRule(tv);
    var g = O.newGrid('z0', { grids: {}, hub_events: [] }, 'call'), free = [];
    g.days.forEach(function (d) { Object.keys(d.cells).forEach(function (k) { var t = d.cells[k]; if (!rule || inside(av, t, t + len)) free.push(new Date(t).toISOString()); }); });   /* off: every cell of the window */
    return { grids: { hire: (tv && tv.hire_slots) || [], manager: free }, hub_events: ((tv && tv.busy) || []).map(function (e) { return { start_at: e.start, end_at: e.end, summary: e.summary || 'A meeting', who: ['hire', 'manager'] }; }) };
  }
  function markOf(tv, kind) {
    var lead = Number(kind === 'bookg' ? tv.yes_lead_hours : tv.lead_hours) || 48, taken = (tv.taken || []).map(function (t) { return [Date.parse(t.start), Date.parse(t.end)]; });
    var lens = tv.lengths || { callMin: 60, oneMin: 30 };
    return function (t, mode) {
      var end = t + (mode === 'one' ? lens.oneMin : lens.callMin) * 60000;
      if (t < Date.now() + lead * 3600000) return 'too soon: at least ' + lead + ' hours from now';
