      if (taken.some(function (k) { return k[0] < end && t < k[1]; })) return 'another new hire\'s time';
      return '';
    };
  }
  async function openPanel(id, kind) {
    var x = rowOf(id); if (!x) return;
    delete S.note[id];
    var A = S.act[id] = { kind: kind, loading: true, err: '', g: null, tv: null, member: '', mname: '', exc: [], sel: {}, subject: '', text: '' };
    R.paint();
    try {
      if (kind === 'welcome') {
        var w = await run('welcome', { flow_id: Number(id) });
        A.subject = w.subject || ''; A.text = w.text || '';
      } else if (kind !== 'mgr') {
        var tv = await run('times', { flow_id: Number(id) });
        if (S.act[id] !== A) return;
        A.tv = tv; A.mname = (tv.manager && tv.manager.name) || x.manager_name || 'the manager';
        if (kind === 'propose' || kind === 'bookg') {
          var g = O.newGrid('q' + id, pvOf(tv), 'call');
          g.both = true; g.mark = markOf(tv, kind);
          g.words = hoursRule(tv) ? { h: 'New hire marked', m: firstOf(A.mname) + ' is free', mTip: firstOf(A.mname) + ' is free', busy: 'A meeting, another new hire, or too soon' }
            : { h: 'New hire marked', m: 'Open to pick', mTip: 'open to pick', busy: 'A hub@ meeting, another new hire, or too soon' };   /* wave275b: the week is not a rule */
          if (kind === 'propose' && tv.proposal && !tv.hire_answer) { g.vals.call = O.toLocal(tv.proposal.call_at); g.vals.one = O.toLocal(tv.proposal.one_on_one_at); }
          A.g = g;
        } else if (kind === 'gridfor') {
          A.g = O.newGrid('h' + id, { grids: {}, hub_events: [] }, 'call');
          (tv.hire_slots || []).forEach(function (s) { A.sel[Date.parse(s)] = true; });
        } else if (kind === 'exc') {
          A.member = (tv.manager && tv.manager.member_id) || x.manager_member_id;
          A.exc = ((tv.availability && tv.availability.except) || []).map(function (d) { return { date: d.date, free: (d.free || []).map(function (b) { return [b[0], b[1]]; }) }; });
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { if (S.act[id] === A) A.err = e.message; }
    if (S.act[id] !== A) return;
    A.loading = false; R.paint();
  }
  function saidOf(g, mode) {
    var v = g.vals[mode]; if (!v) return 'Not picked yet';
    var ms = O.toInstant(v); if (ms == null) return 'That time does not exist on the Toronto clock. Pick another.';
    var why = g.mark && g.mark(ms, mode);
    return O.whenText(ms) + (why ? '. Careful: ' + why : '');
  }
  function pickReady(g) { return !!g && O.toInstant(g.vals.call) != null && O.toInstant(g.vals.one) != null; }
  function goButtons(id, label, ready) {
    var busy = S.busy['q' + id];
    return '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-o275a="go" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (busy || !ready ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + E(busy ? 'Working...' : label) + '</button>'
      + '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o275a="cancel" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Cancel</button></div>';
  }
  function panel275(x, A) {
    var id = x.flow_id, h = '<div class="o271pn" data-o275pn="' + E(A.kind) + '">';
    if (A.loading) return h + '<div class="o271ln">Loading...</div></div>';
    if (A.err && ((A.kind === 'propose' || A.kind === 'bookg' || A.kind === 'gridfor') && !A.g || A.kind === 'exc' && !A.tv)) return h + '<div class="o271err">' + E(A.err) + '</div>' + goButtons(id, 'Try again', false) + '</div>';
    var first = firstOf(x.name), mgr = firstOf(A.mname || x.manager_name);
    if (A.kind === 'propose' || A.kind === 'bookg') {
      var g = A.g, isBook = A.kind === 'bookg';
      var rule = hoursRule(A.tv);   /* wave275b: off, the manager's week does not limit the times */
      h += '<div class="o271ln">' + E(isBook
          ? 'Pick both times from ' + first + '\'s marks' + (rule ? ' over ' + mgr + '\'s hours' : '') + '. The Hub books them at once: the invites, the plan and the welcome email follow.'
          : (rule ? 'Pick both times inside ' + mgr + '\'s hours. ' : 'Pick both times; ' + mgr + '\'s week does not limit them. ') + first + ' is emailed them to say yes or no' + (x.accepted_on ? '.' : ', once they accept the offer.')) + '</div>'
        + (rule && A.tv && A.tv.no_week ? '<div class="o271warn">' + E(mgr + ' has not set their week on the Hub\'s Home page yet, so Monday to Friday, 9 to 5 is used.') + '</div>' : '')
        + '<div class="o271md" role="group" aria-label="Which meeting you are picking"><button type="button" class="' + (g.mode === 'call' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-o275a="mode" data-mode="call" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Onboarding call</button><button type="button" class="' + (g.mode === 'one' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-o275a="mode" data-mode="one" data-f="' + E(id) + '">1-on-1</button></div>'
        + O.gridHtml(g)
        + '<div class="o271two"><div><label class="o271f">Onboarding call (Toronto time)</label><input type="datetime-local" data-o275i="call" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(g.vals.call) + '"><div class="o271said" data-o275said="call">' + E(saidOf(g, 'call')) + '</div></div>'
        + '<div><label class="o271f">1-on-1 with ' + E(mgr) + ' (Toronto time)</label><input type="datetime-local" data-o275i="one" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(g.vals.one) + '"><div class="o271said" data-o275said="one">' + E(saidOf(g, 'one')) + '</div></div></div>'
        + (A.err ? '<div class="o271err">' + E(A.err) + '</div>' : '')
        + goButtons(id, isBook ? 'Book these times' : 'Propose these times', pickReady(g));
    } else if (A.kind === 'gridfor') {
      var n = Object.keys(A.sel).filter(function (k) { return A.sel[k]; }).length;
      h += '<div class="o271ln">' + E('Mark when ' + first + ' is free, as they told you. Their own minimum of hours is for their page; it does not apply here.') + '</div>'
        + gridForHtml(id, A) + '<div class="o275cnt" data-o275cnt>' + E(hoursText(n)) + '</div>'
        + (A.err ? '<div class="o271err">' + E(A.err) + '</div>' : '') + goButtons(id, 'Save their times', true);
    } else if (A.kind === 'exc') {
      h += excHtml(id, A) + (A.err ? '<div class="o271err">' + E(A.err) + '</div>' : '') + goButtons(id, 'Save the dates', true);
    } else if (A.kind === 'welcome') {
      h += '<label class="o271f">Welcome email: subject</label><input type="text" style="max-width:none" data-o275i="wsubj" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(A.subject) + '">'
        + '<label class="o271f">Welcome email: text</label><textarea data-o275i="wtext" data-f="' + E(id) + '">' + E(A.text) + '</textarea>'
        + '<div class="o271ln">Anything in [square brackets] fills in from the times, manager, address and links when the times are booked. Leave the brackets in place, even if you change the rest.</div>'
        + '<div class="o271ln">[first password] is filled in only in the email itself, as it goes: nobody reads the password here or on the card.</div>'
        + '<div class="o271ln"><b>The Hub sends this one</b> from hub@ to their personal address as soon as the times are booked, with your edits.</div>'
        + (A.err ? '<div class="o271err">' + E(A.err) + '</div>' : '') + goButtons(id, 'Save the email', true);
    } else if (A.kind === 'mgr') {
      h += '<label class="o271f">New manager</label><select data-o275i="member" data-f="' + E(id) + '">' + O.memberOptions(A.member) + '</select>'
        + '<div class="o271ln">The Hub asks the new manager for times at once. The old manager\'s link stops working.</div>'
        + (A.err ? '<div class="o271err">' + E(A.err) + '</div>' : '') + goButtons(id, 'Change manager', !!A.member);
    }
    return h + '</div>';
  }
  function hoursText(n) { var hrs = n * S.cfg.cell / 60; return n ? hrs + (hrs === 1 ? ' hour' : ' hours') + ' marked' : 'Nothing marked yet'; }
  /* the hire's grid, marked by HR or the manager: the same window and cells as wave271's grid; a click marks or clears a cell */
  function gridForHtml(id, A) {
    var g = A.g;
    if (!g.days.length) return '';
    var h = '<div class="o271lg"><span><i style="background:rgba(16,63,57,.30)"></i>Free</span><span>Toronto time</span></div><div class="o271gw o275gw"><table class="o271g"><thead><tr><th></th>' + g.days.map(function (d) {
      var at = new Date(d.at);
      return '<th>' + E(at.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', weekday: 'short' })) + '<br>' + E(at.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) + '</th>';
    }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    g.rows.forEach(function (mins) {
      var hh = Math.floor(mins / 60);
      h += '<tr><th>' + E(((hh % 12) || 12) + ':' + O.pad2(mins % 60) + (hh < 12 ? ' am' : ' pm')) + '</th>';
      g.days.forEach(function (d) {
        var t = d.cells[mins];
        if (t == null) { h += '<td></td>'; return; }
        var on = !!A.sel[t], label = O.whenText(t) + (on ? '; marked free' : '');
        h += '<td><button type="button" class="o271c' + (on ? ' h on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + on + '" data-o275cell="' + E(new Date(t).toISOString()) + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '" title="' + E(label) + '" aria-label="' + E(label) + '"></button></td>';
      });
      h += '</tr>';
    });
    return h + '</tbody></table></div>';
  }
  async function goPanel(id) {
    var A = S.act[id], x = rowOf(id); if (!A || !x || S.busy['q' + id] || A.loading) return;
    if (A.err && !A.g && !A.tv && A.kind !== 'welcome' && A.kind !== 'mgr') { openPanel(id, A.kind); return; }   /* Try again */
    S.busy['q' + id] = true; A.err = ''; R.paint();
    var first = firstOf(x.name);
    try {
      var note;
      if (A.kind === 'propose' || A.kind === 'bookg') {
        var call = O.isoOf(A.g.vals.call), one = O.isoOf(A.g.vals.one);
        if (!call || !one) throw new Error('Pick both times first.');
        if (A.kind === 'propose') {
          var p = await run('propose', { flow_id: Number(id), call_at: call, one_on_one_at: one });
          note = { ok: p.repeated ? 'These times were already proposed. Nothing changed.' : p.practice ? 'Practice: the times are proposed, and nobody is emailed.' : 'The times are proposed.' + (p.hire_emailed ? ' ' + first + ' is emailed them to say yes or no.' : x.accepted_on ? '' : ' They go to ' + first + ' once the offer is accepted.'), warnings: p.warnings || [] };
        } else {
          await run('book', { flow_id: Number(id), call_at: call, one_on_one_at: one });
          note = { ok: 'The times are booked. The invites, the plan and the welcome email follow; each step shows on the card as it runs.' };
        }
      } else if (A.kind === 'gridfor') {
        var slots = Object.keys(A.sel).filter(function (k) { return A.sel[k]; }).map(Number).sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; }).map(function (ms) { return new Date(ms).toISOString(); });
        var gr = await run('grid_for', { flow_id: Number(id), who: 'hire', slots: slots });
        note = { ok: 'Their times are saved: ' + hoursText(gr.saved != null ? gr.saved : slots.length).replace(' marked', '') + '.' };
      } else if (A.kind === 'exc') {
        var problem = excProblem(A.exc); if (problem) throw new Error(problem);
        await run('availability_for', { member_id: Number(A.member), except: excClean(A.exc) });
        note = { ok: A.mname + '\'s one-off dates are saved.' };
      } else if (A.kind === 'welcome') {
        var w = await run('set_welcome', { flow_id: Number(id), subject: A.subject, text: A.text });
        note = { ok: w.edited === false ? 'The welcome email is the Hub\'s own again.' : 'The welcome email is saved. The Hub sends it when the times are booked.' };
      } else if (A.kind === 'mgr') {
        if (!A.member) throw new Error('Choose the new manager.');
        await run('set_manager', { flow_id: Number(id), member_id: Number(A.member) });
        note = { ok: 'The manager has changed to ' + O.nameOf(A.member) + '.', warnings: [] };
        try { var a = await run('ask_manager', { flow_id: Number(id) }); if (a.sent) note.ok += ' They were asked for times.'; (a.warnings || []).forEach(function (m) { note.warnings.push(m); }); }
        catch (e) { note.warnings.push('They were not asked for times yet: ' + e.message + ' The hourly check asks them.'); }
      }
      delete S.act[id]; delete S.busy['q' + id];
      if (note) S.note[id] = note;
      await R.load();
    } catch (e) { delete S.busy['q' + id]; A.err = e.message; try { toast(e.message); } catch (x2) { /* no toast */ } R.paint(); }
  }
  async function setAsk(id, on) {
    var key = 'k' + id; if (S.busy[key]) return;
    S.busy[key] = true; delete S.note[id]; R.paint();
    try { await run('set_ask_availability', { flow_id: Number(id), on: on }); delete S.busy[key]; S.note[id] = { ok: on ? 'The new hire is asked for their availability if they say no.' : 'Availability already communicated: if they say no, they are told you will be in touch.' }; await R.load(); }
    catch (e) { delete S.busy[key]; S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; R.paint(); }
  }
  async function askAgain(id) {
    var key = 'm' + id; if (S.busy[key]) return;
    S.busy[key] = true; delete S.note[id]; R.paint();
    try { var r = await run('ask_manager', { flow_id: Number(id) }); S.note[id] = r.skipped ? { ok: r.reason || 'Nothing to send.' } : { ok: (r.manager || 'The manager') + ' was asked for times again.', warnings: r.warnings || [] }; }
    catch (e) { S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; }
    delete S.busy[key]; R.paint();
  }
  function liveUpdate(id) {
    var A = S.act[id], root = cardEl(id); if (!A || !A.g || !root) return;
    ['call', 'one'].forEach(function (m) {
      var inp = root.querySelector('[data-o275i="' + m + '"]'); if (inp && inp !== document.activeElement) inp.value = A.g.vals[m];
      var s = root.querySelector('[data-o275said="' + m + '"]'); if (s) s.textContent = saidOf(A.g, m);
    });
    var go = root.querySelector('[data-o275a="go"]'); if (go && !S.busy['q' + id]) go.disabled = !pickReady(A.g);
  }

  /* ---------- "Your new hires": on Home and on the manager's own team page, one copy at a time ---------- */
  var MINE_EL = null;
  function mineEl() { if (!MINE_EL) { MINE_EL = document.createElement('div'); MINE_EL.id = 'o275mine'; MINE_EL.className = 'o271 o275mine'; } return MINE_EL; }
  function onHome() { var h = String(location.hash || ''); return !h || h === '#' || /^#mypage(\/|$)/.test(h); }
  function myTeam() { try { return (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.department) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function hostEl() {
    if (onHome()) return document.getElementById('mpBody');
    var m = /^#team\/([a-z]+)\/?$/.exec(String(location.hash || ''));   /* the team's Overview only */
    return m && m[1] === myTeam() ? document.getElementById('w116host') : null;
  }
  function mount() {
    if (!staffUser()) return;
    var h = hostEl(), el = mineEl();
    if (!h) { if (el.parentNode) el.parentNode.removeChild(el); return; }
    if (S.hrList == null) { if (!S.probing && !S.probed) probe(); return; }   /* not known: ask the database once; still unknown after that, nothing shows */
    if (hrList()) { if (!S.mineListLoaded) { if (!S.mineListLoading) loadMineList(); return; } }
    else if (!S.mineLoaded) { if (!S.loading) R.load(); return; }
    if (!hasMine()) { if (el.parentNode) el.parentNode.removeChild(el); return; }
    if (el.parentNode !== h) {
      if (h.id === 'w116host') { var head = h.querySelector('.w116grid'); h.insertBefore(el, head || null); } else h.appendChild(el);
      paintMine();
    }
  }
  function hasMine() { return hrList() ? (S.mineList || []).length > 0 : !!(S.mineErr || S.ready.length || S.started.length); }
  function lineHtml(x) {
    var l = stateLines(x);
    return '<div class="o275line" data-o275line="' + E(x.flow_id) + '"><b>' + E(x.name) + '</b><span>' + E(x.role || '') + '</span><div class="o271ln">' + E(l[l.length - 1]) + '</div><a href="#hrcentre/onboarding">Open in Onboarding</a></div>';
  }
  function paintMine() {
    var el = MINE_EL; if (!el) return;
    if (!el.parentNode) { if (hasMine()) mount(); return; }
    if (!hasMine()) { el.parentNode.removeChild(el); return; }
    var html = '<h2>Your new hires</h2>';
    if (hrList()) html += '<p class="o271sub">The people joining whom you manage. Their cards are in HR Center, Onboarding.</p>' + (S.mineList || []).map(lineHtml).join('');
    else {
      html += '<p class="o271sub">The people joining whom you manage: propose or book their times, follow their setup, and change a time when you need to.</p>'
        + (S.mineErr ? '<div class="o271err">' + E(S.mineErr) + '</div>' : '')
        + S.ready.map(function (x) { return R.readyCard(x); }).join('') + S.started.map(function (f) { return R.startedCard(f); }).join('');
    }
    var scrolls = {}; Array.prototype.forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('.o271gw'), function (b, i) { scrolls[i] = b.scrollLeft; });
    var active = document.activeElement, keep = active && el.contains(active) && active.getAttribute && (active.getAttribute('data-o275i') || active.getAttribute('data-o271i'));
    var keepF = keep ? active.getAttribute('data-f') : '', attr = keep && active.getAttribute('data-o275i') ? 'data-o275i' : 'data-o271i';
    el.innerHTML = html;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('.o271gw'), function (b, i) { if (scrolls[i]) b.scrollLeft = scrolls[i]; });
    if (keep) { var again = el.querySelector('[' + attr + '="' + keep + '"][data-f="' + keepF + '"]'); if (again) { try { again.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  /* the team page's Overview is drawn after its data loads, so the block is put back whenever wave116 redraws the host */
  var watched = false;
  function watchTeam() {
    var team = document.getElementById('team'); if (!team || watched) return;
    watched = true;
    new MutationObserver(function (list) {
      if (list.every(function (m) { return MINE_EL && (m.target === MINE_EL || MINE_EL.contains(m.target)); })) return;   /* our own paint */
      try { mount(); } catch (e) { /* the next redraw */ }
    }).observe(team, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var cell = t.closest('[data-o271cell]');
    if (cell) { var key = cell.getAttribute('data-g') || ''; if (key.charAt(0) === 'q') liveUpdate(key.slice(1)); return; }   /* wave271's own handler set the time */
    var mine = t.closest('[data-o275cell]');
    if (mine) {
      var fid = mine.getAttribute('data-f'), A0 = S.act[fid]; if (!A0 || A0.kind !== 'gridfor') return;
      var ms = Date.parse(mine.getAttribute('data-o275cell')); A0.sel[ms] = !A0.sel[ms];
      mine.className = 'o271c' + (A0.sel[ms] ? ' h on' : ''); mine.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(!!A0.sel[ms]));
      var cnt = cardEl(fid) && cardEl(fid).querySelector('[data-o275cnt]'); if (cnt) cnt.textContent = hoursText(Object.keys(A0.sel).filter(function (k) { return A0.sel[k]; }).length);
      return;
    }
    var x = t.closest('[data-o275x]');
    if (x && !x.disabled) { excClick(x); return; }
    var b = t.closest('[data-o275a]'); if (!b || b.disabled) return;
    var a = b.getAttribute('data-o275a'), id = b.getAttribute('data-f');
    if (a === 'open') { openPanel(id, b.getAttribute('data-kind')); return; }
    if (a === 'go') { goPanel(id); return; }
    if (a === 'cancel') { delete S.act[id]; R.paint(); return; }
    if (a === 'mode') { var A = S.act[id]; if (A && A.g) { A.g.mode = b.getAttribute('data-mode'); R.paint(); } return; }
    if (a === 'ask') { setAsk(id, b.getAttribute('data-on') === '1'); return; }
    if (a === 'askmgr') { askAgain(id); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-o275xi')) { excInput(t); return; }
    var k = t.getAttribute('data-o275i'), id = t.getAttribute('data-f'), A = S.act[id]; if (!k || !A) return;
    if ((k === 'call' || k === 'one') && A.g) { A.g.vals[k] = t.value; O.refreshGrid(A.g); liveUpdate(id); return; }
    if (k === 'wsubj') { A.subject = t.value; return; }
    if (k === 'wtext') { A.text = t.value; return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-o275xi')) { excInput(t); return; }
    var id = t.getAttribute('data-f'), A = S.act[id];
    if (t.getAttribute('data-o275i') === 'member' && A) { A.member = t.value; R.paint(); }
  });

  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {

  /* ---------- the manager's one-off dates, set by HR or an admin on the card ("Set <manager>'s exceptions", onboard-run availability_for) ----------
     Amended 02/11/26 (Steel): the manager's usual week is wave276's typical week, which they set on the Hub's Home page; only the dates that are not
     like it are kept here (team_members.availability.except). A date with no free hours means not free that day. onboard-run checks again: real dates,
     each once, times on 5-minute marks, each end after its start. */
  function excHtml(id, A) {
    var h = '<div class="o271ln"><b>' + E(A.mname || 'The manager') + '\'s one-off dates.</b> A date that is not like their usual week: not free at all, or other hours. Their usual week is theirs to set on the Hub\'s Home page.</div>';
    if (!A.exc.length) h += '<div class="o271ln">No one-off dates.</div>';
    A.exc.forEach(function (d, i) {
      h += '<div class="o275xd"><div class="o275xr"><label class="o271f" style="margin:0" for="o275x' + E(id) + '_' + i + '">Date</label><input type="date" id="o275x' + E(id) + '_' + i + '" data-o275xi="date" data-x="' + i + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(d.date) + '">'
        + '<button type="button" class="o271b dng" data-o275x="rmdate" data-x="' + i + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Remove this date</button></div>';
      if (!d.free.length) h += '<div class="o271ln">Not free that day.</div>';
      d.free.forEach(function (blk, j) {
        var lab = (d.date || 'This date') + ', free hours ' + (j + 1);
        h += '<div class="o275xr"><input type="time" step="300" data-o275xi="from" data-x="' + i + '" data-b="' + j + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(blk[0]) + '" aria-label="' + E(lab + ', from') + '"><span>to</span>'
          + '<input type="time" step="300" data-o275xi="to" data-x="' + i + '" data-b="' + j + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '" value="' + E(blk[1]) + '" aria-label="' + E(lab + ', to') + '">'
          + '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o275x="rmblk" data-x="' + i + '" data-b="' + j + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Remove</button></div>';
      });
      h += '<div class="o275xr"><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o275x="addblk" data-x="' + i + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Add free hours</button></div></div>';
    });
    return h + '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o275x="adddate" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (A.exc.length >= 120 ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Add a date</button></div>';
  }
  function excClick(b) {
    var id = b.getAttribute('data-f'), A = S.act[id]; if (!A || A.kind !== 'exc') return;
    var act = b.getAttribute('data-o275x'), i = Number(b.getAttribute('data-x')), j = Number(b.getAttribute('data-b'));
    if (act === 'adddate') A.exc.push({ date: '', free: [] });
    else if (act === 'rmdate') A.exc.splice(i, 1);
    else if (act === 'addblk' && A.exc[i]) A.exc[i].free.push(A.exc[i].free.length ? ['18:00', '21:00'] : ['09:00', '17:00']);   /* a first block is a working day; a second, an evening */
    else if (act === 'rmblk' && A.exc[i]) A.exc[i].free.splice(j, 1);
    A.err = '';
    R.paint();
  }
  function excInput(t) {
    var id = t.getAttribute('data-f'), A = S.act[id]; if (!A || A.kind !== 'exc') return;
    var k = t.getAttribute('data-o275xi'), d = A.exc[Number(t.getAttribute('data-x'))]; if (!d) return;
    if (k === 'date') d.date = t.value;
    else { var blk = d.free[Number(t.getAttribute('data-b'))]; if (blk) blk[k === 'from' ? 0 : 1] = t.value; }
  }
  function excProblem(list) {
    var seen = {};
    for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      var d = list[i];
      if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d.date) || seen[d.date]) return 'Each one-off date needs a date, and each date only once.';
      seen[d.date] = true;
      for (var j = 0; j < d.free.length; j++) {
        var a = hm(d.free[j][0]), z = hm(d.free[j][1]);
        if (isNaN(a) || isNaN(z) || a % 5 || z % 5 || a >= z) return 'Each block of free hours needs a start and an end on 5-minute marks, with the end after the start.';
      }
    }
    return '';
  }
  function excClean(list) { return list.map(function (d) { return { date: d.date, free: d.free.map(function (b) { return [b[0], b[1]]; }) }; }); }

  /* ---------- wave278 (04/11/26, Task 6): the access steps, the leader decision, Re-check, and the welcome copies ----------
     Steel (04/11/26): the Hub itself adds a new hire to their Google Groups, shared drives, weekly meeting and Slack onboarding channel (onboard-run's steps
     groups, drives, meeting, channel). The card shows those four rows through wave271's own step row (O.stepHtml: Retry, and Done for an HR task), numbered
     after wave271's eight; the channel step while pending reads "Waiting for them to join Slack" (the hourly job tries again, so nothing to retry). Above
     them the leader decision from the card row (wa278a's wa275_flow_row: leader true, false or null): "Joining as a member" / "Joining as a leader" with
     Change (set_leader with the other value), "Joining as: not decided yet" with both; while set_leader or recheck_access runs, the Groups and Drives rows
     say "Re-running", then the card reloads and the rows re-paint from onboard-run's answer. Re-check Drives and Groups (recheck_access) shows once either
     row exists. Under them, until the welcome is sent: "Welcome email: from <sender>, copied to <owner> and <n> others" (the sender as Task 4 picks it: who
     pressed Send on the offer, then the candidate's owner, then hub@) and Add people, a picker of active staff from the roster (never the hire, never
     someone who left; the owner always gets a copy), saved by set_welcome_cc. onboard-run checks who may (HR, admins, or the hire's own manager through
     wa275_manager_of) and refuses copies once the welcome is sent; the card only shows. What onboard-run answers is kept: its warnings ("nothing to run
     yet", a step already running, the leaders' groups to take them out of by hand) show under the note, and a refusal shows as the error.
     A started card gets the block under its eight steps (wave271's R.startedAfter), the rundown ticks staying above them. HR reads its started flows
     from the table (wave271's FLOW_COLS, which carries leader since wave278), without offer_sent_by and owner_email, so the copies line stays off there:
     without them it would name hub@ as the sender wrongly (the welcome has normally gone by then anyway).
     Fix wave (04/11/26, after the whole-branch review): Re-check and the leader line show on every accepted or started new-path card, also one with no
     Groups or Drives rows yet (accepted before the release: onboard-run makes the rows and runs them; before Accept it says there is nothing to run).
     Only HR and admins may make a hire a leader (Steel's ruling): a manager who is not HR (R.hrTools false) is offered "As a member" and, on a leader,
     Change to member, never the way up; onboard-run is the lock (403). The welcome line names the sender onboard-run works out (times {only:
     'welcome_from'}, welcomeSender's rule: an active Welfare Avenue sender, else the active owner, else hub@), asked once per card; until it answers, or
     when it cannot tell, the line says "from the offer's sender or hub@welfareavenue.com" and names nobody. From leader to member the note no longer adds
     its own "never removes" sentence: onboard-run's warning says it, with the groups. */
  var ACCESS = [['groups', 'Google Groups'], ['drives', 'Shared drives'], ['meeting', 'Weekly meeting'], ['channel', 'Slack onboarding channel']];
  var HUB_FROM = 'hub@welfareavenue.com';
  var st278 = document.createElement('style'); st278.id = 'o278css';
  st278.textContent =
    '.o278acc{min-width:0;margin:4px 0;}' +
    '.o278ln{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:13.5px;color:' + GROUND + ';margin:8px 0 4px;min-width:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}' +
    '.o278ln > span{min-width:0;}' +
    '.o278ln .o271b{min-height:32px;}' +
    '.o278hint{color:' + MUTE + ';font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.o278pk{border:1px solid ' + LINE + ';border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;margin:6px 0;background:' + CREAM + ';min-width:0;}' +
    '.o278pk label{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start;font-size:13.5px;padding:4px 0;color:' + GROUND + ';min-width:0;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.o278pk label > div{min-width:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}' +
    '.o278pk label span{color:' + MUTE + ';font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.o278pk input{margin-top:3px;flex:none;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st278);
  var CC = {};   /* the open copies pickers: flow id -> { sel: { email: true } } */

  function lower(e) { return String(e || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function hasAccess(x) { return (x.steps || []).some(function (r) { return ACCESS.some(function (a) { return a[0] === r.step; }); }); }
  function rerunning(id) { return !!(S.busy['w278l' + id] || S.busy['w278c' + id]); }
  function accessRow(f, key, label, n) {
    var r = (f.steps || []).filter(function (s) { return s.step === key; })[0];
    if (!r) return '';
    var shown = r;
    /* wave271's row prints a status it has no word for as it is, and offers Retry only for failed or stuck running rows: so neither of these offers one */
    if (rerunning(f.id) && (key === 'groups' || key === 'drives')) shown = Object.assign({}, r, { status: 'Re-running', detail: null });
    else if (key === 'channel' && r.status === 'pending') shown = Object.assign({}, r, { status: 'waiting', detail: 'Waiting for them to join Slack' });
    return O.stepHtml(Object.assign({}, f, { steps: [shown] }), key, label, n);
  }
  function leaderHtml(x, id) {
    if (x.leader === undefined) return '';   /* a row without the decision (HR's started cards): nothing to say */
    var busy = S.busy['w278l' + id] ? ' disabled' : '', up = R.hrTools();   /* fix wave (I3): the way up (leader) is HR's and admins' */
    function b(on, label) { return '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o278a="leader" data-on="' + on + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + busy + '>' + E(label) + '</button>'; }
    var line = x.leader === true ? 'Joining as a leader' : x.leader === false ? 'Joining as a member' : 'Joining as: not decided yet';
    var btns = x.leader == null ? b(0, 'As a member') + (up ? b(1, 'As a leader') : '') : x.leader ? b(0, 'Change') : up ? b(1, 'Change') : '';
    var hint = up || x.leader === true ? '' : '<span class="o278hint">' + E('HR or an admin decides if they join as a leader.') + '</span>';
    return '<div class="o278ln" data-o278lead><span>' + E(line) + '</span>' + btns + hint + '</div>';
  }
  /* the staff who may get a copy: active roster rows with an email (active true, as onboard-run requires), never the hire's own row, and not the owner (who always gets one) */
  function staffFor(x) {
    var owner = lower(x.owner_email), hire = lower(x.wa_email);
    return S.people.filter(function (p) {
      var e = lower(p.email);
      return p.active === true && e && e.indexOf('@') > 0 && e !== owner && e !== hire && !(x.member_id != null && String(p.id) === String(x.member_id));
    });
  }
  /* fix wave (M2): who the welcome comes from, as onboard-run works it out (times {only: 'welcome_from'}), asked once per card and asked again only when
     the row's sender or owner changes; null until it answers, and when it cannot tell (then the line names nobody) */
  var FROM = {};   /* flow id -> { key, done, email } */
  function fromOf(x, id) {
    var key = lower(x.offer_sent_by) + '|' + lower(x.owner_email), F0 = FROM[id];
    if (F0 && F0.key === key) return F0.done ? F0.email : null;
    var me = FROM[id] = { key: key, done: false, email: null };
    setTimeout(function () {
      run('times', { flow_id: Number(id), only: 'welcome_from' }).then(function (r) { var e = lower(r && r.welcome_from); me.email = e.indexOf('@') > 0 ? e : null; })
        .catch(function () { me.email = null; })
        .then(function () { me.done = true; if (FROM[id] === me && me.email) R.paint(); });
    }, 0);
    return null;
  }
  function welcomeHtml(x, id) {
    /* sent (its copy is under the welcome step), or a row without the copies or without the owner (HR's started cards: see the top) */
    if (x.welcome_sent_at || !Array.isArray(x.welcome_cc) || x.owner_email === undefined) return '';
    var owner = lower(x.owner_email), from = fromOf(x, id);
    var cc = x.welcome_cc.map(lower).filter(function (e) { return e && e !== owner; });
    var all = (owner ? [owner] : []).concat(cc);
    var to = !all.length ? ', with no copies' : ', copied to ' + personName(all[0]) + (all.length > 1 ? ' and ' + (all.length - 1) + (all.length === 2 ? ' other' : ' others') : '');
    var P = CC[id];
    return '<div class="o278ln" data-o278wel><span>' + E('Welcome email: from ' + (from ? personName(from) : 'the offer\'s sender or ' + HUB_FROM) + to) + '</span>'
      + (P ? '' : '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o278a="ccopen" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Add people</button>') + '</div>'
      + (P ? pickerHtml(x, id, P) : '');
  }
  function pickerHtml(x, id, P) {
    var list = staffFor(x), owner = lower(x.owner_email), busy = S.busy['w278w' + id];
    return '<div class="o278pk" data-o278pk>'
      + '<div class="o271ln">' + E('Tick the staff who get a copy of the welcome email. ' + (owner ? personName(owner) + ' always gets a copy. ' : '') + 'Their copy has no password in it. Someone who has left the roster is taken off the copies when you save.') + '</div>'
      + (list.length ? list.map(function (p) {
          var e = lower(p.email);
          return '<label><input type="checkbox" data-o278cc="' + E(e) + '" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (P.sel[e] ? ' checked' : '') + '><div>' + E(p.name) + ' <span>' + E(p.email) + '</span></div></label>';
        }).join('') : '<div class="o271ln">No one else on the roster.</div>')
      + '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-o278a="ccsave" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + E(busy ? 'Working...' : 'Save the copies') + '</button>'
      + '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-o278a="ccshut" data-f="' + E(id) + '">Cancel</button></div></div>';
  }
  function accessHtml(x, id, started) {
    var f = { id: id, steps: x.steps || [], wa_email: x.wa_email, canva_link: x.canva_link };
    var rows = ACCESS.map(function (a, i) { return accessRow(f, a[0], a[1], O.STEPS.length + i + 1); }).join('');
    /* fix wave (I2): every accepted or started card on the new path, rows or not (onboard-run makes them for a flow accepted before the release) */
    var again = !!(started || x.accepted_on) || (x.steps || []).some(function (r) { return r.step === 'groups' || r.step === 'drives'; });
    return '<div class="o278acc" data-o278acc="' + E(id) + '">' + leaderHtml(x, id)
      + (rows ? '<ol class="o271st o278st">' + rows + '</ol>' : '')
      + (again ? '<div class="o271btns"><button type="button" class="o271b" data-o278a="recheck" data-f="' + E(id) + '"' + (rerunning(id) ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Re-check Drives and Groups</button></div>' : '')
      + welcomeHtml(x, id) + '</div>';
  }
  /* a started card on the new path gets the same block under its eight steps: one whose access steps ran, whose leader is decided, or (fix wave, I2)
     any other on the new path (proposed, or wa275_my_cards' propose_path), so a flow started before the release gets Re-check too. A wave271 flow
     (none of these) keeps its card as it was. */
  R.startedAfter = function (f) { return hasAccess(f) || f.leader != null || f.propose_path || f.proposed_at ? accessHtml(f, f.id, true) : ''; };

  /* ---------- the calls: each one reloads the card, so what shows is onboard-run's answer, with its warnings ---------- */
  function warningsOf(r) { return ((r && r.warnings) || []).map(String).filter(Boolean); }
  function idle(w) { return w.some(function (m) { return /nothing to run/i.test(m); }); }   /* onboard-run ran nothing (before Accept, or an older flow) */
  async function setLeader(id, on) {
    var key = 'w278l' + id, x = rowOf(id); if (!x || S.busy[key]) return;
    S.busy[key] = true; delete S.note[id]; R.paint();
    try {
      var w = warningsOf(await run('set_leader', { flow_id: Number(id), leader: on })), ran = !idle(w);
      delete S.busy[key];
      S.note[id] = { ok: firstOf(x.name) + (on ? ' joins as a leader now.' + (ran ? ' The Hub ran Groups and Drives again: the rows say what was added.' : '')
        : ' joins as a member now.' + (ran ? ' The Hub ran Groups and Drives again.' : '')), warnings: w };   /* fix wave (M4): from leader to member onboard-run's warning names the groups to take them out of */
      await R.load();
    } catch (e) { delete S.busy[key]; S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; R.paint(); }
  }
  async function recheck(id) {
    var key = 'w278c' + id; if (!rowOf(id) || S.busy[key]) return;
    S.busy[key] = true; delete S.note[id]; R.paint();
    try {
      var w = warningsOf(await run('recheck_access', { flow_id: Number(id) }));
      delete S.busy[key];
      S.note[id] = { ok: idle(w) ? '' : 'Drives and Groups were checked again: the rows say what changed.', warnings: w };
      await R.load();
    } catch (e) { delete S.busy[key]; S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; R.paint(); }
  }
  async function openCc(id) {
    var x = rowOf(id); if (!x) return;
    var sel = {}, owner = lower(x.owner_email);
    (x.welcome_cc || []).forEach(function (e) { e = lower(e); if (e && e !== owner) sel[e] = true; });
    CC[id] = { sel: sel }; delete S.note[id];
    await O.readPeople();
    R.paint();
  }
  async function saveCc(id) {
    var P = CC[id], key = 'w278w' + id; if (!P || !rowOf(id) || S.busy[key]) return;
    var emails = Object.keys(P.sel).filter(function (k) { return P.sel[k]; });
    S.busy[key] = true; delete S.note[id]; R.paint();
    try {
      var w = warningsOf(await run('set_welcome_cc', { flow_id: Number(id), emails: emails }));
      delete CC[id]; delete S.busy[key];
      S.note[id] = { ok: emails.length ? 'Saved: ' + emails.length + (emails.length === 1 ? ' person gets' : ' people get') + ' a copy of the welcome email.' : 'Saved: no copies besides the owner\'s.', warnings: w };
      await R.load();
    } catch (e) { delete S.busy[key]; S.note[id] = { err: e.message }; try { toast(e.message); } catch (x2) { /* no toast */ } R.paint(); }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('[data-o278a]'); if (!b || b.disabled) return;
    var a = b.getAttribute('data-o278a'), id = b.getAttribute('data-f');
    if (a === 'leader') setLeader(id, b.getAttribute('data-on') === '1');
    else if (a === 'recheck') recheck(id);
    else if (a === 'ccopen') openCc(id);
    else if (a === 'ccsave') saveCc(id);
    else if (a === 'ccshut') { delete CC[id]; R.paint(); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute || !t.hasAttribute('data-o278cc')) return;
    var P = CC[t.getAttribute('data-f')]; if (P) P.sel[t.getAttribute('data-o278cc')] = !!t.checked;
  });
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v279 The onboarding plan revamp: the manager's role-pick pop-up and the plan's role steps (04/11/26, Task 7)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (04/11/26): the manager chooses each hire's role trainings in a pop-up that recommends trainings for that person's role or
   department: 2 or 3 for 60 days and 5 or 6 for 90 days.
   - Home: a manager whose hire is at day 21 or later (the database's count: today minus day one) and still has a short list sees
     "Pick <first name>'s role trainings". The hires come from the lead's card (wave166, onboarding_lead_view) and from wa279_my_hires
     (the plans the database says this person manages, the flow's named manager first), each plan once; wa279_role_options is asked
     only for the hires at day 21 or later, and the card shows only where the database says this person may pick.
   - The pop-up (its own layer, so it opens over HR's checklist too): two lists, 60 days and 90 days. Recommended modules first, in
     the database's order, then the ones already picked; a search over every active Academy module (the trainings table, as the
     Academy reads it; planned ones left out). Modules the hire finished (done_ids, search results included) are greyed and cannot
     be picked; a module picked in the other list cannot be picked here. Save is on only inside 2 to 3 and 5 to 6, and sends one
     wa279_role_save per list. The database's refusal shows as it is. A list whose picks are all finished (the section's done) is
     read only: "Done", no search, no Save.
   - HR centre: the person's checklist (wa37OpenModal) gets a Role trainings button that opens the same pop-up.
   - The plan: wave166's checklist hands the role60 / role90 steps to roleStep (one call-site line in parts166/c_plan.js): the
     picked modules with a tick each and who picked them, or "Your manager picks these in week 3."
   Data: build/wa279a_rules.sql (wa279_role_options, wa279_role_save, wa279_my_hires). Test: test/w279drive.js with test/w279fx.js. */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa279) return; window.__wa279 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', INK = '#1F201E', MUTE = '#797773', LINE = '#DEE0D8', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', FLAG = '#B3402F', GOLD = '#DCA527';
  var SECS = [{ key: '60 DAYS', head: '60 days: pick 2 or 3', word: '60 days' }, { key: '90 DAYS', head: '90 days: pick 5 or 6', word: '90 days' }];
  var FRESH = 60000;
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function T(msg) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(msg); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function errText(e) { return String((e && e.message) || e || 'That did not work. Try again.'); }
  function first(n) { return String(n || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || 'them'; }
  function fmtDay(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: 'UTC', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(0, 10); } }
  function secOf(d, key) { return (d && d.sections && d.sections[key]) || { min: key === '60 DAYS' ? 2 : 5, max: key === '60 DAYS' ? 3 : 6, picked: [] }; }
  function isShort(d) { return SECS.some(function (s) { var x = secOf(d, s.key); return (x.picked || []).length < x.min; }); }

  /* ---------- style ---------- */
  var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w279css';
  st.textContent =
    '#mypage.w272 #mpBody > #w279home{order:5;grid-column:1 / -1;}' +
    '#w279home h3{margin:0 0 6px;font:500 22px/1.05 \'Barlow Condensed\',\'Arial Narrow\',\'Helvetica Neue\',Arial,sans-serif;letter-spacing:.01em;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';overflow-wrap:anywhere;}' +   /* as wave272's Home headings */
    '#w279home p{margin:0 0 10px;font-size:14px;color:#4a5551;}' +
    '#w279home .w279hc + .w279hc{border-top:1px solid ' + LINE + ';margin-top:12px;padding-top:12px;}' +

    '.w279btn.ghost{background:' + CREAM + ';color:' + GREEN + ';}' +

    '.w279hr{margin:0 0 10px;}' +

    '#w279sheet .w279pan{background:' + CREAM + ';color:' + INK + ';border-radius:18px;width:100%;max-width:640px;box-sizing:border-box;padding:18px 18px 16px;min-width:0;box-shadow:0 18px 40px rgba(16,63,57,.25);}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279top{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;}' +
    '#w279sheet h3{margin:0;font-size:22px;line-height:1.15;color:' + GREEN + ';overflow-wrap:anywhere;min-width:0;}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279x{font:inherit;font-size:22px;line-height:1;background:none;border:0;color:' + GREEN + ';cursor:pointer;padding:2px 6px;min-height:36px;}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279lead{font-size:13.5px;color:#4a5551;margin:6px 0 4px;}' +
