    '#w279sheet .w279opt input{margin:3px 0 0;flex:0 0 auto;width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:' + GREEN + ';}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279opt span{min-width:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere;font-size:14px;}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279opt small{display:block;color:' + MUTE + ';font-size:12px;}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279opt.off{opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed;}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279grp{font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + MUTE + ';margin:8px 0 2px;}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279q{display:block;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';margin:8px 0 0;}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279q input{display:block;width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;margin-top:4px;font:inherit;font-size:14px;min-height:38px;padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid ' + LINE + ';border-radius:8px;background:' + CREAM + ';color:' + INK + ';}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279foot{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px;}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279cnt{font-size:13px;color:' + INK + ';}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279cnt.bad{color:' + FLAG + ';}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279err{flex-basis:100%;font-size:13px;color:' + FLAG + ';background:#FBEDEA;border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}' +
    '#w279sheet .w279ok{font-size:13px;color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w279step{margin:4px 0 2px;min-width:0;}' +
    '.w279step ul{list-style:none;margin:4px 0 0;padding:0;}' +
    '.w279step li{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start;margin:4px 0;min-width:0;}' +
    '.w279step li > span:last-child{min-width:0;flex:1 1 auto;line-height:1.35;}' +
    '.w279step .w279tick{flex:0 0 auto;margin-top:2px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:16px;height:16px;border-radius:4px;border:1.5px solid ' + LINE + ';color:' + LINE + ';font-size:11px;line-height:1;}' +
    '.w279step li.ok .w279tick{background:' + GREEN + ';border-color:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';}' +
    '.w279step .w279mod{font:inherit;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;color:' + GREEN + ';background:none;border:0;padding:0;cursor:pointer;text-align:left;overflow-wrap:anywhere;min-width:0;}' +
    '.w279step small{font-size:12px;color:' + MUTE + ';margin-left:6px;white-space:nowrap;}' +
    '.w279step .w279none{font-size:13px;color:' + MUTE + ';}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  var OPT = {};   /* onboarding id -> { at, data | err, p } */
  function options(onb, force) {
    var k = String(onb), c = OPT[k];
    if (c && c.p) return c.p;
    if (c && !force && Date.now() - c.at < FRESH) return c.err ? Promise.reject(new Error(c.err)) : Promise.resolve(c.data);
    var p = Promise.resolve(db.rpc('wa279_role_options', { p_onb: Number(onb) })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      OPT[k] = { at: Date.now(), data: (r && r.data) || null };
      return OPT[k].data;
    }).catch(function (e) { OPT[k] = { at: Date.now(), err: errText(e) }; throw e; });
    OPT[k] = { at: 0, p: p };
    return p;
  }
  var MODS = null, modsP = null;
  function modules() {
    if (MODS) return Promise.resolve(MODS);
    if (modsP) return modsP;
    modsP = Promise.resolve(db.from('trainings').select('id, title, path_group, kind, body, active').eq('active', true).order('sort').order('id')).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      MODS = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (t) { return t && t.title && t.active !== false && !(t.body && t.body.planned); });
      modsP = null; return MODS;
    }).catch(function () { modsP = null; return []; });
    return modsP;
  }

  /* ---------- the plan's role steps ---------- */
  function pickedBy(p) { return (p.auto || /^the hub$/i.test(String(p.picked_by || '')) || !p.picked_by) ? 'Picked by the Hub' : 'Picked by ' + p.picked_by; }
  function stepInner(d, key) {
    var picks = secOf(d, key).picked || [];
    if (!picks.length) return '<div class="w279none">Your manager picks these in week 3.</div>';
    return '<ul>' + picks.map(function (p) {
      return '<li class="' + (p.done ? 'ok' : '') + '"><span class="w279tick" aria-label="' + (p.done ? 'Done' : 'Not done yet') + '">&#10003;</span>'
        + '<span><button type="button" class="w279mod" data-w166open="t:' + E(p.training_id) + '">' + E(p.title) + '</button> <small>' + E(pickedBy(p)) + '</small></span></li>';
    }).join('') + '</ul>';
  }
  function fillSteps(onb) {
    var c = OPT[String(onb)];
    document.querySelectorAll('.w279step[data-w279onb="' + String(onb).replace(/"/g, '') + '"]').forEach(function (el) {
      var h = c && c.data ? stepInner(c.data, el.getAttribute('data-w279sec')) : '';
      if (el.__h !== h) { el.innerHTML = h; el.__h = h; }
    });
  }
  function roleStep(t, onb) {
    var key = t.academy_path === 'role60' ? '60 DAYS' : '90 DAYS', id = onb && onb.id;
    if (!id) return '';
    var c = OPT[String(id)], fresh = c && !c.p && Date.now() - c.at < FRESH;
    if (!fresh) options(id).then(function () { fillSteps(id); }, function () { fillSteps(id); });
    var inner = c && c.data ? stepInner(c.data, key) : '';
    return '<div class="w279step" data-w279onb="' + E(id) + '" data-w279sec="' + E(key) + '">' + inner + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the pop-up ---------- */
  var SH = null;   /* { onb, d, sel: {key: [ids]}, q: {key: ''}, busy: {}, err: {}, ok: {} } */
  function selFrom(d) { var o = {}; SECS.forEach(function (s) { o[s.key] = (secOf(d, s.key).picked || []).map(function (p) { return Number(p.training_id); }); }); return o; }
  function sheetEl() { return document.getElementById('w279sheet'); }
  function close() { var el = sheetEl(); if (el) el.remove(); SH = null; }
  function other(key) { return key === '60 DAYS' ? '90 DAYS' : '60 DAYS'; }
  /* fix round 1: done_ids (every module the hire finished) covers what the search finds too */
  function doneOf(id) {
    var d = SH.d, hit = null;
    if ((d.done_ids || []).some(function (x) { return Number(x) === id; })) return true;
    (d.recommended || []).forEach(function (r) { if (Number(r.training_id) === id) hit = r; });
    SECS.forEach(function (s) { (secOf(d, s.key).picked || []).forEach(function (p) { if (Number(p.training_id) === id) hit = hit || p; }); });
    return !!(hit && hit.done);
  }
  /* fix round 1: a section is done when it has picks and every one is finished (wa279_role_save refuses a change then) */
  function secDone(key) { return !!secOf(SH.d, key).done; }
  function canEdit(key) { return !!SH.d.can_pick && !secDone(key); }
  function optHtml(key, m, can) {
    var id = Number(m.training_id != null ? m.training_id : m.id), on = SH.sel[key].indexOf(id) >= 0;
    var done = doneOf(id), there = SH.sel[other(key)].indexOf(id) >= 0;
    var off = !can || there || done;
    var note = done ? first(SH.d.member) + ' finished it already' : (there ? 'Picked for ' + (key === '60 DAYS' ? '90 days' : '60 days') : (m.path || m.path_group || ''));
    return '<label class="w279opt' + (off ? ' off' : '') + '"><input type="checkbox" data-w279pick="' + E(key) + '" value="' + E(id) + '"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + (off ? ' disabled' : '') + ' />'
      + '<span>' + E(m.title) + (note ? '<small>' + E(note) + '</small>' : '') + '</span></label>';
  }
  function listHtml(key) {
    var d = SH.d, can = canEdit(key), seen = {}, h = '';
    if (secDone(key)) return (secOf(d, key).picked || []).map(function (p) { return optHtml(key, p, false); }).join('');   /* a done list shows only its picks */
    var rec = (d.recommended || []).filter(function (r) { var k = Number(r.training_id); if (seen[k]) return false; seen[k] = 1; return true; });
    if (rec.length) h += '<div class="w279grp">Recommended</div>' + rec.map(function (r) { return optHtml(key, r, can); }).join('');
    /* what is picked, or ticked from the search, and not in the recommended list */
    var more = SH.sel[key].filter(function (id) { return !seen[id]; }).map(function (id) {
      seen[id] = 1;
      var p = null; (secOf(d, key).picked || []).forEach(function (x) { if (Number(x.training_id) === id) p = x; });
      var m = null; (MODS || []).forEach(function (x) { if (Number(x.id) === id) m = x; });
      return { training_id: id, title: (p && p.title) || (m && m.title) || 'Module ' + id, path: m && m.path_group };
    });
    if (more.length) h += '<div class="w279grp">Also picked</div>' + more.map(function (m) { return optHtml(key, m, can); }).join('');
    if (!rec.length && !more.length) h += '<div class="w279due">Nothing is recommended for this team yet. Search for a module below.</div>';
    return h;
  }
  function resHtml(key) {
    var q = String(SH.q[key] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return '';
    if (!MODS) return '<div class="w279due">Loading the modules...</div>';
    var shown = {};
    (SH.d.recommended || []).forEach(function (r) { shown[Number(r.training_id)] = 1; });
    SH.sel[key].forEach(function (id) { shown[id] = 1; });
    var hits = MODS.filter(function (m) { return !shown[Number(m.id)] && String(m.title).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0; });
    if (!hits.length) return '<div class="w279due">No module matches that.</div>';
    return hits.slice(0, 8).map(function (m) { return optHtml(key, m, canEdit(key)); }).join('')
      + (hits.length > 8 ? '<div class="w279due">' + (hits.length - 8) + ' more. Type more of the name.</div>' : '');
  }
  function footHtml(key) {
    var x = secOf(SH.d, key), n = SH.sel[key].length, inRange = n >= x.min && n <= x.max;
    if (secDone(key)) return '<span class="w279cnt">' + (n === 1 ? 'It is' : 'All ' + n + ' are') + ' finished, so this list can&rsquo;t change now.</span>';
    if (!SH.d.can_pick) return '<span class="w279cnt">' + n + ' picked</span>';
    var cnt = n + ' picked' + (n < x.min ? '. Pick ' + x.min + ' or ' + x.max + '.' : (n > x.max ? '. That is too many: pick ' + x.min + ' or ' + x.max + '.' : '.'));
    return '<span class="w279cnt' + (inRange ? '' : ' bad') + '">' + cnt + '</span>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w279btn" data-w279save="' + E(key) + '"' + (inRange && !SH.busy[key] ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + (SH.busy[key] ? 'Saving...' : 'Save') + '</button>'
      + (SH.ok[key] ? '<span class="w279ok">Saved.</span>' : '')
      + (SH.err[key] ? '<div class="w279err" role="alert">' + E(SH.err[key]) + '</div>' : '');
  }
  function paintSec(key) {
    var el = sheetEl(); if (!el || !SH) return;
    var box = el.querySelector('.w279sec[data-w279sec="' + key + '"]'); if (!box) return;
    var done = secDone(key);
    box.querySelector('[data-w279part="state"]').innerHTML = done ? '<span class="w279badge">Done</span>' : '';
    box.querySelector('[data-w279part="list"]').innerHTML = listHtml(key);
    var q = box.querySelector('.w279q'); if (q) q.style.display = done ? 'none' : '';
    var r = box.querySelector('[data-w279part="res"]'); if (r) r.innerHTML = done ? '' : resHtml(key);
    box.querySelector('[data-w279part="foot"]').innerHTML = footHtml(key);
  }
  function paintAll() { SECS.forEach(function (s) { paintSec(s.key); }); }
  function sheetHtml() {
    var d = SH.d, nm = first(d.member);
    return '<div class="w279pan" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w279h">'
      + '<div class="w279top"><h3 id="w279h">Pick ' + E(nm) + '&#39;s role trainings</h3><button type="button" class="w279x" data-w279x="1" aria-label="Close">&times;</button></div>'
      + '<p class="w279lead">' + (d.can_pick
        ? 'Recommended modules for ' + E(nm) + '&#39;s team come first. Search for any other module. Modules ' + E(nm) + ' finished already are greyed out.'
        : 'Only their manager, HR or an admin can pick these.') + '</p>'
      + SECS.map(function (s) {
        var x = secOf(d, s.key);
        return '<div class="w279sec" data-w279sec="' + E(s.key) + '"><div class="w279sh"><h4>' + E(s.head) + '</h4><span data-w279part="state"></span></div>'
          + (x.due ? '<div class="w279due">Due ' + E(fmtDay(x.due)) + '</div>' : '')
          + '<div data-w279part="list"></div>'
          + (d.can_pick ? '<label class="w279q">Search all modules<input type="search" data-w279q="' + E(s.key) + '" placeholder="Type a module name" autocomplete="off" /></label><div data-w279part="res"></div>' : '')
          + '<div class="w279foot" data-w279part="foot"></div></div>';
      }).join('')
      + '</div>';
  }
  async function openSheet(onb) {
    close();
    var d;
    try { d = await options(onb, true); } catch (e) { T(errText(e)); return; }
    if (!d) { T('That onboarding plan was not found'); return; }
    SH = { onb: Number(onb), d: d, sel: selFrom(d), q: { '60 DAYS': '', '90 DAYS': '' }, busy: {}, err: {}, ok: {} };
    var el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w279sheet'; el.innerHTML = sheetHtml();
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    paintAll();
    try { el.querySelector('[data-w279x]').focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    modules().then(function () { if (SH) paintAll(); });
  }
  async function save(key) {
    if (!SH || SH.busy[key]) return;
    var onb = SH.onb, ids = SH.sel[key].slice();
    SH.busy[key] = true; SH.err[key] = ''; SH.ok[key] = false; paintSec(key);
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa279_role_save', { p_onb: onb, p_section: key, p_training_ids: ids });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = (r && r.data) || null;
      if (d) { OPT[String(onb)] = { at: Date.now(), data: d }; }
      if (SH && SH.onb === onb) {
        if (d) { SH.d = d; SH.sel[key] = selFrom(d)[key]; }
        SH.busy[key] = false; SH.ok[key] = true; paintAll();
      }
      T('Saved the ' + (key === '60 DAYS' ? '60-day' : '90-day') + ' role trainings');
      fillSteps(onb); paintHome();
    } catch (e) {
      if (SH && SH.onb === onb) { SH.busy[key] = false; SH.err[key] = errText(e); paintSec(key); }
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var o = t.closest('[data-w279open]');
    if (o) { e.preventDefault(); openSheet(o.getAttribute('data-w279open')); return; }
    if (!SH) return;
    if (t.id === 'w279sheet' || t.closest('[data-w279x]')) { e.preventDefault(); close(); return; }
    var s = t.closest('[data-w279save]');
    if (s && !s.disabled) { e.preventDefault(); save(s.getAttribute('data-w279save')); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var c = e.target; if (!SH || !c || !c.getAttribute || !c.getAttribute('data-w279pick')) return;
    var key = c.getAttribute('data-w279pick'), id = Number(c.value), list = SH.sel[key];
    if (c.checked) { if (list.indexOf(id) < 0) list.push(id); } else SH.sel[key] = list.filter(function (x) { return x !== id; });
    SH.ok[key] = false; SH.err[key] = '';
    paintAll();
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var q = e.target; if (!SH || !q || !q.getAttribute || !q.getAttribute('data-w279q')) return;
    var key = q.getAttribute('data-w279q'); SH.q[key] = q.value;
    var box = sheetEl() && sheetEl().querySelector('.w279sec[data-w279sec="' + key + '"] [data-w279part="res"]');
    if (box) box.innerHTML = resHtml(key);
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (SH && e.key === 'Escape') close(); });

  /* ---------- Home: the hires whose role trainings are still to pick ---------- */
  var HOME = [];   /* [{ onb, d }] */
  function homeHtml() {
    return HOME.map(function (h) {
      var nm = first(h.d.member), left = SECS.filter(function (s) { var x = secOf(h.d, s.key); return (x.picked || []).length < x.min; }).map(function (s) { return s.word; });
      return '<div class="w279hc" data-w279home="' + E(h.onb) + '"><h3>Pick ' + E(nm) + '&#39;s role trainings</h3>'
        + '<p>' + E(nm) + ' is on day ' + E(h.d.day) + '. Choose 2 or 3 trainings for 60 days and 5 or 6 for 90 days. Still to pick: ' + E(left.join(' and ')) + '.</p>'
        + '<button type="button" class="w279btn" data-w279open="' + E(h.onb) + '">Pick them</button></div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function paintHome() {
    HOME = HOME.map(function (h) { var c = OPT[String(h.onb)]; return c && c.data ? { onb: h.onb, d: c.data } : h; }).filter(function (h) { return h.d.can_pick && isShort(h.d); });
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody'), el = document.getElementById('w279home');
    if (!body || !HOME.length) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w279home'; el.className = 'mp-sec'; body.insertBefore(el, body.firstChild); }
    var h = homeHtml(); if (el.__h !== h) { el.innerHTML = h; el.__h = h; }
  }
  var homeQ = null;
  function homeRefresh() {
    if (homeQ) return homeQ;
    homeQ = (async function () {
      try {
        if (!document.getElementById('mpBody')) return;
        var W = window.w166, seen = {}, ppl = [];
        /* the hires on the lead's card (onboarding_lead_view). It counts day one as day 1; the database counts today minus day one, so a lead day
           of 21 or more is the wider net */
        if (W && typeof W.load === 'function') {
          try {
            var lead = await W.load(false);
            ((lead && lead.people) || []).forEach(function (p) {
              if (p && p.onboarding_id && !p.starts_on && Number(p.day) >= 21 && !seen[p.onboarding_id]) { seen[p.onboarding_id] = 1; ppl.push(p); }
            });
          } catch (e) { /* the other list still counts */ }
        }
        /* final review: plus the plans the database says this person manages (wa279_my_hires), so a flow's named manager who is not on the lead's
           card still sees the card. Day as the database counts it. */
        try {
          var mh = await db.rpc('wa279_my_hires');
          if (mh && !mh.error) (mh.data || []).forEach(function (p) {
            if (p && p.onboarding_id && Number(p.day) >= 21 && !seen[p.onboarding_id]) { seen[p.onboarding_id] = 1; ppl.push(p); }
          });
        } catch (e) { /* the lead's card still counts */ }
        var out = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < ppl.length; i++) {
          try { var d = await options(ppl[i].onboarding_id); if (d && Number(d.day) >= 21 && d.can_pick && isShort(d)) out.push({ onb: ppl[i].onboarding_id, d: d }); } catch (e) { /* not theirs to pick */ }
        }
        HOME = out; paintHome();
      } finally { homeQ = null; }
    })();
    return homeQ;
  }
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rm279 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rm279.apply(this, arguments);
      try { paintHome(); homeRefresh(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }

  /* ---------- HR centre: the person's checklist gets Role trainings ---------- */
  if (typeof wa37OpenModal === 'function') {
    var __om279 = wa37OpenModal;
    wa37OpenModal = function (onbId) {
      var out = __om279.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var o = (typeof WA37 !== 'undefined' && WA37.onbs) ? WA37.onbs.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(onbId); })[0] : null;
        var m = document.getElementById('w37modal');
        if (o && o.status === 'active' && m && !document.querySelector('[data-w279hr]')) {
          m.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', '<div class="w279hr" data-w279hr="1"><button type="button" class="w279btn ghost" data-w279open="' + E(o.id) + '">Role trainings</button></div>');
        }
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return out;
    };
  }

  /* ---------- test hook ---------- */
  window.w279 = { roleStep: roleStep, open: openSheet, close: close, home: homeRefresh, options: options, state: function () { return { SH: SH, OPT: OPT, HOME: HOME }; } };
})();

/* ---------- wave279 Task 8: the policy reading timer and the HR board's Academy trainings count ----------
   window.wa279Pol: a required policy can be signed only 10 minutes (app_config policy_read_minutes) after it was first opened. The rule itself is
     the database's (trg_wa279_policy_read on wa_policy_signoffs); this is the page's side of it, shared by the three sign paths (Home's Set up
     your Hub in parts272/c_setup.js, the Academy's Onboarding in parts122/d_home.js, the classic training page's card in 105_wave105.seg).
     - A link with data-w279polopen="<key>" calls wa279_policy_open(key) when clicked; its {unlock_at} is kept.
     - wa279_policy_state() (the person's own opens) is read once, the first time a Sign button is drawn.
     - view(key, label, seen) says how a Sign button looks: "Sign in N min" (N = whole minutes left, rounded up) and disabled until unlock_at,
       then the page's own label. With no open recorded it is disabled, and the opens are read again when they are more than a minute old
       (an open on another device shows without a reload); they are also read again whenever the tab comes back into view. When the database could not say (the call failed or gave nothing),
       the page's old rule stands: enabled when seen (Home: opened in this tab; the Academy: always).
     - attrs(key, label, seen) marks the button (data-w279polsign) so refresh() can redraw it in place: after each answer, and every 30
       seconds while a counting button is on screen. When none is, the timer stops. A first draw asks for one redraw (0 ms) to start that
       timer; refresh() itself never asks again, so nothing else repeats.
   window.wa279Acad: "Academy trainings d of t" on each row of the HR centre's onboarding board (046_wave46.seg). t = the plan's steps that have a
     training_id or an academy_path (the policies step counts once), with the role steps (role60, role90) counted as their picks. d = the done
     ones: a step's own done flag; a pick is done when its Academy progress says so (or the whole role step is done). The picks are the role
     trainings' Academy assignments (note "Role training (60 days)" / "(90 days)", see wa279_pick_add), read in one query for every plan. */
(function () {
  if (window.wa279Pol) return;
  var P = { st: null, at: 0, load: null, fail: false, unk: {}, tick: null, soon: null };
  var TICK = 30000, STALE = 60000;
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function client() { try { return (typeof db !== 'undefined' && db && typeof db.rpc === 'function') ? db : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function when(x) { var t = Date.parse(x); return isNaN(t) ? null : t; }
  function loadState() {
    if (P.load) return P.load;
    var d = client(); if (!d) { P.fail = true; return Promise.resolve(); }
    P.load = Promise.resolve(d.rpc('wa279_policy_state')).then(function (r) {
      if (!r || r.error || !r.data || typeof r.data !== 'object') throw (r && r.error) || new Error('no answer');
      var m = P.st || {};
      Object.keys(r.data).forEach(function (k) { var u = when(r.data[k] && r.data[k].unlock_at); if (u !== null) { m[k] = u; delete P.unk[k]; } });
      P.st = m; P.fail = false;
    }).catch(function () { P.fail = true; }).then(function () { P.load = null; P.at = Date.now(); refresh(); });
    return P.load;
  }
  function open(key) {
    var d = client(); if (!d || !key) return Promise.resolve();
    return Promise.resolve(d.rpc('wa279_policy_open', { p_key: key })).then(function (r) {
      var u = when(r && !r.error && r.data && r.data.unlock_at);
      if (u === null) throw (r && r.error) || new Error('no answer');
      P.st = P.st || {}; P.st[key] = u; delete P.unk[key];
    }).catch(function () { P.unk[key] = true; }).then(refresh);
  }
  /* minutes left (0 = unlocked), 'none' (no open recorded, or not known yet) or 'unknown' (the database could not say) */
  function left(key) {
    if (P.unk[key]) return 'unknown';
    if (P.st && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(P.st, key)) return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((P.st[key] - Date.now()) / 60000));
    if (P.fail) return 'unknown';
    return 'none';
  }
  /* inRefresh: called by refresh(), which must not ask for another redraw (the 30 second tick is the only timer that repeats) */
  function view(key, label, seen, inRefresh) {
    if (!P.load && !P.fail && (!P.st || (left(key) === 'none' && Date.now() - P.at > STALE))) loadState();
    var n = left(key), v;
    if (typeof n === 'number') v = n > 0 ? { dis: true, txt: 'Sign in ' + n + ' min', wait: true } : { dis: false, txt: label };
    else if (n === 'unknown') v = { dis: !seen, txt: label };
    else v = { dis: true, txt: label };
    if (v.wait && !inRefresh) later();
    return v;
  }
  function attrs(key, label, seen) { return ' data-w279polsign="' + E(key) + '" data-w279ok="' + E(label) + '"' + (seen ? ' data-w279seen="1"' : ''); }
  function opened(key) { return typeof left(key) === 'number'; }
  function later() { if (!P.soon) P.soon = setTimeout(function () { P.soon = null; refresh(); }, 0); }
  function refresh() {
    var live = false, any = false;
    [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-w279polsign]'), function (b) {
      any = true;
      if (b.getAttribute('aria-busy') === 'true') return;
      var v = view(b.getAttribute('data-w279polsign'), b.getAttribute('data-w279ok') || 'Sign', b.getAttribute('data-w279seen') === '1', true);
      if (b.textContent !== v.txt) b.textContent = v.txt;
      b.disabled = v.dis;
      if (v.wait && b.getClientRects().length) live = true;
    });
    if (live && !P.tick) P.tick = setInterval(refresh, TICK);
    else if (!live && P.tick) { clearInterval(P.tick); P.tick = null; }
    /* wave130's "open it first" lock on the Academy and wave105 buttons looks again (it steps aside when the database keeps the opens) */
    if (any && window.w130 && typeof window.w130.paint === 'function') { try { window.w130.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  /* a refused sign-off: read the opens again, so the count is the database's */
  function reload() { if (P.load) return P.load; return loadState(); }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var a = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w279polopen]');
    if (a) open(a.getAttribute('data-w279polopen'));
  }, true);
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(refresh, 400); });
  /* back in view: the opens may have changed on another device, so read them again (loadState redraws when they arrive) */
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { if (document.visibilityState !== 'visible') return; refresh(); if (document.querySelector('[data-w279polsign]')) reload(); });
  window.wa279Pol = { view: view, attrs: attrs, opened: opened, open: open, refresh: refresh, reload: reload, left: left, ticking: function () { return !!P.tick; } };

  /* ---------- the HR board's Academy trainings count ---------- */
  var A = { picks: null, at: 0, load: null };
  var NOTE = { 'Role training (60 days)': 'role60', 'Role training (90 days)': 'role90' };
  function loadPicks() {
    if (A.load) return A.load;
    if (typeof db === 'undefined' || !db || typeof db.from !== 'function') return Promise.resolve();
    A.load = Promise.resolve(db.from('academy_assignments').select('id,training_id,member_ids,note,training_progress(status)')
      .eq('active', true).in('note', Object.keys(NOTE))).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var m = {};
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (a) {
        var k = NOTE[a.note]; if (!k) return;
        var done = (a.training_progress || []).some(function (p) { return p && p.status === 'done'; });
        (a.member_ids || []).forEach(function (mid) { var x = m[String(mid)] = m[String(mid)] || { role60: [], role90: [] }; x[k].push({ training_id: a.training_id, done: done }); });
      });
      A.picks = m;
    }).catch(function () { A.picks = A.picks || {}; }).then(function () { A.at = Date.now(); A.load = null; fill(); });
    return A.load;
  }
  function count(onbId) {
    var W = (typeof WA37 !== 'undefined' && WA37) || null; if (!W || !A.picks) return null;
    var onb = (W.onbs || []).filter(function (o) { return String(o.id) === String(onbId); })[0]; if (!onb) return null;
    var tpl = {}; (W.tmpl || []).forEach(function (t) { tpl[String(t.id)] = t; });
    var mine = A.picks[String(onb.member_id)] || { role60: [], role90: [] }, d = 0, t = 0;
    (W.steps || []).forEach(function (s) {
      if (String(s.onboarding_id) !== String(onbId)) return;
      var tp = tpl[String(s.template_id)]; if (!tp || !(tp.training_id || tp.academy_path)) return;
      if (tp.academy_path === 'role60' || tp.academy_path === 'role90') {
        var ps = mine[tp.academy_path] || [];
        t += ps.length; d += s.done ? ps.length : ps.filter(function (p) { return p.done; }).length;
        return;
      }
      t++; if (s.done) d++;
    });
    return { done: d, total: t };
  }
  function acadText(onbId) {
    if (!A.picks || Date.now() - A.at > 60000) loadPicks();
    var c = count(onbId);
    return c && c.total ? 'Academy trainings ' + c.done + ' of ' + c.total : '';
  }
  function fill() {
    [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-w279acad]'), function (el) { var t = acadText(el.getAttribute('data-w279acad')); if (el.textContent !== t) el.textContent = t; });
  }
  (function () {
    if (document.getElementById('w279hookcss')) return;
    var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w279hookcss';
    st.textContent = '[data-w279acad]:empty{display:none;}';
    document.head.appendChild(st);
  })();
  window.wa279Acad = { text: acadText, count: count, fill: fill, load: loadPicks };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v274 Toronto Volunteers: the Toronto team's own app (05/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (05/11/26): "a volunteer manager for Toronto ... a robust, easy to use volunteer management tool for Toronto using
   Emil's design and animation skills for this new app in the hub ... It also should integrate with the CRM as well in the
   hub, like the Montreal volunteer manager does."

   Toronto runs a different kind of event from Montreal's Sunday servings: about once a month the team books a community
   kitchen (usually the Ralph Thornton Community Centre) for a time window, one crew cooks there, and the meals go to one or
   two organizations that sent a headcount and dietary needs; sometimes a second crew serves outdoors. Sama confirms people
   by hand, by email, phone or WhatsApp. This app (page #toronto) is built for that, on the same tables as Montreal, so a
   Toronto volunteer is the same CRM contact with the same servings count and history as anyone else.

   - Events (#toronto): the next event as a hero card, then the upcoming ones, then the past ones (folded). New event.
   - The event page (#toronto/e/<id>): Plan (the kitchen; who the meals go to), Crew (crews with targets, Add people from the
     Toronto pool, statuses, Take off the crew with Undo), Confirm (the counts, Called, Copy emails or phone numbers, the
     WhatsApp group link), Wrap up (meals delivered, who came, Mark this event completed).
   - Volunteers (#toronto/people): the Toronto pool from the CRM with search, Students / General, school, area and days.
   - Montreal's Volunteer Manager leaves Toronto out of its lists (the waVolLoad wrapper in z_wire.js).

   A Toronto event is volunteer_events kind 'meal', location 'Toronto' (every Montreal automation filters kind 'service').
   The data: build/data/wa274_toronto.sql (volunteer_event_recipients, wa274_crew_add, wa274_next_code, the page in every
   access profile; not applied yet: everything here works without it, saying plainly what waits for it).
   wave274b: the anticipated spend of an event (volunteer_events.budget, build/data/wa274b_budget.sql), set in New event and
   Edit the event, shown in Plan and on the Hub calendar (parts124); until the column exists the field is hidden.
   wave274 (one app, 06/11/26, Steel: "there should only be one CRM ... combine both Montreal and Toronto in one app but keep
   procedures made differently"): the launcher's tile is the Volunteer Manager with a Montreal | Toronto switch (parts153);
   this app is its Toronto city, its screens, words, stages and data unchanged.
   Not in this wave: sign-up forms, automatic emails or texts, recipes and groceries, Setup, page tours.
   window.w274: state, show, paint, load, loadPool, newEvent, stageOf, crewOf, pool, filterPeople, flush. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__wa274) return; window.__wa274 = true;
  var PURPLE = '#6F64AB';   /* Toronto's accent: the Hub purple (brand Possibility purple) */
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var OUT = 'cubic-bezier(.23,1,.32,1)', DRAWER = 'cubic-bezier(.32,.72,0,1)', MOVE = 'cubic-bezier(.77,0,.175,1)';

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).toLowerCase().trim(); }
  function fold(s) { try { return lower(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, ''); } catch (e) { return lower(s); } }
  function num(v, d) { if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return d; var n = Number(v); return isFinite(n) ? n : d; }
  function msgOf(e) { return String((e && (e.message || e.details || e.hint)) || e || 'unknown error'); }
  var NF = null;
  function nf(n) { try { NF = NF || new Intl.NumberFormat('en-CA'); var v = Number(n); return isFinite(v) ? NF.format(v) : String(n); } catch (e) { return String(n); } }   /* break274: 1,999 and 2,000, not 1999 */
  function plural(n, one, many) { return nf(n) + ' ' + (Number(n) === 1 ? one : (many || one + 's')); }
  /* wave274b: an amount in dollars, the Canadian way, cents only when there are cents: 250 and 1,250.50 (money() adds the $) */
  function amt(v) {
    var n = Number(v); if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '' || !isFinite(n)) return '';
    var c = Math.round(n * 100) % 100 !== 0;
    try { return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-CA', { minimumFractionDigits: c ? 2 : 0, maximumFractionDigits: c ? 2 : 0 }).format(n); } catch (e) { return c ? n.toFixed(2) : String(Math.round(n)); }
  }
  function money(v) { var s = amt(v); return s ? '$' + s : ''; }
  /* wave274b: an event's anticipated spend (volunteer_events.budget) as a number, or null when it has none */
  function budgetOf(e) { var v = e ? e.budget : null; if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return null; var n = Number(v); return isFinite(n) ? n : null; }
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function boardMode() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function me() {
    try { if (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) return lower(USER.email); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (typeof SESSION !== 'undefined' && SESSION && SESSION.user && SESSION.user.email) return lower(SESSION.user.email); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return 'staff';
  }
  function reduced() { try { return window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function audit(id, field, oldV, newV) {   /* the audit trail: best effort, never in the way of a save */
    try { if (typeof logChange === 'function' && String(oldV == null ? '' : oldV) !== String(newV == null ? '' : newV)) logChange('volunteer_event', id, field, oldV, newV); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- dates: plain days, counted in Toronto ---------- */
  var DOW = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'], MON = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
  var WEEK = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];
  function todayIso() {
    try { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date()); }
    catch (e) { var d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + d.getDate()).slice(-2); }
  }
  function dParse(iso) { var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/.exec(String(iso || '')); return m ? new Date(Date.UTC(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3], 12)) : null; }
  function daysTo(iso) { var a = dParse(iso), b = dParse(todayIso()); return a && b ? Math.round((a.getTime() - b.getTime()) / 864e5) : null; }
  function fmtDay(iso) { var d = dParse(iso); if (!d) return ''; var s = DOW[d.getUTCDay()] + ' ' + d.getUTCDate() + ' ' + MON[d.getUTCMonth()]; if (String(d.getUTCFullYear()) !== todayIso().slice(0, 4)) s += ' ' + d.getUTCFullYear(); return s; }
  function hm(t) { var m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/.exec(String(t || '')); return m ? ('0' + m[1]).slice(-2) + ':' + m[2] : ''; }
  function rel(n) { if (n == null) return ''; if (n === 0) return 'today'; if (n === 1) return 'tomorrow'; if (n === -1) return 'yesterday'; return n > 0 ? 'in ' + n + ' days' : -n + ' days ago'; }
  function stamp(iso) {   /* a moment, said in Toronto time: "Fri 25 Sep, 16:00" */
    var d = iso ? new Date(iso) : null; if (!d || isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
    try {
      var p = {}; new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hourCycle: 'h23' }).formatToParts(d).forEach(function (x) { p[x.type] = x.value; });
      return p.weekday.replace(/\.$/, '') + ' ' + p.day + ' ' + p.month.replace(/\.$/, '') + ', ' + p.hour + ':' + p.minute;
    } catch (e) { return DOW[d.getDay()] + ' ' + d.getDate() + ' ' + MON[d.getMonth()] + ', ' + ('0' + d.getHours()).slice(-2) + ':' + ('0' + d.getMinutes()).slice(-2); }
  }

  /* ---------- the stages: Plan, Crew, Confirm, Wrap up (from the date; a completed event is wrapped up) ---------- */
  var STAGES = [['plan', 'Plan'], ['crew', 'Crew'], ['confirm', 'Confirm'], ['wrap', 'Wrap up']];
  function stageOf(ev) {
    if (!ev) return 'plan';
    if (lower(ev.status) === 'completed') return 'wrap';
    var d = daysTo(ev.event_date);
    if (d == null) return 'plan';
    if (d < 0) return 'wrap';
    if (d <= 2) return 'confirm';
    if (d <= 10) return 'crew';
    return 'plan';
  }
  function stageIdx(k) { for (var i = 0; i < STAGES.length; i++) if (STAGES[i][0] === k) return i; return 0; }
  function stageLabel(k) { return STAGES[stageIdx(k)][1]; }

  /* ---------- the crews: the shared role keys, labelled the Toronto way ---------- */
  var ROLES = [['team_leader', 'Lead', 1], ['cook', 'Kitchen crew', 4], ['server', 'Serving crew', 4], ['driver', 'Delivery', 1], ['photographer', 'Photos', 0]];
  var ROLE_IN = { team_leader: 'the lead', cook: 'the kitchen crew', server: 'the serving crew', driver: 'delivery', photographer: 'photos' };
  function roleLabel(k) { for (var i = 0; i < ROLES.length; i++) if (ROLES[i][0] === k) return ROLES[i][1]; var s = String(k || 'Other').replace(/_/g, ' '); return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1); }
  function roleIn(k) { return ROLE_IN[k] || lower(roleLabel(k)); }

  /* ---------- the pool's rule (the same on the server's answer and in the browser) ---------- */
  var TAGS = ['toronto', 'toronto volunteer', 'toronto student'];
  var GTA = ['toronto', 'mississauga', 'brampton', 'scarborough', 'north york', 'etobicoke', 'east york', 'york', 'markham', 'vaughan', 'richmond hill', 'oakville', 'pickering', 'ajax'];
  var AREAS = ['Downtown Toronto', 'Midtown (Yonge-Eglinton)', 'North York', 'East York', 'York', 'Etobicoke', 'Scarborough'];
  var FKEYS = ['gta_area', 'days_available', 'age_16_plus', 'photo_consent'];

  /* ---------- state ---------- */
  var S = {
    loaded: false, loading: null, evErr: null, rosterErr: null,
    events: [], roster: [], rec: [], recOk: true, budgetOk: true,   /* wave274b: budgetOk is false until volunteer_events has its budget column */
    pool: null, poolBusy: null, poolErr: null, byCid: {}, byVid: {}, orgs: {},
    view: 'events', evId: null, key: '', fresh: true, pastOpen: false,
    f: { q: '', kind: 'all', school: '', area: '', days: [], sort: 'newest' },
    scope: 'all', waEdit: false, confirmRec: null, confirmDone: false, err: {},
    pend: {}, snackT: null
  };
  function evById(id) { for (var i = 0; i < S.events.length; i++) if (String(S.events[i].id) === String(id)) return S.events[i]; return null; }
  function rowById(id) { for (var i = 0; i < S.roster.length; i++) if (String(S.roster[i].id) === String(id)) return S.roster[i]; return null; }
  function rowsOf(evId) { return S.roster.filter(function (r) { return String(r.event_id) === String(evId) && !S.pend[r.id]; }); }
  function recsOf(evId) { return S.rec.filter(function (r) { return String(r.event_id) === String(evId); }).sort(function (a, b) { return num(a.sort, 0) - num(b.sort, 0) || num(a.id, 0) - num(b.id, 0); }); }
  function isTo(e) { return !!e && lower(e.kind) === 'meal' && /toronto/i.test(String(e.location || '')); }
  function titleOf(e) { return (e && String(e.title || '').trim()) || 'Free meal event'; }
  function isOpen(e) { var s = lower(e && e.status); return s !== 'completed' && s !== 'cancelled'; }

  /* a roster row's state: yes (confirmed), wait, no (can't come), off (taken off elsewhere) */
  function stOf(r) {
    var s = lower(r && r.status), c = lower(r && r.confirm_status);
    if (s === 'cancelled') return 'off';
    if (s === 'unavailable' || s === 'declined') return 'no';
    if (s === 'confirmed') return 'yes';
    if (c === 'declined') return 'no';
    if (c === 'confirmed') return 'yes';
    return 'wait';
  }
  var ST_LABEL = { yes: 'Confirmed', wait: 'Waiting', no: 'Can\u2019t come', off: 'Off the crew' };

  /* the crews of an event: each role's target (spot_counts first, else the defaults) and its people */
  function targetsOf(ev) {
    var sc = (ev && ev.spot_counts && typeof ev.spot_counts === 'object') ? ev.spot_counts : {};
    var out = {}; ROLES.forEach(function (r) { var v = num(sc[r[0]], null); out[r[0]] = v == null ? r[2] : Math.max(0, Math.round(v)); });
    return out;
  }
  function crewOf(ev) {
    if (!ev) return [];
    var t = targetsOf(ev), rows = rowsOf(ev.id).filter(function (r) { return stOf(r) !== 'off'; });
    var keys = ROLES.map(function (r) { return r[0]; });
    rows.forEach(function (r) { if (keys.indexOf(r.role) < 0) keys.push(r.role); });
    return keys.map(function (k) {
      var ppl = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.role === k; }).sort(function (a, b) { return num(a.sort, 0) - num(b.sort, 0) || num(a.id, 0) - num(b.id, 0); });
      var yes = ppl.filter(function (r) { return stOf(r) === 'yes'; }).length, wait = ppl.filter(function (r) { return stOf(r) === 'wait'; }).length;
      return { role: k, label: roleLabel(k), target: t[k] == null ? 0 : t[k], people: ppl, yes: yes, wait: wait, no: ppl.length - yes - wait };
    }).filter(function (c) { return c.target > 0 || c.people.length > 0; });
  }
  function totals(crews) {
    var o = { yes: 0, wait: 0, no: 0, target: 0, filled: 0 };
    crews.forEach(function (c) { o.yes += c.yes; o.wait += c.wait; o.no += c.no; o.target += c.target; o.filled += Math.min(c.target, c.yes + c.wait); });
    return o;
  }

    '#toronto .w274add', '#toronto .w274tog', '#toronto .w274seg button', '#w274sheet .w274seg button', '#toronto .w274dayb button', '#w274sheet .w274dayb button',

  var FOCUS = ['#toronto a', '#toronto button', '#toronto input', '#toronto select', '#toronto textarea', '#w274sheet a', '#w274sheet button', '#w274sheet input', '#w274sheet select', '#w274sheet textarea', '#w274menu .w274mi', '#w274snack button'];

  if (!document.getElementById('wa274css')) {

  /* ---------- the data: Toronto events (volunteer_events kind 'meal', location Toronto), their crews and who the meals go to ---------- */
  var EV_COLS = 'id,code,event_date,start_time,title,location,kind,status,notes,plan_note,planned_meals,kitchen_place,kitchen_contact,kitchen_shift,whatsapp_url,spot_counts,budget';
  var EV_COLS0 = EV_COLS.replace(/,budget$/, '');   /* wave274b: the same without the budget, until wa274b_budget.sql is applied */
  function evCols() { return S.budgetOk ? EV_COLS : EV_COLS0; }
  var RO_COLS = 'id,event_id,person_id,person_name,role,status,confirm_status,confirmed_at,notes,detail,sort';
  function missingFn(e) { var s = String((e && (e.code || '')) + ' ' + msgOf(e)); return /PGRST202|PGRST205|42883|42P01|could not find|does not exist|schema cache/i.test(s); }
  /* wave274b: a column the database does not have yet (Postgres 42703, PostgREST PGRST204) */
  function missingCol(e) { var s = String((e && (e.code || '')) + ' ' + msgOf(e)); return /42703|PGRST204|column .*does not exist|could not find the .*column/i.test(s); }
  async function loadEvents(force) {
    if (S.loading && !force) return S.loading;
    S.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var ask = function (cols) { return db.from('volunteer_events').select(cols).eq('kind', 'meal').order('event_date', { ascending: true }); };
        var r = await ask(EV_COLS);
        /* wave274b: without the budget column, the events again without it; the spend waits for the database update */
        if (r && r.error && missingCol(r.error)) { r = await ask(EV_COLS0); if (r && !r.error) S.budgetOk = false; }
        else if (r && !r.error) S.budgetOk = true;
        if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
        S.events = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(isTo);
        S.evErr = null;
      } catch (e) { S.evErr = msgOf(e); console.warn('wave274 events', e); }
      var ids = S.events.map(function (e) { return e.id; });
      if (ids.length) {
        var rr = await Promise.all([
          db.from('event_roster').select(RO_COLS).in('event_id', ids).order('sort', { ascending: true }),
          db.from('volunteer_event_recipients').select('*').in('event_id', ids).order('sort', { ascending: true })
        ].map(function (q) { return Promise.resolve(q).then(function (x) { return x; }, function (e) { return { error: e }; }); }));
        if (rr[0] && rr[0].error) { S.rosterErr = msgOf(rr[0].error); }
        else { S.rosterErr = null; S.roster = ((rr[0] && rr[0].data) || []).filter(function (x) { return x && ids.some(function (i) { return String(i) === String(x.event_id); }); }); }
        if (rr[1] && rr[1].error) { S.recOk = false; S.rec = []; }
        else { S.recOk = true; S.rec = ((rr[1] && rr[1].data) || []).filter(function (x) { return x && ids.some(function (i) { return String(i) === String(x.event_id); }); }); }
      } else {
        S.roster = []; S.rec = [];
        try { var p = await db.from('volunteer_event_recipients').select('id').limit(1); S.recOk = !(p && p.error); } catch (e) { S.recOk = false; }
      }
      S.loaded = true;
      reindexPool();
    })();
    try { await S.loading; } finally { S.loading = null; }
  }
  /* one event's crew again (after an add the server made) */
  async function reloadRoster(evId) {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('event_roster').select(RO_COLS).eq('event_id', evId).order('sort', { ascending: true });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var fresh = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.event_id) === String(evId); });
      S.roster = S.roster.filter(function (x) { return String(x.event_id) !== String(evId); }).concat(fresh);
      reindexPool();
    } catch (e) { console.warn('wave274 roster', e); }
  }

  /* ---------- the Toronto pool: the CRM's people in Toronto ----------
     Tagged toronto / toronto volunteer / toronto student, or living in the GTA, or on a Toronto crew. The server is asked for
     each, and the same rule is applied again here (the browser's word is what the page shows). */
  var C_COLS = 'id,full_name,first_name,last_name,primary_email,emails,phones,city,tags,kinds,volunteer_id,org_id,merged_into,created_at,last_activity_at,source';
  function torontoVids() { var v = {}; S.roster.forEach(function (r) { if (r.person_id != null) v[String(r.person_id)] = 1; }); return v; }
  function inPool(c, vids) {
    if (!c || c.merged_into != null) return false;
    if ((c.tags || []).some(function (t) { return TAGS.indexOf(lower(t)) >= 0; })) return true;
    if (GTA.indexOf(lower(c.city)) >= 0) return true;
    return c.volunteer_id != null && !!vids[String(c.volunteer_id)];
  }
  function jv(v) {
    if (typeof v === 'string') { var t = v.trim(); if (/^[\[{"]/.test(t)) { try { return jv(JSON.parse(t)); } catch (e) { return v; } } return v; }
    if (v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v)) { if ('value' in v) return jv(v.value); if ('values' in v) return jv(v.values); }
    return v;
  }
  function asText(v) { v = jv(v); if (v == null) return ''; if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(asText).filter(Boolean).join(', '); if (typeof v === 'object') return String(v.label || v.name || ''); return String(v).trim(); }
  function asList(v) {
    v = jv(v); if (v == null || v === '') return [];
    if (Array.isArray(v)) { var o = []; v.forEach(function (x) { asList(x).forEach(function (y) { o.push(y); }); }); return o; }
    if (typeof v === 'object') return [asText(v)].filter(Boolean);
    return String(v).split(/[,;\n\/|]+|\s+and\s+/i).map(function (s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
  }
  function asBool(v) { v = jv(v); if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v; var s = lower(asText(v)); if (!s) return null; return /^(yes|y|true|oui|1|i am|agree)/.test(s); }
  var DAYK = { mon: 0, lun: 0, tue: 1, mar: 1, wed: 2, mer: 2, thu: 3, jeu: 3, fri: 4, ven: 4, sat: 5, sam: 5, sun: 6, dim: 6 };
  function daysOf(v) {
    var on = [false, false, false, false, false, false, false];
    asList(v).forEach(function (s) {
      var f = fold(s);
      if (/weekend|fin de semaine/.test(f)) { on[5] = on[6] = true; return; }
      if (/weekday|semaine/.test(f)) { for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) on[i] = true; return; }
      if (/^(any|all|every|flexible|tous|toutes)/.test(f)) { for (var j = 0; j < 7; j++) on[j] = true; return; }
      var k = DAYK[f.slice(0, 3)]; if (k != null) on[k] = true;
    });
    return on;
  }
  function areaOf(c, raw) {
    var a = asText(raw); if (a) return a;
    var city = lower(c.city); for (var i = 0; i < AREAS.length; i++) if (lower(AREAS[i]) === city) return AREAS[i];
    return c.city ? String(c.city).trim() : '';
  }
  function chunks(a, n) { var o = []; for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i += n) o.push(a.slice(i, i + n)); return o; }
  async function q(p) { try { var r = await p; if (r && r.error) throw r.error; return (r && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { throw e; } }
  async function loadPool(force) {
    if (S.pool && !force) return S.pool;
    if (S.poolBusy) return S.poolBusy;
    S.poolBusy = (async function () {
      try {
        if (!S.loaded) await loadEvents();
        var vids = torontoVids(), vlist = Object.keys(vids).map(Number).filter(function (n) { return isFinite(n); });
        var tagq = TAGS.concat(TAGS.map(function (t) { return t.replace(/\b\w/g, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }); }));
        var asks = [
          db.from('crm_contacts').select(C_COLS).is('merged_into', null).overlaps('tags', tagq).limit(3000),
          db.from('crm_contacts').select(C_COLS).is('merged_into', null).or(GTA.map(function (c) { return 'city.ilike."' + c + '"'; }).join(',')).limit(3000)
        ];
        chunks(vlist, 150).forEach(function (part) { asks.push(db.from('crm_contacts').select(C_COLS).in('volunteer_id', part).limit(3000)); });
        var got = await Promise.all(asks.map(q));
        var by = {};
        got.forEach(function (rows) { rows.forEach(function (c) { if (c && c.id != null && !by[c.id]) by[c.id] = c; }); });
        var list = Object.keys(by).map(function (k) { return by[k]; }).filter(function (c) { return inPool(c, vids); });
        /* schools, and the answers (area, days, age, consent) */
        var orgIds = list.map(function (c) { return c.org_id; }).filter(function (x, i, a) { return x != null && a.indexOf(x) === i; });
        var orgs = {}, fields = {}, vals = [];
        try { if (orgIds.length) (await q(db.from('volunteer_orgs').select('id,name,short').in('id', orgIds))).forEach(function (o) { orgs[o.id] = o; }); } catch (e) { console.warn('wave274 schools', e); }
        try {
          var fs = await q(db.from('crm_fields').select('id,key').in('key', FKEYS));
          fs.forEach(function (f) { if (FKEYS.indexOf(f.key) >= 0) fields[f.id] = f.key; });
          var fids = Object.keys(fields).map(Number);
          if (fids.length && list.length) {
            var parts = await Promise.all(chunks(list.map(function (c) { return c.id; }), 150).map(function (ids) { return q(db.from('crm_contact_fields').select('contact_id,field_id,value').in('field_id', fids).in('contact_id', ids)); }));
            parts.forEach(function (p) { vals = vals.concat(p); });
          }
        } catch (e) { console.warn('wave274 answers', e); }
        var ans = {};
        vals.forEach(function (v) { var k = fields[v.field_id]; if (!k) return; (ans[v.contact_id] = ans[v.contact_id] || {})[k] = v.value; });
        S.orgs = orgs;
        S.pool = list.map(function (c) { return person(c, ans[c.id] || {}); });
        S.poolErr = null;
      } catch (e) { S.poolErr = msgOf(e); S.pool = S.pool || []; console.warn('wave274 pool', e); }
      reindexPool();
      return S.pool;
    })();
    try { return await S.poolBusy; } finally { S.poolBusy = null; }
  }
  function person(c, a) {
    var tags = (c.tags || []).map(lower), org = c.org_id != null ? S.orgs[c.org_id] : null;
    var student = c.org_id != null || tags.indexOf('toronto student') >= 0;
    var emails = [c.primary_email].concat(c.emails || []).filter(function (x, i, l) { return x && l.indexOf(x) === i; });
    /* break274: a contact with no name is shown by its email (or phone), so two of them in a list can be told apart */
    var name = String(String(c.full_name || '').trim() || [c.first_name, c.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim() || emails[0] || (c.phones || [])[0] || 'No name').trim();
    return {
      id: c.id, c: c, name: name, email: emails[0] || '', phone: (c.phones || [])[0] || '',
      student: student, school: org ? String(org.short || org.name || '') : '', schoolName: org ? String(org.name || org.short || '') : '', schoolId: org ? org.id : null,
      area: areaOf(c, a.gta_area), days: daysOf(a.days_available), age16: asBool(a.age_16_plus), photo: asBool(a.photo_consent),
      created: c.created_at || '', evs: [], count: 0, last: '', next: ''
    };
  }
  /* each person's Toronto events, and the maps from a roster row to its person */
  function reindexPool() {
    S.byCid = {}; S.byVid = {};
    (S.pool || []).forEach(function (p) { S.byCid[p.id] = p; if (p.c.volunteer_id != null) S.byVid[String(p.c.volunteer_id)] = p; p.evs = []; });
    var t = todayIso();
    S.roster.forEach(function (r) {
      if (stOf(r) === 'off' || S.pend[r.id]) return;
      var p = personOf(r), e = evById(r.event_id); if (!p || !e) return;
      p.evs.push({ ev: e, row: r });
    });
    (S.pool || []).forEach(function (p) {
      var ds = p.evs.filter(function (x) { return lower(x.ev.status) !== 'cancelled'; }).map(function (x) { return String(x.ev.event_date); }).sort();
      p.count = ds.length;
      var past = ds.filter(function (d) { return d < t; }), fut = ds.filter(function (d) { return d >= t; });
      p.last = past.length ? past[past.length - 1] : ''; p.next = fut.length ? fut[0] : '';
    });
  }
  function personOf(r) {
    if (!r) return null;
    var cid = r.detail && r.detail.contact_id;
    return (cid != null && S.byCid[cid]) || (r.person_id != null && S.byVid[String(r.person_id)]) || null;
  }
  function onEvent(p, evId) {
    return S.roster.some(function (r) { return String(r.event_id) === String(evId) && !S.pend[r.id] && stOf(r) !== 'off' && ((r.detail && String(r.detail.contact_id) === String(p.id)) || (p.c.volunteer_id != null && String(r.person_id) === String(p.c.volunteer_id))); });
  }
  function contactOfRow(r) {
    var p = personOf(r), d = (r && r.detail) || {};
    return { email: (p && p.email) || d.email || '', phone: (p && p.phone) || d.phone || '' };
  }

  /* ---------- writes (each one optimistic, put back with a plain sentence when it fails) ---------- */
  async function must(p) { var r = await p; if (r && r.error) throw r.error; return r; }
  function nextCodeLocal(date) {
    var yy = String(date || todayIso()).slice(2, 4), n = 0, re = new RegExp('^TO-' + yy + '-(\\d+)$');
    S.events.forEach(function (e) { var m = re.exec(String(e.code || '')); if (m) n = Math.max(n, Number(m[1])); });
    return 'TO-' + yy + '-' + (n + 1);
  }
  async function nextCode(date) {
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa274_next_code', { p_date: date });
      if (r && !r.error && typeof r.data === 'string' && /^TO-\d{2}-\d+$/.test(r.data)) return r.data;
    } catch (e) { /* the SQL is not applied yet */ }
    return nextCodeLocal(date);
  }
  async function createEvent(f) {
    var row = { code: await nextCode(f.event_date), event_date: f.event_date, start_time: f.start_time || '10:00', title: f.title || 'Free meal event', location: 'Toronto', kind: 'meal', status: 'planned',
      notes: f.notes || null, planned_meals: f.planned_meals, kitchen_place: f.kitchen_place || null, kitchen_shift: f.kitchen_shift || null };
    if (S.budgetOk && f.budget != null) row.budget = f.budget;   /* wave274b: never sent before the column exists */
    var r = await must(db.from('volunteer_events').insert(row).select(evCols()).single());
    var made = (r && r.data && r.data.id != null) ? r.data : null;
    if (!made) { await loadEvents(true); made = S.events.filter(function (e) { return e.code === row.code; })[0] || null; }
    else if (!evById(made.id)) S.events.push(Object.assign({}, row, made));
    try { if (typeof logChange === 'function') logChange('volunteer_event', made ? made.id : row.code, 'created', null, row.code + ' ' + row.title + ' on ' + row.event_date); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    eventsChanged(made ? made.id : null);
    return made;
  }
  async function saveEvent(ev, patch) {
    if (!S.budgetOk && 'budget' in patch) { patch = Object.assign({}, patch); delete patch.budget; if (!Object.keys(patch).length) return; }   /* wave274b */
    var old = {}; Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { old[k] = ev[k]; });
    Object.assign(ev, patch);
    try { await must(db.from('volunteer_events').update(patch).eq('id', ev.id)); }
    catch (e) { Object.assign(ev, old); throw e; }
    Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { audit(ev.id, k, old[k] && typeof old[k] === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(old[k]) : old[k], patch[k] && typeof patch[k] === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(patch[k]) : patch[k]); });
    eventsChanged(ev.id);
  }
  /* wave274b: the Hub calendar (wave124) keeps the events it has read; this tells it to read them again on its next draw */
  function eventsChanged(id) { try { document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('wa:volevents', { detail: { id: id } })); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  var ST_PATCH = {
    yes: function () { return { status: 'Confirmed', confirm_status: 'confirmed', confirmed_at: new Date().toISOString() }; },
    no: function () { return { status: 'Unavailable', confirm_status: 'declined' }; },
    wait: function () { return { status: 'TBD', confirm_status: null }; }
  };
  async function setStatus(r, st) {
    var patch = ST_PATCH[st](), old = { status: r.status, confirm_status: r.confirm_status, confirmed_at: r.confirmed_at };
    Object.assign(r, patch);
    try { await must(db.from('event_roster').update(patch).eq('id', r.id)); }
    catch (e) { Object.assign(r, old); throw e; }
    try { if (typeof logChange === 'function' && old.status !== patch.status) logChange('event_roster', r.id, 'status', old.status, patch.status); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function setCalled(r, on) {
    var d = Object.assign({}, r.detail || {}), old = r.detail;
    if (on) { d.called_at = new Date().toISOString(); d.called_by = me(); } else { delete d.called_at; delete d.called_by; }
    r.detail = d;
    try { await must(db.from('event_roster').update({ detail: d }).eq('id', r.id)); }
    catch (e) { r.detail = old; throw e; }
  }
  /* a person onto a crew: the database function first (it gives a contact a volunteer record when it has none) */
  async function crewAdd(ev, p, role) {
    var res = null;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa274_crew_add', { p_event: ev.id, p_contact: p.id, p_role: role });
      if (r && r.error && !missingFn(r.error)) return { ok: false, err: msgOf(r.error) };
      if (r && !r.error && r.data && r.data.ok) res = r.data;
    } catch (e) { if (!missingFn(e)) return { ok: false, err: msgOf(e) }; }
    if (res) {
      if (res.person_id != null && p.c.volunteer_id == null) p.c.volunteer_id = res.person_id;
      if (!res.already && res.roster_id != null && !rowById(res.roster_id)) {
        S.roster.push({ id: res.roster_id, event_id: ev.id, person_id: res.person_id, person_name: p.name, role: role, status: 'TBD', confirm_status: null, confirmed_at: null, notes: null, sort: 9999,
          detail: { source: 'toronto', contact_id: p.id, added_by: me(), email: p.email || null, phone: p.phone || null } });
      } else if (res.already && !rowById(res.roster_id)) await reloadRoster(ev.id);
      return { ok: true, already: !!res.already };
    }
    /* the database update is not applied yet: a person with a volunteer record goes straight onto the crew */
    if (p.c.volunteer_id == null) return { ok: false, needs: true };
    if (onEvent(p, ev.id)) return { ok: true, already: true };
    var row = { event_id: ev.id, person_id: p.c.volunteer_id, person_name: p.c.full_name || p.name, role: role, status: 'TBD', detail: { source: 'toronto', contact_id: p.id } };
    try {
      var ins = await must(db.from('event_roster').insert(row).select(RO_COLS).single());
      var got = ins && ins.data;
      if (got && got.id != null) S.roster.push(Object.assign({ confirm_status: null, confirmed_at: null, notes: null, sort: 9999 }, row, got));
      else await reloadRoster(ev.id);
      return { ok: true, already: false };
    } catch (e) { return { ok: false, err: msgOf(e) }; }
  }
  /* who the meals go to */
  async function saveRecipient(ev, id, f) {
    if (id) {
      var r = S.rec.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!r) throw new Error('That organization is not on this event any more.');
      var old = Object.assign({}, r);
      Object.assign(r, f);
      try { await must(db.from('volunteer_event_recipients').update(f).eq('id', r.id)); } catch (e) { Object.keys(r).forEach(function (k) { delete r[k]; }); Object.assign(r, old); throw e; }
      return r;
    }
    var row = Object.assign({ event_id: ev.id, sort: recsOf(ev.id).reduce(function (m, x) { return Math.max(m, num(x.sort, 0)); }, 0) + 1 }, f);
    var ins = await must(db.from('volunteer_event_recipients').insert(row).select('*').single());
    var got = ins && ins.data;
    if (got && got.id != null) { S.rec.push(Object.assign({}, row, got)); return got; }
    var re = await db.from('volunteer_event_recipients').select('*').eq('event_id', ev.id).order('sort', { ascending: true });
    if (re && !re.error) S.rec = S.rec.filter(function (x) { return String(x.event_id) !== String(ev.id); }).concat((re.data || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.event_id) === String(ev.id); }));
    return row;
  }
  async function removeRecipient(id) {
    var r = S.rec.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!r) return;
    var keep = S.rec.slice();
    S.rec = S.rec.filter(function (x) { return x !== r; });
    try { await must(db.from('volunteer_event_recipients').delete().eq('id', r.id)); } catch (e) { S.rec = keep; throw e; }
  }
  /* taking someone off the crew waits 5 seconds for Undo, then deletes the row */
  function takeOff(r, onDone) {
    if (!r || S.pend[r.id]) return;
    var p = { row: r, t: null, done: onDone };
    p.t = setTimeout(function () { commitOff(r.id); }, 5000);
    S.pend[r.id] = p;
    reindexPool();
  }
  async function commitOff(id) {
    var p = S.pend[id]; if (!p) return;
    clearTimeout(p.t); p.t = null;
    try {
      await must(db.from('event_roster').delete().eq('id', id));
      S.roster = S.roster.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(id); });
      delete S.pend[id];
      try { if (typeof logChange === 'function') logChange('event_roster', id, 'removed', (p.row.person_name || '') + ' (' + p.row.role + ')', null); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      reindexPool();
      if (p.done) p.done(null);
    } catch (e) { delete S.pend[id]; reindexPool(); if (p.done) p.done(e); }
  }
  function undoOff(id) { var p = S.pend[id]; if (!p) return false; clearTimeout(p.t); delete S.pend[id]; reindexPool(); return true; }
  function flushOff() { Object.keys(S.pend).forEach(function (id) { if (S.pend[id] && S.pend[id].t) commitOff(id); }); }

  /* ---------- small drawing helpers ---------- */
  var IC = {
    plus: '<path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/>',
    back: '<path d="m10 6-6 6 6 6"/><path d="M4 12h16"/>',
    chev: '<path d="m9 6 6 6-6 6"/>',
    dots: '<circle cx="5.5" cy="12" r="1.6"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="1.6"/><circle cx="18.5" cy="12" r="1.6"/>',
    lock: '<rect x="5" y="10.5" width="14" height="10" rx="2.2"/><path d="M8.5 10.5V8a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 7 0v2.5"/>',
    copy: '<rect x="8.5" y="8.5" width="11" height="11" rx="2.2"/><path d="M15.5 8.5V6.2a1.7 1.7 0 0 0-1.7-1.7H6.2a1.7 1.7 0 0 0-1.7 1.7v7.6a1.7 1.7 0 0 0 1.7 1.7h2.3"/>',
    check: '<path d="m5 12.5 4.5 4.5L19 7.5"/>',
    x: '<path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6 6 18"/>',
    search: '<circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5"/><path d="m16 16 4.5 4.5"/>',
    ext: '<path d="M14 4.5h5.5V10"/><path d="M19.5 4.5 11 13"/><path d="M17.5 13.5v4.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1-1.5 1.5H6a1.5 1.5 0 0 1-1.5-1.5V8A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 6 6.5h4.5"/>',
    person: '<circle cx="12" cy="8.5" r="3.6"/><path d="M5 20c.8-3.8 3.6-6 7-6s6.2 2.2 7 6"/>',
    pen: '<path d="m4 20 1.2-4.6L15.8 4.8a2.3 2.3 0 0 1 3.3 3.3L8.6 18.8z"/><path d="m14 6.6 3.3 3.3"/>',
    off: '<path d="M5 12h14"/>',
    wait: '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8"/><path d="M12 7.5V12l3 2"/>',
    cal: '<rect x="4" y="5.5" width="16" height="14" rx="2.5"/><path d="M4 10h16M8.5 3.5v4M15.5 3.5v4"/>'
  };
  function ic(n) { return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="' + (n === 'dots' ? 'currentColor' : 'none') + '" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">' + (IC[n] || '') + '</svg>'; }
  function andJoin(a) { a = a.filter(Boolean); if (a.length < 2) return a.join(''); return a.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + a[a.length - 1]; }
  function cap(s) { s = String(s || ''); return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1); }
  function ratio(a, b) { if (!b) return a > 0 ? 1 : 0; return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, a / b)); }
  function barHtml(c) { return '<span class="w274bar" aria-hidden="true"><i class="w" data-x="' + ratio(c.yes + c.wait, c.target).toFixed(3) + '"></i><i class="c" data-x="' + ratio(c.yes, c.target).toFixed(3) + '"></i></span>'; }
  /* break274: "3 of 4"; a crew with no target and someone on it reads "1", not "1 of 0" */
  function ofT(n, t) { return t ? nf(n) + ' of ' + nf(t) : nf(n); }
  function barRow(c) { return '<div class="w274br"><span class="l">' + E(c.label) + '</span>' + barHtml(c) + '<span class="n">' + ofT(c.yes + c.wait, c.target) + '</span></div>'; }
  /* break274: one grid for a card's bars, so they keep one length when a number is wide ("1,200 of 1,200") */
  function barRows(crews) { return crews.length ? '<div class="w274brs">' + crews.map(barRow).join('') + '</div>' : ''; }
  /* break274: no crew places and no one confirmed is said in words, not "0 of 0 confirmed" */
  function confLine(t, cls) { return t.target ? '<p class="' + cls + '"><b>' + nf(t.yes) + '</b> of ' + nf(t.target) + ' confirmed</p>' : t.yes ? '<p class="' + cls + '"><b>' + nf(t.yes) + '</b> confirmed</p>' : '<p class="' + cls + ' none">No crew places set yet</p>'; }
  /* the bars: they fill from the left on a page's first draw, and otherwise just stand at their value */
  function bars(scope, grow) {
    if (!scope) return;
    var els = Array.prototype.slice.call(scope.querySelectorAll('.w274bar i[data-x]'));
    if (!els.length) return;
    var set = function () { els.forEach(function (i) { i.style.transform = 'scaleX(' + i.getAttribute('data-x') + ')'; }); };
    if (grow && !reduced()) { void scope.offsetWidth; requestAnimationFrame(function () { requestAnimationFrame(set); }); return; }
    var bs = Array.prototype.slice.call(scope.querySelectorAll('.w274bar'));
    bs.forEach(function (b) { b.classList.add('w274now'); }); set(); void scope.offsetWidth; bs.forEach(function (b) { b.classList.remove('w274now'); });
  }
  /* a list rises in, 40 ms apart, on a page's first draw only.
     break274: only the rows on screen rise, and the stagger stops at 280 ms (the 8th row), so 300 rows settle as fast as 8;
     the rows below the fold are simply there, never invisible when someone scrolls to them. */
  function rise(scope, sel) {
    if (!scope) return;
    var vh = window.innerHeight || 800;
    var items = Array.prototype.slice.call(scope.querySelectorAll(sel)).filter(function (el) { var r = el.getBoundingClientRect(); return r.bottom > 0 && r.top < vh; });
    if (!items.length) return;
    var still = reduced();
    items.forEach(function (el) { el.classList.add('w274st', 'w274pre'); });
    void scope.offsetWidth;
    items.forEach(function (el, i) { el.style.transitionDelay = still ? '0ms' : Math.min(i, 7) * 40 + 'ms'; el.classList.remove('w274pre'); });
    setTimeout(function () { items.forEach(function (el) { el.classList.remove('w274st'); el.style.transitionDelay = ''; }); }, 600);
  }
  function errLine(key, text) { return '<p class="w274err" data-w274err="' + E(key) + '" aria-live="polite">' + E(text || S.err[key] || '') + '</p>'; }
  function setErr(key, text) {
    S.err[key] = text || '';
    var els = document.querySelectorAll('[data-w274err="' + key + '"]');
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(els, function (el) { el.textContent = text || ''; });
  }
  function kitchenLine(e) { var k = String(e.kitchen_place || '').trim(), s = String(e.kitchen_shift || '').trim(); return k ? k + (s ? ', ' + s : '') : (s ? 'Kitchen not set, ' + s : 'Kitchen not booked yet'); }
  function mealsFor(e) {
    var planned = num(e.planned_meals, null);
    if (!S.recOk) return planned ? plural(planned, 'meal') + ' planned' : '';
    var rs = recsOf(e.id);
    if (!rs.length) return (planned ? plural(planned, 'meal') + ' planned. ' : '') + 'Who the meals go to is not set yet.';
    var named = rs.filter(function (r) { return !r.private; }), priv = rs.filter(function (r) { return r.private; });
    var one = function (r) { return String(r.name || '') + (r.meals != null ? ' (' + nf(r.meals) + ')' : ''); };
    /* break274: more than three organizations: the first two by name and how many more (the event page lists them all) */
    if (rs.length > 3) { var shown = named.slice(0, 2); return 'Meals for: ' + shown.map(one).join(', ') + (shown.length ? ' and ' : '') + plural(rs.length - shown.length, shown.length ? 'more organization' : 'organization', shown.length ? 'more organizations' : 'organizations'); }
    var parts = named.map(one);
    if (priv.length) { var pm = priv.reduce(function (s, r) { return s + num(r.meals, 0); }, 0); parts.push(plural(priv.length, 'organization') + ', not named publicly' + (pm ? ' (' + nf(pm) + ')' : '')); }
    return 'Meals for: ' + andJoin(parts);
  }
  function delivered(e) { var rs = recsOf(e.id).filter(function (r) { return r.meals_delivered != null && r.meals_delivered !== ''; }); return rs.length ? rs.reduce(function (s, r) { return s + num(r.meals_delivered, 0); }, 0) : null; }
  function relLine(e) { var d = daysTo(e.event_date), t = hm(e.start_time); return cap(rel(d)) + (t ? ', at ' + t : ''); }

  /* ---------- the masthead (wave118's): the page's title, its line, the way back. Its primary action is the app's own New event
     (APPS.toronto.actions): wave197 shows it at the masthead's right on a computer, the app bar shows it on a phone ---------- */
  function mastEl() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('toronto'); if (!panel) return null;
    var m = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!m) { m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'w118mast'; m.setAttribute('data-w118', 'toronto'); m.innerHTML = '<div class="crumb">Operations Toronto</div><h1>Toronto Volunteers</h1><p></p>'; panel.insertBefore(m, panel.firstChild); }
    else if (panel.firstElementChild !== m) panel.insertBefore(m, panel.firstChild);
    return m;
  }
  function setMast(title, blurb, o) {
    o = o || {};
    var m = mastEl(); if (!m) return;
    var h1 = m.querySelector('h1'), p = m.querySelector(':scope > p');
    var hh = E(title) + (o.count != null ? ' <span class="w274cnt">' + E(nf(o.count)) + '</span>' : ''), want = title + (o.count != null ? ' ' + nf(o.count) : '');
    if (h1 && h1.textContent !== want) h1.innerHTML = hh;   /* the live text, so a rename by another wave is put right */
    if (p && p.textContent !== blurb) p.textContent = blurb;
    var b = m.querySelector(':scope > .w274back');
    if (o.back && !b) { b = document.createElement('a'); b.className = 'w274back'; b.href = '#toronto'; b.innerHTML = ic('back') + 'All events'; m.insertBefore(b, h1 || m.firstChild); }
    else if (!o.back && b) b.parentNode.removeChild(b);
  }

  /* ---------- Events (#toronto) ---------- */
  function heroHtml(e) {
    var st = stageOf(e), crews = crewOf(e), t = totals(crews);
    return '<a class="w274hero" href="#toronto/e/' + E(e.id) + '" data-w274ev="' + E(e.id) + '">' +
      '<div class="w274hl"><div class="w274ht"><span class="w274date">' + E(fmtDay(e.event_date)) + '</span><span class="w274chip stg">' + E(stageLabel(st)) + '</span></div>' +
      '<p class="w274rel">' + E(relLine(e)) + '</p>' +
      '<h3>' + E(titleOf(e)) + '</h3>' +
      '<p class="w274meta">' + E(kitchenLine(e)) + '</p>' +
      '<p class="w274meta">' + E(mealsFor(e)) + '</p></div>' +
      '<div class="w274hr">' + confLine(t, 'w274conf') + barRows(crews) +
      (crews.length ? '<p class="w274leg" aria-hidden="true"><span><i></i>Confirmed</span><span><i class="w"></i>Waiting</span></p>' : '') + '</div></a>';
  }
  function cardHtml(e) {
    var off = lower(e.status) === 'cancelled', st = stageOf(e), crews = crewOf(e), t = totals(crews);
    return '<a class="w274card' + (off ? ' off' : '') + '" href="#toronto/e/' + E(e.id) + '" data-w274ev="' + E(e.id) + '">' +
      '<div class="w274ct"><span class="w274cd">' + E(fmtDay(e.event_date)) + '</span>' + (off ? '<span class="w274chip st-cancelled">Cancelled</span>' : '<span class="w274chip stg">' + E(stageLabel(st)) + '</span>') + '</div>' +
      '<p class="w274rel">' + E(relLine(e)) + '</p>' +
      '<h3>' + E(titleOf(e)) + '</h3>' +
      '<p class="w274meta">' + E(kitchenLine(e)) + '</p>' +
      '<p class="w274meta">' + E(mealsFor(e)) + '</p>' +
      (off ? '' : confLine(t, 'w274cf') + barRows(crews)) + '</a>';
  }
  function pastRow(e) {
    var s = lower(e.status), d = delivered(e);
    var m = s === 'cancelled' ? 'Cancelled' : d != null ? plural(d, 'meal') + ' delivered' : s === 'completed' ? 'Meals not counted' : 'Not wrapped up yet';
    return '<a class="w274prow" href="#toronto/e/' + E(e.id) + '" data-w274ev="' + E(e.id) + '"><span class="d">' + E(fmtDay(e.event_date)) + '</span><span class="t">' + E(titleOf(e)) + '</span><span class="m">' + E(m) + '</span></a>';
  }
  function eventsHtml() {
    if (!S.loaded) return '<p class="w274quiet" role="status">Loading the Toronto events\u2026</p>';
    var t = todayIso();
    var all = S.events.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.event_date) < String(b.event_date) ? -1 : String(a.event_date) > String(b.event_date) ? 1 : num(a.id, 0) - num(b.id, 0); });
    var head = S.evErr ? '<p class="w274err" role="alert">Couldn\u2019t load the Toronto events. Check your connection, then reload the page.</p>' : '';
    if (!all.length) return head + '<div class="w274empty"><h2>No Toronto events yet.</h2><p>Plan the first one: pick a date and a kitchen, then build the crew.</p><button type="button" class="w274btn pri" data-w274new="1">' + ic('plus') + 'New event</button></div>';
    var up = all.filter(function (e) { return String(e.event_date) >= t && isOpen(e); });
    var offUp = all.filter(function (e) { return String(e.event_date) >= t && lower(e.status) === 'cancelled'; });
    var past = all.filter(function (e) { return up.indexOf(e) < 0 && offUp.indexOf(e) < 0; }).reverse();
    var next = up[0], rest = up.slice(1).concat(offUp);
    var html = head + '<section class="w274blk" aria-labelledby="w274hnext"><h2 class="w274kick" id="w274hnext">Next event</h2>' +
      (next ? heroHtml(next) : '<div class="w274empty"><p>No upcoming event yet.</p><button type="button" class="w274btn pri" data-w274new="1">' + ic('plus') + 'New event</button></div>') + '</section>';
    if (rest.length) html += '<section class="w274blk" aria-labelledby="w274hup"><h2 class="w274h2" id="w274hup">Upcoming</h2><div class="w274grid">' + rest.map(cardHtml).join('') + '</div></section>';
    if (past.length) html += '<section class="w274blk" aria-labelledby="w274hpast"><h2 class="w274h2" id="w274hpast"><button type="button" class="w274fold" data-w274past="1" aria-expanded="' + (S.pastOpen ? 'true' : 'false') + '" aria-controls="w274pastl">' + ic('chev') + 'Past events (' + past.length + ')</button></h2>' +
      '<div id="w274pastl"' + (S.pastOpen ? '' : ' hidden') + '>' + past.map(pastRow).join('') + '</div></section>';
    return html;
  }
  function paintEvents(root, fresh) {
    setMast('Toronto events', 'Cook together, then bring the meals to the people who need them.', {});
    root.innerHTML = eventsHtml();
    bars(root, fresh);
    if (fresh) rise(root, '.w274hero, .w274card');
  }

  /* ---------- the event page (#toronto/e/<id>): Plan, Crew, Confirm, Wrap up ---------- */
  var ADD_TO = { team_leader: 'Add a lead', cook: 'Add to the kitchen crew', server: 'Add to the serving crew', driver: 'Add someone for delivery', photographer: 'Add a photographer' };
  var ST_NAME = { planned: 'Planned', completed: 'Completed', cancelled: 'Cancelled' };
  function curEv() { return S.view === 'event' ? evById(S.evId) : null; }
  function mastLine(e) { var t = hm(e.start_time), k = String(e.kitchen_place || '').trim(); return fmtDay(e.event_date) + (t ? ' at ' + t : '') + (k ? ', ' + k : ''); }
  /* the current stage's main action is the page's primary one */
  function pri(e, k) { return stageOf(e) === k && isOpen(e) ? ' pri' : ''; }
  function val(v) { v = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); return v ? E(v) : '<span class="none">Not set yet</span>'; }
  function secHead(k, e, acts) {
    return '<div class="w274sh"><span class="w274no" aria-hidden="true">' + (stageIdx(k) + 1) + '</span><h2 id="w274h-' + k + '" tabindex="-1">' + E(stageLabel(k)) + '</h2>' +
      (stageOf(e) === k ? '<span class="w274chip stg">Now</span>' : '') + (acts ? '<div class="w274sha">' + acts + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function headHtml(e) {
    var s = lower(e.status) || 'planned';
    return '<div class="w274ehl"><span class="w274chip st-' + E(s) + '">' + E(ST_NAME[s] || cap(s)) + '</span>' + (e.code ? '<span class="w274code">' + E(e.code) + '</span>' : '') + '<span>' + E(cap(rel(daysTo(e.event_date)))) + '</span></div>' +
