      '<div class="w274eha"><button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274edit="1">' + ic('pen') + 'Edit</button><button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274more="1" aria-haspopup="menu" aria-expanded="false">More</button></div>';
  }
  function stepsHtml(e) {
    var cur = stageOf(e);
    return '<nav class="w274steps" aria-label="Stages of this event"><span class="w274mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>' +
      STAGES.map(function (s) { return '<button type="button" data-w274go="' + s[0] + '"' + (s[0] === cur ? ' aria-current="step"' : '') + '>' + E(s[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</nav>';
  }
  function sumHtml(e) {
    var s = lower(e.status);
    if (s === 'cancelled') return '<p class="w274cx" role="status">This event was cancelled. More, then Plan this event again, brings it back.</p>';
    if (s !== 'completed') return '';
    var d = delivered(e), rs = recsOf(e.id), came = rowsOf(e.id).filter(function (r) { return stOf(r) === 'yes'; }).length;
    return '<p class="w274done" role="status"><b>Completed.</b> ' + (d != null ? E(plural(d, 'meal')) + ' delivered' + (rs.length ? ' to ' + E(plural(rs.length, 'organization')) : '') + '. ' : '') + E(plural(came, 'person', 'people')) + ' came.</p>';
  }
  /* Plan: the kitchen, and who the meals go to */
  function dietChips(d) {
    d = (d && typeof d === 'object') ? d : {}; var o = [];
    if (d.halal) o.push('Halal'); if (d.nut_free) o.push('Nut-free');
    if (num(d.vegetarian, 0) > 0) o.push(num(d.vegetarian, 0) + ' vegetarian'); if (num(d.vegan, 0) > 0) o.push(num(d.vegan, 0) + ' vegan');
    if (String(d.other || '').trim()) o.push(String(d.other).trim());
    return o;
  }
  function recHtml(r) {
    var chips = dietChips(r.dietary), id = E(r.id);
    var contact = [r.contact_name ? E(r.contact_name) : '', r.contact_phone ? '<a href="tel:' + E(String(r.contact_phone).replace(/[^\d+]/g, '')) + '">' + E(r.contact_phone) + '</a>' : '', r.contact_email ? '<a href="mailto:' + E(r.contact_email) + '">' + E(r.contact_email) + '</a>' : ''].filter(Boolean);
    var drop = [r.drop_off, r.address].map(function (x) { return String(x || '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
    return '<article class="w274rc" data-w274rid="' + id + '"><div class="w274rct"><h4>' + E(r.name) + (r.private ? '<span class="w274chip">' + ic('lock') + 'Not named publicly</span>' : '') + '</h4>' +
      (r.meals != null ? '<span class="w274ml"><b>' + E(nf(r.meals)) + '</b> ' + (Number(r.meals) === 1 ? 'meal' : 'meals') + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +   /* break274: "1 meal" */
      (chips.length ? '<div class="w274diet" role="list" aria-label="Dietary needs">' + chips.map(function (c) { return '<span class="w274chip" role="listitem">' + E(c) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      (drop.length ? '<p class="w274meta">Drop-off: ' + E(drop.join(', ')) + '</p>' : '') +
      (contact.length ? '<p class="w274meta">Contact: ' + contact.join(', ') + '</p>' : '') +
      (r.note ? '<p class="w274meta">' + E(r.note) + '</p>' : '') +
      (String(S.confirmRec) === String(r.id)
        ? '<div class="w274cfm" role="group" aria-label="Remove ' + E(r.name) + '"><span>Remove ' + E(r.name) + ' from this event?</span><button type="button" class="w274btn sm dng pri" data-w274recy="' + id + '">Remove</button><button type="button" class="w274btn sm" data-w274recn="' + id + '">Keep</button></div>'
        : '<div class="w274rca"><button type="button" class="w274btn sm" data-w274rec="' + id + '">' + ic('pen') + 'Edit</button><button type="button" class="w274btn sm dng" data-w274recx="' + id + '">Remove</button></div>') + '</article>';
  }
  /* wave274b: the anticipated spend in Plan: $250, Not set, or why it is not there yet */
  function spendHtml(e) {
    if (!S.budgetOk) return '<span class="none">Opens once the Toronto database update is applied.</span>';
    var b = budgetOf(e);
    return b == null ? '<span class="none">Not set</span>' : '<span class="w274amt">' + E(money(b)) + '</span>';
  }
  function planHtml(e) {
    var kit = '<div class="w274box"><div class="w274bh"><h3>Kitchen</h3><button type="button" class="w274btn sm" data-w274kit="1">' + ic('pen') + 'Edit</button></div>' +
      '<dl class="w274dl"><dt>Place</dt><dd>' + val(e.kitchen_place) + '</dd><dt>Time</dt><dd>' + val(e.kitchen_shift) + '</dd><dt>Contact</dt><dd>' + val(e.kitchen_contact) + '</dd><dt>Starts</dt><dd>' + val(hm(e.start_time)) + '</dd>' +
      '<dt>Anticipated spend</dt><dd data-w274spend="1">' + spendHtml(e) + '</dd></dl>' + errLine('kit') + '</div>';
    var body;
    if (!S.recOk) body = '<p class="w274quiet">Who the meals go to opens once the Toronto database update is applied.</p>';
    else {
      var rs = recsOf(e.id), planned = num(e.planned_meals, null), placed = rs.reduce(function (s, r) { return s + num(r.meals, 0); }, 0);
      body = (rs.length ? '<div class="w274rcs">' + rs.map(recHtml).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="w274quiet">No organization yet. Add the one that sent the request, with its headcount and dietary needs.</p>') +
        (planned != null && rs.some(function (r) { return r.meals != null; }) ? '<p class="w274placed"><b>' + nf(placed) + ' of ' + nf(planned) + '</b> planned meals placed</p>' : planned != null ? '<p class="w274placed">' + E(plural(planned, 'meal')) + ' planned</p>' : '');
    }
    var rec = '<div class="w274box"><div class="w274bh"><h3>Who the meals go to</h3>' + (S.recOk ? '<button type="button" class="w274btn' + (pri(e, 'plan') ? pri(e, 'plan') : ' sm') + '" data-w274rec="new">' + ic('plus') + 'Add an organization</button>' : '') + '</div>' + body + errLine('rec') + '</div>';
    return secHead('plan', e) + '<p class="w274lead">The kitchen and its time, and the organizations the meals go to, with what each one asked for.</p><div class="w274two">' + kit + rec + '</div>';
  }
  /* Crew: one column per crew, its people, Add people, the targets */
  function stChip(st) { return '<span class="w274chip ' + st + '">' + E(ST_LABEL[st]) + '</span>'; }
  /* break274: a school's long name is cut to the chip's width (b_css.js), the full name on hover */
  function whoChip(p) { return !p ? '' : p.student ? '<span class="w274chip sch" title="' + E(p.schoolName || p.school || 'Student') + '">' + E(p.school || 'Student') + '</span>' : '<span class="w274chip gen">General</span>'; }
  function nameOf(r) { var p = personOf(r); return String((r && r.person_name) || (p && p.name) || 'No name').trim(); }
  function pcHtml(r) {
    var p = personOf(r), st = stOf(r), nm = nameOf(r), chip = whoChip(p);
    return '<div class="w274pc" data-w274r="' + E(r.id) + '" data-st="' + st + '"><div class="w274pm"><span class="w274pn">' + E(nm) + '</span><span class="w274pcs">' + chip +
      '<span class="w274sw" data-w274chip="' + E(r.id) + '">' + stChip(st) + '</span></span></div>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w274ib" data-w274pm="' + E(r.id) + '" aria-haspopup="menu" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="' + E('Change ' + nm + '\u2019s place on the crew') + '">' + ic('dots') + '</button></div>';
  }
  function crewSum(t) { return t.target ? nf(t.filled) + ' of ' + nf(t.target) + ' places filled, ' + nf(t.yes) + ' confirmed.' : 'No crew places set yet. Edit the targets to say how many people each crew needs.'; }
  function colHtml(e, c) {
    return '<div class="w274col" data-w274role="' + E(c.role) + '"><div class="w274colh"><h3>' + E(c.label) + '</h3><span class="w274cn" data-w274cn="' + E(c.role) + '">' + ofT(c.yes + c.wait, c.target) + '</span></div>' + barHtml(c) +
      '<div class="w274ppl">' + (c.people.length ? c.people.map(pcHtml).join('') : '<p class="w274quiet">No one yet.</p>') + '</div>' +
      (isOpen(e) ? '<button type="button" class="w274add" data-w274addp="' + E(c.role) + '">' + ic('plus') + E(ADD_TO[c.role] || 'Add people') + '</button>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function crewHtml(e) {
    var crews = crewOf(e), t = totals(crews);
    var acts = isOpen(e) ? '<button type="button" class="w274btn' + pri(e, 'crew') + '" data-w274addp="">' + ic('plus') + 'Add people</button><button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274tg="1">Edit the targets</button>' : '';
    return secHead('crew', e, acts) + '<p class="w274lead" data-w274crewsum="1">' + E(crewSum(t)) + '</p>' +
      (S.rosterErr ? '<p class="w274err" role="alert">Couldn\u2019t load the crew. Reload the page to try again.</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="w274crews">' + crews.map(function (c) { return colHtml(e, c); }).join('') + '</div>' + errLine('crew');
  }
  /* Confirm: the counts, who to call, the crew's emails and numbers, the WhatsApp group */
  function linksOf(r) {
    var k = contactOfRow(r), o = [];
    if (k.phone) o.push('<a href="tel:' + E(String(k.phone).replace(/[^\d+]/g, '')) + '">' + E(k.phone) + '</a>');
    if (k.email) o.push('<a href="mailto:' + E(k.email) + '">' + E(k.email) + '</a>');
    return o.length ? o.join('<span class="w274sep">, </span>') : 'No phone or email on file';   /* on a phone each link is its own 44 px line */
  }
  function who(email) { var s = String(email || ''); return s.split('@')[0].split('.')[0].replace(/^\w/, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }); }
  function scopeRows(e) { return rowsOf(e.id).filter(function (r) { var s = stOf(r); return S.scope === 'wait' ? s === 'wait' : (s === 'yes' || s === 'wait'); }); }
  function confirmHtml(e) {
    var rows = rowsOf(e.id).filter(function (r) { return stOf(r) !== 'off'; });
    var yes = rows.filter(function (r) { return stOf(r) === 'yes'; }).length, wait = rows.filter(function (r) { return stOf(r) === 'wait'; }).length, no = rows.length - yes - wait;
    var waiting = rows.filter(function (r) { return stOf(r) === 'wait'; });
    var list = waiting.length ? '<div class="w274wl">' + waiting.map(function (r) {
      var d = r.detail || {}, on = !!d.called_at;
      return '<div class="w274wr" data-w274wr="' + E(r.id) + '"><div class="who"><span class="w274pn">' + E(nameOf(r)) + '</span> <span class="w274chip">' + E(roleLabel(r.role)) + '</span><div class="how">' + linksOf(r) + '</div>' +
        (on ? '<div class="when">Called ' + E(stamp(d.called_at)) + (d.called_by ? ' by ' + E(who(d.called_by)) : '') + '</div>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<button type="button" class="w274tog" data-w274call="' + E(r.id) + '" aria-pressed="' + (on ? 'true' : 'false') + '" aria-label="' + E('Called ' + nameOf(r)) + '"><span class="bx">' + ic('check') + '</span>Called</button></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="w274quiet">' + (rows.length ? 'No one is waiting: everyone on the crew has answered.' : 'No one on the crew yet. Add people in Crew first.') + '</p>';
    var n = yes + wait;
    var copy = '<div class="w274copy"><div class="w274seg" role="group" aria-label="Whose details to copy">' +
      '<button type="button" data-w274scope="all" aria-pressed="' + (S.scope !== 'wait' ? 'true' : 'false') + '">All the crew (' + nf(n) + ')</button>' +
      '<button type="button" data-w274scope="wait" aria-pressed="' + (S.scope === 'wait' ? 'true' : 'false') + '">Only those waiting (' + nf(wait) + ')</button></div>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w274btn' + pri(e, 'confirm') + '" data-w274copy="phone">' + ic('copy') + 'Copy phone numbers</button><button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274copy="email">' + ic('copy') + 'Copy emails</button></div>' + errLine('copy') +
      (S.copyShow ? '<label class="w274vh" for="w274cpt">The copied list</label><textarea class="w274i" id="w274cpt" readonly rows="3">' + E(S.copyShow) + '</textarea>' : '');
    var url = String(e.whatsapp_url || '').trim();
    var wa = '<div class="w274box"><div class="w274bh"><h3>WhatsApp group</h3></div>' + (S.waEdit
      ? '<label class="w274vh" for="w274wa">WhatsApp group link</label><div class="w274wa"><input class="w274i" id="w274wa" type="url" inputmode="url" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="https://chat.whatsapp.com/your-group" value="' + E(url) + '" aria-describedby="w274wae"><button type="button" class="w274btn pri" data-w274wa="save">Save the link</button><button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274wa="cancel">Cancel</button>' + (url ? '<button type="button" class="w274btn dng" data-w274wa="clear">Remove the link</button>' : '') + '</div><p class="w274err" id="w274wae" data-w274err="wa" aria-live="polite">' + E(S.err.wa || '') + '</p>'
      : (url ? '<div class="w274wa"><a class="w274btn" href="' + E(url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">' + ic('ext') + 'Open the group</a><button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274wa="edit">' + ic('pen') + 'Edit the link</button></div><p class="w274meta">' + E(url) + '</p>'
        : '<p class="w274quiet">No group link yet. Add it so the crew can find the chat.</p><button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274wa="edit">' + ic('plus') + 'Add the group link</button>') + errLine('wa')) + '</div>';
    return secHead('confirm', e) + '<p class="w274cnts" data-w274cnts="1"><span><b>' + nf(yes) + '</b> confirmed</span><span><b>' + nf(wait) + '</b> waiting</span><span><b>' + nf(no) + '</b> can\u2019t come</span></p>' +
      '<div class="w274two"><div class="w274box"><div class="w274bh"><h3>Waiting to hear back</h3></div>' + list + errLine('call') + copy + '</div>' + wa + '</div>';
  }
  /* Wrap up: meals delivered, who came, Mark this event completed */
  function wrapHtml(e) {
    var s = lower(e.status), rs = recsOf(e.id), d = delivered(e);
    var md = !S.recOk ? '<p class="w274quiet">Counting meals delivered opens once the Toronto database update is applied.</p>'
      : !rs.length ? '<p class="w274quiet">Add who the meals go to in Plan, then count what each organization got here.</p>'
        : '<div class="w274mds">' + rs.map(function (r) {
          return '<div class="w274md"><label for="w274md' + E(r.id) + '"><span class="w274pn">' + E(r.name) + '</span>' + (r.meals != null ? ' <span class="w274meta">' + E(nf(r.meals)) + ' asked for</span>' : '') + '</label>' +
            '<input class="w274i" type="number" min="0" max="2000" step="1" inputmode="numeric" id="w274md' + E(r.id) + '" data-w274md="' + E(r.id) + '" value="' + E(r.meals_delivered == null ? '' : r.meals_delivered) + '"></div>';
        }).join('') + '</div><div class="w274copy"><button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274mdsave="1">Save meals delivered</button><span class="w274meta">' + (d != null ? E(plural(d, 'meal')) + ' delivered in all' : 'Not counted yet') + '</span></div>' + errLine('md');
    var came = rowsOf(e.id).filter(function (r) { var x = stOf(r); return x === 'yes' || (x === 'no' && r.confirmed_at); });
    var whoList = came.length ? '<div class="w274wl">' + came.map(function (r) {
      var y = stOf(r) === 'yes', nm = nameOf(r);
      return '<div class="w274wr w274cr"><div class="who"><span class="w274pn">' + E(nm) + '</span> <span class="w274chip">' + E(roleLabel(r.role)) + '</span></div>' +
        '<div class="w274seg" role="group" aria-label="' + E('Did ' + nm + ' come?') + '"><button type="button" data-w274came="' + E(r.id) + '" data-v="yes" aria-pressed="' + (y ? 'true' : 'false') + '">Came</button><button type="button" data-w274came="' + E(r.id) + '" data-v="no" aria-pressed="' + (y ? 'false' : 'true') + '">Didn\u2019t come</button></div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="w274quiet">No one has confirmed yet. The confirmed crew shows here, to mark who came.</p>';
    var fin;
    if (s === 'cancelled') fin = '<p class="w274quiet">This event was cancelled, so there is nothing to wrap up.</p>';
    else if (s === 'completed') fin = '<p class="w274quiet">This event is completed. The CRM counts it for everyone marked as came.</p>';
    else if (S.confirmDone) fin = '<div class="w274cfm ok" role="group" aria-label="Mark completed"><span>This event is ' + E(rel(daysTo(e.event_date))) + '. Mark it completed now?</span><button type="button" class="w274btn sm pri" data-w274done="yes">Mark completed</button><button type="button" class="w274btn sm" data-w274done="no">Not yet</button></div>';
    else fin = '<button type="button" class="w274btn' + pri(e, 'wrap') + '" data-w274done="ask">' + ic('check') + 'Mark this event completed</button>';
    return secHead('wrap', e) + '<p class="w274lead">After the event: what each organization got, who came, and the event marked completed so the CRM counts it.</p>' +
      '<div class="w274two"><div class="w274box"><div class="w274bh"><h3>Meals delivered</h3></div>' + md + '</div><div class="w274box"><div class="w274bh"><h3>Who came</h3></div>' + whoList + errLine('came') + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="w274copy">' + fin + '</div>' + errLine('done');
  }
  var PART = { head: headHtml, sum: sumHtml, plan: planHtml, crew: crewHtml, confirm: confirmHtml, wrap: wrapHtml };
  function selOf(a) {
    if (!a || !a.getAttribute) return null;
    for (var i = 0; i < a.attributes.length; i++) {
      var n = a.attributes[i].name;
      if (/^data-w274/.test(n)) { var s = '[' + n + '="' + String(a.attributes[i].value).replace(/["\\]/g, '\\$&') + '"]'; if (a.getAttribute('data-v')) s += '[data-v="' + a.getAttribute('data-v') + '"]'; return s; }
    }
    return a.id ? '#' + a.id : null;
  }
  function keepFocus(fn) {
    var a = document.activeElement, sel = a && a !== document.body ? selOf(a) : null;
    fn();
    if (sel) { var b = document.querySelector(sel); if (b && b !== document.activeElement && b.offsetParent !== null) { try { b.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  function paintParts(names) {
    var e = curEv(), root = document.getElementById('w274root'); if (!e || !root) return;
    keepFocus(function () {
      names.forEach(function (n) {
        var el = root.querySelector('[data-w274part="' + n + '"]'); if (!el || !PART[n]) return;
        el.innerHTML = PART[n](e); bars(el, false);
        if (/^(plan|crew|confirm|wrap)$/.test(n)) el.classList.toggle('cur', stageOf(e) === n);
      });
    });
  }
  /* the stage marker: placed at once on a first draw, moved (240 ms) when the stage changes */
  function marker(root, e) {
    var st = root.querySelector('.w274steps'), mk = st && st.querySelector('.w274mk'); if (!mk) return;
    var to = stageIdx(stageOf(e)), from = S.mk && String(S.mk.id) === String(e.id) ? S.mk.i : to;
    mk.style.transform = 'translateX(' + (from * 100) + '%)';
    void mk.offsetWidth;
    st.classList.add('w274go');
    if (from !== to) requestAnimationFrame(function () { mk.style.transform = 'translateX(' + (to * 100) + '%)'; });
    S.mk = { id: e.id, i: to };
  }
  function paintEvent(root, fresh) {
    var e = curEv();
    if (!S.loaded) { setMast('Toronto event', 'Loading the event\u2026', { back: true }); root.innerHTML = '<p class="w274quiet" role="status">Loading the event\u2026</p>'; return; }
    if (!e) { setMast('This event isn\u2019t here', 'It may have been removed, or it is not a Toronto event.', { back: true }); root.innerHTML = '<div class="w274empty"><p>Go back to all the Toronto events to find the one you need.</p><a class="w274btn" href="#toronto">All events</a></div>'; return; }
    setMast(titleOf(e), mastLine(e), { back: true });
    var st = stageOf(e);
    keepFocus(function () {
      root.innerHTML = '<div class="w274eh" data-w274part="head">' + headHtml(e) + '</div>' + stepsHtml(e) + '<div data-w274part="sum">' + sumHtml(e) + '</div>' +
        ['plan', 'crew', 'confirm', 'wrap'].map(function (k) { return '<section class="w274sec' + (k === st ? ' cur' : '') + '" id="w274s-' + k + '" data-w274part="' + k + '" aria-labelledby="w274h-' + k + '">' + PART[k](e) + '</section>'; }).join('');
    });
    marker(root, e);
    bars(root, fresh);
  }
  /* a status changed: the chip crossfades in place, the counts follow, Confirm and Wrap up redraw */
  function crossfade(sw, html) {
    var old = Array.prototype.slice.call(sw.children), tmp = document.createElement('span'); tmp.innerHTML = html;
    var nu = tmp.firstChild; if (!nu) return;
    if (S.kbd) { sw.innerHTML = html; return; }   /* a keyboard action: the new status at once */
    nu.classList.add('w274fo'); sw.appendChild(nu); void nu.offsetWidth;
    nu.classList.remove('w274fo'); old.forEach(function (o) { o.classList.add('w274fo'); });
    setTimeout(function () { old.forEach(function (o) { if (o.parentNode) o.parentNode.removeChild(o); }); }, 220);
  }
  function statusInPlace(r) {
    var root = document.getElementById('w274root'), e = evById(r.event_id); if (!root || !e) return;
    var st = stOf(r), sw = root.querySelector('[data-w274chip="' + r.id + '"]'), card = root.querySelector('[data-w274r="' + r.id + '"]');
    if (sw) crossfade(sw, stChip(st));
    if (card) card.setAttribute('data-st', st);
    crewOf(e).forEach(function (c) {
      var cn = root.querySelector('[data-w274cn="' + c.role + '"]'); if (cn) cn.textContent = ofT(c.yes + c.wait, c.target);
      var col = root.querySelector('.w274col[data-w274role="' + c.role + '"]'), w = col && col.querySelector('.w274bar .w'), cc = col && col.querySelector('.w274bar .c'), bar = col && col.querySelector('.w274bar');
      if (bar && S.kbd) bar.classList.add('w274now');   /* break274: a keyboard action: the bar stands at its new value at once */
      if (w) { w.setAttribute('data-x', ratio(c.yes + c.wait, c.target).toFixed(3)); w.style.transform = 'scaleX(' + w.getAttribute('data-x') + ')'; }
      if (cc) { cc.setAttribute('data-x', ratio(c.yes, c.target).toFixed(3)); cc.style.transform = 'scaleX(' + cc.getAttribute('data-x') + ')'; }
      if (bar && S.kbd) { void bar.offsetWidth; bar.classList.remove('w274now'); }
    });
    var sum = root.querySelector('[data-w274crewsum]'); if (sum) sum.textContent = crewSum(totals(crewOf(e)));
    paintParts(['confirm', 'wrap', 'sum']);
  }
  function repaintCrew() { paintParts(['crew', 'confirm', 'wrap', 'sum']); }

  /* ---------- Volunteers (#toronto/people): the Toronto pool from the CRM ---------- */
  function schoolsOf(list) {
    var seen = {}, o = [];
    list.forEach(function (p) { if (p.schoolId != null && !seen[p.schoolId]) { seen[p.schoolId] = 1; o.push({ id: p.schoolId, name: p.schoolName || p.school, short: p.school }); } });
    return o.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
  }
  function areasOf(list) {
    var o = AREAS.slice();
    list.forEach(function (p) { if (p.area && o.indexOf(p.area) < 0) o.push(p.area); });
    return o;
  }
  function filterPeople(list, f) {
    var qn = fold(f.q), dg = String(f.q || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
    return (list || []).filter(function (p) {
      if (f.kind === 'students' && !p.student) return false;
      if (f.kind === 'general' && p.student) return false;
      if (f.school && String(p.schoolId) !== String(f.school)) return false;
      if (f.area && p.area !== f.area) return false;
      if (f.days && f.days.length && !f.days.some(function (d) { return p.days[d]; })) return false;
      if (qn) {
        var hay = fold([p.name, p.email, (p.c.emails || []).join(' '), (p.c.phones || []).join(' '), p.school, p.schoolName].join(' '));
        var tel = dg.length >= 3 && (p.c.phones || []).some(function (x) { return String(x).replace(/\D/g, '').indexOf(dg) >= 0; });
        if (hay.indexOf(qn) < 0 && !tel) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
  }
  function sortPeople(list, how) {
    var byName = function (a, b) { return a.name.localeCompare(b.name); };
    return list.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
      if (how === 'name') return byName(a, b);
      if (how === 'last') { var x = a.last || a.next || '', y = b.last || b.next || ''; return x === y ? byName(a, b) : x < y ? 1 : -1; }
      var c = String(a.created || ''), d = String(b.created || ''); return c === d ? byName(a, b) : c < d ? 1 : -1;
    });
  }
  function daysDots(on) {
    var names = WEEK.filter(function (d, i) { return on[i]; }), lbl = names.length ? 'Can give ' + andJoin(names) : 'Days not given';
    return '<span class="w274d7" role="img" aria-label="' + E(lbl) + '" title="' + E(lbl) + '">' + on.map(function (x) { return '<i' + (x ? ' class="on"' : '') + '></i>'; }).join('') + '</span>';
  }
  function anyFilter(f) { return f.kind !== 'all' || !!f.school || !!f.area || (f.days && f.days.length > 0); }
  function describe(f) {
    var o = [];
    if (f.q) o.push('\u201c' + f.q + '\u201d');
    if (f.kind === 'students') o.push('Students'); if (f.kind === 'general') o.push('General');
    if (f.school) { var s = schoolsOf(S.pool || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(f.school); })[0]; o.push(s ? s.short || s.name : 'that school'); }
    if (f.area) o.push(f.area);
    if (f.days && f.days.length) o.push(f.days.slice().sort().map(function (d) { return WEEK[d]; }).join(' or '));
    return o;
  }
  /* the search and the filters, shared by the page (scope p) and the Add people sheet (scope k) */
  function toolsHtml(f, sc) {
    var pool = S.pool || [];
    return '<div class="w274srch">' + ic('search') + '<label class="w274vh" for="w274q' + sc + '">Search the Toronto volunteers</label><input class="w274i" type="search" id="w274q' + sc + '" data-w274q="' + sc + '" placeholder="Name, email, phone or school" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" value="' + E(f.q) + '"></div>' +
      '<div class="w274fl"><div class="w274seg" role="group" aria-label="Students or general">' + [['all', 'All'], ['students', 'Students'], ['general', 'General']].map(function (k) { return '<button type="button" data-w274kind="' + k[0] + '" data-w274fs="' + sc + '" aria-pressed="' + (f.kind === k[0] ? 'true' : 'false') + '">' + k[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<label class="w274sl">School <select class="w274i" data-w274fsel="school" data-w274fs="' + sc + '"><option value="">All schools</option>' + schoolsOf(pool).map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + E(s.id) + '"' + (String(f.school) === String(s.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(s.short && s.short !== s.name ? s.short + ', ' + s.name : s.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' +
      '<label class="w274sl">Area <select class="w274i" data-w274fsel="area" data-w274fs="' + sc + '"><option value="">All areas</option>' + areasOf(pool).map(function (a) { return '<option' + (f.area === a ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(a) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' +
      '<div class="w274dayb" role="group" aria-label="Days they can give">' + WEEK.map(function (d, i) { return '<button type="button" data-w274day="' + i + '" data-w274fs="' + sc + '" aria-pressed="' + (f.days.indexOf(i) >= 0 ? 'true' : 'false') + '">' + d + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      (sc === 'p' ? '<label class="w274sl">Sort <select class="w274i" data-w274fsel="sort" data-w274fs="p">' + [['newest', 'Newest'], ['last', 'Last event'], ['name', 'Name']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (f.sort === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function noneHtml(f) {
    return '<div class="w274empty"><p>No one matches ' + E(andJoin(describe(f))) + '.</p><div class="w274copy">' +
      (anyFilter(f) ? '<button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274clear="f" data-w274fs="' + (f === S.f ? 'p' : 'k') + '">Clear the filters</button>' : '') +
      (f.q ? '<button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274clear="q" data-w274fs="' + (f === S.f ? 'p' : 'k') + '">Clear the search</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function rowHtml(p) {
    var evs = p.count ? '<b>' + E(plural(p.count, 'Toronto event')) + '</b>' + (p.last ? 'Last: ' + E(fmtDay(p.last)) : p.next ? 'Next: ' + E(fmtDay(p.next)) : '') : '<b>No Toronto event yet</b>';
    return '<article class="w274row" data-w274cid="' + E(p.id) + '"><div><div class="a"><span class="w274pn">' + E(p.name) + '</span>' + whoChip(p) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="b"><span>' + E(p.area || 'Area not given') + '</span>' + daysDots(p.days) + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="ev">' + evs + '</div>' +
      '<div class="x">' + (p.email ? '<button type="button" class="w274ib" data-w274cpe="' + E(p.id) + '" aria-label="' + E('Copy ' + p.name + '\u2019s email') + '" title="Copy the email">' + ic('copy') + '</button>' : '<span class="w274ib w274ph" aria-hidden="true"></span>') +   /* break274: no email, the columns still line up */
      '<a class="w274btn sm" href="#crm/c/' + E(p.id) + '" data-w274crm="' + E(p.id) + '">Open in the CRM</a>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w274btn sm" data-w274to="' + E(p.id) + '">' + ic('plus') + 'Add to an event</button></div></article>';
  }
  function countLine(n, all) { return n === all ? plural(all, 'person', 'people') : nf(n) + ' of ' + plural(all, 'person', 'people'); }
  function paintList(fresh) {
    var rows = document.getElementById('w274rows'), cnt = document.getElementById('w274pcount'); if (!rows) return;
    var pool = S.pool || [], list = sortPeople(filterPeople(pool, S.f), S.f.sort);
    if (cnt) cnt.textContent = pool.length ? countLine(list.length, pool.length) : '';
    if (S.poolErr && !pool.length) rows.innerHTML = '<p class="w274err" role="alert">Couldn\u2019t load the Toronto volunteers from the CRM. Check your connection, then reload the page.</p>';
    else if (!pool.length) rows.innerHTML = '<div class="w274empty"><h2>No Toronto volunteers in the CRM yet.</h2><p>Contacts tagged toronto, toronto volunteer or toronto student, or living in the GTA, show here.</p></div>';
    else if (!list.length) rows.innerHTML = noneHtml(S.f);
    else rows.innerHTML = list.map(rowHtml).join('');
    if (fresh) rise(rows, '.w274row');
  }
  function paintPeople(root, fresh) {
    if (!S.pool) { setMast('Toronto volunteers', 'Everyone in the CRM who volunteers with us in Toronto.', {}); root.innerHTML = '<p class="w274quiet" role="status">Loading the Toronto volunteers\u2026</p>'; return; }
    setMast('Toronto volunteers', 'Everyone in the CRM who volunteers with us in Toronto: students and general volunteers.', { count: S.pool.length });
    var sig = S.pool.length + '|' + schoolsOf(S.pool).length;
    if (!root.querySelector('#w274rows') || S.toolsSig !== sig) {
      keepFocus(function () {
        root.innerHTML = (S.poolErr ? '<p class="w274err" role="alert">Some of the CRM didn\u2019t load. The list may be missing people: reload the page to try again.</p>' : '') +
          (S.pool.length ? '<div class="w274tools" data-w274tools="p">' + toolsHtml(S.f, 'p') + '</div>' : '') +   /* break274: no one in the pool: the empty state first, no search over nobody */
          '<p class="w274count" id="w274pcount" aria-live="polite"></p><div class="w274rows" id="w274rows"></div>';
      });
      S.toolsSig = sig;
    }
    paintList(fresh);
  }
  /* a filter changed: its buttons follow in place (the search keeps its focus), then the list redraws, never rising */
  function syncTools(sc) {
    var f = sc === 'k' ? S.pk.f : S.f, host = sc === 'k' ? document.getElementById('w274pane') : document.getElementById('w274root'); if (!host) return;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(host.querySelectorAll('[data-w274kind][data-w274fs="' + sc + '"]'), function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.getAttribute('data-w274kind') === f.kind ? 'true' : 'false'); });
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(host.querySelectorAll('[data-w274day][data-w274fs="' + sc + '"]'), function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', f.days.indexOf(Number(b.getAttribute('data-w274day'))) >= 0 ? 'true' : 'false'); });
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(host.querySelectorAll('[data-w274fsel][data-w274fs="' + sc + '"]'), function (s) { var k = s.getAttribute('data-w274fsel'); if (s.value !== String(f[k] || '')) s.value = String(f[k] || ''); });
    var qi = host.querySelector('[data-w274q="' + sc + '"]'); if (qi && qi.value !== f.q) qi.value = f.q;
    if (sc === 'k') paintPick(); else paintList(false);
  }

  /* ---------- the sheet: one at a time, Escape closes it, focus stays inside and goes back to what opened it ---------- */
  var SH = { kind: null, opener: null, openerSel: null, ctx: null };
  function sheetRoot() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w274sheet');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w274sheet'; el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="w274bd" data-w274x="1"></div><div id="w274pane" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w274sht" tabindex="-1"></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    return el;
  }
  function sheetOpen() { var el = document.getElementById('w274sheet'); return !!el && el.classList.contains('open'); }
  function openSheet(kind, o) {
    var el = sheetRoot(), pane = document.getElementById('w274pane');
    closeMenu(true);
    try { if (window.w272 && window.w272.closeTip) window.w272.closeTip(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }   /* wave272's corner note never sits over a window */
    if (!S.snackFn) hideSnack();   /* a plain note (saved, copied) makes way for the sheet; an Undo stays its 5 seconds */
    /* break274: Safari does not focus a button on a tap, so focus can still sit on an older control (the opener of the last
       sheet): the control just tapped or pressed is the opener, then whatever has focus */
    if (!sheetOpen()) {
      var ae = document.activeElement, tapped = S.tapEl && document.contains(S.tapEl) && !el.contains(S.tapEl) && performance.now() - (S.tapAt || 0) < 1500 ? S.tapEl : null;
      SH.opener = o.opener || tapped || (ae && ae !== document.body && !el.contains(ae) ? ae : null) || ae; SH.openerSel = selOf(SH.opener);
    }
    SH.kind = kind; SH.ctx = o.ctx || null;
    pane.innerHTML = '<form id="w274f" class="w274fx" novalidate data-w274form="' + E(kind) + '">' +   /* no grab handle: there is no drag to dismiss */
      '<div class="w274shd"><div><h2 id="w274sht">' + E(o.title) + '</h2>' + (o.sub ? '<p>' + E(o.sub) + '</p>' : '') + '</div><button type="button" class="w274ib" data-w274x="1" aria-label="Close">' + ic('x') + '</button></div>' +
      '<div class="w274sbd">' + o.body + '</div><div class="w274sft">' + errLine('sheet', '') + (o.foot || '') + '</div></form>';
    S.err.sheet = '';
    el.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
    void pane.offsetWidth;
    still(el, function () { el.classList.add('open'); });
    placeSnack();
    /* on a touch screen the sheet itself takes focus: a keyboard or date wheel rising during the slide covers the list and the action */
    var touch = !S.kbd && matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches;
    var f = o.focus ? pane.querySelector(o.focus) : null;
    if (!f && !touch) f = pane.querySelector('.w274sbd input:not([type=hidden]):not([disabled]), .w274sbd select, .w274sbd textarea');
    if (touch && f && f.matches('input:not([type=checkbox]):not([type=radio]), textarea')) f = null;
    try { (f || pane).focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function closeSheet(noReturn) {
    var el = document.getElementById('w274sheet'); if (!el || !el.classList.contains('open')) return;
    still(el, function () { el.classList.remove('open'); });
    SH.kind = null; SH.ctx = null; placeSnack();
    var op = SH.opener, sel = SH.openerSel; SH.opener = null; SH.openerSel = null;
    if (!noReturn) {
      if (!op || !document.contains(op) || op.offsetParent === null) op = sel ? document.querySelector(sel) : null;
      if (op) { try { op.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    }
    /* break274: focus leaves the sheet before it is hidden from assistive tech (never focus inside aria-hidden) */
    if (el.contains(document.activeElement)) { try { document.activeElement.blur(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
  }
  /* a keyboard action changes at once (no slide, no fade); a pointer gets the motion */
  function still(el, fn) {
    if (!S.kbd) { el.classList.remove('w274inst'); fn(); return; }
    el.classList.add('w274inst'); fn(); void el.offsetWidth;
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { el.classList.remove('w274inst'); });
  }
  function focusables(root) { return Array.prototype.filter.call(root.querySelectorAll('a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]):not([type=hidden]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'), function (x) { return x.offsetParent !== null || x === document.activeElement; }); }
  function busy(on) { var b = document.querySelector('#w274f [type="submit"]'); if (b) { b.disabled = !!on; b.setAttribute('aria-busy', on ? 'true' : 'false'); } }
  function fld(label, input) { return '<label class="w274lb" for="' + input.id + '">' + label + '</label>' + input.html; }
  function inp(id, name, type, value, extra) { return { id: id, html: '<input class="w274i" id="' + id + '" name="' + name + '" type="' + type + '" value="' + E(value == null ? '' : value) + '"' + (extra || '') + '>' }; }
  function bad(el, msg) { if (!el) return; el.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true'); setErr('sheet', msg); try { el.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function wholeOrNull(v, max) { v = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); if (v === '') return null; var n = Number(v); return isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= (max || 2000) && Math.round(n) === n ? n : NaN; }
  /* wave274b: an amount in dollars (250, 250.5, $1,250.00): null when empty, NaN when it is not an amount, Infinity over 100,000 */
  function dollarsOrNull(v) {
    v = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); if (v === '') return null;
    var m = /^\$?\s*(\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+|\d+)(?:\.(\d{1,2}))?$/.exec(v); if (!m) return NaN;
    var n = Number(m[1].replace(/,/g, '') + (m[2] ? '.' + m[2] : ''));
    return n > 100000 ? Infinity : Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
  }

  /* ---------- the small menus ---------- */
  var MN = { trigger: null, sel: null, fn: null };
  function menuEl() { var m = document.getElementById('w274menu'); if (!m) { m = document.createElement('div'); m.id = 'w274menu'; m.setAttribute('role', 'menu'); m.tabIndex = -1; document.body.appendChild(m); } return m; }
  function openMenu(trigger, items, fn, label, kbd) {
    closeMenu(true);
    var m = menuEl();
    m.setAttribute('aria-label', label || 'Options');
    m.innerHTML = items.map(function (it) {
      if (it === '-') return '<hr role="separator">';
      var a = ' class="w274mi' + (it.dng ? ' dng' : '') + '" data-w274mk="' + E(it.k) + '"';
      if (it.href) return '<a role="menuitem"' + a + ' href="' + E(it.href) + '" tabindex="-1">' + ic(it.ic) + E(it.l) + '</a>';
      return '<button type="button" role="' + (it.radio ? 'menuitemradio' : 'menuitem') + '"' + (it.radio ? ' aria-checked="' + (it.on ? 'true' : 'false') + '"' : '') + a + ' tabindex="-1">' + ic(it.radio ? (it.on ? 'check' : '') : it.ic) + E(it.l) + '</button>';
    }).join('');
    var r = trigger.getBoundingClientRect(), w = m.offsetWidth, h = m.offsetHeight;
    var left = Math.max(8, Math.min(r.right - w, window.innerWidth - w - 8)), top = r.bottom + 6, up = false;
    if (top + h > window.innerHeight - 8 && r.top - h - 6 > 8) { top = r.top - h - 6; up = true; }
    m.style.left = Math.round(left) + 'px'; m.style.top = Math.round(top) + 'px';
    m.style.transformOrigin = Math.round(Math.min(w, Math.max(0, r.right - left - r.width / 2))) + 'px ' + (up ? h + 'px' : '0px');
    m.classList.toggle('up', up);
    void m.offsetWidth; still(m, function () { m.classList.add('on'); });
    trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    MN.trigger = trigger; MN.sel = selOf(trigger); MN.fn = fn; MN.top = r.top;   /* break274: where its button was, for the scroll rule */
    var first = kbd ? m.querySelector('.w274mi') : m; if (first) { try { first.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function menuOpen() { var m = document.getElementById('w274menu'); return !!m && m.classList.contains('on'); }
  function closeMenu(silent) {
    var m = document.getElementById('w274menu'); if (!m || !m.classList.contains('on')) return;
    still(m, function () { m.classList.remove('on'); });
    var t = MN.trigger; if (t) t.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    if (!silent) { if (!t || !document.contains(t)) t = MN.sel ? document.querySelector(MN.sel) : null; if (t) { try { t.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
    MN.trigger = null; MN.fn = null;
  }

  /* ---------- the note at the foot of the screen ---------- */
  function snack(msg, act, fn) {
    var s = document.getElementById('w274snack');
    if (!s) { s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w274snack'; s.setAttribute('role', 'status'); s.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite'); s.innerHTML = '<span></span><button type="button"></button>'; document.body.appendChild(s); }
    s.querySelector('span').textContent = msg;
    s.querySelector('button').textContent = act || '';
    S.snackFn = fn || null;
    placeSnack();
    s.classList.add('on');
    clearTimeout(S.snackT); S.snackT = setTimeout(hideSnack, act ? 5000 : 3200);
  }
  function hideSnack() { var s = document.getElementById('w274snack'); if (s) s.classList.remove('on'); S.snackFn = null; clearTimeout(S.snackT); }
  /* break274: a note shown while a sheet is open (an Undo keeps its 5 seconds; a partial add says who was added) sits clear of
     the sheet: above its footer on a phone, centred in the page beside it on a computer, never over Cancel or the primary.
     It moves there on its transform (200 ms, with the sheet), at once on a keyboard action, without moving with reduced motion. */
  function placeSnack() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w274snack'); if (!s) return;
    var pane = document.getElementById('w274pane'), ft = pane && pane.querySelector('.w274sft'), x = 0, y = 0, mw = '';
    if (pane && sheetOpen()) {
      if (window.innerWidth <= 900) y = Math.max(0, Math.round((ft ? ft.offsetHeight : 0) + 12 - (parseFloat(getComputedStyle(s).bottom) || 0)));
      else { var free = window.innerWidth - pane.offsetWidth; x = Math.round(free / 2 - window.innerWidth / 2); mw = Math.max(200, free - 32) + 'px'; }
    }
    still(s, function () { s.style.setProperty('--w274sx', x + 'px'); s.style.setProperty('--w274sy', y + 'px'); s.style.maxWidth = mw; });
  }
  /* ---------- New event and Edit (one sheet) ---------- */
  function kitchens() {
    var o = ['Ralph Thornton Community Centre'];
    S.events.slice().reverse().forEach(function (e) { var k = String(e.kitchen_place || '').trim(); if (k && o.indexOf(k) < 0) o.push(k); });
    return o;
  }
  function kitchenList() { return '<datalist id="w274kl">' + kitchens().map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + E(k) + '"></option>'; }).join('') + '</datalist>'; }
  function eventSheet(ev) {
    var e = ev || {};
    var body = fld('Date', inp('w274fd', 'event_date', 'date', e.event_date || '', ' required aria-required="true"')) +
      '<div class="w274row2"><div>' + fld('Start time', inp('w274ft', 'start_time', 'time', hm(e.start_time) || '10:00')) + '</div><div>' +
      fld('Meals planned', inp('w274fm', 'planned_meals', 'number', e.planned_meals == null ? '' : e.planned_meals, ' min="0" max="2000" step="1" inputmode="numeric"')) + '</div></div>' +
      spendField(e) +
      fld('Kitchen', inp('w274fk', 'kitchen_place', 'text', e.kitchen_place || '', ' list="w274kl" autocomplete="off"')) + kitchenList() +
      fld('Kitchen time', inp('w274fs', 'kitchen_shift', 'text', e.kitchen_shift || '', ' placeholder="10:00 to 14:00" autocomplete="off"')) +
      fld('Title', inp('w274fti', 'title', 'text', ev ? titleOf(e) : 'Free meal event', ' autocomplete="off"')) +
      '<label class="w274lb" for="w274fn">Note <small>(optional)</small></label><textarea class="w274i" id="w274fn" name="notes" rows="3">' + E(e.notes || '') + '</textarea>';
    openSheet('event', { title: ev ? 'Edit the event' : 'New event', sub: ev ? (e.code || '') : 'A date and a kitchen to start. The crew and who the meals go to come next.', body: body, ctx: ev ? ev.id : null,
      foot: '<button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274x="1">Cancel</button><button type="submit" class="w274btn pri">' + (ev ? 'Save the changes' : 'Create the event') + '</button>' });
  }
  /* wave274b: Anticipated spend, after Meals planned: a $ before it (decorative: the label says "in dollars"), its words
     beside it when it is not an amount; not there at all until the database has the column */
  function spendField(e) {
    if (!S.budgetOk) return '';
    var b = budgetOf(e);
    return '<label class="w274lb" for="w274fb">Anticipated spend<span class="w274vh">, in dollars</span> <small>(optional)</small></label>' +
      '<div class="w274cur"><span aria-hidden="true">$</span><input class="w274i" id="w274fb" name="budget" type="text" inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" value="' + E(b == null ? '' : amt(b)) + '" aria-describedby="w274fbe w274fbh"></div>' +
      '<p class="w274err" id="w274fbe" data-w274err="budget" aria-live="polite"></p>' +
      '<p class="w274hint" id="w274fbh">Groceries and supplies you expect to pay for. It shows on the Hub calendar.</p>';
  }
  function newEvent() {
    if (!staffOk()) return;
    if (!S.loaded) { loadEvents().then(function () { eventSheet(null); }); return; }
    eventSheet(null);
  }
  async function submitEvent(form) {
    var ev = SH.ctx != null ? evById(SH.ctx) : null;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(form.querySelectorAll('[aria-invalid]'), function (x) { x.removeAttribute('aria-invalid'); });
    setErr('budget', '');
    var d = form.event_date.value;
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d)) return bad(form.event_date, 'Pick a date for the event.');
    var meals = wholeOrNull(form.planned_meals.value);
    if (meals !== meals) return bad(form.planned_meals, 'Meals planned is a whole number from 0 to 2000.');
    var f = { event_date: d, start_time: form.start_time.value || '10:00', planned_meals: meals, kitchen_place: form.kitchen_place.value.trim() || null, kitchen_shift: form.kitchen_shift.value.trim() || null, title: form.title.value.trim() || 'Free meal event', notes: form.notes.value.trim() || null };
    /* wave274b: the anticipated spend, said beside its field when it is not an amount */
    var bi = S.budgetOk ? form.querySelector('#w274fb') : null;
    if (bi) {
      var spend = dollarsOrNull(bi.value);
      if (spend !== spend || spend === Infinity) {
        setErr('sheet', ''); bi.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
        setErr('budget', spend === Infinity ? 'Anticipated spend can be up to $100,000.' : 'Anticipated spend is an amount in dollars, like 250 or 1,250.50.');
        try { bi.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
        return;
      }
      f.budget = spend;
    }
    setErr('sheet', ''); busy(true);
    try {
      if (ev) {
        var patch = {};
        Object.keys(f).forEach(function (k) { var o = k === 'start_time' ? hm(ev[k]) : k === 'title' ? titleOf(ev) : k === 'budget' ? budgetOf(ev) : ev[k]; if (String(o == null ? '' : o) !== String(f[k] == null ? '' : f[k])) patch[k] = f[k]; });
        if (Object.keys(patch).length) await saveEvent(ev, patch);
        closeSheet();
        if (Object.keys(patch).join() === 'budget') paintParts(['plan']); else paint();   /* wave274b: only the spend changed: Plan, in place */
        snack(Object.keys(patch).length ? 'Saved the event.' : 'Nothing changed.');
      } else {
        var made = await createEvent(f);
        if (!made || made.id == null) throw new Error('no id');
        closeSheet(true);
        location.hash = '#toronto/e/' + made.id;
        snack('Created ' + (made.code || 'the event') + '. Next: who the meals go to, then the crew.');
      }
    } catch (e) { busy(false); setErr('sheet', ev ? 'Couldn\u2019t save the event. Try again.' : 'Couldn\u2019t create the event. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 event', e); }
  }

  /* ---------- the kitchen ---------- */
  function kitchenSheet(ev) {
    var body = fld('Kitchen', inp('w274kp', 'kitchen_place', 'text', ev.kitchen_place || '', ' list="w274kl" autocomplete="off"')) + kitchenList() +
      fld('Kitchen time', inp('w274ks', 'kitchen_shift', 'text', ev.kitchen_shift || '', ' placeholder="10:00 to 14:00" autocomplete="off"')) +
      fld('Kitchen contact', inp('w274kc', 'kitchen_contact', 'text', ev.kitchen_contact || '', ' placeholder="Who to reach at the kitchen" autocomplete="off"')) +
      '<p class="w274hint">The person at the kitchen, by name or role. Keep personal phone numbers in the CRM.</p>';
    openSheet('kitchen', { title: 'The kitchen', sub: titleOf(ev) + ', ' + fmtDay(ev.event_date), body: body, ctx: ev.id, foot: '<button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274x="1">Cancel</button><button type="submit" class="w274btn pri">Save the kitchen</button>' });
  }
  async function submitKitchen(form) {
    var ev = evById(SH.ctx); if (!ev) return closeSheet();
    var f = { kitchen_place: form.kitchen_place.value.trim() || null, kitchen_shift: form.kitchen_shift.value.trim() || null, kitchen_contact: form.kitchen_contact.value.trim() || null };
    var patch = {}; Object.keys(f).forEach(function (k) { if (String(ev[k] == null ? '' : ev[k]) !== String(f[k] == null ? '' : f[k])) patch[k] = f[k]; });
    setErr('sheet', ''); busy(true);
    try { if (Object.keys(patch).length) await saveEvent(ev, patch); closeSheet(); paint(); snack('Saved the kitchen.'); }
    catch (e) { busy(false); setErr('sheet', 'Couldn\u2019t save the kitchen. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 kitchen', e); }
  }

  /* ---------- an organization the meals go to ---------- */
  function recSheet(ev, rid) {
    var r = rid ? S.rec.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(rid); })[0] : null;
    if (rid && !r) return;
    var x = r || {}, d = (x.dietary && typeof x.dietary === 'object') ? x.dietary : {};
    var ck = function (id, name, on, label) { return '<label class="w274ck" for="' + id + '"><input type="checkbox" id="' + id + '" name="' + name + '"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + '>' + label + '</label>'; };
    var body = fld('Organization', inp('w274rn', 'name', 'text', x.name || '', ' required aria-required="true" autocomplete="off" maxlength="160"')) +
      fld('Meals <small>(the headcount they sent)</small>', inp('w274rm', 'meals', 'number', x.meals == null ? '' : x.meals, ' min="0" max="2000" step="1" inputmode="numeric"')) +
      '<fieldset><legend>Dietary needs</legend>' + ck('w274rh', 'halal', d.halal, 'Halal') + ck('w274rnf', 'nut_free', d.nut_free, 'Nut-free') +
      '<div class="w274row2"><div>' + fld('Vegetarian meals', inp('w274rv', 'vegetarian', 'number', num(d.vegetarian, 0) || '', ' min="0" max="2000" step="1" inputmode="numeric"')) + '</div><div>' +
      fld('Vegan meals', inp('w274rvg', 'vegan', 'number', num(d.vegan, 0) || '', ' min="0" max="2000" step="1" inputmode="numeric"')) + '</div></div>' +
      fld('Anything else', inp('w274ro', 'other', 'text', d.other || '', ' placeholder="No pork, low sodium" autocomplete="off"')) + '</fieldset>' +
      fld('Drop-off', inp('w274rd', 'drop_off', 'text', x.drop_off || '', ' placeholder="12:30 at the side door" autocomplete="off"')) +
      fld('Address', inp('w274ra', 'address', 'text', x.address || '', ' autocomplete="off"')) +
      fld('Contact name', inp('w274rcn', 'contact_name', 'text', x.contact_name || '', ' autocomplete="off"')) +
      '<div class="w274row2"><div>' + fld('Contact phone', inp('w274rcp', 'contact_phone', 'tel', x.contact_phone || '', ' autocomplete="off"')) + '</div><div>' +
      fld('Contact email', inp('w274rce', 'contact_email', 'email', x.contact_email || '', ' autocomplete="off"')) + '</div></div>' +
      '<fieldset><legend>Naming them</legend>' + ck('w274rp', 'private', x.private, 'Supported but not named publicly') + '<p class="w274hint">The Hub then says \u201c1 organization, not named publicly\u201d wherever it lists who the meals go to.</p></fieldset>' +
      '<label class="w274lb" for="w274rnote">Note <small>(optional)</small></label><textarea class="w274i" id="w274rnote" name="note" rows="2">' + E(x.note || '') + '</textarea>';
    openSheet('rec', { title: r ? 'Edit ' + (x.name || 'the organization') : 'Add an organization', sub: 'Who the meals go to, ' + titleOf(ev) + ', ' + fmtDay(ev.event_date), body: body, ctx: { ev: ev.id, id: r ? r.id : null },
      foot: '<button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274x="1">Cancel</button><button type="submit" class="w274btn pri">' + (r ? 'Save the changes' : 'Add the organization') + '</button>' });
  }
  async function submitRec(form) {
    var ctx = SH.ctx || {}, ev = evById(ctx.ev); if (!ev) return closeSheet();
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(form.querySelectorAll('[aria-invalid]'), function (x) { x.removeAttribute('aria-invalid'); });
    var name = form.name.value.trim();
    if (!name) return bad(form.name, 'Give the organization\u2019s name.');
    var meals = wholeOrNull(form.meals.value), veg = wholeOrNull(form.vegetarian.value), vg = wholeOrNull(form.vegan.value);
    if (meals !== meals) return bad(form.meals, 'Meals is a whole number from 0 to 2000.');
    if (veg !== veg) return bad(form.vegetarian, 'Vegetarian meals is a whole number.');
    if (vg !== vg) return bad(form.vegan, 'Vegan meals is a whole number.');
    var em = form.contact_email.value.trim();
    if (em && !/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(em)) return bad(form.contact_email, 'That email doesn\u2019t look right.');
    var diet = { halal: !!form.halal.checked, nut_free: !!form.nut_free.checked, vegetarian: veg || 0, vegan: vg || 0, other: form.other.value.trim() };
    var f = { name: name, meals: meals, dietary: diet, drop_off: form.drop_off.value.trim() || null, address: form.address.value.trim() || null, contact_name: form.contact_name.value.trim() || null,
      contact_phone: form.contact_phone.value.trim() || null, contact_email: em || null, private: !!form.private.checked, note: form.note.value.trim() || null };
    setErr('sheet', ''); busy(true);
    try { await saveRecipient(ev, ctx.id, f); closeSheet(); paintParts(['plan', 'wrap', 'sum']); snack(ctx.id ? 'Saved ' + name + '.' : 'Added ' + name + ' to who the meals go to.'); }
    catch (e) { busy(false); setErr('sheet', 'Couldn\u2019t save the organization. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 recipient', e); }
  }

  /* ---------- the crew targets (volunteer_events.spot_counts) ---------- */
  function targetsSheet(ev) {
    var t = targetsOf(ev);
    var body = '<p class="w274hint">How many people each crew needs. Photos shows on the page once it needs someone.</p>' +
      ROLES.map(function (r) { return fld(r[1], inp('w274t' + r[0], r[0], 'number', t[r[0]], ' min="0" max="40" step="1" inputmode="numeric"')); }).join('');
    openSheet('targets', { title: 'Crew targets', sub: titleOf(ev) + ', ' + fmtDay(ev.event_date), body: body, ctx: ev.id, foot: '<button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274x="1">Cancel</button><button type="submit" class="w274btn pri">Save the targets</button>' });
  }
  async function submitTargets(form) {
    var ev = evById(SH.ctx); if (!ev) return closeSheet();
    var sc = Object.assign({}, (ev.spot_counts && typeof ev.spot_counts === 'object') ? ev.spot_counts : {});
    for (var i = 0; i < ROLES.length; i++) {
      var k = ROLES[i][0], v = wholeOrNull(form[k].value, 40);
      if (v !== v) return bad(form[k], ROLES[i][1] + ' is a whole number from 0 to 40.');
      sc[k] = v == null ? 0 : v;
    }
    setErr('sheet', ''); busy(true);
    try { await saveEvent(ev, { spot_counts: sc }); closeSheet(); repaintCrew(); snack('Saved the crew targets.'); }
    catch (e) { busy(false); setErr('sheet', 'Couldn\u2019t save the targets. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 targets', e); }
  }

  /* ---------- Add people: the Toronto pool in a sheet, a crew, Add ---------- */
  function roleRadios(sel, roles) {
    return '<fieldset><legend>Crew</legend><div class="w274rad">' + roles.map(function (r) { var on = r[0] === sel; return '<label class="' + (on ? 'on' : '') + '" for="w274ro' + r[0] + '"><input type="radio" name="w274role" id="w274ro' + r[0] + '" value="' + r[0] + '"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + '>' + E(r[1]) + '</label>'; }).join('') + '</div></fieldset>';
  }
  function pickSheet(ev, role) {
    S.pk = { ev: ev.id, role: role || 'cook', f: { q: '', kind: 'all', school: '', area: '', days: [] }, sel: {}, order: [] };
    var go = function () {
      openSheet('pick', { title: 'Add people to the crew', sub: titleOf(ev) + ', ' + fmtDay(ev.event_date), ctx: ev.id, focus: '#w274qk',
        body: roleRadios(S.pk.role, ROLES) + '<div class="w274tools" data-w274tools="k">' + toolsHtml(S.pk.f, 'k') + '</div><p class="w274pkc" id="w274pkc" aria-live="polite"></p><div class="w274pks" id="w274pkl"></div>',
        foot: '<button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274x="1">Cancel</button><button type="submit" class="w274btn pri" id="w274pkgo" disabled>Add people</button>' });
      paintPick();
    };
    if (S.pool) go(); else loadPool().then(go);
  }
  function paintPick() {
    var box = document.getElementById('w274pkl'), cnt = document.getElementById('w274pkc'), k = S.pk; if (!box || !k) return;
    var pool = S.pool || [], list = sortPeople(filterPeople(pool, k.f), 'name'), shown = list.slice(0, 150);
    if (cnt) cnt.textContent = !pool.length ? '' : (list.length > shown.length ? 'Showing ' + shown.length + ' of ' + list.length + '. Search to find someone.' : countLine(list.length, pool.length));
    box.innerHTML = !pool.length ? '<p class="w274quiet">No Toronto volunteers in the CRM yet.</p>' : !list.length ? noneHtml(k.f) : shown.map(function (p) {
      var on = onEvent(p, k.ev), sel = !!k.sel[p.id];
      return '<label class="w274pk' + (on ? ' dis' : sel ? ' on' : '') + '" for="w274pk' + E(p.id) + '"><input type="checkbox" id="w274pk' + E(p.id) + '" data-w274pick="' + E(p.id) + '"' + (on ? ' checked disabled' : sel ? ' checked' : '') + '>' +
        '<span><span class="w274pn">' + E(p.name) + '</span><span class="s">' + whoChip(p) + '<span>' + E(p.area || 'Area not given') + '</span>' + daysDots(p.days) + (on ? '<span>Already on this event</span>' : '') + '</span></span></label>';
    }).join('');
    pickFoot();
  }
  function pickFoot() {
    var b = document.getElementById('w274pkgo'), k = S.pk; if (!b || !k) return;
    var n = k.order.filter(function (id) { return k.sel[id]; }).length;
    b.disabled = !n;
    b.textContent = n ? 'Add ' + plural(n, 'person', 'people') + ' to ' + roleIn(k.role) : 'Add people';
  }
  async function submitPick() {
    var k = S.pk, ev = k && evById(k.ev); if (!ev) return closeSheet();
    var ids = k.order.filter(function (id) { return k.sel[id]; }); if (!ids.length) return;
    setErr('sheet', ''); busy(true);
    var added = [], already = [], needs = [], errs = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
      var p = S.byCid[ids[i]]; if (!p) continue;
      var r = await crewAdd(ev, p, k.role);
      if (r.ok && r.already) already.push(p.name); else if (r.ok) added.push(p.name); else if (r.needs) needs.push(p.name); else errs.push(p.name);
      if (r.ok) delete k.sel[p.id];
    }
    reindexPool(); repaintCrew();
    /* break274: up to three names are said; more are counted, so the note at the foot stays one line or two */
    var who = function (l) { return l.length > 3 ? plural(l.length, 'person', 'people') : andJoin(l); };
    var said = added.length ? 'Added ' + who(added) + ' to ' + roleIn(k.role) + '.' : already.length ? who(already) + (already.length === 1 ? ' is' : ' are') + ' already on this event.' : '';
    if (!needs.length && !errs.length) { closeSheet(); if (said) snack(said); return; }
    busy(false); paintPick();
    setErr('sheet', [needs.length ? who(needs) + (needs.length === 1 ? ' needs' : ' need') + ' the Toronto database update first: there is no volunteer record for ' + (needs.length === 1 ? 'them' : 'them') + ' yet.' : '',
      errs.length ? 'Couldn\u2019t add ' + who(errs) + '. Try again.' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' '));
    if (said) snack(said);
  }

  /* ---------- Add to an event (from Volunteers): an upcoming Toronto event and a crew ---------- */
  function toEventSheet(p) {
    var t = todayIso(), evs = S.events.filter(function (e) { return isOpen(e) && String(e.event_date) >= t; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.event_date) < String(b.event_date) ? -1 : 1; });
    var first = evs.filter(function (e) { return !onEvent(p, e.id); })[0];
    S.te = { cid: p.id, ev: first ? first.id : null, role: 'cook' };
    var body = !evs.length ? '<p class="w274quiet">There is no upcoming Toronto event to add them to yet. Create one with New event.</p>' :
      '<fieldset><legend>Event</legend><div class="w274pks">' + evs.map(function (e) {
        var on = onEvent(p, e.id), sel = first && first.id === e.id;
        return '<label class="w274evp' + (on ? ' dis' : sel ? ' on' : '') + '" for="w274te' + E(e.id) + '"><input type="radio" name="w274tev" id="w274te' + E(e.id) + '" value="' + E(e.id) + '"' + (on ? ' disabled' : sel ? ' checked' : '') + '>' +
          '<span><span class="w274pn">' + E(fmtDay(e.event_date)) + ', ' + E(titleOf(e)) + '</span><span class="s"><span>' + E(kitchenLine(e)) + '</span>' + (on ? '<span>Already on the crew</span>' : '<span>' + E(cap(rel(daysTo(e.event_date)))) + '</span>') + '</span></span></label>';
      }).join('') + '</div></fieldset>' + roleRadios('cook', ROLES);
    openSheet('toev', { title: 'Add ' + p.name + ' to an event', sub: p.student ? (p.school || 'Student') : 'General volunteer', body: body, ctx: p.id,
      foot: '<button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274x="1">' + (evs.length ? 'Cancel' : 'Close') + '</button>' + (evs.length ? '<button type="submit" class="w274btn pri" id="w274tego"' + (first ? '' : ' disabled') + '>Add to the crew</button>' : '') });
  }
  async function submitToEvent() {
    var k = S.te, p = k && S.byCid[k.cid], ev = k && evById(k.ev); if (!p || !ev) return;
    setErr('sheet', ''); busy(true);
    var r = await crewAdd(ev, p, k.role);
    if (r.ok) { reindexPool(); closeSheet(); if (S.view === 'people') paintList(false); else if (S.view === 'event') repaintCrew(); snack(r.already ? p.name + ' is already on that event.' : 'Added ' + p.name + ' to ' + roleIn(k.role) + ' on ' + fmtDay(ev.event_date) + '.'); return; }
    busy(false);
    setErr('sheet', r.needs ? p.name + ' needs the Toronto database update first: there is no volunteer record for them yet.' : 'Couldn\u2019t add ' + p.name + ' to the crew. Try again.');
  }

  /* ---------- Cancel this event (a confirm step) ---------- */
  function cancelSheet(ev) {
    openSheet('cancel', { title: 'Cancel this event?', sub: (ev.code ? ev.code + ', ' : '') + fmtDay(ev.event_date), ctx: ev.id, focus: '[data-w274keep]',
      body: '<p>' + E(titleOf(ev)) + ' moves to the past events as cancelled. Nobody is told automatically: let the crew and the organizations know yourself.</p><p class="w274hint">You can plan it again later from More.</p>',
      foot: '<button type="button" class="w274btn" data-w274x="1" data-w274keep="1">Keep the event</button><button type="submit" class="w274btn pri dng">Cancel the event</button>' });
  }
  async function submitCancel() {
    var ev = evById(SH.ctx); if (!ev) return closeSheet();
    setErr('sheet', ''); busy(true);
    try { await saveEvent(ev, { status: 'cancelled' }); closeSheet(); paint(); snack('Cancelled the event.'); }
    catch (e) { busy(false); setErr('sheet', 'Couldn\u2019t cancel the event. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 cancel', e); }
  }
  var SUBMIT = { event: submitEvent, kitchen: submitKitchen, rec: submitRec, targets: submitTargets, pick: submitPick, toev: submitToEvent, cancel: submitCancel };

  /* ---------- the page: one root inside #toronto, under wave118's masthead ---------- */
  function rootEl() {
    var p = document.getElementById('toronto'); if (!p) return null;
    var r = document.getElementById('w274root');
    if (!r || r.parentNode !== p) { r = document.createElement('div'); r.id = 'w274root'; p.appendChild(r); }
    return r;
  }
  function paint() {
    var root = rootEl(); if (!root || !staffOk()) return;
    try { root.parentNode.setAttribute('data-w274v', S.view); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var ready = S.loaded && (S.view === 'events' || !!S.pool), fresh = false;
    if (ready && S.fresh) { S.fresh = false; fresh = true; }
    if (S.view === 'event') paintEvent(root, fresh);
    else if (S.view === 'people') paintPeople(root, fresh);
    else paintEvents(root, fresh);
  }
  function show(h) {
    if (!staffOk()) return;
    h = String(h || '').replace(/^#/, '');
    var p = h.split('/'); if (p[0] !== 'toronto') return;
    var v = 'events', id = null;
    if (p[1] === 'e' && p[2]) { v = 'event'; id = p[2]; } else if (p[1] === 'people') v = 'people';
    var key = v + ':' + (id || '');
    if (key !== S.key) {
      var first = !S.key;
      S.key = key; S.view = v; S.evId = id; S.fresh = true; S.mk = null; S.waEdit = false; S.confirmRec = null; S.confirmDone = false; S.err = {}; S.copyShow = ''; S.toolsSig = null;
      closeMenu(true); if (sheetOpen()) closeSheet(true);
      if (!first) { try { window.scrollTo(0, 0); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    }
    paint();
    var need = [];
    if (!S.loaded) need.push(loadEvents());
    if (v !== 'events' && !S.pool) need.push(loadPool());
    if (need.length) Promise.all(need).then(function () { if (S.key === key) paint(); }, function (e) { console.warn('wave274 load', e); if (S.key === key) paint(); });
  }
  function scrollToSec(k, pointer) {
    var s = document.getElementById('w274s-' + k), h = document.getElementById('w274h-' + k); if (!s) return;
    try { s.scrollIntoView({ behavior: pointer && !reduced() ? 'smooth' : 'auto', block: 'start' }); } catch (e) { s.scrollIntoView(); }
    if (h) { try { h.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }

  /* ---------- the actions on the event page ---------- */
  function personMenu(btn, r, kbd) {
    var p = personOf(r), st = stOf(r), nm = nameOf(r);
    var items = [{ k: 'yes', l: 'Confirmed', ic: 'check', radio: true, on: st === 'yes' }, { k: 'no', l: 'Can\u2019t come', ic: 'off', radio: true, on: st === 'no' }, { k: 'wait', l: 'Waiting', ic: 'wait', radio: true, on: st === 'wait' }, '-'];
    if (p) items.push({ k: 'crm', l: 'Open in the CRM', ic: 'person', href: '#crm/c/' + p.id });
    items.push({ k: 'off', l: 'Take off the crew', ic: 'x', dng: true });
    openMenu(btn, items, function (k) {
      if (k === 'crm') return;
      if (k === 'off') return doTakeOff(r);
      if (stOf(r) === k) return;
      setErr('crew', '');
      setStatus(r, k).then(function () { statusInPlace(r); snack(nm + ': ' + ST_LABEL[k] + '.'); }, function (e) { statusInPlace(r); setErr('crew', 'Couldn\u2019t change ' + nm + '\u2019s status. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 status', e); });
      statusInPlace(r);
    }, 'Options for ' + nm, kbd);
  }
  function doTakeOff(r) {
    var nm = nameOf(r), role = roleLabel(r.role), root = document.getElementById('w274root'), card = root && root.querySelector('[data-w274r="' + r.id + '"]');
    setErr('crew', '');
    takeOff(r, function (err) { if (err) { repaintCrew(); setErr('crew', 'Couldn\u2019t take ' + nm + ' off the crew, so they are still on it. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 off', err); } });
    var redraw = function () { repaintCrew(); var c = root && root.querySelector('.w274col[data-w274role="' + r.role + '"] [data-w274addp], [data-w274addp=""]'); if (c) { try { c.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } };
    if (card) { card.classList.add('gone'); setTimeout(redraw, 200); } else redraw();
    snack(nm + ' is off the ' + lower(role) + '.', 'Undo', function () { if (undoOff(r.id)) { repaintCrew(); snack(nm + ' is back on the crew.'); var b = document.querySelector('[data-w274pm="' + r.id + '"]'); if (b) { try { b.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } } });
  }
  function moreMenu(btn, e, kbd) {
    var s = lower(e.status), items = [];
    if (s === 'cancelled') items.push({ k: 'plan', l: 'Plan this event again', ic: 'cal' });
    else if (s === 'completed') items.push({ k: 'plan', l: 'Reopen this event', ic: 'cal' });
    else items.push({ k: 'cancel', l: 'Cancel this event', ic: 'x', dng: true });
    openMenu(btn, items, function (k) {
      if (k === 'cancel') return cancelSheet(e);
      if (k === 'plan') saveEvent(e, { status: 'planned' }).then(function () { paint(); snack(s === 'cancelled' ? 'The event is planned again.' : 'The event is open again.'); }, function (x) { setErr('done', 'Couldn\u2019t change the event. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 status', x); });
    }, 'More for this event', kbd);
  }
  async function copyText(txt) {
    try { if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt); return true; } } catch (e) { /* the fallback below */ }
    try {
      var ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = txt; ta.setAttribute('readonly', ''); ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.top = '0'; ta.style.left = '0'; ta.style.opacity = '0';
      document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); var ok = document.execCommand('copy'); document.body.removeChild(ta); return !!ok;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  async function doCopy(e, kind) {
    var rows = scopeRows(e), vals = [], miss = 0;
    rows.forEach(function (r) { var k = contactOfRow(r), v = kind === 'email' ? k.email : k.phone; if (v && vals.indexOf(v) < 0) vals.push(v); else if (!v) miss++; });
    setErr('copy', ''); S.copyShow = '';
    var what = kind === 'email' ? 'email' : 'phone number';
    if (!vals.length) { setErr('copy', rows.length ? 'No ' + what + 's on file for ' + (S.scope === 'wait' ? 'the people waiting.' : 'this crew.') : 'No one to copy yet.'); return; }
    var txt = vals.join(', ');
    S.lastCopy = txt;
    var ok = await copyText(txt);
    if (ok) snack('Copied ' + plural(vals.length, what) + '.' + (miss ? ' ' + plural(miss, 'person', 'people') + ' have none on file.' : ''));
    else { S.copyShow = txt; paintParts(['confirm']); setErr('copy', 'Couldn\u2019t copy by itself. The list is in the box below: select it and copy.'); }
  }
  async function waAction(e, k) {
    if (k === 'edit') { S.waEdit = true; S.err.wa = ''; paintParts(['confirm']); var i = document.getElementById('w274wa'); if (i) { try { i.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } return; }
    if (k === 'cancel') { S.waEdit = false; S.err.wa = ''; paintParts(['confirm']); var b = document.querySelector('[data-w274wa="edit"]'); if (b) { try { b.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } return; }
    var input = document.getElementById('w274wa'), v = k === 'clear' ? '' : String((input && input.value) || '').trim();
    if (v && !/^https:\/\/(chat\.whatsapp\.com|wa\.me|whatsapp\.com)\//.test(v)) { if (input) input.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true'); setErr('wa', 'That isn\u2019t a WhatsApp group link. It starts with https://chat.whatsapp.com/'); if (input) { try { input.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } return; }
    try { await saveEvent(e, { whatsapp_url: v || null }); S.waEdit = false; S.err.wa = ''; paintParts(['confirm']); snack(v ? 'Saved the WhatsApp group link.' : 'Removed the WhatsApp group link.'); var b2 = document.querySelector('[data-w274wa="edit"]'); if (b2) { try { b2.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } }
    catch (x) { setErr('wa', 'Couldn\u2019t save the link. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 whatsapp', x); }
  }
  async function saveDelivered(e) {
    var inputs = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w274root [data-w274md]')), todo = [];
    setErr('md', '');
    for (var i = 0; i < inputs.length; i++) {
      var el = inputs[i], v = wholeOrNull(el.value); el.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
      if (v !== v) { el.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true'); setErr('md', 'Meals delivered is a whole number from 0 to 2000.'); try { el.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
      var r = S.rec.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === el.getAttribute('data-w274md'); })[0];
      if (r && String(r.meals_delivered == null ? '' : r.meals_delivered) !== String(v == null ? '' : v)) todo.push([r, v]);
    }
    if (!todo.length) { snack('Nothing changed.'); return; }
    try {
      await Promise.all(todo.map(function (x) { return saveRecipient(e, x[0].id, { meals_delivered: x[1] }); }));
      paintParts(['wrap', 'sum']); var d = delivered(e); snack('Saved the meals delivered' + (d != null ? ': ' + d + ' in all.' : '.'));
    } catch (x) { setErr('md', 'Couldn\u2019t save the meals delivered. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 delivered', x); }
  }
  async function complete(e) {
    setErr('done', '');
    try { await saveEvent(e, { status: 'completed' }); S.confirmDone = false; paint(); snack('Marked completed. The CRM counts it now.'); var h = document.getElementById('w274h-wrap'); if (h) { try { h.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } }
    catch (x) { setErr('done', 'Couldn\u2019t mark the event completed. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 complete', x); }
  }

  /* ---------- clicks ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    var t = ev.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var el = t.closest('#w274menu [data-w274mk]');
    if (el) { var fn = MN.fn, k = el.getAttribute('data-w274mk'); if (k !== 'crm') ev.preventDefault(); closeMenu(k === 'crm'); if (fn) fn(k); return; }
    if (menuOpen() && !t.closest('#w274menu')) { var trig = MN.trigger; closeMenu(true); if (trig && t.closest('[data-w274pm], [data-w274more]') === trig) return; }
    if (!t.closest('#toronto, #w274sheet, #w274snack')) return;
    var e = curEv();
    if ((el = t.closest('#w274snack button'))) { var sf = S.snackFn; hideSnack(); if (sf) sf(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274x]'))) { ev.preventDefault(); closeSheet(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274new]'))) { ev.preventDefault(); newEvent(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274past]'))) { S.pastOpen = el.getAttribute('aria-expanded') !== 'true'; el.setAttribute('aria-expanded', S.pastOpen ? 'true' : 'false'); var pl = document.getElementById('w274pastl'); if (pl) pl.hidden = !S.pastOpen; return; }
    /* the Volunteers page and the Add people sheet */
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274kind]'))) { var sc = el.getAttribute('data-w274fs'), f = sc === 'k' ? S.pk.f : S.f; f.kind = el.getAttribute('data-w274kind'); syncTools(sc); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274day]'))) { var sc2 = el.getAttribute('data-w274fs'), f2 = sc2 === 'k' ? S.pk.f : S.f, d = Number(el.getAttribute('data-w274day')), at = f2.days.indexOf(d); if (at >= 0) f2.days.splice(at, 1); else f2.days.push(d); syncTools(sc2); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274clear]'))) { var sc3 = el.getAttribute('data-w274fs'), f3 = sc3 === 'k' ? S.pk.f : S.f; if (el.getAttribute('data-w274clear') === 'q') f3.q = ''; else { f3.kind = 'all'; f3.school = ''; f3.area = ''; f3.days = []; } syncTools(sc3); var qi = document.querySelector('[data-w274q="' + sc3 + '"]'); if (qi) { try { qi.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274cpe]'))) { var pp = S.byCid[el.getAttribute('data-w274cpe')]; if (pp && pp.email) { S.lastCopy = pp.email; copyText(pp.email).then(function (ok) { snack(ok ? 'Copied ' + pp.email + '.' : 'Couldn\u2019t copy. The email is ' + pp.email + '.'); }); } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274to]'))) { var pt = S.byCid[el.getAttribute('data-w274to')]; if (pt) { if (!S.loaded) loadEvents().then(function () { toEventSheet(pt); }); else toEventSheet(pt); } return; }
    if (!e) return;
    /* the event page */
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274go]'))) { scrollToSec(el.getAttribute('data-w274go'), ev.detail > 0); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274edit]'))) { eventSheet(e); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274more]'))) { moreMenu(el, e, ev.detail === 0); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274kit]'))) { kitchenSheet(e); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274recx]'))) { S.confirmRec = el.getAttribute('data-w274recx'); paintParts(['plan']); var y = document.querySelector('[data-w274recn="' + S.confirmRec + '"]'); if (y) { try { y.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274recn]'))) { var rn = el.getAttribute('data-w274recn'); S.confirmRec = null; paintParts(['plan']); var rb = document.querySelector('[data-w274recx="' + rn + '"]'); if (rb) { try { rb.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274recy]'))) {
      var rid = el.getAttribute('data-w274recy'), rr = S.rec.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === rid; })[0], rname = rr ? rr.name : 'the organization';
      setErr('rec', '');
      removeRecipient(rid).then(function () { S.confirmRec = null; paintParts(['plan', 'wrap', 'sum']); snack('Removed ' + rname + '.'); var ab = document.querySelector('[data-w274rec="new"]'); if (ab) { try { ab.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } },
        function (x) { S.confirmRec = null; paintParts(['plan']); setErr('rec', 'Couldn\u2019t remove ' + rname + '. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 recipient', x); });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274rec]'))) { var w = el.getAttribute('data-w274rec'); recSheet(e, w === 'new' ? null : w); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274addp]'))) { pickSheet(e, el.getAttribute('data-w274addp') || stageOfRole(e)); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274tg]'))) { targetsSheet(e); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274pm]'))) { var pr = rowById(el.getAttribute('data-w274pm')); if (pr) personMenu(el, pr, ev.detail === 0); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274call]'))) {
      var cr = rowById(el.getAttribute('data-w274call')); if (!cr) return;
      var on = el.getAttribute('aria-pressed') !== 'true'; el.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on ? 'true' : 'false'); setErr('call', '');
      setCalled(cr, on).then(function () { paintParts(['confirm']); snack(on ? 'Marked ' + nameOf(cr) + ' as called.' : nameOf(cr) + ': not called yet.'); }, function (x) { el.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on ? 'false' : 'true'); setErr('call', 'Couldn\u2019t save that call. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 called', x); });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274scope]'))) { S.scope = el.getAttribute('data-w274scope'); Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-w274scope]'), function (b) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.getAttribute('data-w274scope') === S.scope ? 'true' : 'false'); }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274copy]'))) { doCopy(e, el.getAttribute('data-w274copy')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274wa]'))) { waAction(e, el.getAttribute('data-w274wa')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274mdsave]'))) { saveDelivered(e); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274came]'))) {
      var wr = rowById(el.getAttribute('data-w274came')), want = el.getAttribute('data-v') === 'yes' ? 'yes' : 'no'; if (!wr || stOf(wr) === want) return;
      setErr('came', '');
      setStatus(wr, want).then(function () { statusInPlace(wr); }, function (x) { statusInPlace(wr); setErr('came', 'Couldn\u2019t save that for ' + nameOf(wr) + '. Try again.'); console.warn('wave274 came', x); });
      statusInPlace(wr);
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w274done]'))) {
      var dk = el.getAttribute('data-w274done');
      if (dk === 'no') { S.confirmDone = false; paintParts(['wrap']); var ab2 = document.querySelector('[data-w274done="ask"]'); if (ab2) { try { ab2.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } return; }
