/* ---------- wave285 b_page.js: HR Centre > Onboarding (Needs you, Everyone by stage), the sub-pages Plan template and Onboarding insights ---------- */
(function () {
  var W = window.w285; if (!W || W.__page) return; W.__page = true;
  var h = W.h, E = h.E, plural = h.plural, first = h.first, fmtDay = h.fmtDay;
  var ST = { seeDone: false, seeBacked: false, open: {}, html: {} };
  var STAGES = [['offer', 'Offer out'], ['accepted', 'Accepted'], ['starting', 'Starting'], ['weeks', 'Weeks 1 to 4'], ['later', '60 and 90 days']];
  var TASK = { slack: ['create their Slack onboarding channel', 'create their Slack onboarding channels'], google: ['make their Google account', 'make their Google accounts'],
    hub: ['invite them to the Hub', 'invite them to the Hub'], welcome: ['send the welcome email', 'send the welcome emails'], meeting: ['add them to their weekly meeting', 'add them to their weekly meetings'],
    channel: ['add them to their Slack onboarding channel', 'add them to their Slack onboarding channels'], invites: ['send the calendar invites', 'send the calendar invites'] };

  /* ---------- the sub-pages: names and the new Onboarding insights (wave197's page list, read live by its rail and router) ---------- */
  function pages() {
    var P = window.w197 && window.w197.pages; if (!P || P.__w285) return;
    P.__w285 = true;
    P.forEach(function (p) {
      if (p.k === 'onboarding') p.d = 'From offer to full member, one row per person.';
      if (p.k === 'plan') { p.l = 'Plan template'; p.d = 'The plan everyone starts from: every week and step a new person gets. Changes reach everyone still onboarding.'; }
    });
    var at = 0; P.forEach(function (p, i) { if (p.k === 'plan') at = i + 1; });
    if (!P.some(function (p) { return p.k === 'insights'; })) P.splice(at || P.length, 0, { k: 'insights', l: 'Onboarding insights', ic: 'chart', g: 'Growth', tab: 'onboarding', d: 'The onboarding quizzes, and what new members said about their onboarding.' });
  }
  pages();

  /* ---------- where it sits ---------- */
  function pageNow() { var p = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); return p ? p.getAttribute('data-w197') || '' : ''; }
  function onHrc() { var p = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); return !!p && p.classList.contains('active') && String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#hrcentre') === 0; }
  var MINE = [['w285', 'onboarding'], ['w285set', 'plan'], ['w285ins', 'insights']];
  function ensure() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body'); if (!body || !h.isHr()) return false;
    var before = body.firstChild;
    MINE.forEach(function (m, i) {
      var el = document.getElementById(m[0]);
      if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = m[0]; el.className = 'w197blk'; el.setAttribute('data-w197s', m[1]); if (ST.html[m[0]]) el.innerHTML = ST.html[m[0]]; }
      var want = i === 0 ? body.firstChild : document.getElementById(MINE[i - 1][0]).nextSibling;
      if (el.parentNode !== body || (i === 0 ? body.firstChild !== el : el.previousSibling !== document.getElementById(MINE[i - 1][0]))) body.insertBefore(el, want);
      if (pageNow() === m[1] && !el.classList.contains('w197on')) el.classList.add('w197on');
    });
    return before !== body.firstChild;
  }
  function set(id, html) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (!el) return; if (ST.html[id] !== html || el.innerHTML === '') { ST.html[id] = html; el.innerHTML = html; } }

  /* ---------- 1. Needs you ---------- */
  function needs(c) {
    var out = [], O = h.O(), S = h.S() || {}, R = h.R();
    var hrTools = !R || typeof R.hrTools !== 'function' || R.hrTools();
    /* an admin item: the first password, when it is not set (never its value) */
    if (h.isAdmin() && S.pw && !S.pw.set) {
      out.push({ red: false, html: '<p><b>Set the first password.</b><span class="w285sub">Every Google account the Hub makes starts with it, and Google asks the new person to change it. At least 8 characters, with a letter and a digit.</span>' + (O ? O.noteHtml('pw') : '') + '</p>',
        acts: '<input type="password" autocomplete="new-password" aria-label="The first password" data-o271i="pw" data-f="pw"><button type="button" class="w285b pri" data-o271a="pwset" data-f="pw"' + (S.busy && S.busy.pw ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Set</button>' });
    }
    var tasks = {};
    c.people.forEach(function (P) {
      var f = P.flow, nm = '<b>' + E(P.name) + '</b>';
      if (f && f.steps) {
        W.AUTO.forEach(function (a) {
          var r = h.stepRow(f, a[0]); if (!r) return;
          if (r.status === 'failed' || h.stuck(r)) {
            out.push({ red: true, html: '<p>' + nm + ': ' + E(a[1]) + (r.status === 'failed' ? ' failed' : ' is stuck') + (r.detail ? '. <span class="w285sub">' + E(r.detail) + '</span>' : '.') + '</p>',
              acts: '<button type="button" class="w285b" data-o271a="retry" data-f="' + E(P.fid) + '" data-step="' + E(a[0]) + '"' + (S.busy && S.busy['r' + P.fid + a[0]] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Retry</button>' + openBtn(P, 'su', 'Open') });
          } else if (r.status === 'hr_task') (tasks[a[0]] = tasks[a[0]] || []).push(P);
        });
      }
      if (P.turn && P.turn.who === 'hr' && P.stage !== 'weeks' && P.stage !== 'later' && P.stage !== 'starting') {
        out.push({ red: false, html: '<p>' + nm + ': ' + E(P.turn.t.replace(/^HR to /, '')) + '.</p>', acts: openBtn(P, 'ov', 'Open') });
      } else if (P.turn && P.turn.who === 'mgr' && P.mgr && f && !f.proposed_at && !f.call_at && hrTools) {
        var since = f.accepted_on || h.dayOf(f.offer_sent_at);
        if (since && h.diffDays(h.today(), since) >= 1) {
          out.push({ red: false, html: '<p>' + nm + ': ' + E(first(P.mgr.name)) + ' has not proposed times.<span class="w285sub">' + (f.accepted_on ? 'Accepted ' + E(fmtDay(f.accepted_on)) : 'Offer sent ' + E(fmtDay(f.offer_sent_at))) + '</span></p>',
            acts: (f.propose_path || f.status === 'offered' ? '<button type="button" class="w285b" data-o275a="askmgr" data-f="' + E(P.fid) + '"' + (S.busy && S.busy['m' + P.fid] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Ask ' + E(first(P.mgr.name)) + ' again</button>' : '') + openBtn(P, 'ov', 'Open') });
        }
      } else if (P.turn && P.turn.who === 'mgr' && f && f.hire_answer === 'no') {
        out.push({ red: false, html: '<p>' + nm + ': ' + E(first(P.name)) + ' said no to the times.</p>', acts: openBtn(P, 'ov', 'Open') });
      }
      if (P.plan && P.plan.behindWeek && (P.stage === 'weeks' || P.stage === 'later')) {
        var ended = P.plan.sections.filter(function (s) { return s.ended && s.done < s.total; }).pop();
        out.push({ red: true, html: '<p>' + nm + ': ' + plural(P.plan.behind, 'step') + ' overdue.' + (ended ? '<span class="w285sub">' + E(ended.label) + ' ended ' + E(fmtDay(ended.end)) + '.</span>' : '') + '</p>',
          acts: (P.email ? '<a class="w285b" href="mailto:' + E(P.email) + '">Message ' + E(first(P.name)) + '</a>' : '') + openBtn(P, 'pl', 'Open') });
      }
    });
    Object.keys(tasks).forEach(function (k) {
      var L = tasks[k], words = TASK[k] || [W.AUTO.filter(function (a) { return a[0] === k; })[0][1].toLowerCase(), W.AUTO.filter(function (a) { return a[0] === k; })[0][1].toLowerCase()];
      var names = L.map(function (P) { return E(P.name); });
      var who = names.length > 3 ? names.slice(0, 3).join(', ') + ' and ' + (names.length - 3) + ' more' : names.length > 1 ? names.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + names[names.length - 1] : names[0];
      var open = ST.open['t' + k];
      out.push({ red: false, html: '<p><b>' + who + '</b>: ' + E(L.length === 1 ? words[0] : words[1]) + '.</p>',
        acts: L.length === 1 ? openBtn(L[0], 'su', 'Open') : '<button type="button" class="w285b" data-w285fold="t' + E(k) + '" aria-expanded="' + !!open + '">' + (open ? 'Hide the list' : 'Open the list') + '</button>',
        more: L.length > 1 && open ? L.map(function (P) { return { red: false, html: '<p><b>' + E(P.name) + '</b>: ' + E(words[0]) + '.</p>', acts: openBtn(P, 'su', 'Open') }; }) : null });
    });
    /* a hire asking for help (wave39's onboarding_help, open ones): Dealt with is wave166's own button (onboarding_help_resolve) */
    W.helpAll().forEach(function (q) {
      var P = c.people.filter(function (x) { return x.onb && String(x.onb.id) === String(q.onboarding_id); })[0];
      out.unshift({ red: true, html: '<p><b>' + E(P ? P.name : q.person || 'Someone') + '</b> asked for help' + (q.step ? ' with ' + E(q.step) : '') + (q.note ? ': \u201c' + E(q.note) + '\u201d' : '.') + (q.at ? '<span class="w285sub">' + E(fmtDay(q.at)) + '</span>' : '') + '</p>',
        acts: '<button type="button" class="w285b" data-w166resolve="' + E(q.id) + '">Dealt with</button>' + (P ? openBtn(P, 'ov', 'Open') : '') });
    });
    c.backed.forEach(function (P) {
      var n = removals(P.flow);
      if (n) out.push({ red: false, html: '<p><b>' + E(P.name) + '</b> backed out: take ' + plural(n, 'thing') + ' out by hand.</p>', acts: openBtn(P, 'ov', 'Open') });
    });
    return out;
  }
  /* how many things a flow that backed out left to take out (wave271 draws the list itself, on its card) */
  function removals(f) {
    if (!f || f.practice) return 0;
    var n = 0, st = {}; (f.steps || []).forEach(function (r) { st[r.step] = r.status; });
    if (st.google === 'done' && f.wa_email) n++;
    if (st.roster === 'done' && f.member_id) n++;
    if (f.slack_channel_id) n++;
    if (st.hub === 'done') n++;
    return n;
  }
  function openBtn(P, tab, label) { return '<button type="button" class="w285b" data-w285open="' + E(P.key) + '" data-tab="' + tab + '" data-ctx="hr">' + E(label) + '</button>'; }
  function itemHtml(it) { return '<div class="w285it"><i class="w285dot' + (it.red ? ' r' : '') + '"></i>' + it.html + '<span class="w285acts">' + (it.acts || '') + '</span></div>' + (it.more ? it.more.map(itemHtml).join('') : ''); }

  /* ---------- 2. Everyone, by stage ---------- */
  function rowHtml(P) {
    var g = P.prog || {}, mgr = P.mgr ? E(P.mgr.name) : 'No manager yet';
    return '<div class="w285row" data-w285key="' + E(P.key) + '">'
      + '<div class="w285who"><b>' + E(P.name) + (P.practice ? '<span class="w285tag">Practice</span>' : '') + '</b><small>' + (P.role ? E(P.role) : 'No role on file') + '<span class="m2"> &middot; ' + (P.mgr ? 'Manager: ' + mgr : mgr) + '</span></small></div>'
      + '<div class="w285mgr">' + mgr + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w285turn' + (P.turn.cls ? ' ' + P.turn.cls : '') + '">' + E(P.turn.t) + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w285prog">' + (g.pct != null ? '<div class="w285bar" role="img" aria-label="' + E(g.label) + '"><i style="width:' + Math.max(0, Math.min(100, g.pct)) + '%"></i></div>' : '') + '<div class="w285pct">' + E(g.label || '') + '</div></div>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w285b" data-w285open="' + E(P.key) + '" data-ctx="hr" aria-label="Open ' + E(P.name) + '">Open</button></div>';
  }
  function everyone(c) {
    var ppl = c.people, behind = ppl.filter(function (P) { return P.plan && P.plan.behind && (P.stage === 'weeks' || P.stage === 'later'); }).length;
    var mon = h.today().slice(0, 7), fin = c.finished.filter(function (P) { return P.onb && h.dayOf(P.onb.completed_at).slice(0, 7) === mon; }).length;
    var bits = [ppl.length + ' onboarding'];
    if (behind) bits.push(behind + ' behind');
    if (fin) bits.push(fin + ' finished this month');
    var html = '<p class="w285sum">' + bits.join(' &middot; ') + '</p>';
    if (!ppl.length) html += '<p class="w285none">Nobody is onboarding right now. When someone accepts an offer, they show here.</p>';
    STAGES.forEach(function (s) {
      var L = ppl.filter(function (P) { return P.stage === s[0]; }); if (!L.length) return;
      html += '<div class="w285stg">' + E(s[1]) + ' <span>' + L.length + '</span></div><div class="w285box">' + L.map(rowHtml).join('') + '</div>';
    });
    if (c.finished.length) {
      html += '<p class="w285done">' + (c.finished.length === 1 ? '1 member finished their onboarding.' : c.finished.length + ' members finished their onboarding.') + ' <button type="button" class="w285link" data-w285fold="done" aria-expanded="' + ST.seeDone + '">' + (ST.seeDone ? 'Hide them' : 'See them') + '</button></p>';
      if (ST.seeDone) html += '<div class="w285box w285fin">' + c.finished.map(function (P) { return '<div class="w285it"><p><b>' + E(P.name) + '</b><span class="w285sub">' + E(P.turn.t) + (P.plan && P.plan.total ? ' &middot; ' + E(P.prog.label) : '') + '</span></p><span class="w285acts">' + openBtn(P, 'pl', 'Open') + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    if (c.backed.length) {
      html += '<p class="w285done">' + (c.backed.length === 1 ? '1 hire backed out in the last 30 days.' : c.backed.length + ' hires backed out in the last 30 days.') + ' <button type="button" class="w285link" data-w285fold="backed" aria-expanded="' + ST.seeBacked + '">' + (ST.seeBacked ? 'Hide them' : 'See them') + '</button></p>';
      if (ST.seeBacked) html += '<div class="w285box w285fin">' + c.backed.map(function (P) { return '<div class="w285it"><p><b>' + E(P.name) + (P.practice ? '<span class="w285tag">Practice</span>' : '') + '</b><span class="w285sub">' + E(P.prog.label || 'Backed out') + '</span></p><span class="w285acts">' + openBtn(P, 'ov', 'Open') + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- the page ---------- */
  function pageHtml() {
    var S = h.S() || {}, c = W.collect('hr', { finished: ST.seeDone });
    var html = '';
    if (S.err) html += '<div class="o271err" style="margin:0 0 14px">' + E(S.err) + '</div>';
    var n = needs(c);
    html += '<section class="w285sec" aria-labelledby="w285hn"><h2 class="w285h" id="w285hn">Needs you</h2>'
      + (!S.loaded && !S.err ? '<p class="w285none">Loading...</p>' : n.length ? '<div class="w285box">' + n.map(itemHtml).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="w285none">Nothing needs you.</p>') + '</section>';
    html += '<section class="w285sec" aria-labelledby="w285he"><h2 class="w285h" id="w285he">Everyone</h2>' + everyone(c) + '</section>';
    return html;
  }
  /* the Plan template page's settings line: the first password once it is set (admins only) */
  function setHtml() {
    var S = h.S() || {}, O = h.O();
    if (!h.isAdmin() || !S.pw || !S.pw.set) return '';
    var open = ST.open.pw;
    return '<div class="w285box" style="margin:0 0 16px"><div class="w285it"><p><b>First password:</b> set' + (S.pw.set_at ? ' on ' + E(O && O.dayLong ? O.dayLong(S.pw.set_at) : fmtDay(S.pw.set_at)) : '') + '.<span class="w285sub">Every Google account the Hub makes starts with it; only the welcome email carries it.</span>' + (O ? O.noteHtml('pw') : '') + '</p>'
      + '<span class="w285acts">' + (open ? '<input type="password" autocomplete="new-password" aria-label="A new first password" data-o271i="pw" data-f="pw"><button type="button" class="w285b pri" data-o271a="pwset" data-f="pw"' + (S.busy && S.busy.pw ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Set</button><button type="button" class="w285b" data-w285fold="pw">Cancel</button>'
        : '<button type="button" class="w285b" data-w285fold="pw">Change it</button>') + '</span></div></div>';
  }
  function insHtml() {
    if (document.getElementById('w145quiz')) return '';
    return '<div class="w285box"><div class="w285it"><p>No onboarding quiz answers yet. The policy quiz and the 30 day quiz show here, with what new members said about their onboarding, once someone takes one.</p></div></div>';
  }
  var pend = null, busy = false;
  function paint() {
    if (busy) return; busy = true;
    try {
      if (!onHrc() || !document.getElementById('w46body')) return;
      ensure();
      /* the first password box survives a repaint as a property, never in the HTML */
      var pw = document.querySelector('#w285 [data-o271i="pw"],#w285set [data-o271i="pw"]'), typed = pw ? pw.value : '', had = pw && document.activeElement === pw;
      var k = pageNow();
      if (k === 'onboarding') set('w285', pageHtml());
      if (k === 'plan') { var S = h.S(), O = h.O(); if (h.isAdmin() && S && S.pw == null && O && O.readPw && !ST.pwAsked) { ST.pwAsked = true; O.readPw().then(paintSoon); } set('w285set', setHtml()); }
      if (k === 'insights') set('w285ins', insHtml());
      if (typed) { var again = document.querySelector('#w285 [data-o271i="pw"],#w285set [data-o271i="pw"]'); if (again) { again.value = typed; if (had) try { again.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
      typed = '';
      /* wave271's own copy of the box is not drawn twice */
      var dup = document.querySelector('#o271 [data-o271pw]'); if (dup) dup.remove();
    } finally { busy = false; }
  }
  function paintSoon() { clearTimeout(pend); pend = setTimeout(function () { try { paint(); } catch (e) { /* the next one */ } }, 30); }
  W.paintPage = paint; W.paintPageSoon = paintSoon;
  /* the open help requests: wave39's list (HR) and the lead's card (the manager), one shape: {id, onboarding_id, step, note, at} */
  W.helpAll = function () {
    var out = [], seen = {};
    try { (typeof WA39_HELP !== 'undefined' ? WA39_HELP : []).forEach(function (x) { if (x && (x.status == null || x.status === 'open') && !seen[x.id]) { seen[x.id] = 1; out.push({ id: x.id, onboarding_id: x.onboarding_id, step: x.step_name || '', note: x.note || '', at: x.created_at || null }); } }); } catch (e) { /* none */ }
    try { var L = window.w166 && window.w166.state && window.w166.state().lead; ((L && L.people) || []).forEach(function (p) { (p.help || []).forEach(function (q) { if (q && !seen[q.id]) { seen[q.id] = 1; out.push({ id: q.id, onboarding_id: p.onboarding_id, step: q.step || '', note: q.note || '', at: q.at || null, person: p.name }); } }); }); } catch (e) { /* none */ }
    return out;
  };
  W.helpOf = function (onbId) { return W.helpAll().filter(function (q) { return onbId != null && String(q.onboarding_id) === String(onbId); }); };
  /* after wave166 marks a request dealt with, wave39's list is read again (wave166 refreshes the lead's card itself) */
  window.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w166resolve]'); if (!b) return;
    var id = b.getAttribute('data-w166resolve');
    setTimeout(async function () {
      try { if (typeof WA39_HELP !== 'undefined') WA39_HELP = WA39_HELP.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(id); }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (typeof wa39OnbLoad === 'function') await wa39OnbLoad(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      W.paintAll();
    }, 900);
  }, true);

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  /* wave271's painter: after it, the page (and the panel and Home, see c_panel.js) draw from the same state */
  (function hook(tries) {
    var R = h.R();
    if (!R || typeof R.paint !== 'function') { if ((tries || 0) < 20) setTimeout(function () { hook((tries || 0) + 1); }, 500); return; }
    if (R.paint.__w285) return;
    var orig = R.paint;
    R.paint = function () { var out = orig.apply(this, arguments); try { W.afterPaint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
    R.paint.__w285 = true;
  })(0);
  W.afterPaint = function () {
    try { if (W.stripOpen) W.stripOpen(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    paintSoon();
    try { if (W.paintPanel) W.paintPanel(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (W.paintHomeSoon) W.paintHomeSoon(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  };
  W.paintAll = function () { paintSoon(); try { if (W.paintPanel) W.paintPanel(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { if (W.paintHomeSoon) W.paintHomeSoon(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } };
  function arm(tries) {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel) { if ((tries || 0) < 40) setTimeout(function () { arm((tries || 0) + 1); }, 800); return; }
    if (panel.dataset.w285mo) return; panel.dataset.w285mo = '1';
    new MutationObserver(function (list) {
      for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        var t = list[i].target;
        if (t && t.closest && (t.closest('#w285') || t.closest('#w285set') || t.closest('#w285ins'))) continue;
        paintSoon(); return;
      }
    }).observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    new MutationObserver(function () { pages(); paintSoon(); if (pageNow() === 'onboarding') W.loadPositions(); }).observe(panel, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-w197', 'class'] });
    paintSoon();
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa285 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var out = await __sa285.apply(this, arguments); try { pages(); arm(0); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } return out; };
  }
  arm(0);
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(paintSoon, 150); });

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var fd = t.closest('[data-w285fold]');
    if (fd) {
      var k = fd.getAttribute('data-w285fold');
      if (k === 'done') ST.seeDone = !ST.seeDone; else if (k === 'backed') ST.seeBacked = !ST.seeBacked; else ST.open[k] = !ST.open[k];
      paint();
      var back = document.querySelector('[data-w285fold="' + k + '"]'); if (back) try { back.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      return;
    }
    /* the page's own help, in the words of this page (capture: before wave46's general help) */
  });
  var HELP = {
    onboarding: ['Onboarding', 'From offer to full member, one row per person.', ['<b>Needs you</b>: only what waits on you or on HR, one line and one button each. When it is empty, nothing waits.',
      '<b>Everyone</b>: each person under their stage (Offer out, Accepted, Starting, Weeks 1 to 4, 60 and 90 days), with their manager, whose turn it is and one progress figure. Finished plans are counted under the list.',
      '<b>Open</b>: the person\'s page. Overview has their timeline and the actions (Change time, Change manager, Book the 1-on-1, Edit the welcome email, They backed out). Setup has the Hub\'s twelve steps, Plan has their plan by section with the role trainings, Academy has their trainings.',
      'Onboarding starts when someone accepts an offer. For someone who joined without recruitment, start a plan from their Team profile.',
      'Progress counts real steps only: a group of steps is done when everything in it is done, and a step done in the Avenue Academy counts. Behind means steps whose section has ended are still open.']],
    plan: ['Plan template', 'The plan everyone starts from.', ['Every week and step a new person gets. Changes reach everyone still onboarding: a step moved to another week takes that week\'s due date in every plan in progress.',
      'Edit what people see sets a week\'s title, photo, goals, why it matters and the lesson that opens it. Each step can be edited, retired or linked to the Avenue Academy, so it ticks itself when done there.']],
    insights: ['Onboarding insights', 'How onboarding is going for the people who did it.', ['The policy quiz and the 30 day quiz, read from their Google Forms, and what new members said about their onboarding.']]
  };
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var hb = t.closest('[data-w46help]'); if (!hb || !onHrc()) return;
    var k = pageNow(), x = HELP[k]; if (!x || typeof openModal !== 'function') return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    openModal('<h3>' + E(x[0]) + '</h3><div class="w43hm"><p>' + E(x[1]) + '</p>' + x[2].map(function (p) { return '<p>' + p + '</p>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w285hc">Got it</button></div>');
    var c = document.getElementById('w285hc'); if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { try { closeModal(); } catch (x2) { /* fine */ } });
  }, true);
})();
/* ---------- wave285 c_panel.js: one person, opened: Overview, Setup, Plan, Academy ----------
   The panel draws wave271's or wave275's card for the person's flow into a detached element (window.o271R.readyCard / startedCard, so the buttons
   and words are theirs, for HR and for a manager alike) and places its parts: the action row and whatever opens under it (Change time, Change manager,
   Book the 1-on-1, the picker, Edit the welcome email, They backed out) on Overview; the twelve automatic steps (window.o271.stepHtml) and the access
   block on Setup. Everything sits inside one element with data-o271flow, so wave271's and wave275's document handlers find their fields there; the
   same card is taken out of wave271's hidden section (#o271) and Home's #o275mine after each paint (W.stripOpen). */
(function () {
  var W = window.w285; if (!W || W.__panel) return; W.__panel = true;
  var h = W.h, E = h.E, plural = h.plural, first = h.first, fmtDay = h.fmtDay;
  var PAN = { key: null, ctx: 'hr', tab: 'ov', from: null, folds: {}, fid: null, ask: {}, asked: {} };
  var TABS = [['ov', 'Overview'], ['su', 'Setup'], ['pl', 'Plan'], ['ac', 'Academy']];

  function root() {
    var r = document.getElementById('w285pan');
    if (!r) {
      r = document.createElement('div'); r.id = 'w285pan';
      r.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w285x="1"></div><div class="pn" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w285pt" tabindex="-1"></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(r);
    }
    return r;
  }
  function person() { return PAN.key ? W.lookup(PAN.ctx, PAN.key) : null; }

  /* ---------- the card's parts ---------- */
  function cardOf(P) {
    var R = h.R(); if (!R || !P || !P.flow || P.kind === 'backed') return null;
    var html = '';
    try { html = P.kind === 'started' ? R.startedCard(P.flow) : R.readyCard(P.flow); } catch (e) { return null; }
    var d = document.createElement('div'); d.innerHTML = html;
    var card = d.firstElementChild; if (!card) return null;
    var kids = [].slice.call(card.children), last = -1, out = { notes: [], lines: [], btns: null, after: [], acc: null };
    kids.forEach(function (k, i) { if (k.classList.contains('o271btns')) last = i; });
    kids.forEach(function (k, i) {
      if (k.classList.contains('o271top')) return;
      if (i === last) { out.btns = k; return; }
      if (last >= 0 && i > last) { out.after.push(k); return; }
      if (k.tagName === 'OL' && k.classList.contains('o271st')) return;   /* the steps: drawn on Setup, one by one */
      if (k.hasAttribute('data-o278acc')) { var ol = k.querySelector('ol'); if (ol) ol.remove(); out.acc = k; return; }
      if (k.hasAttribute('data-o275ticks')) return;   /* the hire's ticks: on Setup */
      if (k.classList.contains('o271okn') || k.classList.contains('o271warn') || k.classList.contains('o271err')) { out.notes.push(k); return; }
      if (/^The other steps run when/.test((k.textContent || '').trim())) return;
      out.lines.push(k);
    });
    return out;
  }
  function outer(list) { return (list || []).map(function (k) { return k.outerHTML; }).join(''); }

  /* ---------- Overview ---------- */
  function timeline(P) {
    var f = P.flow, J = [], t0 = h.today(), p = P.plan;
    if (f || P.cand) {
      var sent = (f && f.offer_sent_at) || (P.cand && P.cand.offer_sent_at);
      J.push({ t: 'Offer sent', d: sent ? fmtDay(sent) : '', on: !!sent });
      J.push({ t: 'Accepted', d: f && f.accepted_on ? fmtDay(f.accepted_on) : '', on: !!(f && f.accepted_on) });
      J.push({ t: f && f.call_at ? 'Times booked' : (f && f.proposed_at ? 'Times proposed' : 'Times booked'), d: f && f.call_at ? 'call ' + fmtDay(f.call_at) : (f && f.proposed_at ? fmtDay(f.proposed_at) : ''), on: !!(f && f.call_at) });
      J.push({ t: 'Day one', d: P.dayOne ? fmtDay(P.dayOne) : '', on: !!(P.dayOne && P.dayOne <= t0) });
    } else if (p && p.start) J.push({ t: 'Plan started', d: fmtDay(p.start), on: p.start <= t0 });
    if (p) p.sections.forEach(function (s) {
      J.push({ t: s.label + (s.total ? ': ' + s.done + ' of ' + s.total + ' done' : ''), d: s.end ? (s.ended ? 'ended ' : 'ends ') + fmtDay(s.end) : '', on: s.ended || s.done >= s.total, late: s.ended && s.done < s.total });
    });
    else if (!P.finished) ['Weeks 1 to 4', '60 days', '90-day review'].forEach(function (t) { J.push({ t: t, d: '', on: false }); });
    var now = false;
    return '<ul class="w285tl">' + J.map(function (x) {
      var cls = x.on ? '' : (!now ? (now = true, 'now') : 'next');
      return '<li class="' + cls + '"><span>' + (cls === 'now' ? '<b>' + E(x.t) + '</b>' : E(x.t)) + (x.late ? ' <em class="late" style="color:#B3402F;margin:0">(open steps)</em>' : '') + '</span>' + (x.d ? '<em>' + E(x.d) + '</em>' : '') + '</li>';
    }).join('') + '</ul>';
  }
  function overview(P, C) {
    var html = '<p class="w285now' + (P.turn.cls === 'late' ? ' late' : '') + '">Now: <b>' + E(P.turn.t) + '</b></p>';
    if (C) html += outer(C.notes);
    /* a help request from the hire, with wave166's own Dealt with */
    (P.onb && W.helpOf ? W.helpOf(P.onb.id) : []).forEach(function (q) {
      html += '<div class="o271warn" style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap"><span style="flex:1 1 200px;min-width:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><b>Asked for help</b>' + (q.step ? ' with ' + E(q.step) : '') + (q.note ? ': \u201c' + E(q.note) + '\u201d' : '.') + '</span><button type="button" class="o271b" data-w166resolve="' + E(q.id) + '">Dealt with</button></div>';
    });
    if (P.kind === 'backed') {
      var src = document.querySelector('#o271 [data-o271backed="' + String(P.fid).replace(/"/g, '') + '"]');
      return html + (src ? src.outerHTML : '<p class="w285line">They backed out' + (P.flow && P.flow.updated_at ? ' on ' + E(fmtDay(P.flow.updated_at)) : '') + '. Nothing else to do here.</p>');
    }
    html += timeline(P);
    var menu = C && C.btns ? C.btns.innerHTML : '';
    if (!P.flow && P.email) menu += '<a class="o271b" href="mailto:' + E(P.email) + '">Message ' + E(first(P.name)) + '</a>';
    if (P.onb && h.isHr() && h.onbById(P.onb.id)) menu += '<button type="button" class="o271b" data-w37open="' + E(P.onb.id) + '">Open the full checklist</button>';
    if (menu) html += '<div class="w285lbl">Actions</div><div class="w285menu">' + menu + '</div>';
    if (C) html += outer(C.after);
    if (C && C.lines.length) html += '<details class="w285fold" data-w285f="more"' + (PAN.folds.more ? ' open' : '') + '><summary><span>More about this onboarding</span><span></span></summary><div class="in">' + outer(C.lines) + '</div></details>';
    if (!P.flow && !P.cand) html += '<p class="w285line mut">They joined without an offer through the Hub, so there are no automatic steps for them.</p>';
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- Setup ---------- */
  function setup(P, C) {
    var f = P.flow, O = h.O(), S = h.S() || {}, html = '';
    if (f && P.kind !== 'backed' && O && O.stepHtml) {
      var c = W.setupCount(f), open = [], rest = [];
      var line = c.done + ' of ' + c.total + ' done by the Hub' + (c.hr ? ' &middot; ' + c.hr + ' for HR' : '') + (c.failed ? ' &middot; ' + c.failed + ' failed' : '');
      W.AUTO.forEach(function (a, i) {
        var r = h.stepRow(f, a[0]);
        if (!r) { rest.push('<li><div class="ttl">' + (i + 1) + '. ' + E(a[1]) + ' <span class="o271tag">Not run yet</span></div></li>'); return; }
        var shown = r, id = P.fid;
        if ((S.busy && (S.busy['w278l' + id] || S.busy['w278c' + id])) && (a[0] === 'groups' || a[0] === 'drives')) shown = Object.assign({}, r, { status: 'Re-running', detail: null });
        else if (a[0] === 'channel' && r.status === 'pending') shown = Object.assign({}, r, { status: 'waiting', detail: 'Waiting for them to join Slack' });
        var li = O.stepHtml(Object.assign({}, f, { id: id, steps: [shown] }), a[0], a[1], i + 1);
        (r.status === 'failed' || r.status === 'hr_task' || h.stuck(r) ? open : rest).push(li);
      });
      html += '<p class="w285line"><b>' + line + '</b></p>';
      if (open.length) html += '<div class="w285lbl">' + (c.failed ? 'Needs a look' : 'For HR') + '</div><ol class="o271st">' + open.join('') + '</ol>';
      html += '<details class="w285fold" data-w285f="all12"' + (PAN.folds.all12 ? ' open' : '') + '><summary><span>' + (open.length ? 'The other ' + rest.length + ' steps' : 'All ' + rest.length + ' steps') + '</span><span></span></summary><div class="in"><ol class="o271st">' + rest.join('') + '</ol></div></details>';
      if (C && C.acc) html += '<div class="w285lbl">Groups, drives and the welcome</div>' + C.acc.outerHTML;
      var tk = W.ticks(f), mine = {}; tk.forEach(function (r) { mine[r[0]] = 1; });
      html += '<div class="w285lbl">' + E(first(P.name) ? first(P.name) + '\'s own setup' : 'Their own setup') + ': ' + tk.length + ' of ' + W.RUNDOWN.length + '</div>'
        + W.RUNDOWN.map(function (r) { return '<div class="w285step' + (mine[r[0]] ? ' ok' : '') + '"><i>' + (mine[r[0]] ? '&#10003;' : '') + '</i><span>' + E(r[1]) + '</span></div>'; }).join('');
    } else if (P.kind === 'backed') html += '<p class="w285line">They backed out, so the Hub runs nothing more for them.</p>';
    else if (P.cand) html += '<p class="w285line">The Hub makes their accounts when they accept the offer.</p>';
    else html += '<p class="w285line">' + E(first(P.name) || 'They') + ' joined before automatic setup, so the setup steps in their plan count as done.</p>';
    var hrs = (P.onb && typeof WA39_HRS !== 'undefined' ? WA39_HRS : []).filter(function (x) { return P.onb && String(x.onboarding_id) === String(P.onb.id); });
    if (hrs.length && h.isHr()) html += '<div class="w285lbl">HR\'s own checklist</div><div class="w285menu"><button type="button" class="o271b" data-w39hr="' + E(P.onb.id) + '">Open it: ' + hrs.filter(function (x) { return x.done; }).length + ' of ' + hrs.length + ' done</button></div>';
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- Plan ---------- */
  function startBtn(P) {
    if (!h.isHr() || P.mid == null || P.cand) return '';
    var busy = PAN.asked[P.mid];
    if (PAN.ask[P.mid]) return '<p class="w285line">The plan starts today: every step of the Plan template is copied in with its due date, and what they already did in the Avenue Academy counts.</p><div class="w285menu"><button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-w285start="' + E(P.mid) + '" data-go="1" data-name="' + E(P.name) + '"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (busy ? 'Starting...' : 'Start it today') + '</button><button type="button" class="o271b" data-w285start="' + E(P.mid) + '" data-go="0">Cancel</button></div>';
    return '<div class="w285menu"><button type="button" class="o271b pri" data-w285start="' + E(P.mid) + '" data-name="' + E(P.name) + '">Start an onboarding plan</button></div>';
  }
  function planTab(P) {
    var p = P.plan;
    if (!p || !p.total) {
      if (P.flow && !P.onb) return '<p class="w285line">Their plan is made when the times are booked.</p>';
      var active = !!W.openPlanOf(P.mid);
      return '<p class="w285line">' + (p ? 'Their plan has no steps yet.' : 'No plan yet.') + '</p>' + (!active ? startBtn(P) : '');
    }
    var html = '<p class="w285line"><b>Plan: ' + p.done + ' of ' + p.total + ' done</b>' + (p.w14.total ? ' &middot; Weeks 1 to 4: ' + p.w14.done + ' of ' + p.w14.total : '') + (p.behind ? ' &middot; <span style="color:#B3402F;font-weight:700">Behind: ' + plural(p.behind, 'step') + '</span>' : '') + '</p>'
      + '<p class="w285line mut">A group of steps counts once everything in it is done; a step done in the Avenue Academy counts.</p>';
    var t0 = h.today();
    p.sections.forEach(function (s) {
      var key = 'pl:' + s.key, open = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(PAN.folds, key) ? PAN.folds[key] : s.current;
      var lastParent = null, role = false;
      var body = s.leaves.map(function (l) {
        var g = '';
        if (l.child && l.parent && l.parent !== lastParent) g = '<div class="w285grp">' + E(l.parent) + '</div>';
        lastParent = l.child ? l.parent : null;
        if (l.path === 'role60' || l.path === 'role90') role = true;
        var late = !l.done && s.ended;
        return g + '<div class="w285step' + (l.done ? ' ok' : '') + (l.child ? ' kid' : '') + '"><i>' + (l.done ? '&#10003;' : '') + '</i><span>' + E(l.name)
          + (l.academy ? '<em>Avenue Academy</em>' : '') + (l.how === 'setup' ? '<em>done at setup</em>' : l.how === 'academy' ? '<em>done in the Academy</em>' : '') + (late ? '<em class="late">overdue</em>' : '') + '</span></div>';
      }).join('');
      if (role && P.onb) body += '<div class="w285menu" style="margin-top:8px"><button type="button" class="o271b" data-w279open="' + E(P.onb.id) + '">Role trainings</button></div>';
      html += '<details class="w285fold" data-w285f="' + E(key) + '"' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '><summary><span><b>' + E(s.label) + '</b>' + (s.end ? ' &middot; ' + (s.ended ? 'ended ' : 'ends ') + E(fmtDay(s.end)) : '') + '</span><span>' + s.done + ' of ' + s.total + ' done</span></summary><div class="in">' + body + '</div></details>';
    });
    void t0;
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- Academy ---------- */
  function acadTab(P) {
    var list = null, who = { name: P.name, email: P.email };
    if (P.lead && !(h.academy() && (h.academy().prog || []).length)) list = (P.lead.academy || []).filter(function (a) { return a && a.total; }).map(function (a) { return { title: a.title || a.key, done: Number(a.done) || 0, total: Number(a.total) || 0 }; });
    else list = W.avenues(who);
    if (!list) {
      if (window.w122 && typeof window.w122.load === 'function' && !PAN.acadAsked) { PAN.acadAsked = true; Promise.resolve(window.w122.load()).then(paintPanel, paintPanel); }
      return '<p class="w285line mut">Loading the Avenue Academy...</p>';
    }
    if (!list.length) return '<p class="w285line">No Avenue Academy trainings for them yet.</p>';
    var d = 0, t = 0; list.forEach(function (a) { d += a.done; t += a.total; });
    return '<p class="w285line"><b>Academy trainings ' + d + ' of ' + t + '</b></p><div class="w285box">' + list.map(function (a) {
      return '<div class="w285it"><p><b>' + E(a.title) + '</b></p><span class="w285acts" style="color:rgba(16,63,57,.64);font-size:13.5px">' + a.done + ' of ' + a.total + '</span></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div><p class="w285line mut" style="margin-top:10px">Academy trainings count on their own. They are not the plan\'s figure.</p>';
  }

  /* ---------- the panel ---------- */
  function sub(P) {
    var bits = [P.role || 'No role on file', P.mgr ? 'Manager: ' + P.mgr.name : 'No manager yet'];
    if (P.day) bits.push(P.day <= 90 ? 'Day ' + P.day + ' of 90' : 'Day ' + P.day);
    else if (P.stage === 'starting' && P.dayOne) bits.push('Starts ' + fmtDay(P.dayOne));
    else if (P.stage === 'offer') bits.push('Offer out');
    else if (P.stage === 'accepted') bits.push('Accepted');
    return bits.map(E).join(' &middot; ');
  }
  function html(P) {
    if (!P) return '<div class="ph"><div><h3 id="w285pt">Not on the list</h3><small>This person is no longer onboarding.</small></div><button type="button" class="x" data-w285x="1" aria-label="Close">&times;</button></div>';
    var C = cardOf(P), tab = PAN.tab, body;
    if (tab === 'su') body = setup(P, C); else if (tab === 'pl') body = planTab(P); else if (tab === 'ac') body = acadTab(P); else body = overview(P, C);
    return '<button type="button" class="back" data-w285x="1">&larr; Back</button>'
      + '<div class="ph"><div><h3 id="w285pt">' + E(P.name) + (P.practice ? '<span class="w285tag">Practice</span>' : '') + '</h3><small>' + sub(P) + '</small></div><button type="button" class="x" data-w285x="1" aria-label="Close">&times;</button></div>'
      + '<div class="w285tabs" role="tablist">' + TABS.map(function (t) { return '<button type="button" role="tab" id="w285t' + t[0] + '" aria-selected="' + (t[0] === tab) + '" aria-controls="w285tp" class="' + (t[0] === tab ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w285tab="' + t[0] + '">' + t[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div id="w285tp" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="w285t' + tab + '" class="o271" data-o271flow="' + E(P.fid != null && P.kind !== 'backed' ? P.fid : '') + '">' + body + '</div>';
  }
  /* what the person was doing survives a repaint: focus, the caret, the scroll, the grids' scroll, a password being typed */
  function keyOf(a) {
    if (!a || !a.getAttribute) return null;
    var names = ['data-o271i', 'data-o275i', 'data-o278cc', 'data-o271a', 'data-o275a', 'data-o278a', 'data-w285tab', 'data-w285start', 'data-kind', 'data-step', 'data-f'], sel = '';
    names.forEach(function (n) { var v = a.getAttribute(n); if (v != null) sel += '[' + n + '="' + String(v).replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]'; });
    return sel || (a.classList && a.classList.contains('x') ? '.x' : null);
  }
  function paintPanel() {
    if (!PAN.key) return;
    var r = root(), pn = r.querySelector('.pn'), P = person();
    PAN.fid = P && P.fid != null && P.kind !== 'backed' ? P.fid : null;
    W.stripOpen();
    var a = document.activeElement, k = a && pn.contains(a) ? keyOf(a) : null, sel = a && k && a.selectionStart != null ? [a.selectionStart, a.selectionEnd] : null;
    var top = pn.scrollTop, grids = [].map.call(pn.querySelectorAll('.o271gw,[data-o271grid]'), function (g) { return g.scrollLeft; });
    var pws = {}; [].forEach.call(pn.querySelectorAll('input[type=password]'), function (x) { pws[x.getAttribute('data-o271i') || ''] = x.value; });
    var out = html(P);
    if (pn.__h === out) return;
    pn.innerHTML = out; pn.__h = out;
    [].forEach.call(pn.querySelectorAll('input[type=password]'), function (x) { var v = pws[x.getAttribute('data-o271i') || '']; if (v) x.value = v; });
    pws = null;
    [].forEach.call(pn.querySelectorAll('.o271gw,[data-o271grid]'), function (g, i) { if (grids[i]) g.scrollLeft = grids[i]; });
    pn.scrollTop = top;
    if (k) { var again = null; try { again = pn.querySelector(k); } catch (e) { again = null; } if (again) { try { again.focus({ preventScroll: true }); if (sel) again.setSelectionRange(sel[0], sel[1]); } catch (e) { /* not a text box */ } } }
  }
  W.paintPanel = paintPanel;
  W.stripOpen = function () {
    if (!PAN.key || PAN.fid == null) return;
    ['o271', 'o275mine'].forEach(function (id) {
      var host = document.getElementById(id); if (!host) return;
      [].forEach.call(host.querySelectorAll('[data-o271flow="' + String(PAN.fid).replace(/"/g, '') + '"]'), function (x) { x.remove(); });
    });
  };
  function open(key, tab, ctx, from) {
    PAN.key = key; PAN.tab = tab || 'ov'; PAN.ctx = ctx || 'hr'; PAN.from = from || null; PAN.folds = {}; PAN.acadAsked = false;
    var r = root(); r.classList.add('open'); document.body.classList.add('w285open');
    var pn = r.querySelector('.pn'); pn.__h = ''; pn.scrollTop = 0;
    paintPanel();
    try { (pn.querySelector('.x') || pn).focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    /* what the panel reads: HR's list when HR opens one of their own hires on Home, the plans, the Academy for its tab */
    var P = person(), S = h.S() || {};
    if (P && P.fid != null && h.isHr() && !S.loading && !(S.ready || []).concat(S.started || []).some(function (x) { return String(h.flowId(x)) === String(P.fid); }) && h.R() && h.R().load) Promise.resolve(h.R().load()).then(paintPanel, paintPanel);
    if (!h.W37().loaded && typeof wa37Load === 'function') Promise.resolve(wa37Load(false)).then(paintPanel, paintPanel);
    W.loadPositions();
  }
  function close() {
    if (!PAN.key) return;
    var from = PAN.from, fid = PAN.fid;
    PAN.key = null; PAN.fid = null;
    var r = document.getElementById('w285pan'); if (r) { r.classList.remove('open'); var pn = r.querySelector('.pn'); if (pn) { pn.innerHTML = ''; pn.__h = ''; } }
    document.body.classList.remove('w285open');
    /* the card taken out of wave271's section comes back */
    if (fid != null) { try { var R = h.R(); if (R && R.paint) R.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    if (from && document.body.contains(from)) { try { from.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    else if (PAN.key == null) { var again = from && from.getAttribute && document.querySelector('[data-w285open="' + String(from.getAttribute('data-w285open')).replace(/"/g, '') + '"]'); if (again) try { again.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  W.open = open; W.close = close; W.panel = function () { return PAN; };

  /* ---------- starting a plan for someone who joined without recruitment (the Team profile and the Plan tab): onboarding_assign, as wave46 did ---------- */
  async function startPlan(mid, name) {
    if (PAN.asked[mid] || !h.isHr()) return;
    PAN.asked[mid] = true; paintPanel(); if (W.paintProfile) W.paintProfile();
    /* the server lets any staff member assign one and keeps no guard against a second: the page asks HR only, and reads the plans again first */
    try { if (typeof wa37Load === 'function') await wa37Load(true); } catch (e) { /* the list in memory */ }
    var had = W.openPlanOf(mid);
    if (had) { h.toast('They already have an onboarding plan (' + had.status + '), so no second one was started.'); delete PAN.asked[mid]; delete PAN.ask[mid]; W.paintAll(); if (W.paintProfile) W.paintProfile(); return; }
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('onboarding_assign', { p_member_id: Number(mid), p_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'HR' });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      h.toast('Onboarding plan started' + (name ? ' for ' + name : ''));
      try { await wa37Load(true); } catch (e) { /* the next load */ }
    } catch (e) { h.toast('Could not start the plan: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    delete PAN.asked[mid]; delete PAN.ask[mid];
    W.paintAll(); if (W.paintProfile) W.paintProfile();
  }
  W.startPlan = startPlan; W.askStart = PAN.ask;
  /* a plan that is not finished (active, or any other state but completed and cancelled) */
  W.openPlanOf = function (mid) { if (mid == null) return null; return (h.W37().onbs || []).filter(function (o) { return String(o.member_id) === String(mid) && o.status !== 'completed' && o.status !== 'cancelled'; })[0] || null; };

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var o = t.closest('[data-w285open]');
    if (o) { e.preventDefault(); open(o.getAttribute('data-w285open'), o.getAttribute('data-tab') || 'ov', o.getAttribute('data-ctx') || 'hr', o); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w285x]') && t.closest('#w285pan')) { close(); return; }
    var tb = t.closest('#w285pan [data-w285tab]');
    if (tb) { PAN.tab = tb.getAttribute('data-w285tab'); var pn = root().querySelector('.pn'); paintPanel(); pn.scrollTop = 0; var nb = pn.querySelector('[data-w285tab="' + PAN.tab + '"]'); if (nb) try { nb.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    var sb = t.closest('[data-w285start]');
    if (sb && !sb.disabled) {
      var mid = sb.getAttribute('data-w285start'), go = sb.getAttribute('data-go');
      if (go === '1') startPlan(mid, sb.getAttribute('data-name') || '');
      else { if (go === '0') delete PAN.ask[mid]; else PAN.ask[mid] = true; paintPanel(); if (W.paintProfile) W.paintProfile(); }
      return;
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (!PAN.key) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      if (document.getElementById('w279sheet') || (document.getElementById('modalBack') && getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('modalBack')).display !== 'none')) return;   /* a layer above the panel closes first */
      close(); return;
    }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
      var a = document.activeElement; if (!a || !a.getAttribute || !a.getAttribute('data-w285tab')) return;
      var i = TABS.map(function (x) { return x[0]; }).indexOf(a.getAttribute('data-w285tab')), n = TABS[(i + (e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : TABS.length - 1)) % TABS.length][0];
      PAN.tab = n; paintPanel(); var nb = root().querySelector('[data-w285tab="' + n + '"]'); if (nb) try { nb.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('toggle', function (e) {
    var d = e.target; if (!d || !d.getAttribute || !d.closest || !d.closest('#w285pan')) return;
    var k = d.getAttribute('data-w285f'); if (k) PAN.folds[k] = d.open;
  }, true);
  /* a layer that opens from the panel (the checklist modal, the role picks) and then changes the plan: the panel follows */
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { if (PAN.key && e.target && e.target.closest && (e.target.closest('#w37modal') || e.target.closest('#modalBox'))) setTimeout(paintPanel, 600); }, true);
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (PAN.key) close(); });
})();
/* ---------- wave285 d_home.js: the manager's Home (one card per hire), the Team profile, the Applications hint, the walkthroughs ---------- */
(function () {
  var W = window.w285; if (!W || W.__home) return; W.__home = true;
  var h = W.h, E = h.E, first = h.first, fmtDay = h.fmtDay;

  /* ---------- Home: "Your new hires", one card each, in place of the lead's card (#w166lead) and wave275's list (#o275mine) ---------- */
  function onHome() { var x = String(location.hash || ''); return !x || x === '#' || /^#mypage(\/|$)/.test(x); }
  var STAGE = { offer: 'Offer out', accepted: 'Accepted', starting: 'Starting', weeks: 'Weeks 1 to 4', later: '60 and 90 days' };
  function weekSteps(P) {
    var L = P.lead, soon = h.addDays(h.today(), 7);
    return ((L && L.lead_steps) || []).filter(function (s) { return s && !s.done && (!s.due || String(s.due) <= soon); });
  }
  function cardHtml(P) {
    var g = P.prog || {}, t0 = h.today(), wk = weekSteps(P), me = h.meMember(), mine = !!(me && P.mgr && String(P.mgr.id) === String(me.id));
    var act = '';
    if (P.turn.who === 'mgr' && (mine || h.isHr()) && P.flow) act = '<button type="button" class="w285b pri" data-w285open="' + E(P.key) + '" data-ctx="home" data-tab="ov">' + (/book/i.test(P.turn.t) ? 'Book the times' : 'Propose times') + '</button>';
    return '<div class="w285hc" data-w285hkey="' + E(P.key) + '"><div class="top"><div><b>' + E(P.name) + (P.practice ? '<span class="w285tag">Practice</span>' : '') + '</b><small>' + E([P.role, STAGE[P.stage]].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</small></div>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w285b" data-w285open="' + E(P.key) + '" data-ctx="home" aria-label="Open ' + E(P.name) + '">Open</button></div>'
      + '<div class="w285turn' + (P.turn.cls ? ' ' + P.turn.cls : '') + '">' + E(P.turn.t) + '</div>'

/* =====================================================================
   WA v287 team overview, tasks and projects (06/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "rebuilding the UI of the overview using Emil's skills... the
   Apple design for the task management... the work stream should just
   turn into the different projects, and each task should be
   categorized in that way."
   Design: claude/06-11-26 - Team overview, tasks and projects, design
   (wave287).md, sections 2 and 3. The data half is
   build/wa287a_projects.sql (projects.is_general, projects.workstream,
   every task in a project).
   Where the work lives:
   - 116_wave116 (edited in place): the project helpers every page
     reads (w116IsGeneral, w116GeneralOf, w116ProjOf, w116ProjColor,
     w116Ink, w116Elsewhere, w116ProjForWs), the Overview rebuilt (Now,
     Needs attention, project cards; the cards later waves inject into
     keep their ids), the tabs' thumb and the clamped read in header(),
     old #team/<d>/tasks/<workstream> links opening that project.
   - 116b_wave116b (edited in place): the Reminders-style task page
     (smart lists, projects in a sidebar, rows with a tick and one line
     of facts, Undo, the drawer without Workstream, the new API).
   - 116t_wave116t (edited in place): the Tree no longer sorts by
     workstream; tasks with no project sit in General.
   - 258_wave258 (edited in place): the "Asked of" banner stays off the
     Overview (the Team inbox card holds the same requests); Agenda and
     Tasks keep it.
   - This segment: the CSS, and the motion that needs script: the tabs'
     sliding thumb, More on the read, the Overview's task rows (tick,
     open), keyboard on the tiles and cards.
   Motion follows Emil's rules: feedback on pointer-down, the tick fills
   in 160ms ease-out, the drawer uses the iOS curve (280ms in, 200ms
   out, the same way back), list switches are instant, only transform
   and opacity animate, and prefers-reduced-motion turns slides into
   short fades. Hover effects only where a fine pointer can hover.
   Runs after every wave that wraps the board or the team pages (after
   285, before 164 and 110), so its CSS lands after theirs.
   ===================================================================== */

  /* ---------- the tabs' sliding thumb (FLIP: only transform animates) ---------- */
  var TH = { last: null };
  function reduced() { try { return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function placeThumb(force) {
    var seg = document.querySelector('#w116host > .w287tabs'); if (!seg) return;
    var th = seg.querySelector('.w287thumb'), on = seg.querySelector('a.on');
    if (!th) return;
    if (!on || !on.offsetWidth) { seg.classList.remove('w287th'); return; }
    if (seg.scrollWidth > seg.clientWidth + 2 && (on.offsetLeft < seg.scrollLeft || on.offsetLeft + on.offsetWidth > seg.scrollLeft + seg.clientWidth)) seg.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, on.offsetLeft - 8);   /* the phone's scrolling tabs keep the current one in view */
    var pos = { d: seg.getAttribute('data-d'), x: on.offsetLeft, y: on.offsetTop, w: on.offsetWidth, h: on.offsetHeight };
    var key = [pos.d, pos.x, pos.y, pos.w, pos.h].join('|');
    if (!force && th.getAttribute('data-k') === key) return;
    var from = TH.last && TH.last.d === pos.d && th.getAttribute('data-k') == null ? TH.last : null;
    th.setAttribute('data-k', key);
    seg.classList.remove('w287anim');
    th.style.width = pos.w + 'px'; th.style.height = pos.h + 'px';
    if (from && !reduced() && (from.x !== pos.x || from.y !== pos.y || from.w !== pos.w)) {
      th.style.transform = 'translate(' + from.x + 'px,' + from.y + 'px) scaleX(' + (from.w / pos.w).toFixed(4) + ')';
      seg.classList.add('w287th');
      void th.offsetWidth;
      seg.classList.add('w287anim');
      th.style.transform = 'translate(' + pos.x + 'px,' + pos.y + 'px)';
    } else {
      th.style.transform = 'translate(' + pos.x + 'px,' + pos.y + 'px)';
      seg.classList.add('w287th');
    }
    TH.last = pos;
  }
  /* the read: More shows only when two lines cut it */
  function syncRead() {
    document.querySelectorAll('#w116host .w287read').forEach(function (p) {
      var b = p.parentNode && p.parentNode.querySelector('[data-w287more]'); if (!b) return;
      if (p.classList.contains('open')) { b.hidden = false; return; }
      b.hidden = !(p.scrollHeight > p.clientHeight + 2);
    });
  }
  var pend = null;
  function sync() { if (pend) return; pend = (window.requestAnimationFrame || setTimeout)(function () { pend = null; try { placeThumb(false); syncRead(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }); }
  function watch() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('team'); if (!sec || sec.__w287) return;
    sec.__w287 = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(sync).observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    sync();
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize', function () { try { var th = document.querySelector('#w116host > .w287tabs .w287thumb'); if (th) th.removeAttribute('data-k'); TH.last = null; placeThumb(true); syncRead(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
  try { if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(function () { TH.last = null; placeThumb(true); syncRead(); }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- clicks and keys on the Overview ---------- */
  function taskOf(el) { var r = el && el.closest && el.closest('#w116host .w287or[data-tid]'); return r ? r.getAttribute('data-tid') : null; }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var more = t.closest('[data-w287more]');
    if (more) { e.preventDefault(); var p = document.getElementById(more.getAttribute('aria-controls')); if (p) { var open = !p.classList.contains('open'); p.classList.toggle('open', open); more.textContent = open ? 'Less' : 'More'; more.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false'); } return; }
    var id = taskOf(t); if (!id || !window.w116Board) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    if (t.closest('[data-act="toggle"]')) { try { window.w116Board.complete(id); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    try { window.w116Board.open(id); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key !== 'Enter' && e.key !== ' ') return;
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    if (taskOf(t) && t.closest('[data-act="toggle"]')) { e.preventDefault(); try { window.w116Board.complete(taskOf(t)); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    var go = t.closest('#w116host .w287tile[role="link"], #w116host .w287pc[tabindex]');
    if (go) { e.preventDefault(); go.click(); }
  });

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa287 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa287.apply(this, arguments);
      try { watch(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  window.w287Debug = function () { return { thumb: TH.last, board: window.w116Board && window.w116Board.state ? window.w116Board.state() : null }; };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v296 a link to each applicant (08/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "in each applicant ... share a link to that applicant, like just
   copying a link to the applicant so that we can send it on Slack and
   have the person open up the applicant."
   #hrcentre/applications/<id> opens the board with that applicant's sheet
   (the address 197 already accepts: its page is the second part). The
   sheet gets a "Copy link" button beside its title. The HR channel post
   for a new applicant (recruit-actions new_applicant) uses the same link.
   Page only; reads w197.state(), w197.loadCands and w197.openSheet.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.w296) return;
  var RE = /^#hrcentre\/applications\/(\d+)/;
  function linkFor(id) { return location.origin + '/#hrcentre/applications/' + id; }
  function say(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  var tries = 0, want = null;
  function openWanted() {
    var m = String(location.hash || '').match(RE); if (!m) { want = null; return; }
    if (want !== m[1]) { want = m[1]; tries = 0; }
    var W = window.w197; if (!W || !W.state) { if (tries++ < 60) setTimeout(openWanted, 500); return; }
    var S = W.state(); var s = document.getElementById('w197sheet');
    if (s && s.classList.contains('open') && String(S.open) === want) return;
    var has = (S.cands || []).some(function (c) { return String(c.id) === want; });
    if (!has) {
      if (tries === 8 && typeof W.loadCands === 'function') { try { W.loadCands(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      if (tries++ < 40) { setTimeout(openWanted, 500); return; }
      say('That applicant is not on the board any more (or you cannot see it)'); return;
    }
    try { W.openSheet(want); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function copy(id) {
    var u = linkFor(id);
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(u); say('Link copied: paste it in Slack'); }
    catch (e) { try { window.prompt('Copy this link', u); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function addButton() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w197sheet'); if (!s || !s.classList.contains('open')) return;
    var S = window.w197 && window.w197.state ? window.w197.state() : null; if (!S || !S.open) return;
    var h = s.querySelector('.pn h3'); if (!h) return;
    var b = s.querySelector('[data-w296copy]');
    if (b && b.getAttribute('data-w296copy') === String(S.open)) return;
    if (b) b.remove();
    b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'w197b sm'; b.setAttribute('data-w296copy', String(S.open));
    b.textContent = 'Copy link'; b.title = 'Copy a link that opens this applicant';
    b.style.cssText = 'margin:0 0 10px;';
    var role = s.querySelector('.pn p.role'); (role || h).insertAdjacentElement('afterend', b);
    /* the address follows the open applicant, so the browser's own Share or copy works too */
    var want2 = '#hrcentre/applications/' + S.open;
    if (String(location.hash).indexOf('#hrcentre/applications') === 0 && location.hash !== want2) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', want2); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function onShut() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w197sheet');
    if (s && !s.classList.contains('open') && RE.test(String(location.hash || ''))) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#hrcentre/applications'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } want = null; }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w296copy]') : null; if (t) { e.preventDefault(); copy(t.getAttribute('data-w296copy')); } });
  (function watch() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w197sheet');
    if (!s) { setTimeout(watch, 1000); return; }
    new MutationObserver(function () { addButton(); onShut(); }).observe(s, { childList: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
    addButton();
  })();
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (RE.test(String(location.hash || ''))) setTimeout(openWanted, 200); });
  if (RE.test(String(location.hash || ''))) setTimeout(openWanted, 1500);
  window.w296 = { link: linkFor, copy: copy, open: openWanted };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v298 Program Builder: the Program Development team's app (09/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (08/11/26): "make an app for the program development team called program builder that brings in the survey
   feedback from beneficiaries, volunteers, and community leads to a dashboard for reporting", with their SOPs; then
   "replace the Google Forms with the Hub survey and Tally ... keep every data point ... put it in a dashboard".
   Page #programs (Apps menu, gate member; access profile key 'programs'):
   - Dashboard (#programs): a period (the last 12 months by default; fiscal years run Oct to Sep) and Montreal | Toronto | Both; people served
     from the tally (age, sex, Indigenous, meal), servings, needs and barriers heard, the community leads' meal and service
     ratings, volunteer experience, clothing given, conversations, every serving in a table, and what people said.
     "Print or save as PDF" prints the page alone, for the board and funders. One call: pb_dashboard(from, to, city).
   - Programs and KPIs (#programs/kpis): the three programs' indicators (pb_kpis); the ones the Hub counts fill themselves
     for the period, the others take a value per month and city (pb_kpi_values).
   - Improvements (#programs/improve): issue, action, owner, status (pb_improvements), "you said, we did".
   - Library (#programs/library): the Program Development drive, read nightly by program-pull as hub@ (pb_library).
   Data: build/data/wa298_program_builder.sql (applied 09/11/26). The tally itself is on the team leader's and the community
   lead's own page (c/index.html, wave298). Survey questions stay in Volunteer Manager, Text settings.
   window.w298: show, load, state. */

  function range() {
    var t = new Date(), y = t.getFullYear(), fy = t.getMonth() >= 9 ? y : y - 1;
    if (P.range === 'fy') return { from: fy + '-10-01', to: (fy + 1) + '-09-30', label: 'Fiscal year ' + fy + '-' + String(fy + 1).slice(2) };
    if (P.range === 'lastfy') return { from: (fy - 1) + '-10-01', to: fy + '-09-30', label: 'Fiscal year ' + (fy - 1) + '-' + String(fy).slice(2) };
    if (P.range === 'q') { var a = new Date(t); a.setMonth(a.getMonth() - 3); return { from: iso(a), to: iso(t), label: 'The last 3 months' }; }
    if (P.range === 'year') { var b = new Date(t); b.setFullYear(b.getFullYear() - 1); return { from: iso(b), to: iso(t), label: 'The last 12 months' }; }
    if (P.range === 'cal') return { from: y + '-01-01', to: y + '-12-31', label: 'Calendar ' + y };
    return { from: '2020-01-01', to: iso(t), label: 'Since we started counting' };
  }
