  function labelOf(el) { var t = ''; if (!el) return t; el.childNodes.forEach(function (n) { if (n.nodeType === 3) t += n.textContent; else if (n.nodeType === 1 && !n.classList.contains('tabdot') && !n.classList.contains('w134n') && !n.classList.contains('caret') && n.tagName !== 'svg') t += n.textContent; }); return t.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); }
  function dotOf(el) { var d = el && el.querySelector('.tabdot'); return d ? (d.style.background || d.style.backgroundColor || '') : ''; }
  function badgeOf(el) { var n = el && el.querySelector('.w134n'); return n ? (n.textContent || '').trim() : ''; }
  function boardView() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function hashRoot() { var h = ''; try { h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || ''); } catch (e) { h = location.hash || ''; } return h.replace(/^#/, '').split('/'); }
  function activeId() { var t = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab.active'); return t ? t.getAttribute('data-tab') : ''; }
  /* the page you are on as the menu names it (Home has no marked button: its panel says so) */
  function curId() {
    var a = activeId(); if (a) return a;
    var p = document.querySelector('.panel.active'); if (p && p.id && tabEl(p.id)) return p.id;
    return hashRoot()[0] || 'mypage';
  }
  /* the signed-in person's team: their roster department, when it has a team page they can open */
  function myTeamId() {
    if (boardView()) return shownEl(tabEl('governance')) ? 'governance' : '';
    var d = '';
    try { d = (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.department) || ''; } catch (e) { d = ''; }
    if (!d) { try { var em = String((USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); var m = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.email || '').toLowerCase() === em; })[0]; d = (m && m.department) || ''; } catch (e) { d = ''; } }
    return d && shownEl(tabEl(d)) ? d : '';
  }
  /* which bar button the page you are on belongs to */
  function barKey() {
    if (PH.open) return 'menu';
    var p = hashRoot(), root = p[0] || '', act = curId();
    var first = shownEl(tabEl('mypage')) ? 'mypage' : 'overview';
    if (act === first || (!root && first === 'mypage')) return 'home';
    if (act === 'calendar') return 'cal';
    var mine = myTeamId();
    if (mine) {
      if (act === mine || (root === 'team' && p[1] === mine)) return 'team';
      var t = tabEl(act), row = t && t.closest ? t.closest('.w42d') : null, main = row ? row.querySelector(':scope > .tab') : null;
      if (main && main.getAttribute('data-tab') === mine) return 'team';
    }
    return 'menu';
  }

  /* ---------- the bar ---------- */
  function slots() {
    var out = [];
    if (shownEl(tabEl('mypage'))) out.push({ k: 'home', l: 'Home', ic: 'home', tab: 'mypage' });
    else if (tabEl('overview') && shownEl(tabEl('overview'))) out.push({ k: 'home', l: 'Dashboard', ic: 'chart', tab: 'overview' });
    if (shownEl(tabEl('calendar'))) out.push({ k: 'cal', l: 'Calendar', ic: 'cal', tab: 'calendar' });
    if (document.getElementById('waChatBtn')) out.push({ k: 'ask', l: 'Ask', ic: 'ask' });
    var mine = myTeamId();
    out.push({ k: 'team', l: boardView() ? 'Board' : (mine ? 'My team' : 'Teams'), ic: 'team', tab: mine || '' });
    out.push({ k: 'menu', l: 'Menu', ic: 'menu' });
    return out;
  }
  function paintBar() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('w163bar');
    if (!bar) {
      bar = document.createElement('nav'); bar.id = 'w163bar'; bar.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Hub');
      document.body.appendChild(bar);
    }
    var ss = slots(), key = barKey();
    var sig = ss.map(function (s) { return s.k + ':' + s.l + ':' + (s.tab || ''); }).join('|') + '#' + key;
    if (bar.getAttribute('data-sig') === sig) return;
    bar.setAttribute('data-sig', sig);
    bar.classList.toggle('n4', ss.length === 4);
    bar.innerHTML = ss.map(function (s) {
      var on = s.k !== 'ask' && s.k === key;
      return '<button type="button" class="' + (s.k === 'ask' ? 'ask' : '') + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w163slot="' + s.k + '"' + (s.tab ? ' data-tab-go="' + Esc(s.tab) + '"' : '') + (on ? ' aria-current="page"' : '') + (s.k === 'menu' ? ' aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-expanded="' + (PH.open ? 'true' : 'false') + '"' : '') + (s.k === 'ask' ? ' aria-label="Ask the Hub"' : '') + '><span class="pill">' + PIc(s.ic) + '</span>' + Esc(s.l) + '</button>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function press(el) { if (!el) return false; try { el.click(); return true; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function slotGo(k, btn) {
    if (k === 'menu') { if (PH.open) closeSheet(); else openSheet(); return; }
    if (k === 'ask') { closeSheet(true); press(document.getElementById('waChatBtn')); return; }
    var id = btn && btn.getAttribute('data-tab-go');
    if (k === 'team' && !id) { openSheet('departments'); return; }
    closeSheet(true);
    if (id) {
      /* inside an app a Hub page takes you out of it (wave153's rule); the menu's own button does the rest */
      if (!press(tabEl(id))) location.hash = '#' + id;
    }
  }

  /* ---------- the sheet ---------- */
  function ensureSheet() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w163sheet');
    if (s) return s;
    s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w163sheet'; s.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); s.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true'); s.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Menu'); s.tabIndex = -1;
    s.innerHTML = '<div class="hd"><h2>Menu</h2><button type="button" class="x" data-w163close="1" aria-label="Close the menu">' + PIc('x') + '</button></div>' +
      '<label class="q">' + PIc('search') + '<input type="search" data-w163q="1" placeholder="Find a page" aria-label="Find a page" autocomplete="off" enterkeyhint="go"></label>' +
      '<div class="bd" data-w163bd="1"></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(s);
    return s;
  }
  /* every place the menu offers, in the menu's order, as {k: an id, l: label, sec: section, dot, badge, on, el: the button to press, go: an app address, sub: [chips], mine} */
  function hubItems() {
    var b = tabBar(); if (!b) return [];
    var act = curId(), mine = myTeamId(), out = [];
    function add(el, sec) {
      if (!shownEl(el)) return;
      var id = el.getAttribute('data-tab');
      out.push({ k: id, l: labelOf(el), sec: sec, dot: dotOf(el), badge: badgeOf(el), on: id === act, el: el });
    }
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(b.children, function (el) {
      if (el.classList.contains('tab')) { add(el, ''); return; }
      if (!el.classList.contains('tab-group') || !shownEl(el)) return;
      var h = el.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group-h'), sec = h ? labelOf(h) : '';
      var host = el.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group-p') || el;
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(host.children, function (c) {
        if (c.classList.contains('w42d')) {
          var main = c.querySelector(':scope > .tab');
          /* a team page this person cannot open (wave127 flattens the row): its own pages stand alone */
          if (!main || !shownEl(main)) { Array.prototype.forEach.call(c.querySelectorAll('.tab'), function (t) { if (t !== main) add(t, sec); }); return; }
          var id = main.getAttribute('data-tab');
          var subs = Array.prototype.filter.call(c.querySelectorAll('.w42sub .tab'), shownEl).map(function (t) { return { k: t.getAttribute('data-tab'), l: labelOf(t), el: t, on: t.getAttribute('data-tab') === act }; });
          out.push({ k: id, l: labelOf(main), sec: sec, dot: dotOf(main), badge: badgeOf(main), on: id === act, el: main, sub: subs, mine: (id === mine) });
          return;
        }
        if (c.classList.contains('tab')) add(c, sec);
      });
    });
    /* directors: their team is the board (the Board group's first page) */
    if (boardView()) out.forEach(function (it) { if (it.k === mine) it.mine = true; });
    /* wave262: your team first in its section */
    var mi = -1; out.forEach(function (it, i) { if (it.mine && mi < 0) mi = i; });
    if (mi > 0) { var sec0 = out[mi].sec, fi = -1; out.forEach(function (it, i) { if (fi < 0 && it.sec === sec0) fi = i; }); if (fi >= 0 && fi < mi) out.splice(fi, 0, out.splice(mi, 1)[0]); }
    return out;
  }
  function appList() {
    var A = window.WA_APP; if (!A || !A.order) return [];
    var ids = A.order.filter(function (id) { try { return A.ok(id); } catch (e) { return false; } });
    /* wave262: the Hub, then your team's apps, then the others */
    try { if (typeof A.yours === 'function') { var Y = A.yours(); if (!Y.all) ids = ids.filter(function (id) { return id === 'hub'; }).concat(Y.yours, Y.more); } } catch (e) { /* the menu order */ }
    return ids.map(function (id) { return A.apps[id]; });
  }
  /* 01/10/26: links shown after the apps (the website), from wave153's list */
  function linkList() { var A = window.WA_APP; return A && Array.isArray(A.links) ? A.links : []; }
  function appItems(app) {
    var A = window.WA_APP; if (!A) return [];
    var out = [];
    try { A.items(app).forEach(function (g) { g.items.forEach(function (it) { out.push({ k: it.h + (it.sec ? '|' + it.sec : ''), l: it.l, sec: g.solo ? '' : g.label, go: it.h, gsec: it.sec || '', ic: it.ic, on: !!(A.isOn && A.isOn(app, it)), app: app, badge: it.n || null }); }); }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }   /* wave164: no heading that repeats the app's name; the HR centre's counts */
    return out;
  }
  function rowHtml(it, i) {
    var lead = it.ic && window.WA_APP && window.WA_APP.icon ? '<span class="ai">' + window.WA_APP.icon(it.ic) + '</span>' : '<i class="dot" style="background:' + Esc(it.dot || 'rgba(16,63,57,.35)') + '"></i>';
    return '<button type="button" class="it' + (it.on ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w163i="' + i + '"' + (it.on ? ' aria-current="page"' : '') + '>' + lead +
      '<span class="lb">' + Esc(it.l) + (it.hint ? '<small>' + Esc(it.hint) + '</small>' : '') + '</span>' +
      (it.mine ? '<span class="mine">' + (boardView() ? 'Yours' : 'Your team') + '</span>' : '') + (it.badge ? '<span class="n">' + Esc(it.badge) + '</span>' : '') + PIc('chev', 'ch') + '</button>' +
      (it.sub && it.sub.length && !PH.q ? '<div class="chips">' + it.sub.map(function (s, j) { return '<button type="button" class="chip' + (s.on ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w163i="' + i + '" data-w163sub="' + j + '">' + Esc(s.l) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
  }
  function paintSheet(scrollTo) {
    var s = ensureSheet(), bd = s.querySelector('[data-w163bd]'); if (!bd) return;
    var A = window.WA_APP, app = null;
    try { app = A && A.current ? A.current() : null; } catch (e) { app = null; }
    var apps = appList(), q = foldS(PH.q), list = [], html = '';
    var h2 = s.querySelector('.hd h2'); if (h2) h2.textContent = app && A.apps[app] ? A.apps[app].label : 'Menu';
    if (q) {
      /* Find a page: the Hub's pages, their teams' own pages and every app's pages this person can open */
      hubItems().forEach(function (it) {
        list.push(it);
        (it.sub || []).forEach(function (sb) { list.push({ k: sb.k, l: sb.l, sec: it.l, el: sb.el, on: sb.on }); });
      });
      apps.forEach(function (a) {
        if (a.id === 'hub') return;
        /* wave274 (one app): the Volunteer Manager's pages in each city this person can open, said with the city */
        var cs = []; try { cs = A.cities ? A.cities(a.id) : []; } catch (e) { cs = []; } if (!cs.length) cs = [a.id];
        cs.forEach(function (c) { appItems(c).forEach(function (it) { it.hint = a.label + (cs.length > 1 && A.apps[c] && A.apps[c].city ? ', ' + A.apps[c].city : ''); list.push(it); }); });
      });
      var words = q.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
      list = list.filter(function (it) { var hay = foldS(it.l + ' ' + (it.sec || '') + ' ' + (it.hint || '')); return words.every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0; }); });
      list.forEach(function (it) { if (!it.hint) it.hint = it.sec || ''; });
      html = list.length ? list.map(rowHtml).join('') : '<div class="none">No page is called that. Try another word.</div>';
    } else {
      html += apps.length > 1 ? '<div class="sh">Apps</div><div class="apps" role="list">' + apps.map(function (a) {
        var on = (A.tileOf ? A.tileOf(app || 'hub') : (app || 'hub')) === a.id;   /* wave274 (one app): in Toronto the Volunteer Manager's tile is lit */
        return '<button type="button" class="app' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" role="listitem" data-w163app="' + Esc(a.id) + '"' + (on ? ' aria-current="true"' : '') + '><span class="t" style="background:' + Esc(a.color) + '">' + (A.icon ? A.icon(a.ic) : '') + '</span>' + Esc(a.label) + '</button>';
      }).join('') + linkList().map(function (l) {
        return '<div class="appx" role="listitem"><a class="app ext" href="' + Esc(l.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-w163ext="' + Esc(l.id) + '" aria-label="' + Esc(l.label + ', opens in a new tab') + '"><span class="t" style="background:' + Esc(l.color) + '">' + (A.icon ? A.icon(l.ic) : '') + '</span><span class="xl">' + Esc(l.label) + (A.icon ? A.icon('ext', 'w163xi') : '') + '</span></a></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '';
      if (app) {
        html += '<button type="button" class="back" data-w163back="1">' + PIc('back') + ' Back to the Hub</button>';
        try { if (A.switchHtml) html += A.switchHtml(app); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }   /* wave274 (one app): Montreal | Toronto, at the top of the app's own menu (wave153's buttons and click) */
        list = appItems(app);
      } else list = hubItems();
      var sec = null;
      list.forEach(function (it, i) {
        if (it.sec !== sec) { sec = it.sec; if (sec) html += '<div class="sh" data-w163sec="' + Esc(foldS(sec)) + '">' + Esc(sec) + '</div>'; }
        html += rowHtml(it, i);
      });
      if (!list.length) html += '<div class="none">The menu is still loading.</div>';
    }
    PH.items = list;
    bd.innerHTML = html;
    PH.built++;
    if (scrollTo) { var t = bd.querySelector('[data-w163sec="' + scrollTo + '"]'); if (t) { try { bd.scrollTop = t.offsetTop - 8; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
    else if (!q) { var on = bd.querySelector('.it.on'); if (on && on.offsetTop > bd.clientHeight - 80) { try { bd.scrollTop = on.offsetTop - 120; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  function openSheet(scrollTo) {
    var s = ensureSheet();
    PH.lastFocus = document.activeElement;
    PH.q = ''; var inp = s.querySelector('[data-w163q]'); if (inp) inp.value = '';
    try { document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    PH.open = true;
    paintSheet(scrollTo ? foldS(scrollTo) : '');
    s.classList.add('on'); document.body.classList.add('w163sheet');
    paintBar();
    try { s.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function closeSheet(quiet) {
    var s = document.getElementById('w163sheet');
    if (!PH.open) return;
    PH.open = false;
    if (s) s.classList.remove('on');
    document.body.classList.remove('w163sheet');
    paintBar();
    if (!quiet && PH.lastFocus && PH.lastFocus.focus) { try { PH.lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function choose(i, j) {
    var it = PH.items && PH.items[i]; if (!it) return;
    closeSheet(true);
    if (j != null && it.sub && it.sub[j]) { press(it.sub[j].el); return; }
    if (it.el) { press(it.el); return; }
    if (it.go && window.WA_APP && window.WA_APP.go) { window.WA_APP.go(it.go, it.gsec || null); return; }
    if (it.go) location.hash = it.go;
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    var t = ev.target && ev.target.closest ? ev.target : null; if (!t) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('#w163bar [data-w163slot]'))) { ev.preventDefault(); slotGo(el.getAttribute('data-w163slot'), el); return; }
    if (!t.closest('#w163sheet')) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w163close]'))) { ev.preventDefault(); closeSheet(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w163back]'))) { ev.preventDefault(); closeSheet(true); try { window.WA_APP.close(); } catch (e) { location.hash = '#mypage'; } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w163ext]'))) { closeSheet(true); return; }   /* 01/10/26: the website opens its own tab (no preventDefault) */
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w163app]'))) { ev.preventDefault(); var id = el.getAttribute('data-w163app'); closeSheet(true); try { if (id === 'hub') window.WA_APP.close(); else window.WA_APP.open(id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w163i]'))) { ev.preventDefault(); var sub = el.getAttribute('data-w163sub'); choose(Number(el.getAttribute('data-w163i')), sub == null ? null : Number(sub)); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (ev) {
    if (!ev.target || !ev.target.hasAttribute || !ev.target.hasAttribute('data-w163q')) return;
    PH.q = ev.target.value || ''; paintSheet('');
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) {
    if (!PH.open) return;
    if (ev.key === 'Escape') { ev.preventDefault(); closeSheet(); return; }
    if (ev.key === 'Enter' && ev.target && ev.target.hasAttribute && ev.target.hasAttribute('data-w163q') && PH.q && PH.items && PH.items.length) { ev.preventDefault(); choose(0, null); }
  });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (PH.open) closeSheet(true); setTimeout(paintBar, 0); });
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp163 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function () { var r = __sp163.apply(this, arguments); try { setTimeout(paintBar, 0); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return r; };
  }
  /* Ask the Hub opens full screen on a phone: while it is open the bar steps aside, so its message box is reachable */
  function chatOpen() { var p = document.getElementById('waChatPanel'); if (!p || p.style.display === 'none') return false; try { return getComputedStyle(p).display !== 'none'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function watchChat() {
    var p = document.getElementById('waChatPanel'); if (!p) return false;
    if (p.__w163) return true; p.__w163 = true;
    var f = function () { document.body.classList.toggle('w163chat', chatOpen()); };
    try { new MutationObserver(f).observe(p, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['style', 'class'] }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    f(); return true;
  }
  /* the bar follows the menu: drawn at once (Home and Menu work before the Hub has started), again when the menu is whole */
  function initPhone() { try { paintBar(); watchChat(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initPhone); else initPhone();
  document.addEventListener('wa163nav', function () { try { paintBar(); if (PH.open) paintSheet(''); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
  document.addEventListener('wa153app', function () { try { paintBar(); if (PH.open) paintSheet(''); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
  /* the chat button can arrive after the bar (waves 75 and 117 draw it on timers) */
  var phTries = 0, phIv = setInterval(function () { phTries++; var w = false; try { paintBar(); w = watchChat(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } if (phTries > 20 || (w && document.getElementById('waChatBtn') && G.ready)) clearInterval(phIv); }, 500);

  /* ---------- test hook ---------- */
  window.w163 = { state: function () { return G; }, ready: function () { return G.ready; }, settle: settle,
    phone: { state: function () { return PH; }, open: openSheet, close: closeSheet, bar: paintBar, key: barKey, team: myTeamId, items: hubItems } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v297 Messages to volunteers: automatic emails and texts per city, and Send now (09/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (08/11/26): "we should have the ability to confirm people via email as well, and follow up via email as
   well, and not always have texts ... both the optional ability to send out automatic texts and automatic emails,
   and also have the button to send those out, both in Montreal and Toronto."

   - One box, "Messages to volunteers", under the Texts strip of a Montreal serving (#volunteering/e/<id>) and in
     the Confirm part of a Toronto event (#toronto/e/<id>).
   - Automatic: two switches for the city, Emails and Texts (wa297_auto_get / wa297_auto_set). They decide how the
     confirmation (10 days before), the Monday reminder, the Thursday check-in, the sign-up message and the
     thank-you reach people: email, text, both, or nothing automatic. Montreal started on emails only (what it
     already did); Toronto too.
   - Send now: Confirmation, Reminder, Check-in or Thank-you for this event, by email, text or both, to everyone
     on the list who has not had that message yet (wa297_send_now). Emails go within a minute; texts wait for
     9 am to 7 pm. Each message reaches a person once.
   - Who: Montreal, the people who can text volunteers (leaders, HR, admins, Ibrahim, Mary, Terry); Toronto, the
     same plus the Toronto team. Everyone else sees the settings read-only and no buttons.
   - The database checks every call; this page only draws. No timers: one MutationObserver puts the box back
     when the serving or event page redraws.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.w297) return;
  var S = { cfg: null, loading: false, pick: null, busy: false, msg: {} };
  var KINDS = [['confirm', 'Confirmation'], ['reminder', 'Reminder'], ['checkin', 'Check-in'], ['thanks', 'Thank-you']];
  var CH = [['email', 'By email'], ['text', 'By text'], ['both', 'Email and text']];
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function client() { try { if (typeof db !== 'undefined' && db && db.rpc) return db; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return window.sb || null; }
  function where() {
    var h = String(location.hash || '');
    var m = h.match(/^#toronto\/e\/(\d+)/);
    if (m) return { city: 'toronto', id: Number(m[1]), host: document.getElementById('w274s-confirm') };
    m = h.match(/^#volunteering\/e\/(\d+)/);
    if (m) { var st = document.querySelector('#w119 .w258strip'); return { city: 'montreal', id: Number(m[1]), host: st, after: true }; }
    return null;
  }
  async function load(force) {
    if (S.loading || (S.cfg && !force)) return;
    var c = client(); if (!c) return;
    S.loading = true;
    try { var r = await c.rpc('wa297_auto_get'); if (!r.error) S.cfg = r.data; else S.failAt = Date.now(); } catch (e) { S.failAt = Date.now(); /* the box stays hidden */ }
    S.loading = false; if (S.cfg) paint();   /* wave304: no retry loop when it fails */
  }
  function sched(c) {
    return 'Confirmation ' + (c.confirm_days || 10) + ' days before, a reminder the Monday, a check-in the Thursday, a thank-you after. Emails go within a minute; texts between 9 am and 7 pm.';
  }
  function boxHtml(w) {
    var c = S.cfg, s = c && c[w.city]; if (!s) return '';
    var can = !!s.can, city = w.city === 'toronto' ? 'Toronto' : 'Montreal';
    var mode = s.emails && s.texts ? 'by email and text' : s.emails ? 'by email' : s.texts ? 'by text' : 'not sent automatically';
    var h = '<div class="w297box" data-w297ev="' + w.id + '" data-w297city="' + w.city + '">'
      + '<div class="hd"><b>Messages to volunteers</b><span>' + E(city) + ': automatic messages go ' + E(mode) + '.</span></div>'
      + '<div class="auto"><span class="lb">Automatic</span>'
      + '<label class="sw"><input type="checkbox" data-w297sw="emails"' + (s.emails ? ' checked' : '') + (can ? '' : ' disabled') + '> Emails</label>'
      + '<label class="sw"><input type="checkbox" data-w297sw="texts"' + (s.texts ? ' checked' : '') + (can ? '' : ' disabled') + '> Texts</label>'
      + '<span class="nt">For every ' + E(city) + (w.city === 'toronto' ? ' event' : ' serving') + '. ' + E(sched(c)) + '</span></div>';
    if (can) {
      h += '<div class="now"><span class="lb">Send now</span>' + KINDS.map(function (k) {
        return '<button type="button" class="w297b' + (S.pick === k[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w297k="' + k[0] + '">' + E(k[1]) + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
      if (S.pick) {
        var lab = KINDS.filter(function (k) { return k[0] === S.pick; })[0][1];
        var def = s.emails && s.texts ? 'both' : s.texts ? 'text' : 'email';
        h += '<div class="pick"><span>' + E(lab) + ' to everyone on this list who has not had it yet:</span>'
          + CH.map(function (x) { return '<button type="button" class="w297b pri' + (x[0] === def ? ' def' : '') + '" data-w297go="' + x[0] + '"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + E(x[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('')
          + '<button type="button" class="w297b ghost" data-w297x="1">Cancel</button></div>';
      }
    } else {
      h += '<p class="ro">Only the team that runs ' + E(city) + ' volunteers can change these or send messages.</p>';
    }
    var m = S.msg[w.id]; if (m) h += '<p class="res' + (m.err ? ' err' : '') + '">' + E(m.t) + '</p>';
    return h + '</div>';
  }
  function paint() {
    var w = where(); if (!w || !w.host) return;
    if (!S.cfg) { if (!S.failAt || Date.now() - S.failAt > 30000) load(); return; }
    var html = boxHtml(w); if (!html) return;
    var old = document.querySelector('.w297box');
    if (old && old.__h === html && old.getAttribute('data-w297ev') === String(w.id) && (w.after ? old.previousElementSibling === w.host : old.parentNode === w.host)) return;
    if (old) old.remove();
    var t = document.createElement('div'); t.innerHTML = html; var el = t.firstChild; el.__h = html;
    if (w.after) w.host.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el);
    else { var cn = w.host.querySelector('.w274cnts'); if (cn) cn.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el); else w.host.appendChild(el); }
  }
  function words(r, ch) {
    if (!r) return 'Done.';
    var n = 0, k = r.kind;
    if (k === 'confirm') n = (r.queued || 0) + (r.emailed || 0);
    else if (k === 'reminder' || k === 'checkin') n = (r[k] && r[k].queued) || 0;
    else n = r.queued || 0;
    var how = ch === 'both' ? 'by email and text' : ch === 'text' ? 'by text' : 'by email';
    if (!n) return 'Nobody new to send to: everyone on the list has had this message already, or is not on the list yet.';
    return n + (n === 1 ? ' person gets it ' : ' people get it ') + how + '.' + (ch !== 'email' ? ' Texts go between 9 am and 7 pm.' : ' Emails go within a minute.');
  }
  async function send(ch) {
    var w = where(); if (!w || !S.pick || S.busy) return;
    var c = client(); if (!c) return;
    S.busy = true; paint();
    var kind = S.pick;
    try {
      var r = await c.rpc('wa297_send_now', { p_event: w.id, p_kind: kind, p_channel: ch });
      if (r.error) S.msg[w.id] = { t: r.error.message || 'That did not go through.', err: true };
      else { S.msg[w.id] = { t: words(r.data, ch) }; S.pick = null; }
    } catch (e) { S.msg[w.id] = { t: 'That did not go through. Try again.', err: true }; }
    S.busy = false; paint();
  }
  async function toggle(w, which, on) {
    var c = client(); if (!c || !S.cfg) return;
    var s = S.cfg[w.city], em = which === 'emails' ? on : !!s.emails, tx = which === 'texts' ? on : !!s.texts;
    try {
      var r = await c.rpc('wa297_auto_set', { p_city: w.city, p_texts: tx, p_emails: em });
      if (r.error) S.msg[w.id] = { t: r.error.message, err: true };
      else { S.cfg = r.data; S.msg[w.id] = { t: 'Saved. Automatic ' + (w.city === 'toronto' ? 'Toronto' : 'Montreal') + ' messages now go ' + (em && tx ? 'by email and text.' : em ? 'by email.' : tx ? 'by text.' : 'nowhere: use Send now.') }; }
    } catch (e) { S.msg[w.id] = { t: 'Not saved. Try again.', err: true }; }
    paint();
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('.w297box button'); if (!b) return;
    if (b.hasAttribute('data-w297k')) { S.pick = S.pick === b.getAttribute('data-w297k') ? null : b.getAttribute('data-w297k'); var w0 = where(); if (w0) delete S.msg[w0.id]; paint(); return; }
    if (b.hasAttribute('data-w297go')) { send(b.getAttribute('data-w297go')); return; }
    if (b.hasAttribute('data-w297x')) { S.pick = null; paint(); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.matches || !t.matches('.w297box input[data-w297sw]')) return;
    var w = where(); if (w) toggle(w, t.getAttribute('data-w297sw'), t.checked);
  });
  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent = '.w297box{margin:10px 0 14px;padding:12px 14px;border-radius:14px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.05);border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.12);font-size:13px;color:#103F39;}'
    + '.w297box .hd{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px 12px;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:8px;}'
    + '.w297box .hd b{font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.6px;text-transform:uppercase;}.w297box .hd span{color:#5f6b68;}'
    + '.w297box .auto,.w297box .now,.w297box .pick{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:6px 10px;margin:6px 0;}'
    + '.w297box .lb{font-weight:700;min-width:84px;}.w297box .nt{flex-basis:100%;color:#5f6b68;font-size:12px;}'
    + '.w297box .sw{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:999px;background:#fff;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);cursor:pointer;}'
    + '.w297box .sw input{accent-color:#103F39;}'
    + '.w297b{font:inherit;font-weight:600;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.25);background:#fff;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;}'
    + '.w297b.on{background:#103F39;color:#fff;border-color:#103F39;}.w297b.pri{background:#103F39;color:#fff;border-color:#103F39;}'
    + '.w297b.pri.def{box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(226,170,60,.45);}.w297b.ghost{background:transparent;}.w297b[disabled]{opacity:.55;cursor:default;}'
    + '.w297box .pick{padding:8px 10px;border-radius:10px;background:#fff;}.w297box .pick>span{flex-basis:100%;}'
    + '.w297box .ro{margin:6px 0 0;color:#5f6b68;}.w297box .res{margin:8px 0 0;font-weight:600;}.w297box .res.err{color:#9b2c2c;}'
    /* the org chart tree (parts133 treeHtml), in the brand: the co-EDs on Community green with Dignity cream type,

    + '#w133 .w297tree>h3{position:relative;padding-bottom:10px;}#w133 .w297tree>h3::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:0;bottom:0;width:40px;height:3px;border-radius:2px;background:#DCA527;}'

  var queued = false;
  function soon() { if (queued) return; queued = true; requestAnimationFrame(function () { queued = false; try { paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }); }
  new MutationObserver(function () { if (where()) soon(); }).observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { S.pick = null; soon(); });
  /* the org chart's entrance: once per visit to #orgchart. The chart redraws two or three times in the first few
     milliseconds as data lands, so any tree drawn within 250ms of the first one gets the entrance too (invisible
     restart); after that, redraws from search, filters and Show more stay still. */
  var orgT0 = 0, orgLast = null;
  function orgIn() {
    if (String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#orgchart') !== 0) { orgT0 = 0; orgLast = null; return; }
    var tree = document.querySelector('#w133 .w297tree'); if (!tree || tree === orgLast) return;
    orgLast = tree;
    var now = Date.now(); if (!orgT0) orgT0 = now;
    if (now - orgT0 > 250) return;
    tree.querySelectorAll('.w297br').forEach(function (b, i) {
      b.style.setProperty('--i', String(i));
      b.querySelectorAll('.w297tm').forEach(function (t, j) { t.style.setProperty('--j', String(Math.min(j, 6))); });
    });
    tree.classList.add('w297in');
    setTimeout(function () { tree.classList.remove('w297in'); }, 1400);
  }
  new MutationObserver(orgIn).observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', orgIn);
  window.w297 = { state: function () { return S; }, paint: paint, reload: function () { return load(true); } };
})();

/* =====================================================================
   WA v300 Board & Governance on one page, one menu (08/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (08/11/26), before the 12 November board meeting: the board should see the overview, the three
   jobs, the committees and the directors "in the same page. So I don't know why it's two tabs on its own";
   "the team page brings it back to overview ... Tasks brings it to [another page] ... there's like two
   different [menus]. Navigation really needs to improve here." He wants directors to open the next
   meeting's agenda in the Hub.

   - One page (#governance) with five sections in this order: Overview (the next meeting, its agenda and
     the three jobs), Committees, Directors, Board tasks, Meetings. wave126 still draws every card and owns
     every editor; this wave lays its two pages out as one (#w126gov is display:contents, #w126com moves in)
     and adds the next-meeting hero, the board tasks and the meetings list.
   - One menu everywhere: the page's section bar, the Board & Governance row in the side menu and the
     Board group for directors all name the same five sections. Committees is a section, not a page:
     #committees and #committees/<key> land on #governance/committees. The team workspace (task board,
     agenda builder, transcripts) stays one click away as "Board workspace", and its tab strip gets one
     way back instead of a second Overview.
   - Nothing restricted is added: the hero and lists read board_home() through wave126, as before.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa300) return; window.__wa300 = true;
  var G = '#103F39', CR = '#FFFAF2', GO = '#DCA527', PU = '#6F64AB', INK = '#1F201E', FLAG = '#B3402F';
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var OUT = 'cubic-bezier(.23,1,.32,1)';
  var SECS = [['overview', 'Overview'], ['committees', 'Committees'], ['directors', 'Directors'], ['tasks', 'Board tasks'], ['meetings', 'Meetings']];

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function W() { return window.w126 && typeof window.w126.state === 'function' ? window.w126 : null; }

  function boardMode() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function tabOk(id) { try { return typeof wa40Allowed !== 'function' || (wa40Allowed() || []).indexOf(id) >= 0; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function staffWs() { return !boardMode() && tabOk('board'); }
  function onGov() { var a = document.querySelector('.panel.active'); return !!(a && a.id === 'governance'); }
  function reduce() { try { return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function tz(iso, o) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-CA', Object.assign({ timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }, o)); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function dayKey(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function niceTime(iso) { return tz(iso, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }).replace(':00', '').replace(/\s?([ap])\.?m\.?/i, function (x, p) { return p.toLowerCase() + 'm'; }); }
  function niceDay(iso, yr) { var d = new Date(iso); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return ''; return tz(iso, { weekday: 'long' }) + ', ' + tz(iso, { month: 'long' }) + ' ' + tz(iso, { day: 'numeric' }) + (yr ? ', ' + tz(iso, { year: 'numeric' }) : ''); }
  function shortDay(iso) { var d = new Date(iso); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return ''; return tz(iso, { month: 'short' }) + ' ' + tz(iso, { day: 'numeric' }) + ', ' + tz(iso, { year: 'numeric' }); }
  function daysTo(iso) { var a = dayKey(new Date().toISOString()), b = dayKey(iso); if (!a || !b) return null; return Math.round((Date.parse(b + 'T12:00:00Z') - Date.parse(a + 'T12:00:00Z')) / 86400000); }
  function ico(p) { return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + p + '</svg>'; }
  var IC = {
    doc: '<path d="M6.5 3.5h7L18 8v12.5h-11.5z"/><path d="M13.5 3.5V8H18M9 12.5h6M9 16h6"/>',

  };

    /* the section bar */
    + '#w300nav{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:6;margin:0 0 18px;padding:8px 0;background:linear-gradient(var(--wa-bg,' + CR + ') 78%,rgba(255,250,242,0));}'

  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- the next meeting ---------- */
  function meetings() { var d = D(); return d && Array.isArray(d.meetings) ? d.meetings : []; }
  function nextMeeting() {
    var now = Date.now(), best = null;
    meetings().forEach(function (m) {
      if (!m.meets_at || m.kind === 'strategy' || m.status === 'cancelled' || m.status === 'held') return;
      var t = Date.parse(m.meets_at); if (isNaN(t) || t < now - 4 * 3600000) return;
      if (!best || t < Date.parse(best.meets_at)) best = m;
    });
    return best;
  }
  function lastMeeting() {
    var best = null;
    meetings().forEach(function (m) { if (m.status !== 'held' || !m.meets_at) return; if (!best || Date.parse(m.meets_at) > Date.parse(best.meets_at)) best = m; });
    return best;
  }
  function strategy() { return meetings().filter(function (m) { return m.kind === 'strategy' && m.status !== 'held' && m.status !== 'cancelled'; })[0] || null; }
  function canEditMeet() { var b = W() ? W().state() : null; var me = (b && b.me) || {}; var d = D(); var dm = (d && d.me) || {}; return !!(me.leader || me.admin || dm.leader || dm.admin); }
  function heroHtml() {
    var n = nextMeeting(), l = lastMeeting(), s = strategy(), p = (n && n.pack) || {};
    var agenda = n && (n.agenda_url || p.agenda_url), deck = n && (n.deck_url || p.deck_url), notes = n && (n.notes_url || p.notes_url), meet = p.meet_url, folder = p.folder_url;
    var left = n ? daysTo(n.meets_at) : null, live = false;
    if (n) { var st = Date.parse(n.meets_at), en = p.ends_at ? Date.parse(p.ends_at) : st + 2 * 3600000; live = Date.now() >= st - 15 * 60000 && Date.now() <= en; }
    var cd = !n ? '' : live ? 'Happening now' : left === 0 ? 'Today' : left === 1 ? 'Tomorrow' : left != null && left > 1 ? 'In ' + left + ' days' : '';
    var when = n ? niceDay(n.meets_at) + ' \u00b7 ' + niceTime(n.meets_at) + (p.ends_at ? ' to ' + niceTime(p.ends_at) : '') + ' ET' + (n.status === 'tentative' ? ' (tentative)' : '') : '';
    var acts = n ? [
      agenda ? '<a class="b p" href="' + E(agenda) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + ico(IC.doc) + 'Open the agenda</a>' : '',
      deck ? '<a class="b" href="' + E(deck) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + ico(IC.deck) + 'The deck</a>' : '',
      notes ? '<a class="b" href="' + E(notes) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + ico(IC.pen) + 'Meeting notes</a>' : '',
      meet ? '<a class="b" href="' + E(meet) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + ico(IC.video) + 'Join on Google Meet</a>' : '',
      folder ? '<a class="b" href="' + E(folder) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + ico(IC.folder) + 'Folder</a>' : '',
      canEditMeet() ? '<a class="b" data-w300meet="' + n.id + '">Edit</a>' : ''
    ].join('') : (canEditMeet() ? '<a class="b p" data-w300meet="new">Schedule the next meeting</a>' : '');
    var due = n ? new Date(Date.parse(n.meets_at) - 7 * 86400000).toISOString() : null;
    var items = Array.isArray(p.items) ? p.items : [];
    var right = items.length
      ? '<h3>The agenda <span>' + items.length + ' items' + (n ? ', ' + E(niceTime(n.meets_at)) + ' start' : '') + '</span></h3><ol>' + items.map(function (it) {
          return '<li><span class="t">' + E(it.t || it.n) + '</span><div><b>' + E(it.title) + '</b>' + (it.who ? '<div style="font-size:12px;color:#6b6e68;margin-top:2px">' + E(it.who) + '</div>' : '') + '</div><em>' + E(it.for || (it.mins ? it.mins + ' min' : '')) + '</em></li>';
        }).join('') + '</ol>'
      : '<h3>The agenda</h3><p class="empty">' + (n ? (agenda ? 'The agenda is in the Google Doc. Open it from the button on the left.' : 'The agenda will be posted here once it is ready.') : 'Four board meetings a year, plus an AGM. The next one appears here, with its agenda, as soon as it is scheduled.') + '</p>';
    return '<div class="l"><div class="k">Next board meeting' + (cd ? '<span class="cd' + (live ? ' live' : '') + '">' + E(cd) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<h2>' + E(n ? n.title : 'Not scheduled yet') + '</h2>'
      + (n ? '<p class="when">' + E(when) + '</p>' : '<p class="when">Directors and the co-executive directors meet four times a year.</p>')
      + '<div class="acts">' + acts + '</div>'
      + '<div class="mini">'
      + '<div><small>Committees meet by</small><b>' + (due ? E(tz(due, { month: 'long' }) + ' ' + tz(due, { day: 'numeric' })) : 'A week before') + '</b></div>'
      + '<div><small>Last meeting</small><b>' + (l ? E(shortDay(l.meets_at)) + (l.notes_url ? ' \u00b7 <a href="' + E(l.notes_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">minutes</a>' : '') : 'None yet') + '</b></div>'
      + '<div><small>Strategy session</small><b>' + (s ? (s.meets_at ? E(shortDay(s.meets_at)) : 'To schedule') : 'Every year') + '</b></div>'
      + '</div></div><div class="r">' + right + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- board tasks ---------- */
  function tasksHtml() {
    var d = D(), ts = d && Array.isArray(d.tasks) ? d.tasks : [];
    var open = ts.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; }), done = ts.filter(function (t) { return t.done; });
    var today = dayKey(new Date().toISOString());
    var rows = open.map(function (t) {
      var late = t.due_date && t.due_date < today;
      return '<div class="w300row"' + (staffWs() ? ' data-w300task="' + E(t.id) + '" role="button" tabindex="0"' : '') + '><div><b>' + E(t.title) + '</b></div><span class="m">' + E(t.owner || '') + '</span><span class="m' + (late ? ' late' : '') + '">' + (t.due_date ? (late ? 'Overdue \u00b7 ' : 'Due ') + E(shortDay(t.due_date + 'T12:00:00Z')) : (t.status || 'Open')) + '</span></div>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="w300card">' + (rows || '<p class="w300empty">No open board tasks.</p>')
      + '<div class="w300foot"><span>' + open.length + ' open \u00b7 ' + done.length + ' done</span><span class="sp"></span>'
      + (staffWs() ? '<a data-w300go="#team/board/tasks">' + ico(IC.arrow) + 'Open the task board</a>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- meetings ---------- */
  function meetingsHtml() {
    var ms = meetings().slice().sort(function (a, b) { var x = a.meets_at ? Date.parse(a.meets_at) : 9e15, y = b.meets_at ? Date.parse(b.meets_at) : 9e15; return y - x; });
    var CH = { held: ['Held', 'rgba(16,63,57,.1)', G], scheduled: ['Scheduled', 'rgba(111,100,171,.15)', '#4a4180'], tentative: ['Tentative', 'rgba(220,165,39,.2)', '#6b4d05'], 'to schedule': ['To schedule', 'rgba(220,165,39,.2)', '#6b4d05'], cancelled: ['Not held', 'rgba(121,119,115,.14)', '#5c5a56'] };
    var rows = ms.map(function (m) {
      var c = CH[m.status] || CH.scheduled, p = m.pack || {};
      var links = [['Agenda', m.agenda_url || p.agenda_url], [m.status === 'held' ? 'Minutes' : 'Notes', m.notes_url || p.notes_url], ['Deck', m.deck_url || p.deck_url]].filter(function (x) { return x[1] && m.status !== 'cancelled'; });
      return '<div class="w300row"><div><b>' + E(m.title) + '</b><div class="m" style="margin-top:3px">' + (m.meets_at ? E(shortDay(m.meets_at)) : 'Date to set') + (m.note ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(String(m.note).slice(0, 110)) : '') + '</div></div>'
        + '<span class="w300chip" style="background:' + c[1] + ';color:' + c[2] + '">' + c[0] + '</span>'
        + '<span class="w300lk">' + links.map(function (x) { return '<a href="' + E(x[1]) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(x[0]) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span></div>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="w300card">' + (rows || '<p class="w300empty">No board meetings recorded yet.</p>')
      + (staffWs() ? '<div class="w300foot"><span>Board meeting files live in the Board of Directors shared drive.</span><span class="sp"></span><a data-w300go="#team/board/agenda">' + ico(IC.doc) + 'Agenda builder</a></div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the section bar ---------- */
  function navHtml() {
    var secs = SECS.filter(function (s) { return s[0] !== 'directors' || document.getElementById('w126dirs'); });
    var x = '';
    if (tabOk('overview')) x += '<a class="x" data-w300tab="overview">' + ico('<path d="M4 19V9M10 19V5M16 19v-7M22 19H2"/>') + '<span>Executive dashboard</span></a>';
    if (tabOk('finances')) x += '<a class="x" data-w300tab="finances">' + ico('<rect x="3" y="6.5" width="18" height="11" rx="2"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2.6"/>') + '<span>Finance</span></a>';
    if (staffWs()) x += '<a class="x" data-w300go="#team/board">' + ico(IC.folder) + '<span>Board workspace</span></a>';
    return '<div class="in"><div class="seg" role="tablist"><span class="thumb" aria-hidden="true"></span>' + secs.map(function (s) { return '<a role="tab" data-w300sec="' + s[0] + '">' + E(s[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div><span class="sp"></span>' + x + '</div>';
  }
  var CUR = 'overview';
  function mark(sec) {
    CUR = sec || CUR;
    var nav = document.getElementById('w300nav');
    if (nav) {
      var on = null;
      nav.querySelectorAll('.seg a').forEach(function (a) { var y = a.getAttribute('data-w300sec') === CUR; a.classList.toggle('on', y); a.setAttribute('aria-selected', y ? 'true' : 'false'); if (y) on = a; });
      var th = nav.querySelector('.thumb');
      if (th && on) { th.style.width = on.offsetWidth + 'px'; th.style.transform = 'translateX(' + on.offsetLeft + 'px)';
        var sg = nav.querySelector('.seg'); if (sg && sg.scrollWidth > sg.clientWidth) { var l = on.offsetLeft - 8, r = on.offsetLeft + on.offsetWidth + 8; if (l < sg.scrollLeft) sg.scrollLeft = l; else if (r > sg.scrollLeft + sg.clientWidth) sg.scrollLeft = r - sg.clientWidth; } }
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .w300fly[data-w300sec]').forEach(function (a) { a.classList.toggle('on', onGov() && a.getAttribute('data-w300sec') === CUR); });
  }
  function goSec(sec, smooth) {
    var el = document.getElementById(ANCHOR[sec] || 'w300hero'); if (!el) return;
    try { el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start', behavior: smooth && !reduce() ? 'smooth' : 'auto' }); } catch (e) { el.scrollIntoView(); }
    LOCK = Date.now() + 700; mark(sec);
    try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#governance' + (sec && sec !== 'overview' ? '/' + sec : '')); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- building the page ---------- */
  function ensure() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('governance'); if (!sec) return false;
    if (!document.getElementById('w126gov')) return false;
    var nav = document.getElementById('w300nav');
    if (!nav) { nav = document.createElement('nav'); nav.id = 'w300nav'; nav.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Board & Governance'); sec.appendChild(nav); }
    var html = navHtml(); if (nav.__h !== html) { nav.innerHTML = html; nav.__h = html; mark(); }
    var hero = document.getElementById('w300hero');
    if (!hero) { hero = document.createElement('section'); hero.id = 'w300hero'; hero.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Next board meeting'); sec.appendChild(hero); }
    var hc = document.getElementById('w300h-com');
    if (!hc) { hc = document.createElement('div'); hc.id = 'w300h-com'; hc.className = 'w126sec'; hc.innerHTML = '<h2>Committees</h2><span>Four working committees, each chaired by a director: meet the team, bring in an outside professional, and arrive at the board with a recommendation.</span>'; sec.appendChild(hc); }
    var com = document.getElementById('w126com');
    if (!com) { com = document.createElement('div'); com.id = 'w126com'; sec.appendChild(com); }
    else if (com.parentNode !== sec) sec.appendChild(com);
    ['w300tasks', 'w300meetings'].forEach(function (id) {
      if (document.getElementById(id)) return;
      var w = document.createElement('section'); w.id = id;
      w.innerHTML = '<div class="w126sec"><h2>' + (id === 'w300tasks' ? 'Board tasks' : 'Meetings') + '</h2><span>' + (id === 'w300tasks' ? 'What the board has committed to, from its meetings and its committees.' : 'Every board meeting: the agenda, the minutes and the deck.') + '</span></div><div class="b"></div>';
      sec.appendChild(w);
    });
    return true;
  }
  var LAST = { data: null, hero: '', tasks: '', meets: '' };
  function paint() {
    if (!ensure()) return;
    var d = D(), sec = document.getElementById('governance');
    var n = nextMeeting(); sec.classList.toggle('w300own', !!(n && n.pack && Array.isArray(n.pack.items) && n.pack.items.length));
    var h = d ? heroHtml() : '<div class="l"><div class="k">Next board meeting</div><h2>Loading\u2026</h2></div><div class="r"></div>';
    var hero = document.getElementById('w300hero'); if (LAST.hero !== h) { hero.innerHTML = h; LAST.hero = h; }
    var t = d ? tasksHtml() : '', m = d ? meetingsHtml() : '';
    var tb = document.querySelector('#w300tasks > .b'); if (tb && LAST.tasks !== t) { tb.innerHTML = t; LAST.tasks = t; }
    var mb = document.querySelector('#w300meetings > .b'); if (mb && LAST.meets !== m) { mb.innerHTML = m; LAST.meets = m; }
    var com = document.getElementById('w126com');
    if (d && W() && com && (LAST.data !== d || !com.children.length)) { LAST.data = d; try { W().com(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    var ms = document.querySelector('#governance > .w118mast');
    if (ms) { var h1 = ms.querySelector('h1'), cr = ms.querySelector('.crumb'), pp = ms.querySelector('p');
      if (h1 && h1.textContent !== 'Board & Governance') h1.textContent = 'Board & Governance';
      if (cr && cr.textContent !== 'The board') cr.textContent = 'The board';
      var bl = 'The next meeting and its agenda, the three jobs of the board, the committees and each director, in one place.';
      if (pp && pp.textContent !== bl) pp.textContent = bl; }
    var nav = document.getElementById('w300nav'); if (nav) { var nh = navHtml(); if (nav.__h !== nh) { nav.innerHTML = nh; nav.__h = nh; } mark(); }
  }
  var T = null;
  function soon(ms) { clearTimeout(T); T = setTimeout(function () { try { if (onGov()) paint(); } catch (e) { console.warn('wave300', e); } }, ms == null ? 60 : ms); }
  function watchPage() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('governance'); if (!sec || sec.__w300obs) return;
    sec.__w300obs = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { soon(80); }).observe(sec, { childList: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var g = document.getElementById('w126gov');
    if (g && !g.__w300obs) { g.__w300obs = true; try { new MutationObserver(function () { soon(80); }).observe(g, { childList: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  /* the section in view lights in the bar (and in the side menu) */
  var LOCK = 0, IO = null;
  function spy() {
    if (IO || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;
    IO = new IntersectionObserver(function (ents) {
      if (Date.now() < LOCK || !onGov()) return;
      var best = null;
      ents.forEach(function (e) { if (e.isIntersecting && (!best || e.boundingClientRect.top < best.boundingClientRect.top)) best = e; });
      if (!best) return;

/* =====================================================================
   WA v301 The Executive dashboard tells the story (08/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (08/11/26): the board needs the Finance app and the Executive dashboard, and the dashboard "needs an
   entire revamp ... pulling in everything, all the analytics and insights that we have across projects, goal
   progression, volunteer engagement and insights, program development, survey insights, marketing analytics,
   fundraising numbers, finance and budgeting numbers, all in graphs ... a story through the executive dashboard."

   - #overview opens on the story: a hero with the headline numbers, then eight chapters, each a few
     hand-drawn charts (SVG, no library): six years on the Avenue, feeding people, the people behind every
     serving, getting the word out, money, our community, where we are going, how the work moves.
   - The numbers are aggregates only, built by SQL from the Hub's own tables into app_config exec_story (and,
     once it exists, the exec_story() function, which this page tries first). No names, no transactions.
   - The older dashboard (the brief, decisions waiting, overdue tasks, delivery numbers) stays one click away:
     "The working view" at the foot of the page.
   - Directors open the Executive dashboard and the Finance app (wa40Allowed gains overview and finances for
     anyone the board pages recognise as a director).
   ===================================================================== */

    + '#w301 details{margin-top:10px;font-size:12.5px;}#w301 details summary{cursor:pointer;color:' + G + ';font-weight:700;}#w301 details table{border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:8px;width:100%;}#w301 details td,#w301 details th{padding:4px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid ' + LN + ';text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;}#w301 details th:first-child,#w301 details td:first-child{text-align:left;}'
    + '#w301tip{position:fixed;z-index:9999;pointer-events:none;background:' + INK + ';color:' + CR + ';font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.35;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:9px;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.18);opacity:0;transform:translateY(2px) scale(.98);transform-origin:bottom center;transition:opacity 125ms ease-out,transform 125ms ease-out;max-width:260px;}'
    + '#w301tip.on{opacity:1;transform:none;}#w301tip b{color:' + GO + ';}'
    /* motion: bars grow from the baseline, lines draw, once per chapter */
    + '#w301 .gb{transform-box:fill-box;transform-origin:50% 100%;}#w301 .gbn{transform-box:fill-box;transform-origin:50% 0;}'
    + '#w301 .ch.pre .gb,#w301 .ch.pre .gbn{transform:scaleY(.02);}#w301 .ch.pre .fi{transform:scaleX(.02);}#w301 .ch.pre .ln{stroke-dashoffset:var(--len);}#w301 .ch.pre .fade{opacity:0;}'
    + '#w301 .gb,#w301 .gbn,#w301 .fi{transition:transform 620ms ' + OUT + ';transition-delay:calc(var(--i,0) * 22ms);}'
    + '#w301 .ln{stroke-dasharray:var(--len);stroke-dashoffset:0;transition:stroke-dashoffset 900ms ' + OUT + ';}'
    + '#w301 .fade{transition:opacity 500ms ease-out;transition-delay:calc(var(--i,0) * 22ms);}'
    + '@media (hover:hover) and (pointer:fine){#w301 [data-tip]:hover{opacity:.82;}}'
    + '@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){#w301 .gb,#w301 .gbn,#w301 .fi,#w301 .ln,#w301 .fade,#w301 .thumb,#w301tip{transition:none !important;}#w301 .ch.pre .gb,#w301 .ch.pre .gbn,#w301 .ch.pre .fi{transform:none;}#w301 .ch.pre .ln{stroke-dashoffset:0;}#w301 .ch.pre .fade{opacity:1;}}'
    + '@media (max-width:1000px){#w301 .s8,#w301 .s7,#w301 .s6,#w301 .s5,#w301 .s4{grid-column:span 12;}#w301 .kpis{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));}#w301 .hero h1{font-size:38px;}}'
    + '@media (max-width:560px){#w301 .hero{padding:22px 18px;}#w301 .kpi b{font-size:32px;}#w301 .row3{grid-template-columns:1fr;}#w301 .hbar{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,110px) minmax(0,1fr) 52px;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- charts (plain SVG) ---------- */
  var VW = 640, VW0 = 640;
  function niceMax(v) { if (!(v > 0)) return 1; var p = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(v))), m = v / p; return (m <= 1 ? 1 : m <= 2 ? 2 : m <= 2.5 ? 2.5 : m <= 5 ? 5 : 10) * p; }
  function grid(h, top, max, fmt, x0) {
    var s = '';
    [0, .5, 1].forEach(function (f) { var y = top + (h - top) * (1 - f); s += '<line x1="' + x0 + '" x2="' + VW + '" y1="' + y + '" y2="' + y + '" stroke="' + (f === 0 ? 'rgba(16,63,57,.22)' : 'rgba(16,63,57,.07)') + '" stroke-width="1"/>' + (f > 0 && !(max < 20 && (max * f) % 1) ? '<text x="' + (x0 - 6) + '" y="' + (y + 4) + '" text-anchor="end">' + E(fmt(max * f)) + '</text>' : ''); });
    return s;
  }
  /* grouped or stacked columns; series: [{k,label,color}] ; rows: [{m, ...}] */
  function columns(rows, series, o) {
    o = o || {}; VW = o.vw || VW0; var H = o.h || 220, top = 16, x0 = o.fmt === money || o.fmt === kmoney ? 46 : 30, bot = 22, ih = H - bot;
    var stack = !!o.stack, n = rows.length, band = (VW - x0) / Math.max(n, 1), gap = Math.max(2, band * .22);
    var max = niceMax(Math.max.apply(null, rows.map(function (r) { return stack ? series.reduce(function (a, s) { return a + Number(r[s.k] || 0); }, 0) : Math.max.apply(null, series.map(function (s) { return Number(r[s.k] || 0); })); }).concat([1])));
    var fmt = o.fmt || n0, s = '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + VW + ' ' + H + '" role="img" aria-label="' + E(o.aria || '') + '">' + grid(ih, top, max, fmt, x0);
    rows.forEach(function (r, i) {
      var bx = x0 + i * band + gap / 2, bw = band - gap, acc = 0;
      series.forEach(function (sr, j) {
        var v = Number(r[sr.k] || 0); if (!v) return;
        var h = (ih - top) * v / max, w = stack ? bw : (bw - 2 * (series.length - 1)) / series.length, x = stack ? bx : bx + j * (w + 2), y = ih - h - (stack ? acc : 0);
        if (stack) acc += h + (h > 3 ? 2 : 0);
        var rr = Math.min(4, w / 2, h / 2);
        s += '<path class="gb" style="--i:' + i + '" fill="' + sr.color + '" d="M' + x + ',' + (y + h) + 'V' + (y + rr) + 'Q' + x + ',' + y + ' ' + (x + rr) + ',' + y + 'H' + (x + w - rr) + 'Q' + (x + w) + ',' + y + ' ' + (x + w) + ',' + (y + rr) + 'V' + (y + h) + 'Z" data-tip="' + E('<b>' + (o.tipLab ? o.tipLab(r) : mLab(r.m, true)) + '</b><br>' + sr.label + ': ' + fmt(v)) + '"/>';
      });
      var lab = o.lab ? o.lab(r, i) : (i % (o.every || 1) === 0 ? mLab(r.m, String(r.m).slice(5) === '01' || i === 0) : '');
      if (lab) s += '<text x="' + (bx + bw / 2) + '" y="' + (H - 6) + '" text-anchor="middle">' + E(lab) + '</text>';
    });
    return s + '</svg>';
  }
  /* money in above the line, money out below it */
  function diverging(rows) {
    VW = VW0;
    var H = 250, x0 = 46, mid = 118, half = 100, n = rows.length, band = (VW - x0) / n, gap = Math.max(2, band * .24);
    var max = niceMax(Math.max.apply(null, rows.map(function (r) { return Math.max(r['in'] || 0, r.out || 0); }).concat([1])));
    var s = '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + VW + ' ' + H + '" role="img" aria-label="Money in and money out, month by month">';
    [1, .5].forEach(function (f) { [-1, 1].forEach(function (sg) { var y = mid - sg * half * f; s += '<line x1="' + x0 + '" x2="' + VW + '" y1="' + y + '" y2="' + y + '" stroke="rgba(16,63,57,.07)"/><text x="' + (x0 - 6) + '" y="' + (y + 4) + '" text-anchor="end">' + (sg < 0 ? '-' : '') + kmoney(max * f) + '</text>'; }); });
    s += '<line x1="' + x0 + '" x2="' + VW + '" y1="' + mid + '" y2="' + mid + '" stroke="rgba(16,63,57,.3)"/>';
    rows.forEach(function (r, i) {
      var x = x0 + i * band + gap / 2, w = band - gap, a = half * (r['in'] || 0) / max, b = half * (r.out || 0) / max, rr = Math.min(3, w / 2);
      var tip = E('<b>' + mLab(r.m, true) + '</b><br>In: ' + money(r['in']) + '<br>Out: ' + money(r.out) + '<br>Net: ' + money((r['in'] || 0) - (r.out || 0)));
      if (a > 0.5) s += '<path class="gb" style="--i:' + i + '" fill="' + G + '" d="M' + x + ',' + (mid - 1) + 'V' + (mid - a + rr) + 'Q' + x + ',' + (mid - a) + ' ' + (x + rr) + ',' + (mid - a) + 'H' + (x + w - rr) + 'Q' + (x + w) + ',' + (mid - a) + ' ' + (x + w) + ',' + (mid - a + rr) + 'V' + (mid - 1) + 'Z" data-tip="' + tip + '"/>';
      if (b > 0.5) s += '<path class="gbn" style="--i:' + i + '" fill="' + GO + '" d="M' + x + ',' + (mid + 1) + 'V' + (mid + b - rr) + 'Q' + x + ',' + (mid + b) + ' ' + (x + rr) + ',' + (mid + b) + 'H' + (x + w - rr) + 'Q' + (x + w) + ',' + (mid + b) + ' ' + (x + w) + ',' + (mid + b - rr) + 'V' + (mid + 1) + 'Z" data-tip="' + tip + '"/>';
      if (i % 3 === 0) s += '<text x="' + (x + w / 2) + '" y="' + (H - 4) + '" text-anchor="middle">' + mLab(r.m, String(r.m).slice(5) === '01' || i === 0) + '</text>';
    });
    return s + '</svg>';
  }
  /* one or more lines over months */
  function lines(rows, series, o) {
    o = o || {}; VW = o.vw || VW0; var H = o.h || 200, top = 14, x0 = 34, ih = H - 22, n = rows.length;
    var vals = []; series.forEach(function (sr) { rows.forEach(function (r) { if (r[sr.k] != null) vals.push(Number(r[sr.k])); }); });
    var lo = o.zero === false ? Math.min.apply(null, vals) * .96 : 0, max = niceMax(Math.max.apply(null, vals.concat([1])));
    var X = function (i) { return x0 + (VW - x0 - 6) * (n <= 1 ? 0 : i / (n - 1)); }, Y = function (v) { return ih - (ih - top) * (v - lo) / (max - lo || 1); };
    var fmt = o.fmt || n0, s = '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + VW + ' ' + H + '" role="img" aria-label="' + E(o.aria || '') + '">';
    [0, .5, 1].forEach(function (f) { var v = lo + (max - lo) * f, y = Y(v); s += '<line x1="' + x0 + '" x2="' + VW + '" y1="' + y + '" y2="' + y + '" stroke="' + (f === 0 ? 'rgba(16,63,57,.22)' : 'rgba(16,63,57,.07)') + '"/>' + (f > 0 ? '<text x="' + (x0 - 6) + '" y="' + (y + 4) + '" text-anchor="end">' + E(fmt(v)) + '</text>' : ''); });
    series.forEach(function (sr) {
      var pts = []; rows.forEach(function (r, i) { if (r[sr.k] != null) pts.push([X(i), Y(Number(r[sr.k])), r]); });
      if (!pts.length) return;
      var d = pts.map(function (p, i) { return (i ? 'L' : 'M') + p[0].toFixed(1) + ',' + p[1].toFixed(1); }).join(''), len = 0;
      for (var i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) len += Math.hypot(pts[i][0] - pts[i - 1][0], pts[i][1] - pts[i - 1][1]);
      if (o.area) s += '<path class="fade" d="' + d + 'L' + pts[pts.length - 1][0] + ',' + ih + 'L' + pts[0][0] + ',' + ih + 'Z" fill="' + sr.color + '" opacity=".1"/>';
      s += '<path class="ln" style="--len:' + Math.ceil(len) + '" d="' + d + '" fill="none" stroke="' + sr.color + '" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"/>';
      pts.forEach(function (p, k) { s += '<circle class="fade" style="--i:' + k + '" cx="' + p[0] + '" cy="' + p[1] + '" r="' + (k === pts.length - 1 ? 4.5 : 3) + '" fill="' + (k === pts.length - 1 ? sr.color : '#fff') + '" stroke="' + sr.color + '" stroke-width="2" data-tip="' + E('<b>' + mLab(p[2].m, true) + '</b><br>' + sr.label + ': ' + fmt(p[2][sr.k])) + '"/>'; });
      var lp = pts[pts.length - 1]; if (o.endLabel) s += '<text class="val" x="' + (lp[0] - 6) + '" y="' + (lp[1] - 10) + '" text-anchor="end">' + E(fmt(lp[2][sr.k])) + '</text>';
    });
    rows.forEach(function (r, i) { if (i % (o.every || 2) === 0 || i === n - 1) s += '<text x="' + X(i) + '" y="' + (H - 4) + '" text-anchor="middle">' + mLab(r.m, String(r.m).slice(5) === '01' || i === 0) + '</text>'; });
    return s + '</svg>';
  }
  /* a heatmap: rows x columns, one hue light to dark */
  function heat(rowKeys, colKeys, val, o) {
    o = o || {}; VW = o.vw || VW0; var lw = o.lw || 120, cw = (VW - lw) / colKeys.length, rh = o.rh || 26, H = rowKeys.length * rh + 24;
    var max = 1; rowKeys.forEach(function (r) { colKeys.forEach(function (c) { max = Math.max(max, val(r, c) || 0); }); });
    var s = '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + VW + ' ' + H + '" role="img" aria-label="' + E(o.aria || '') + '">';
    rowKeys.forEach(function (r, i) {
      s += '<text x="' + (lw - 8) + '" y="' + (i * rh + rh / 2 + 4) + '" text-anchor="end">' + E(o.rowLab ? o.rowLab(r) : r) + '</text>';
      colKeys.forEach(function (c, j) {
        var v = val(r, c) || 0, t = v / max, a = v ? .12 + .88 * Math.pow(t, .7) : 0;
        s += '<rect class="fade" style="--i:' + (i + j) + '" x="' + (lw + j * cw + 1) + '" y="' + (i * rh + 1) + '" width="' + (cw - 2) + '" height="' + (rh - 2) + '" rx="4" fill="' + (v ? (o.color || G) : 'rgba(16,63,57,.05)') + '" fill-opacity="' + (v ? a.toFixed(3) : 1) + '" data-tip="' + E('<b>' + (o.rowLab ? o.rowLab(r) : r) + ', ' + (o.colLab ? o.colLab(c) : c) + '</b><br>' + (o.unit ? o.unit(v) : v)) + '"/>';
        if (v && o.showVal) s += '<text x="' + (lw + j * cw + cw / 2) + '" y="' + (i * rh + rh / 2 + 4) + '" text-anchor="middle" style="fill:' + (t > .55 ? CR : INK) + ';font-weight:700">' + v + '</text>';
      });
    });
    colKeys.forEach(function (c, j) { if (!o.colEvery || j % o.colEvery === 0 || j === colKeys.length - 1) s += '<text x="' + (lw + j * cw + cw / 2) + '" y="' + (H - 6) + '" text-anchor="middle">' + E(o.colLab ? o.colLab(c) : c) + '</text>'; });
    return s + '</svg>';
  }
  function ring(p, color, size) {
    size = size || 72; var r = size / 2 - 6, c = 2 * Math.PI * r, v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p || 0));
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + size + ' ' + size + '" width="' + size + '" height="' + size + '" role="img" aria-label="' + Math.round(v * 100) + ' percent"><circle cx="' + size / 2 + '" cy="' + size / 2 + '" r="' + r + '" fill="none" stroke="rgba(16,63,57,.08)" stroke-width="7"/>'
      + '<circle class="ln" style="--len:' + c.toFixed(1) + '" cx="' + size / 2 + '" cy="' + size / 2 + '" r="' + r + '" fill="none" stroke="' + color + '" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="' + (c * v).toFixed(1) + ' ' + c.toFixed(1) + '" transform="rotate(-90 ' + size / 2 + ' ' + size / 2 + ')"/>'
      + '<text x="50%" y="54%" text-anchor="middle" style="font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:20px;fill:' + G + '">' + Math.round(v * 100) + '%</text></svg>';
  }
  function hbars(list, o) {
    o = o || {}; var max = Math.max.apply(null, list.map(function (x) { return x[1]; }).concat([1]));
    return list.map(function (x, i) { return '<div class="hbar" data-tip="' + E('<b>' + x[0] + '</b><br>' + (o.fmt || n0)(x[1])) + '"><span>' + E(x[0]) + '</span><div class="tr"><div class="fi" style="--i:' + i + ';width:' + Math.max(1.5, 100 * x[1] / max) + '%;background:' + (x[2] || G) + '"></div></div><b>' + E((o.fmt || n0)(x[1])) + '</b></div>'; }).join('');
  }
  function legend(items) { return '<div class="lg">' + items.map(function (x) { return '<span><i style="background:' + x[1] + '"></i>' + E(x[0]) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }
  function table(head, rows) { return '<details><summary>The numbers</summary><table><tr>' + head.map(function (h) { return '<th>' + E(h) + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr>' + rows.map(function (r) { return '<tr>' + r.map(function (c) { return '<td>' + E(c) + '</td>'; }).join('') + '</tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></details>'; }

  /* ---------- the story ---------- */
  function chapter(key, num, title, lead, body) { return '<section class="ch pre" id="w301-' + key + '" data-w301ch="' + key + '"><div class="chh"><i>' + num + '</i><h2>' + E(title) + '</h2><p>' + lead + '</p></div><div class="grid">' + body + '</div></section>'; }
  function card(span, title, sub, inner, dark) { return '<div class="c ' + span + (dark ? ' dark' : '') + '">' + (title ? '<h3>' + E(title) + '</h3>' : '') + (sub ? '<p class="sub">' + sub + '</p>' : '') + inner + '</div>'; }
  function last12(a) { return arr(a).slice(-12); }
  function build(S) {
    var h = S.headline || {}, f = S.finance || {}, fu = S.fund || {}, so = S.social || {}, cr = S.crm || {}, sv = S.survey || {}, rt = S.retention || {};
    var asOf = S.built_at ? new Date(S.built_at).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }) : '';
    var years = (function () { var y0 = 2020, y1 = new Date().getFullYear(), out = []; for (var y = y0; y <= y1; y++) out.push(y); return out; })();
    var html = '';
    /* hero */
    html += '<div class="hero"><span class="blob" aria-hidden="true"></span><div class="k">Executive dashboard \u00b7 where Welfare Avenue stands</div>'
      + '<h1>Six years on the Avenue. <em>' + n0(h.servings_all) + ' servings</em>, ' + n0(h.volunteers_all) + ' volunteers, one community.</h1>'
      + '<p>Everything below comes from the Hub\u2019s own records: the serving calendar and its rosters, the bank statements, the CRM, our social accounts and the goals. Scroll it like a story, or jump to a chapter.</p>'
      + '<div class="kpis">'
      + [[h.servings_all, 'Servings', 'since our first in 2020'], [h.volunteer_shifts_all, 'Volunteer shifts', n0(h.volunteers_all) + ' different people'], [h.contacts, 'In our community', 'contacts in the CRM'], [f.fy_prev_in, 'Raised in FY2026', 'money in, Oct 2025 to Sep 2026', true], [h.team, 'On the team', n0(h.projects_active) + ' active projects']]
        .map(function (k) { return '<div class="kpi"><b data-w301count="' + Number(k[0] || 0) + '"' + (k[3] ? ' data-money="1"' : '') + '>' + (k[3] ? money(k[0]) : n0(k[0])) + '</b><span>' + E(k[1]) + '</span><small>' + E(k[2]) + '</small></div>'; }).join('')
      + '</div><div class="asof">Numbers as of ' + E(asOf) + '. Bank figures are unaudited.</div></div>';
    html += '<div class="nav"><div class="seg" role="tablist"><span class="thumb" aria-hidden="true"></span>' + CH.map(function (c) { return '<a role="tab" data-w301go="' + c[0] + '">' + E(c[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>';

    /* 1 impact */
    var hl = arr(S.history_highlights), byY = {}; hl.forEach(function (x) { (byY[x.y] = byY[x.y] || []).push(x); });
    var tl = '<div class="tl">' + Object.keys(byY).sort().map(function (y) { return '<div class="yr"><b>' + y + '</b>' + byY[y].map(function (x) { return '<div class="ev">' + E(x.t) + (x.c ? '<small>' + E(x.c) + '</small>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    var hh = arr(S.history_heat), kinds = Object.keys(KIND).filter(function (k) { return hh.some(function (x) { return x.k === k; }); });
    var srv = arr(S.servings);
    var impact = card('', 'Milestones that made us', 'From a car and a GoFundMe to two cities and a registered charity.', tl, true)
      + card('s7', 'Servings, month by month', 'Completed servings in the last two years. Montr\u00e9al and Toronto.', legend([['Montr\u00e9al', G], ['Toronto', GO]]) + columns(srv, [{ k: 'mtl', label: 'Montr\u00e9al', color: G }, { k: 'to', label: 'Toronto', color: GO }], { stack: true, every: 3, aria: 'Servings per month' })
        + table(['Month', 'Montr\u00e9al', 'Toronto', 'Cancelled'], srv.map(function (r) { return [mLab(r.m, true), r.mtl, r.to, r.cancelled]; })))
      + card('s5', 'What each year was about', 'How many milestones of each kind we recorded, by year.', heat(kinds, years, function (k, y) { var x = hh.filter(function (z) { return z.k === k && z.y === y; })[0]; return x ? x.n : 0; }, { vw: 400, lw: 92, rh: 26, rowLab: function (k) { return KIND[k] || k; }, unit: function (v) { return v + (v === 1 ? ' milestone' : ' milestones'); }, color: PU, aria: 'Milestones by kind and year' }))
      + card('s4', '', '', '<div class="stat"><span class="big">' + n0(h.servings_fy_prev) + '</span><small>servings in FY2026 (Oct 2025 to Sep 2026)</small></div><div class="stat" style="margin-top:14px"><span class="big">' + n0(h.servings_fy) + '</span><small>so far in FY2027</small></div><p class="note">Toronto\u2019s servings move into the Hub with the Toronto Volunteers app, so Toronto shows here from this fall.</p>')
      + card('s8', 'Goal 1: feed more people, more reliably', 'Meals served a year.', '<div class="row3"><div class="stat"><span class="big">2,269</span><small>meals in 2025 (our baseline)</small></div><div class="stat"><span class="big" style="color:' + PU + '">4,000</span><small>a year by FY2029</small></div><div class="stat"><span class="big" style="color:#9a7414">6,000</span><small>a year by FY2031</small></div></div>');
    html += chapter('impact', '1', 'Feeding people', 'The meal is the mission. This is how often we show up, and how far we have come since 2020.', impact);

    /* 2 people */
    var vol = arr(S.volunteers).filter(function (r) { return r.m >= '2026-01'; }), rm = arr(S.roles_m), months12 = last12(S.volunteers).map(function (r) { return r.m; });
    var roleKeys = Object.keys(ROLE).filter(function (k) { return rm.some(function (x) { return x.r === k; }); }).sort(function (a, b) { var sa = 0, sb = 0; rm.forEach(function (x) { if (x.r === a) sa += x.n; if (x.r === b) sb += x.n; }); return sb - sa; });
    var surveyIn = sv.n ? '<div class="row3"><div class="stat"><span class="big">' + (sv.meal != null ? Number(sv.meal).toFixed(1) : '\u2013') + '</span><small>meal rating out of 5</small></div><div class="stat"><span class="big">' + (sv.service != null ? Number(sv.service).toFixed(1) : '\u2013') + '</span><small>service rating out of 5</small></div><div class="stat"><span class="big">' + n0((sv.again || {}).yes || 0) + '/' + n0(sv.n) + '</span><small>would volunteer again</small></div></div><p class="note">From ' + n0(sv.n) + ' post-serving surveys so far. Program development brings every survey into the Hub, so this grows with each serving.</p>' : '<p class="note">Post-serving surveys start arriving with the Program Builder.</p>';

G : 'rgba(16,63,57,.2)') + '"></div></div><b>' + (r ? money(r) : e.status === 'done' ? 'to log' : 'underway') + '</b></div>'; }).join('') + '<p class="note">Rally Paper 2026 placed third (a $1,000 prize) and is still to be logged.</p>' : '<p class="note">No event results logged yet.</p>')

      + card('s6', 'Two chapters', 'Contacts tied to each city.', hbars([['Montr\u00e9al', chp.montreal || 0, G], ['Toronto', chp.toronto || 0, GO]]))
      + card('s6', 'Who we can email', 'Consent on file (Canada\u2019s anti-spam law).', '<div style="display:flex;height:14px;border-radius:7px;overflow:hidden;gap:2px;margin:6px 0 10px">' + [['express', G], ['implied', PU], ['none', 'rgba(16,63,57,.15)']].map(function (x) { return '<div data-tip="' + E('<b>' + x[0] + '</b><br>' + n0(cons[x[0]] || 0)) + '" style="flex:' + (cons[x[0]] || 0) + ';background:' + x[1] + '"></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' + legend([['Express ' + n0(cons.express || 0), G], ['Implied ' + n0(cons.implied || 0), PU], ['Not recorded ' + n0(cons.none || 0), 'rgba(16,63,57,.15)']]) + '<p class="note">' + Math.round(100 * ((cons.express || 0) + (cons.implied || 0)) / cTot) + '% of contacts have consent on file.</p>');

    var gl = arr(S.goals);
