      S.err[id] = 'Could not save: ' + (msgOf(e) || 'try again');
    }
    S.busy[id] = false; repaint();
    return ok;
  }
  /* taps: capture first, so the card's own drag and click handlers never see them */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.w304 [data-w304f], .w304 [data-w304i], .w304 [data-w304w], .w304 [data-w304o], .w304 [data-w304x]') : null;
    if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    if (b.disabled) return;
    if (b.hasAttribute('data-w304o') || b.hasAttribute('data-w304x')) {   /* Change opens the editor, Done folds it */
      var oid = b.getAttribute('data-w304o') || b.getAttribute('data-w304x');
      if (b.hasAttribute('data-w304o')) S.open[oid] = true; else delete S.open[oid];
      repaint(); return;
    }
    var id = b.getAttribute('data-w304id'), r = row(id); if (!r) return;
    S.open[id] = true;   /* stays open while they work on it */
    var d = r.detail || {}, v;
    if (b.hasAttribute('data-w304f')) {
      v = b.getAttribute('data-w304f');
      if (food(r) === v && d.food_team) return;                  /* already the team's call */
      save(id, { food: v });
    } else if (b.hasAttribute('data-w304i')) {
      v = b.getAttribute('data-w304i');
      if (ingr(r) === v && d.ingredients_team) return;
      save(id, { ingredients: v });
    } else {
      var k = b.getAttribute('data-w304w'), w = wins(r), i = w.indexOf(k);
      if (i >= 0) w.splice(i, 1); else w.push(k);
      save(id, { windows: w });
    }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var el = e.target; if (!el || !el.getAttribute || !el.hasAttribute('data-w304a')) return;
    e.stopPropagation();
    var id = el.getAttribute('data-w304a'), r = row(id); if (!r) return;
    var v = String(el.value || '').trim(); if (v === addr(r).trim()) return;
    S.open[id] = true; save(id, { address: v });
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    var el = e.target; if (!el || !el.getAttribute || !el.hasAttribute('data-w304a')) return;
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); el.blur(); }
  }, true);
  /* typing in the address: the card stops being draggable so the text can be selected */
  document.addEventListener('focusin', function (e) { var el = e.target; if (el && el.getAttribute && el.hasAttribute('data-w304a')) { var pc = el.closest('.pc'); if (pc) { pc.setAttribute('data-w304drag', pc.getAttribute('draggable') || ''); pc.setAttribute('draggable', 'false'); } } });
  document.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) { var el = e.target; if (el && el.getAttribute && el.hasAttribute('data-w304a')) { var pc = el.closest('.pc'); if (pc && pc.hasAttribute('data-w304drag')) { pc.setAttribute('draggable', pc.getAttribute('data-w304drag') || 'true'); pc.removeAttribute('data-w304drag'); } } });

  window.w304 = { html: html, save: save, food: food, ingr: ingr, state: function () { return S; } };
})();

/* =====================================================================
   WA v305 Email copies and replies, chosen per template (10/11/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (10/11/26): "Can we also choose what emails in our team or group emails we can CC ... And also the reply to ...
   can we also choose who that goes to as well?"
   - CRM, Email, every template: a line "Copies: ... · Replies go to: ..." and a Copies and replies button. It opens a
     picker: the group addresses (fundraising@, volunteer@, operations@ ...) and the team, ticked for a copy, and one
     address for replies (or the sending address). Saved through wa305_mail_routing_set (staff, welfareavenue.com
     addresses only, logged in change_log); mail-send v9 copies them on every real send and sets the reply-to.
   - CRM, Forms, a form's editor: "After they send it, email them" picks the confirmation email (transactional
     templates) and shows who that email copies and where replies go. Saved with the form (settings.confirm and
     settings.confirm_template), so forms and automations follow the template's choices.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.w305) return;
  var S = { opt: null, loading: false, failed: false, open: {}, busy: {}, err: {}, tpl: {}, css: false, paint: null, reload: null };
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase(); }
  function rp() { try { if (S.paint) S.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function css() {
    if (S.css) return; S.css = true;
    var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w305css';
    st.textContent = '.w305{margin:6px 0 0;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.45;color:#3d3c37;}'
      + '.w305 .w305l{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:4px 10px;}'
      + '.w305 .w305l b{font-weight:600;color:#103f39;}'
      + '.w305p{margin:8px 0 4px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);border-radius:10px;background:#fff;}'
      + '.w305p h5{margin:4px 0 6px;font-size:11.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:#6d6a60;}'
      + '.w305p .w305g{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(210px,1fr));gap:2px 12px;max-height:220px;overflow:auto;padding:2px;}'
      + '.w305p label.w305c{display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:7px;font-size:12.5px;padding:3px 0;cursor:pointer;}'
      + '.w305p label.w305c input{margin-top:3px;accent-color:#103f39;}'
      + '.w305p label.w305c small{display:block;color:#6d6a60;font-size:11px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}'
      + '.w305p select{max-width:100%;}'
      + '.w305p .w305f{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-top:10px;}'
      + '.w305p .w305e{color:#b3402f;font-weight:600;}'
      + '.w305form{margin:4px 0 10px;}';
    document.head.appendChild(st);
  }
  function loadOpts() {
    if (S.opt || S.loading) return;
    if (S.failed && Date.now() - S.failed < 30000) return;
    S.loading = true;
    Promise.resolve(db.rpc('wa305_mail_routing_options')).then(function (r) {
      S.loading = false;
      if (r && !r.error && r.data) { S.opt = r.data; S.failed = false; } else S.failed = Date.now();
      rp();
    }, function () { S.loading = false; S.failed = Date.now(); rp(); });
  }
  function groupLabel(g) { return String(g.label || '').replace(/^Hr$/, 'HR (jobs)'); }
  function label(email) {
    var e = lower(email), o = S.opt || {};
    var g = (o.groups || []).filter(function (x) { return x.email === e; })[0]; if (g) return groupLabel(g) + ' (' + e.split('@')[0] + '@)';
    var p = (o.people || []).filter(function (x) { return x.email === e; })[0]; if (p) return p.name;
    return e;
  }
  function sender(t) { var o = S.opt || {}; return lower((o.senders || {})[t.key] || o.from || 'info@welfareavenue.com'); }
  function ccOf(t) { return Array.isArray(t.cc) ? t.cc.map(lower) : []; }
  function summary(t) {
    var cc = ccOf(t), rt = lower(t.reply_to);
    return 'Copies: <b>' + (cc.length ? E(cc.map(label).join(', ')) : 'nobody') + '</b> · Replies go to: <b>' + E(rt ? label(rt) : (S.opt ? label(sender(t)) + ', the sending address' : 'the sending address')) + '</b>';
  }
  function panel(t) {
    var key = t.key, o = S.opt;
    if (!o) { loadOpts(); return '<div class="w305p" data-w305p="' + E(key) + '"><span class="m">' + (S.failed ? 'The team list did not load. Close and try again.' : 'Loading the team…') + '</span></div>'; }
    var cc = ccOf(t), rt = lower(t.reply_to), busy = !!S.busy[key];
    var box = function (x, name, sub) {
      return '<label class="w305c"><input type="checkbox" data-w305cc="' + E(x.email) + '"' + (cc.indexOf(x.email) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '><span>' + E(name) + '<small>' + E(sub) + '</small></span></label>';
    };
    var groups = (o.groups || []).map(function (g) { return box(g, groupLabel(g), g.email); }).join('');
    var people = (o.people || []).map(function (p) { return box(p, p.name, p.email); }).join('');
    var extra = cc.filter(function (e) { return !(o.groups || []).concat(o.people || []).some(function (x) { return x.email === e; }); })
      .map(function (e) { return box({ email: e }, e, 'no longer on the team list'); }).join('');
    var opt = function (x, name) { return '<option value="' + E(x.email) + '"' + (rt === x.email ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(name) + '</option>'; };
    var sel = '<select class="w152sel" data-w305rt="' + E(key) + '"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + ' aria-label="Where replies go">'
      + '<option value=""' + (rt ? '' : ' selected') + '>The sending address (' + E(sender(t)) + ')</option>'
      + '<optgroup label="Group addresses">' + (o.groups || []).map(function (g) { return opt(g, groupLabel(g) + ' (' + g.email + ')'); }).join('') + '</optgroup>'
      + '<optgroup label="Team">' + (o.people || []).map(function (p) { return opt(p, p.name + ' (' + p.email + ')'); }).join('') + '</optgroup>'
      + (rt && !(o.groups || []).concat(o.people || []).some(function (x) { return x.email === rt; }) ? '<option value="' + E(rt) + '" selected>' + E(rt) + '</option>' : '')
      + '</select>';
    return '<div class="w305p" data-w305p="' + E(key) + '">'
      + '<p class="m" style="margin:0 0 6px">Everyone ticked gets a copy of every email this template sends (never of a test). Welfare Avenue addresses only.</p>'
      + '<h5>Copy these group addresses</h5><div class="w305g">' + groups + '</div>'
      + '<h5>Copy these team members</h5><div class="w305g">' + people + extra + '</div>'
      + '<h5>When someone replies, it goes to</h5>' + sel
      + '<div class="w305f"><button class="w152btn sm" data-w305s="' + E(key) + '"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (busy ? 'Saving…' : 'Save') + '</button>'
      + '<button class="w152btn ghost sm" data-w305x="' + E(key) + '"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Cancel</button>'
      + (S.err[key] ? '<span class="w305e" role="alert">' + E(S.err[key]) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  /* a template's line on CRM, Email */
  function row(t, paint, reload) {
    if (!t || !t.key) return '';
    css(); S.paint = paint || S.paint; S.reload = reload || S.reload; S.tpl[t.key] = t;
    if (!S.opt) loadOpts();
    var on = !!S.open[t.key];
    return '<div class="w305"><div class="w305l"><span>' + summary(t) + '</span><button class="w152mini" data-w305o="' + E(t.key) + '" aria-expanded="' + (on ? 'true' : 'false') + '">' + (on ? 'Close' : 'Copies and replies') + '</button></div>'
      + (on ? panel(t) : '') + '</div>';
  }
  /* a form's confirmation email on CRM, Forms */
  function formHtml(f, templates, paint, reload) {
    css(); S.paint = paint || S.paint; S.reload = reload || S.reload;
    if (!templates) { if (reload) Promise.resolve(reload()).then(rp); return '<div class="w305form"><span class="m">Loading the emails…</span></div>'; }
    if (!S.opt) loadOpts();
    var st = f.settings || {}, cur = String(st.confirm_template || '');   /* saved with confirm = !!confirm_template (wave305 in saveForm) */
    var list = templates.filter(function (t) { return t.kind === 'transactional' && (t.active || t.key === cur) && !t.one_off; });
    list.forEach(function (t) { S.tpl[t.key] = t; });
    var t = list.filter(function (x) { return x.key === cur; })[0];
    return '<div class="w305form"><label class="w155lb">After they send it, email them<select class="w152sel" data-w156set="confirm_template" data-w305form="1">'
      + '<option value=""' + (cur ? '' : ' selected') + '>No email</option>'
      + list.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + E(x.key) + '"' + (x.key === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(x.name) + (x.lang === 'fr' ? ' (FR)' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('')
      + '</select></label>'
      + (t ? '<div class="w305"><span class="m">' + summary(t) + '. Change them on CRM, Email, under the template.</span></div>' : '<p class="m" style="margin:4px 0 0">Confirmations and reminders only (transactional emails). Make one in the Email tab or the studio.</p>')
      + '</div>';
  }
  function save(key, el) {
    var p = el.closest('[data-w305p]'); if (!p) return;
    var cc = Array.prototype.map.call(p.querySelectorAll('input[data-w305cc]:checked'), function (i) { return i.getAttribute('data-w305cc'); });
    var rtEl = p.querySelector('select[data-w305rt]'), rt = rtEl ? rtEl.value : '';
    S.busy[key] = true; S.err[key] = ''; rp();
    Promise.resolve(db.rpc('wa305_mail_routing_set', { p_key: key, p_cc: cc, p_reply: rt || null })).then(function (r) {
      S.busy[key] = false;
      if (r && r.error) { S.err[key] = 'Not saved: ' + String(r.error.message || r.error).replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, '').slice(0, 140); rp(); return; }
      var t = S.tpl[key]; if (t && r && r.data) { t.cc = r.data.cc || []; t.reply_to = r.data.reply_to || null; }
      delete S.open[key];
      try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('Copies and replies saved'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      Promise.resolve(S.reload ? S.reload() : null).then(rp, rp);
    }, function (e) { S.busy[key] = false; S.err[key] = 'Not saved: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 140); rp(); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w305o], [data-w305s], [data-w305x]') : null;
    if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    if (b.disabled) return;
    if (b.hasAttribute('data-w305o')) { var k = b.getAttribute('data-w305o'); if (S.open[k]) delete S.open[k]; else { S.open[k] = true; S.err[k] = ''; } rp(); return; }
    if (b.hasAttribute('data-w305x')) { delete S.open[b.getAttribute('data-w305x')]; rp(); return; }
    save(b.getAttribute('data-w305s'), b);
  }, true);
  /* the form editor's select: wave156 stores it in settings; repaint so the line under it follows */
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var el = e.target; if (!el || !el.hasAttribute || !el.hasAttribute('data-w305form')) return;
    setTimeout(rp, 0);
  });
  window.w305 = { row: row, formHtml: formHtml, state: function () { return S; } };
})();

/* =====================================================================
   WA v110 boot veil + self-provisioning + access requests (08/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   1. Boot veil (with the head CSS in 000_head_base): html.wa-ready is
      set once startApp has fully resolved through every wave wrapper
      (this wave loads last, so its startApp wrap finishes last) plus a
      short settle. Ends the "last five versions flash by" on load.
      wave222 (09/10/26): the veil no longer gives up after 9 seconds.
      On a slow connection the start chain takes longer than that, so
      the page used to appear half-built and then rebuild itself in
      front of the reader for several seconds (every page, computer and
      phone). Now the veil waits for the start to finish, then for the
      menu (wave163) and for the page to be still for a moment, and
      lifts once. Only a start that never finishes (a request that
      hangs) is cut short, at 25 seconds, as the menu's own failsafe
      is at 20. The sign-in screen still shows at once (wave175b).
   2. Self-provisioning: any @welfareavenue.com sign-in with no
      hub_roles row writes its own row as 'member' (RLS allows exactly
      that and nothing else), so admins see every real user in the
      roles table instead of an implicit default.
   3. Access requests: a "Request more access" button on My page (and
      on the overview for anyone) opens a modal: pick what you want
      (a role step up, or specific pages) + a reason; lands in
      wa_access_requests, pings the Slack channel via notify. Admins get
      an "Access requests" card in the Admin panel (above the roles
      table): Grant applies the change to hub_roles (role change or tab
      override) under the admin's own RLS and marks the request; Decline
      marks it. Everything change_logged.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa110) return; window.__wa110 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w110btn{margin-left:8px;}' +
    '#w110ar table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '#w110ar td{padding:6px;border-top:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.18);vertical-align:top;}' +
    '#w110ar .mut{color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);font-size:11px;}' +
    '.w110st{font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;border-radius:6px;padding:1px 8px;}' +
    '.w110st.new{background:rgba(255,171,0,.16);color:#a06f00;}' +
    '.w110st.granted{background:rgba(16,63,57,.14);color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w110st.declined{background:rgba(133,146,163,.16);color:#103F39;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* wave131: today's pages, named as the menu names them */
  var TAB_LABEL = {
    dashboard: 'Delivery numbers and donations', opsmtl: 'Operations Montreal', progdev: 'Program Development', opsto: 'Operations Toronto',
    marketing: 'Marketing, Branding & Web', contentcal: 'Content calendar', socialstats: 'Analytics', studio: 'Content Studio', fundraising: 'Fundraising',
    fundevents: 'Events calendar', grants: 'Grants', fund: 'Fundraising app', finances: 'Finance', hr: 'Human Resources', hrcentre: 'HR centre', board: 'Board team page',
    governance: 'Board overview', committees: 'Committees', agendas: 'Agendas', hubmail: 'Hub inbox', transcripts: 'Transcript archive',
    activity: 'Activity', training: 'Avenue Academy', brain: 'The Brain', orgchart: 'Org chart', bank: 'The Task Bank', crm: 'CRM', orggoals: 'Goals and the Strategy Library', adminpanel: 'Admin',
    toronto: 'Toronto Volunteers'   /* wave274 */
  };
  function me() {
    return {
      email: ((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(),
      name: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && (USER.name || USER.email)) || ''
    };
  }

  /* ---------- 1. boot veil ---------- */
  var w222now = function () { try { return performance.now(); } catch (e) { return Date.now(); } };
  var W222 = { t0: w222now(), started: null, lifted: null, why: null };
  function w110Reveal(why) {
    if (W222.lifted !== null) return;
    W222.lifted = Math.round(w222now() - W222.t0); W222.why = why || 'reveal';
    try { document.documentElement.classList.add('wa-ready'); } catch (e) { /* no */ }
    try { setTimeout(function () { var v = document.getElementById('wa110veil'); if (v) v.remove(); }, 450); } catch (e) { /* no */ }
  }
  /* wave222: the page lifts the veil when the Hub has started (below); this only catches a start that never finishes */
  setTimeout(function () { w110Reveal('failsafe'); }, 25000);
  /* wave222: after the start chain, let the page be still for a moment (no visible change for 180 ms, at most 1.2 s) before it shows */
  function w110Settle(done) {
    var main = document.body;
    if (!main || typeof MutationObserver !== 'function') { done(); return; }
    var now = w222now;
    var start = now(), last = now(), mo = null;
    try {
      mo = new MutationObserver(function (list) {
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
          var t = list[i].target, e = t && t.nodeType === 1 ? t : (t && t.parentElement);
          if (!e || (e.closest && e.closest('#wa110veil, head, script, style'))) continue;
          last = now(); return;
        }
      });
      mo.observe(main, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class', 'style', 'hidden'] });
    } catch (e) { done(); return; }
    (function check() {
      if (W222.lifted !== null) { try { mo.disconnect(); } catch (e) { /* no */ } return; }
      if (now() - last >= 180 || now() - start > 1200) { try { mo.disconnect(); } catch (e) { /* no */ } done(); return; }
      setTimeout(check, 40);
    })();
  }
  try { window.w222 = { state: function () { return { started: W222.started, lifted: W222.lifted, why: W222.why }; } }; } catch (e) { /* no */ }

  /* ---------- 2. self-provisioning ---------- */
  async function w110Provision() {
    try {
      var m = me();
      if (!m.email || m.email.indexOf('@welfareavenue.com') < 0) return;
      var roles = (typeof WA40_ROLES !== 'undefined' && WA40_ROLES) || [];
      if (roles.some(function (r) { return r.email === m.email; })) return;
      var r = await db.from('hub_roles').select('email').eq('email', m.email);
      if (r.data && r.data.length) return;
      var ins = await db.from('hub_roles').insert({ email: m.email, role: 'member', note: m.name || null, updated_by: 'self (first sign-in)', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
      if (!ins.error) {
        try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'hub_role', entity_id: m.email, field: 'created', old_value: null, new_value: 'member (self-provisioned on first sign-in)', changed_by: m.email }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
        if (typeof WA40_ROLES !== 'undefined') WA40_ROLES.push({ email: m.email, role: 'member', tabs: null, note: m.name });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* silent: member default applies anyway */ }
  }

  /* ---------- 3. access requests ---------- */
  function w110Modal() {
    var m = me();
    var role = (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || 'member';
    /* wave131: say it in profiles, and only offer the pages they do not have */
    var have = []; try { have = (typeof wa40Allowed === 'function' && wa40Allowed()) || []; } catch (e) { have = []; }
    var pages = Object.keys(TAB_LABEL).filter(function (k) { return k !== 'adminpanel' && have.indexOf(k) < 0; });
    var prof = ''; try { var pk = window.w127 && window.w127.mine ? window.w127.mine() : null; var st = pk && window.w127.state ? window.w127.state() : null; if (st && st.byKey && st.byKey[pk]) prof = st.byKey[pk].label + (role === 'leader' && st.byKey[pk].tier === 'member' ? ' (lead)' : ''); } catch (e) { prof = ''; }
    openModal(
      '<h3>Request more access</h3>' +
      '<p class="read" style="margin:4px 0 8px">' + (prof ? 'Your access profile is <b>' + esc(prof) + '</b>.' : 'You have the classic <b>' + esc(role) + '</b> access.') + ' Ask for a page you are missing, the lead option for your team, or Admin; an admin reviews it and you will see the answer on this page.</p>' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">What do you need?</label>' +
      '<select id="w110want" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box">' +
      pages.map(function (k) { return '<option value="tab:' + k + '">Page: ' + esc(TAB_LABEL[k]) + '</option>'; }).join('') +
      (role === 'leader' || role === 'admin' ? '' : '<option value="role:leader">Lead access for my team (Agendas, Activity, the HR centre for my team, the board pages)</option>') +
      (role === 'admin' ? '' : '<option value="role:admin">Admin (people and access, settings, AI models)</option>') +
      '<option value="other">Something else (describe below)</option>' +
      '</select>' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">Why (one or two lines)</label>' +
      '<input type="text" id="w110why" maxlength="300" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" placeholder="Example: I co-lead the Toronto serving events" />' +
      '<div style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn" id="w110go">Send the request</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w110x">Cancel</button></div>'
    );
    document.getElementById('w110x').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w110go').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var want = (document.getElementById('w110want') || {}).value || 'other';
      var why = ((document.getElementById('w110why') || {}).value || '').trim();
      if (want === 'other' && why.length < 6) { toast('Tell us what you need'); return; }
      this.disabled = true;
      var ins = await db.from('wa_access_requests').insert({ email: m.email, name: m.name, role_now: role, wanted: want, reason: why || null });
      if (ins.error) { this.disabled = false; toast('Could not send it'); return; }
      try {
        await db.functions.invoke('notify', { body: { event: 'change_request_text', text: ':key: Access request from ' + m.name + ' (' + m.email + '): ' + want + (why ? ' - ' + why : '') + '. Review in Admin > Access requests.' } });
      } catch (e) { /* best-effort ping */ }
      closeModal();
      toast('Request sent to the admins');
      w110MyStatus();
    });
  }
  function w110Button(host, id) {
    if (!host || document.getElementById(id)) return;
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'btn ghost mini';
    b.id = id;
    b.textContent = 'Request more access';
    b.addEventListener('click', w110Modal);
    host.appendChild(b);
  }
  async function w110MyStatus() {
    try {
      var m = me();
      if (!m.email) return;
      var box = document.getElementById('w110mine');
      var r = await db.from('wa_access_requests').select('*').eq('email', m.email).order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(3);
      var rows = (r.data) || [];
      if (!box) {
        var panel = document.getElementById('mypage');
        var heading = panel && panel.querySelector('h2');
        if (!heading) return;
        box = document.createElement('div');
        box.id = 'w110mine';
        box.style.cssText = 'margin:6px 0 10px;font-size:12px;';
        heading.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', box);
        w110Button(box, 'w110btn');
      }
      var list = box.querySelector('.w110list');
      if (!list) { list = document.createElement('span'); list.className = 'w110list'; list.style.marginLeft = '10px'; box.appendChild(list); }
      list.innerHTML = rows.map(function (q) {
        var w = q.wanted.indexOf('tab:') === 0 ? (TAB_LABEL[q.wanted.slice(4)] || q.wanted.slice(4)) : q.wanted.replace('role:', '') + ' role';
        return '<span class="w110st ' + esc(q.status) + '" title="' + esc(q.reason || '') + '">' + esc(w) + ': ' + esc(q.status) + '</span> ';
      }).join('');
    } catch (e) { /* optional */ }
  }

  /* admin card */
  async function w110AdminCard() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w40body');
    if (!body || document.getElementById('w110ar')) return;
    if (!(typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin')) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.id = 'w110ar';
    card.style.cssText = 'margin:0 0 14px;padding:10px 12px;background:#fbfbfd;border:1px solid #eef0f4;border-radius:10px;';
    card.innerHTML = '<h3 class="block" style="margin-top:0">Access requests</h3><div id="w110arbody">Loading...</div>';
    body.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', card);
    await w110AdminRender();
  }
  async function w110AdminRender() {
    var b = document.getElementById('w110arbody');
    if (!b) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('wa_access_requests').select('*').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(40);
      var rows = (r.data) || [];
      var open = rows.filter(function (q) { return q.status === 'new'; });
      if (!rows.length) { b.innerHTML = '<p class="read">No requests yet. People ask from their My page.</p>'; return; }
      var html = '<table><tbody>' + rows.slice(0, 25).map(function (q) {
        var w = q.wanted.indexOf('tab:') === 0 ? 'Page: ' + (TAB_LABEL[q.wanted.slice(4)] || q.wanted.slice(4)) : (q.wanted.indexOf('role:') === 0 ? q.wanted.slice(5) + ' role' : 'Other');
        return '<tr><td><b>' + esc(q.name || q.email) + '</b><div class="mut">' + esc(q.email) + ' &middot; now ' + esc(q.role_now || 'member') + ' &middot; ' + esc(String(q.created_at).slice(0, 10)) + '</div></td>'
          + '<td>' + esc(w) + (q.reason ? '<div class="mut">' + esc(q.reason) + '</div>' : '') + '</td>'
          + '<td style="white-space:nowrap">' + (q.status === 'new'
            ? '<button class="btn mini" data-w110g="' + q.id + '">Grant</button> <button class="btn ghost mini" data-w110d="' + q.id + '">Decline</button>'
            : '<span class="w110st ' + esc(q.status) + '">' + esc(q.status) + '</span><div class="mut">' + esc((q.decided_by || '').split('@')[0]) + '</div>') + '</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table>';
      html += '<p class="mut" style="margin:8px 0 0">' + open.length + ' waiting. Grant applies the change to the roles table below immediately.</p>';
      b.innerHTML = html;
      b.querySelectorAll('[data-w110g],[data-w110d]').forEach(function (btn) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', async function () {
          var grant = this.hasAttribute('data-w110g');
          var id = Number(this.getAttribute(grant ? 'data-w110g' : 'data-w110d'));
          var q = rows.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
          if (!q) return;
          this.disabled = true;
          var admin = me().email;
          if (grant) {
            var ok = await w110Apply(q, admin);
            if (!ok) { this.disabled = false; return; }
          }
          var up = await db.from('wa_access_requests').update({ status: grant ? 'granted' : 'declined', decided_by: admin, decided_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', id);
          if (up.error) { toast('Could not save the decision'); this.disabled = false; return; }
          toast(grant ? 'Granted' : 'Declined');
          w110AdminRender();
          try { if (typeof wa40Load === 'function') { await wa40Load(); } if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') wa40Render(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        });
      });
    } catch (e) { b.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load requests.</p>'; }
  }
  async function w110Apply(q, admin) {
    try {
      var cur = (typeof WA40_ROLES !== 'undefined' ? WA40_ROLES : []).filter(function (r) { return r.email === q.email; })[0];
      var patch;
      if (q.wanted.indexOf('role:') === 0) {
        var role = q.wanted.slice(5);
        if (['admin', 'leader', 'member', 'viewer'].indexOf(role) < 0) { toast('Unknown role'); return false; }
        patch = { email: q.email, role: role, updated_by: admin, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        if (cur && Array.isArray(cur.tabs)) patch.tabs = cur.tabs;
      } else if (q.wanted.indexOf('tab:') === 0) {
        var tab = q.wanted.slice(4);
        var base = cur && Array.isArray(cur.tabs) && cur.tabs.length ? cur.tabs.slice()
          : ((typeof WA40_DEFAULTS !== 'undefined' && WA40_DEFAULTS[(cur && cur.role) || 'member']) || []).slice();
        if (base.indexOf(tab) < 0) base.push(tab);
        patch = { email: q.email, role: (cur && cur.role) || 'member', tabs: base, updated_by: admin, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      } else {
        toast('Free-text request: set the role or tabs in the table below, then decline or grant to close it');
        return true;
      }
      var r = await db.from('hub_roles').upsert(patch, { onConflict: 'email' });
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not update the role: ' + (r.error.message || '')); return false; }
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'hub_role', entity_id: q.email, field: q.wanted.indexOf('role:') === 0 ? 'role' : 'tabs', old_value: cur ? (q.wanted.indexOf('role:') === 0 ? cur.role : JSON.stringify(cur.tabs || null)) : null, new_value: q.wanted + ' (access request #' + q.id + ')', changed_by: admin }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
      return true;
    } catch (e) { toast('Could not apply the change'); return false; }
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __w40r110 = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () {
      await __w40r110.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w110AdminCard(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  (function () {
    var t = null;
    function arm() {
      var my = document.getElementById('mypage');
      if (my && !my.dataset.w110mo) {
        my.dataset.w110mo = '1';
        new MutationObserver(function () {
          clearTimeout(t);
          t = setTimeout(function () { try { if (my.children.length && !document.getElementById('w110mine')) w110MyStatus(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 380);
        }).observe(my, { childList: true });
        if (my.children.length) { try { w110MyStatus(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
      }
    }
    if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
      var __sa110 = startApp;
      startApp = async function () {
        try { await __sa110.apply(this, arguments); }
        finally {
          /* every earlier wave's wrapper has resolved by now; wave163: the menu settles first (at most 1.5 s), then wave222:
             the page is still for a moment, so it appears whole, once */
          W222.started = Math.round(w222now() - W222.t0);
          var w163wait = 0;
          (function w163when() {
            if (document.documentElement.classList.contains('w163nav') || w163wait >= 1500) { w110Settle(function () { w110Reveal('started'); }); return; }
            w163wait += 50; setTimeout(w163when, 50);
          })();
        }
        try { arm(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
        try { await w110Provision(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      };
    } else {
      setTimeout(function () { w110Reveal('no startApp'); }, 1500);
    }
  })();
})();
</script>
